function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
}
function minutesOfClock(value) {
  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}
function slotRange(slot) {
  return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
}
function isWednesdayKey(value) {
  const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
    const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
    return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
  }
  return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
}
function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).filter(program=>program?.kind!=="after");
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}
function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){
  const key=String(day||'').slice(0,10);
  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);
  if(fixed2026.has(key))return true;
  const closureRx=/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일|석가탄신일|부처님오신날|어린이날|현충일|광복절|개천절|한글날|성탄절|설날|추석/;
  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;
    const start=String(event.date||'').slice(0,10), end=String(event.endDate||event.date||'').slice(0,10);
    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}
function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
  return day;
}
function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
  const selectedNo=String(row?.studentNo||"");
  const options=students.map(student=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"")}" ${String(student.studentNo)===selectedNo?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(student.name||"")} · ${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(student))}반</option>`).join("");
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent=row?"OFFICIAL ATTENDANCE · EDIT":"OFFICIAL ATTENDANCE · NEW";

    <section class="official-editor-section"><header><b>대상·일자</b><small>학생과 공결 적용일을 선택합니다.</small></header><div class="official-editor-grid two">

      <label>학생<select id="officialStudent" required><option value="">학생 선택</option>${options}</select></label>
    </div></section>
    <section class="official-editor-section"><header><b>출결 구분</b><small>하루 전체 또는 교시 단위로 기록할 수 있습니다.</small></header><div class="official-editor-grid four">
      <label>출결구분<select id="officialAttendanceType"><option ${row?.attendanceType==="출석인정"?"selected":""}>출석인정</option><option ${row?.attendanceType==="질병"?"selected":""}>질병</option><option ${row?.attendanceType==="미인정"?"selected":""}>미인정</option><option ${row?.attendanceType==="기타"?"selected":""}>기타</option></select></label>

      <label>시작교시<input id="officialStartPeriod" inputmode="numeric" value="${escapeHtml(row?.startPeriod||"")}" placeholder="예: 5"></label>
      <label>종료교시<input id="officialEndPeriod" inputmode="numeric" value="${escapeHtml(row?.endPeriod||"")}" placeholder="예: 7"></label>
    </div></section>
    <section class="official-editor-section"><header><b>사유·근거</b><small>담임이 바로 이해할 수 있도록 구체적으로 작성합니다.</small></header><div class="official-editor-grid two">
      <label class="span-two">사유<input id="officialReason" required value="${escapeHtml(row?.reasonText||row?.reason||"")}" placeholder="공결 사유를 입력하세요"></label>
      <label>장소<input id="officialPlace" value="${escapeHtml(row?.place||"")}" placeholder="장소"></label>
      <label>증빙상태<select id="officialEvidence"><option ${row?.evidence==="불필요"?"selected":""}>불필요</option><option ${row?.evidence==="확인완료"?"selected":""}>확인완료</option><option ${row?.evidence==="미확인"?"selected":""}>미확인</option><option ${row?.evidence==="제출대기"?"selected":""}>제출대기</option></select></label>

    const student=students.find(item=>String(item.studentNo)===String(studentNo));
    if(!student)return toast("학생을 선택하세요.");

    }catch(error){toast(error?.message||"공결 저장에 실패했습니다.");}
  };
}
async function deleteOfficialAttendanceRow(row){
  if(!row?.id)return;
  if(!confirm(`${row.date} ${row.studentNo} ${row.name} 공결 기록을 삭제할까요?`))return;
  try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.deleteOfficialAttendance(row.id);if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};render("attendance");toast("공결 기록을 삭제했습니다.");}catch(error){toast(error?.message||"공결 삭제에 실패했습니다.");}
}

function openLateAttendanceEditor(row=null){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin")return toast("지각 기록 수정은 관리자만 가능합니다.");
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
  const selectedNo=String(row?.studentNo||"");
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent=row?"LATE ATTENDANCE · EDIT":"LATE ATTENDANCE · NEW";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent=row?"지각 기록 수정":"지각 기록 등록";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<form id="lateAttendanceEditor" class="official-attendance-editor">
    <div class="official-editor-grid two"><label>일자<input type="date" id="lateDate" required value="${escapeHtml(row?.date||attendanceViewDate||dateKey(today))}"></label><label>학생<select id="lateStudent" required><option value="">학생 선택</option>${students.map(st=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(st.studentNo||"")}" ${String(st.studentNo)===selectedNo?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(st.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(st.name||"")} · ${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(st))}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
    <label>제출시각<input type="time" id="lateTime" value="${escapeHtml(row?.time||"")}"></label><label>지각시간(분)<input type="number" min="0" id="lateMinutes" value="${escapeHtml(row?.minutes||"")}"></label>
    <label class="span-two">사유<input id="lateReason" required value="${escapeHtml(row?.reason||"")}"></label><label>지도상태<select id="lateGuidance"><option ${row?.guidance==="일반"?"selected":""}>일반</option><option ${String(row?.guidance||"").includes("필요")?"selected":""}>지도필요</option><option ${row?.guidance==="지도완료"?"selected":""}>지도완료</option></select></label><label>비고<input id="lateNote" value="${escapeHtml(row?.note||"")}"></label></div>
    <div class="official-editor-actions">${row?'<button type="button" class="btn danger" id="lateDelete">삭제</button>':''}<button type="button" class="btn secondary" id="lateCancel">취소</button><button class="btn primary">${row?"수정 저장":"지각 등록"}</button></div></form>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");$("#lateCancel").onclick=closeDrawer;
  if(row&&$("#lateDelete"))$("#lateDelete").onclick=async()=>{await deleteLateAttendanceRow(row);closeDrawer();};
  $("#lateAttendanceEditor").onsubmit=async event=>{event.preventDefault();const student=students.find(st=>String(st.studentNo)===String($("#lateStudent").value));if(!student)return toast("학생을 선택하세요.");
    const previous=(readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(x=>String(x.studentId)===String(student.id)&&String(x.id)!==String(row?.id||""));
    const minutes=Number($("#lateMinutes").value||0), cumulativeCount=previous.length+1, cumulativeMinutes=previous.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+Number(x.minutes||0),0)+minutes;
    const payload={id:row?.id||"",studentId:student.id,studentNo:student.studentNo,name:student.name,grade:String(student.studentNo||"").slice(0,1),className:classNumberOf(student),date:$("#lateDate").value,time:$("#lateTime").value,reason:$("#lateReason").value.trim(),minutes,cumulativeCount,cumulativeMinutes,guidance:$("#lateGuidance").value,note:$("#lateNote").value.trim(),editor:currentLoginTeacherName()};
    try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveLateAttendance(payload);if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};attendanceViewDate=payload.date;closeDrawer();render("attendance");toast(row?"지각 기록을 수정했습니다.":"지각 기록을 등록했습니다.");}catch(error){toast(error?.message||"지각 저장에 실패했습니다.");}
  };
}
async function deleteLateAttendanceRow(row){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin"||!row?.id)return;
  if(!confirm(`${row.date} ${row.studentNo} ${row.name} 지각 기록을 삭제할까요?`))return;
  try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.deleteLateAttendance(row.id);if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};render("attendance");toast("지각 기록을 삭제했습니다.");}catch(error){toast(error?.message||"지각 삭제에 실패했습니다.");}
}

function attendancePersonalStatisticsView(officialRows,lateRows,nightRows) {
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
  if(attendanceStatStudentFilter!=="all"&&!students.some(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)===attendanceStatStudentFilter))attendanceStatStudentFilter="all";
  const selectedStudent=attendanceStatStudentFilter==="all"?null:students.find(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)===attendanceStatStudentFilter);
  const filterRows=rows=>selectedStudent?rows.filter(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===attendanceStudentKey(selectedStudent)):rows;
  const official=filterRows(officialRows).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const late=filterRows(lateRows).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const night=filterRows(nightRows).filter(row=>{
    const sourceText=`${row.result||""} ${row.type||""} ${row.affiliation||""} ${row.detail||""}`;
    const dormOnly=/학사/.test(sourceText)&&!/방과후|야간심화|공동교육과정|프로그램|출석|지각/.test(sourceText);
    return !dormOnly;
  }).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const dates=rows=>[...new Set(rows.map(x=>x.date).filter(Boolean))].sort().reverse();
  const classes=[...new Set(students.map(s=>String(classNumberOf(s)||'')).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  if(selectedStudent) attendanceStatClassFilter=String(classNumberOf(selectedStudent)||attendanceStatClassFilter||'all');
  const scopedStudents=attendanceStatClassFilter==='all'?students:students.filter(s=>String(classNumberOf(s))===String(attendanceStatClassFilter));
  const classOptions=`<option value="all">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(c)}" ${String(attendanceStatClassFilter)===String(c)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(c)}반</option>`).join('')}`;
  const studentOptions=scopedStudents.map(s=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(s))}" ${attendanceStatStudentFilter===attendanceStudentKey(s)?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(s.name||"")}</option>`).join("");
  const metricCard=(label,rows,detail)=>`<article class="attendance-personal-metric"><small>${label}</small><b>${rows.length}<em>건</em></b><span>${detail(rows)}</span></article>`;
  const history=(label,rows,value)=>`<section class="attendance-personal-history"><header><h3>${label}</h3><span>${rows.length}건 · ${dates(rows).length}일</span></header>${rows.length?rows.slice(0,60).map(row=>`<article><time>${escapeHtml(row.date||"-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(value(row))}</b><p>${escapeHtml(row.reason||row.detail||row.result||row.programTitle||"")}</p></article>`).join(""):'<div class="empty-inline">기록이 없습니다.</div>'}</section>`;
  return `<div class="attendance-stat-page"><section class="attendance-stat-selector"><div><small>ATTENDANCE · PERSONAL INSIGHT</small><h3>${selectedStudent?`${escapeHtml(selectedStudent.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(selectedStudent.name)}`:"학생 개인 출결 현황판"}</h3><p>반별 비교 없이 학생 한 명의 공결·지각·야자출결 누적 기록과 날짜를 확인합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-stat-pickers"><label>반 선택<select id="attendanceStatClassFilter">${classOptions}</select></label><label>학생 선택<select id="attendanceStatStudentFilter"><option value="all">학생을 선택하세요</option>${studentOptions}</select></label></div></section>${selectedStudent?`<div class="attendance-personal-metrics">${metricCard("공결",official,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 최근 ${dates(r)[0]||"-"}`)}${metricCard("지각",late,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 최근 ${dates(r)[0]||"-"}`)}${metricCard("야자출결",night,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 학사 제외 · 실제 출석·프로그램만`)}</div><div class="attendance-personal-history-grid">${history("공결 기록",official,row=>`${row.type||"공결"} · ${row.period||"하루 전체"}`)}${history("지각 기록",late,row=>`${row.time||row.period||"지각"}`)}${history("야자출결 기록",night,row=>`${row.result||row.type||row.detail||"출결"}`)}</div>`:'<div class="query-empty attendance-stat-empty"><b>학생을 선택하면 개인 출결 통계가 표시됩니다.</b><small>공결 횟수와 날짜, 지각 횟수와 날짜, 야자출결 누적 현황을 한 화면에서 확인합니다.</small></div>'}</div>`;
}

function uepComparableDate(value,basis=attendanceViewDate||dateKey(today)){
  if(value==null||value==='')return '';
  if(typeof value==='object')value=value.formattedValue||value.value||value.date||value.serial||'';
  const text=String(value).trim();
  // __UEP_08160_ISO_CALENDAR_DATE__
  if(/^20\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2}T.*(?:Z|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/i.test(text)){
    const instant=new Date(text);
    if(!Number.isNaN(instant.getTime())){
      const parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts(instant);
      const p=Object.fromEntries(parts.map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
      if(p.year&&p.month&&p.day)return p.year+'-'+p.month+'-'+p.day;
    }
  }
  if(/^\d{5}(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(text)){
    const serial=Number(text),utc=new Date(Math.round((serial-25569)*86400000));
    if(!Number.isNaN(utc.getTime()))return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  }
  const full=text.match(/(20\d{2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})/);
  if(full)return `${full[1]}-${full[2].padStart(2,'0')}-${full[3].padStart(2,'0')}`;
  const compact=text.replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(/^20\d{6}$/.test(compact))return compact.slice(0,4)+'-'+compact.slice(4,6)+'-'+compact.slice(6,8);
  const monthDay=text.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})\s*(?:월|[.\-/])\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*일)?(?:\D|$)/);
  if(monthDay){const year=String(basis||dateKey(today)).slice(0,4)||'2026';return year+'-'+monthDay[1].padStart(2,'0')+'-'+monthDay[2].padStart(2,'0');}
  const parsed=new Date(text);
  if(!Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()))return `${parsed.getFullYear()}-${String(parsed.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(parsed.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  return text.slice(0,10);
}
function attendanceView() {
  const officialRows = readonlyCache?.officialAttendance || [];
  const lateRows = readonlyCache?.lateAttendance || [];
  const nightRows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];
  if(attendanceMode==="night"&&!isNightOperatingDay(attendanceViewDate)){
    attendanceViewDate=nearestNightOperatingDay(attendanceViewDate);
    nightViewMonth=attendanceViewDate.slice(0,7);
  }
  const tabs = `<div class="attendance-tabs attendance-tabs-four"><button data-attendance-mode="official" class="${attendanceMode === "official" ? "active" : ""}">공결</button><button data-attendance-mode="late" class="${attendanceMode === "late" ? "active" : ""}">지각</button><button data-attendance-mode="night" class="${attendanceMode === "night" ? "active" : ""}">야자출결</button><button data-attendance-mode="statistics" class="${attendanceMode === "statistics" ? "active" : ""}">통계</button></div>`;
  if(attendanceMode === "statistics") return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendancePersonalStatisticsView(officialRows,lateRows,nightRows)}</div>`;
  const genericRows = (rows, mode) => {
    if (!rows.length) return `<div class="empty-state data-card"><b>선택일에 ${mode} 기록이 없습니다.</b><span>${escapeHtml(attendanceViewDate)} 기준</span></div>`;
    return `<div class="data-card" id="attendanceRows"><div class="data-head attendance-detail-grid"><span>일자</span><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>구분</span><span>시간·교시</span><span>사유·상태</span><span>확인</span></div>${rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date))).map(row => `<div class="data-row attendance-detail-grid attendance-row" data-search="${escapeHtml([row.date,row.studentNo,row.name,row.className,row.type,row.detail,row.reason,row.result].join(" ").toLowerCase())}"><time>${escapeHtml(row.date || "-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo || "-")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row.name || "-")} · ${escapeHtml(row.className || "-")}반</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.type || mode)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.time || row.period || "-")}</span><span title="${escapeHtml(row.sourceTab?`출처: ${row.sourceTab}`:"")}">${escapeHtml(row.reason || row.detail || row.result || "-")}${row.sourceTab?`<small class="attendance-source-tab">${escapeHtml(row.sourceTab)}</small>`:""}</span><span class="state ${String(row.result || row.guidance).includes("필요") || /결석|미입실|미제출/.test(String(row.result)) ? "warn" : ""}">${escapeHtml(row.result || row.guidance || "확인")}</span></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
  };
  if (attendanceMode === "official") {
    const selectedDay = officialAttendanceRowsForDate08162(attendanceViewDate);
    const classes = Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1));
    const classSummary = classes.map(classNo=>{
      const rows=selectedDay.filter(row=>String(row.className||row.classNo||"")===classNo);
      return {classNo,records:rows.length,students:new Set(rows.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size};
    });
    const classFiltered = officialClassFilter === "all" ? selectedDay : selectedDay.filter(row=>String(row.className||row.classNo||"")===String(officialClassFilter));
    const rosterStudents=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(student=>officialClassFilter==="all"||String(classNumberOf(student))===String(officialClassFilter)).sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
    const studentOptions=rosterStudents;
    if(officialStudentFilter!=="all"&&!studentOptions.some(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===officialStudentFilter))officialStudentFilter="all";
    const selected = officialStudentFilter === "all" ? classFiltered : classFiltered.filter(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===officialStudentFilter);
    const cumulativeRows=officialStudentFilter==="all"?[]:officialRows.filter(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===officialStudentFilter).sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
    const classCards=`<div class="night-class-overview official-class-overview"><button data-official-class="all" class="${officialClassFilter==="all"?"active":""}"><small>전체</small><b>${selectedDay.length}<em>기록</em></b><span>${new Set(selectedDay.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}명</span></button>${classSummary.map(item=>`<button data-official-class="${item.classNo}" class="${officialClassFilter===item.classNo?"active":""}"><small>${item.classNo}반</small><b>${item.records}<em>기록</em></b><span>${item.students}명</span></button>`).join("")}</div>`;
    const studentSelect=`<label class="official-student-filter">개인별 조회<select id="officialStudentFilter"><option value="all">${officialClassFilter==="all"?"전체 학생":"선택 반 전체"}</option>${studentOptions.map(row=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(row))}" ${officialStudentFilter===attendanceStudentKey(row)?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(row.name||"")}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>`;
    const canManageOfficial=currentRoleId()==="admin";
    const officialTable=(rows,emptyLabel="공결 기록이 없습니다.")=>rows.length?`<div class="data-card" id="attendanceRows"><div class="data-head attendance-detail-grid official-detail-grid${canManageOfficial?'':' readonly'}"><span>일자</span><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>구분</span><span>시간·교시</span><span>사유·상태</span>${canManageOfficial?'<span>관리</span>':''}</div>${rows.map(row=>`<div class="data-row attendance-detail-grid official-detail-grid attendance-row${canManageOfficial?'':' readonly'}" data-search="${escapeHtml([row.date,row.studentNo,row.name,row.className,row.type,row.reason,row.result].join(" ").toLowerCase())}"><time>${escapeHtml(row.date||"-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||"-")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row.name||"-")} · ${escapeHtml(row.className||"-")}반</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.type||"공결")}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.period||"하루 전체")}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.reason||"-")}<small class="attendance-source-tab">${escapeHtml(row.sourceTab||"30_공결기록")}</small></span>${canManageOfficial?`<span class="official-row-actions"><button class="link-button" data-official-edit="${escapeHtml(row.id)}">수정</button><button class="link-button danger" data-official-delete="${escapeHtml(row.id)}">삭제</button></span>`:''}</div>`).join("")}</div>`:`<div class="empty-state data-card"><b>${escapeHtml(emptyLabel)}</b></div>`;
    const selectedStudent=officialStudentFilter==="all"?null:rosterStudents.find(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===officialStudentFilter);
    const cumulativeMarkup=officialStudentFilter!=="all"?`<div class="section-title-row official-cumulative-title"><div><small>CUMULATIVE · PERSONAL</small><h3>개인 누적 공결 현황</h3><p>${cumulativeRows.length}건 · ${escapeHtml(selectedStudent?.studentNo||cumulativeRows[0]?.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(selectedStudent?.name||cumulativeRows[0]?.name||"")}</p></div></div>${officialTable(cumulativeRows,"누적 공결 기록이 없습니다.")}`:"";
    return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendanceDateControls("공결 조회일",canManageOfficial?'<button class="btn primary" id="officialAttendanceAdd">＋ 공결 등록</button>':'')}<section class="attendance-dashboard-head"><div><small>OFFICIAL ATTENDANCE · ${attendanceViewDate}</small><h3>공결 현황</h3><p>날짜별 전체 현황에서 반별·개인별로 조회하며, 등록·수정·삭제 내용은 기본정보 연결시트에 즉시 반영합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-head-metrics"><span><b>${selectedDay.length}</b>선택일 기록</span><span><b>${new Set(selectedDay.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}</b>선택일 학생</span><span><b>${officialRows.length}</b>전체 기록</span><span><b>${selectedDay.filter(x=>String(x.evidence).includes("미")).length}</b>증빙 확인</span></div></section>${classCards}<div class="toolbar official-toolbar">${studentSelect}<input class="search" id="attendanceSearch" placeholder="학번·이름·공결구분·사유 검색"><button class="btn secondary" id="attendanceSearchReset">검색 지우기</button><span class="state">기본정보 연결시트 · 30_공결기록</span></div>${officialTable(selected)}${cumulativeMarkup}</div>`;
  }

  if (attendanceMode === "late") {
    const selectedKey = attendanceViewDate;
    const allSelectedRows = lateRows.filter(row => row.date === selectedKey).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time).localeCompare(String(b.time)));
    const byStudent = new Map();
    lateRows.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date))).forEach(row => {
      const key = attendanceStudentKey(row);
      const current = byStudent.get(key) || { ...row, recordCount:0, totalMinutes:0, studentKey:key };
      current.recordCount += 1; current.totalMinutes += Number(row.minutes || 0);
      if (!current.date || String(row.date) >= String(current.date)) Object.assign(current, row);
      current.cumulativeCount = Math.max(Number(current.cumulativeCount || 0), Number(row.cumulativeCount || 0), current.recordCount);
      current.cumulativeMinutes = Math.max(Number(current.cumulativeMinutes || 0), current.totalMinutes);
      current.studentKey = key; byStudent.set(key, current);
    });
    const allCumulative = [...byStudent.values()].sort((a,b)=>Number(b.cumulativeCount)-Number(a.cumulativeCount) || Number(b.cumulativeMinutes)-Number(a.cumulativeMinutes));
    const selectedRows = lateClassFilter === "all" ? allSelectedRows : allSelectedRows.filter(row=>String(row.className)===String(lateClassFilter));
    const cumulative = lateClassFilter === "all" ? allCumulative : allCumulative.filter(row=>String(row.className)===String(lateClassFilter));
    const classSummary = Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1)).map(classNo=>{ const rows=lateRows.filter(row=>String(row.className)===classNo); const selectedStudents=new Set(allSelectedRows.filter(row=>String(row.className)===classNo).map(attendanceStudentKey)).size; return {classNo,selectedStudents,records:rows.length,students:new Set(rows.map(attendanceStudentKey)).size}; });
    const classCards = `<div class="night-class-overview late-class-overview"><button data-late-class="all" class="${lateClassFilter==="all"?"active":""}"><small>전체</small><b>${allSelectedRows.length}<em>선택일</em></b><span>누적 ${lateRows.length}건</span></button>${classSummary.map(item=>`<button data-late-class="${item.classNo}" class="${lateClassFilter===item.classNo?"active":""}"><small>${item.classNo}반</small><b>${item.selectedStudents}<em>선택일</em></b><span>${item.students}명 · 누적 ${item.records}건</span></button>`).join("")}</div>`;
    const selectedMarkup = selectedRows.length ? `<div class="data-card"><div class="data-head late-today-grid"><span>제출시각</span><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>지각시간</span><span>사유</span><span>지도상태</span></div>${selectedRows.map(row=>`<button class="data-row late-today-grid attendance-row history-row" data-late-student="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(row))}" data-late-record-id="${escapeHtml(row.id||'')}" data-search="${escapeHtml([row.studentNo,row.name,row.className,row.reason,row.guidance].join(" ").toLowerCase())}"><time>${escapeHtml(row.time || "-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.className)}반</span><span>${Number(row.minutes||0)}분</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.reason||"-")}</span><span class="state ${String(row.guidance).includes("필요")?"warn":""}">${escapeHtml(row.guidance||"일반")}</span></button>`).join("")}</div>` : `<div class="empty-state compact-empty data-card"><b>${lateClassFilter==="all"?"선택일":`${lateClassFilter}반 선택일`} 지각 학생이 없습니다.</b><span>${selectedKey} 기준</span></div>`;
    const cumulativeMarkup = cumulative.length ? `<div class="data-card" id="attendanceRows"><div class="data-head late-cumulative-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>누적횟수</span><span>누적시간</span><span>최근 지각일</span><span>최근 사유</span><span>지도상태</span></div>${cumulative.map(row=>`<button class="data-row late-cumulative-grid attendance-row history-row" data-late-student="${escapeHtml(row.studentKey)}" data-search="${escapeHtml([row.studentNo,row.name,row.className,row.reason,row.guidance].join(" ").toLowerCase())}"><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.className)}반</span><strong>${Number(row.cumulativeCount||0)}회</strong><span>${Number(row.cumulativeMinutes||0)}분</span><time>${escapeHtml(row.date||"-")}</time><span>${escapeHtml(row.reason||"-")}</span><span class="state ${String(row.guidance).includes("필요")?"warn":""}">${escapeHtml(row.guidance||"일반")}</span></button>`).join("")}</div>` : `<div class="empty-state compact-empty data-card"><b>누적 지각 기록이 없습니다.</b></div>`;
    return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendanceDateControls("지각 조회일",currentRoleId()==="admin"?'<button class="btn primary" id="lateAttendanceAdd">＋ 지각 등록</button>':'')}<section class="attendance-dashboard-head"><div><small>SELECTED DAY · LATE</small><h3>${selectedKey} 지각</h3><p>날짜와 학급을 바꾸고, 학생을 눌러 이력을 확인합니다. 관리자는 기록을 직접 등록·수정할 수 있습니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-head-metrics"><span><b>${allSelectedRows.length}</b>선택일</span><span><b>${lateRows.length}</b>전체기록</span><span><b>${allCumulative.length}</b>누적학생</span><span><b>${allCumulative.filter(x=>String(x.guidance).includes("필요")).length}</b>지도필요</span></div></section>${classCards}${selectedMarkup}<div class="section-title-row"><div><small>CUMULATIVE · ${lateClassFilter==="all"?"전체":`${lateClassFilter}반`}</small><h3>누적 지각</h3></div><div class="toolbar inline-toolbar"><input class="search" id="attendanceSearch" placeholder="학번·이름·반·사유 검색"><button class="btn secondary" id="attendanceSearchReset">검색 지우기</button></div></div>${cumulativeMarkup}</div>`;
  }

  nightViewMonth = attendanceViewDate.slice(0,7);
  const classNo = nightClassFilter === "all" ? "1" : String(nightClassFilter || "1");
  const allStudents = readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const students = allStudents.filter(student=>classNumberOf(student)===classNo).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number)-Number(b.number));
  const monthRows = nightRows.filter(row=>String(row.date||"").startsWith(nightViewMonth));
  const slots = ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"];
  const positive = new Set(["present","dorm","after","program"]);
  const currentDormIds=new Set((readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[]).flatMap(item=>[String(item.studentId||""),String(item.studentNo||"")]).filter(Boolean));
  const firstSemesterDormRows=(readonlyCache?.pastDormStudents||[]).filter(item=>String(item.year||"2026")==="2026"&&(/1/.test(String(item.semester||"1"))));
  const firstSemesterDormIds=new Set(firstSemesterDormRows.flatMap(item=>[String(item.studentId||""),String(item.studentNo||"")]).filter(Boolean));
  const isDormForDay=(student,day)=>{
    const ids=[String(student.id||student.studentId||""),String(student.studentNo||"")].filter(Boolean);
    if(!isNightOperatingDay(day)) return false;
    // 2026학년도 학사 운영 기준: 여름방학에도 운영하며 실제 미운영은 8/8~8/17 재입소 전까지입니다.
    if(day>="2026-08-08"&&day<="2026-08-17") return false;
    if(day>="2026-03-03"&&day<="2026-07-16"&&ids.some(id=>firstSemesterDormIds.has(id))) return true;
    const roster=(readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[]).find(item=>ids.includes(String(item.studentId||""))||ids.includes(String(item.studentNo||"")));
    if(!roster) return false;
    const entered=String(roster.entryDate||roster.startDate||roster.moveInDate||"2026-07-20").slice(0,10);
    const exited=String(roster.exitDate||roster.endDate||roster.moveOutDate||"").slice(0,10);
    return day>=entered && (!exited||day<=exited);
  };

  const rowIndex = new Map();
  monthRows.forEach(row=>{
    const slot=nightSlotName(row); if(!slots.includes(slot)||!row.date) return;
    const ids=[String(row.studentId||""),String(row.studentNo||""),attendanceStudentKey(row)].filter(Boolean);
    ids.forEach(id=>{const key=`${row.date}|${slot}|${id}`; if(!rowIndex.has(key)) rowIndex.set(key,row);});
  });
  const programIndex = new Map();
  const programs=[...(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>program?.kind!=="after"),...(typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups==="function"?afterSchoolProgramGroups():[])];
  const monthDateCandidates=[...new Set([...(readonlyCache?.nightCalendarDates||[]),...monthRows.map(row=>row.date),attendanceViewDate].filter(day=>String(day||"").startsWith(nightViewMonth)))].sort();
  const weekdayToken=day=>["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay()];
  const programRunsOn=(program,day)=>{
    const statusText=`${program.status||""} ${program.changeType||""} ${program.sessionStatus||""}`;
    if(/결강|휴강|취소|폐강|미운영/.test(statusText)) return false;
    const start=String(program.date||program.startDate||"").slice(0,10);
    const end=String(program.endDate||"").slice(0,10);
    const explicitDates=[...(Array.isArray(program.dates)?program.dates:[]),...(Array.isArray(program.sessionDates)?program.sessionDates:[])].map(value=>String(value||"").slice(0,10)).filter(Boolean);
    const days=String(program.weekdays||program.scheduleText||"");
    if(program.kind==="after"){
      if(explicitDates.length) return explicitDates.includes(day);
      if(start&&end&&end!==start){
        if(day<start||day>end) return false;
        if(days&&/[월화수목금토일]/.test(days)&&!days.includes(weekdayToken(day))) return false;
        return true;
      }
      return Boolean(start&&start===day);
    }
    if(start&&day<start) return false;
    if(end&&day>end) return false;
    if(days&&/[월화수목금토일]/.test(days) && !days.includes(weekdayToken(day))) return false;
    return start===day || (start&&end&&day>=start&&day<=end);
  };
  const seenPrograms=new Set();
  programs.filter(program=>programHasNightStudyImpact(program)).forEach(program=>{
    const pKey=`${program.id||""}|${program.date||""}|${program.title||""}|${program.time||""}`;
    if(seenPrograms.has(pKey)) return; seenPrograms.add(pKey);
    monthDateCandidates.filter(day=>programRunsOn(program,day)).forEach(day=>{
      slots.filter(slot=>programOverlapsSlot(program,slot)).forEach(slot=>{
        (program.students||[]).forEach(target=>{
          const result=String(target.result||"대상");
          const confirmed=nightRecordIsAttendance(target)||/출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|수강|대상/.test(result);
          const pending=!confirmed&&!/결석|불참|취소/.test(result);
          const value={program,target,confirmed,pending};
          const ids=[String(target.studentId||""),String(target.studentNo||target.number||""),attendanceStudentKey(target)].filter(Boolean);
          ids.forEach(id=>{
            const key=`${day}|${slot}|${id}`, prev=programIndex.get(key);
            if(!prev||confirmed||(!prev.confirmed&&pending)) programIndex.set(key,value);
          });
        });
      });
    });
  });
  const resolveStatus=(student,day,slot)=>{
    const ids=[String(student.id||student.studentId||""),String(student.studentNo||""),attendanceStudentKey(student)].filter(Boolean);
    let participation=null,row=null;
    for(const id of ids){ participation=participation||programIndex.get(`${day}|${slot}|${id}`); row=row||rowIndex.get(`${day}|${slot}|${id}`); }
    if(participation?.confirmed){
      const display=nightProgramStatus(participation.program);
      const fullTitle=participation.program.actualTitle||participation.program.title||"프로그램";
      return {cls:display.cls,label:display.label,title:`${fullTitle} 참여 · ${participation.program.subject||"교과 미입력"} · ${participation.program.department||"부서 미입력"}`,programId:participation.program.id||"",programKind:display.kind};
    }
    if(participation?.pending) return {cls:"pending",label:"확인",title:`${participation.program.actualTitle||participation.program.title} 대상·출석·참여 중`};
    if(row){
      const rowText=`${row.result||""} ${row.type||""} ${row.affiliation||""} ${row.detail||""}`;
      if(row.recognizedByProgram||/프로그램 인정/.test(String(row.result))){const display=nightProgramStatus({kind:row.programKind,afterType:row.programType,type:row.type,actualTitle:row.programTitle,title:row.programTitle});return {cls:display.cls,label:display.label,title:`${row.programTitle||"프로그램"} 참여`,programId:row.programId||"",programKind:display.kind};}
      if(row.programPending||/프로그램 확인/.test(String(row.result))) return {cls:"pending",label:"확인",title:`${row.programTitle||"프로그램"} 참여 확인 필요`};
      if(/결석|미대상|명단외|미입실|미제출|불참/.test(rowText)) return {cls:"none",label:"-",title:"참여 기록 없음"};
      if(/방과후|야간모의|방학/.test(rowText) && nightRecordIsAttendance(row)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",title:String(row.type||row.result||"방과후 참여")};
      if(/프로그램/.test(rowText) && nightRecordIsAttendance(row)) return {cls:"program",label:"프로그램",title:String(row.type||row.result||"프로그램 참여")};
      if(/지각/.test(String(row.result))) return {cls:"late",label:"지각",title:String(row.result)};
      if(nightRecordIsAttendance(row)){
        const isDorm=/학사/.test(rowText)||isDormForDay(student,day);
        return isDorm?{cls:"dorm",label:"학사",title:"학사생 참여"}:{cls:"present",label:"출석",title:String(row.result||"출석")};
      }
    }
    if(isDormForDay(student,day)) return {cls:"dorm",label:"학사",title:day<="2026-07-16"?"1학기 학사생 자동 표시":"학사생 자동 표시"};
    return {cls:"none",label:"-",title:"기록 없음"};
  };

  const programDates=monthDateCandidates.filter(day=>programs.some(program=>programHasNightStudyImpact(program)&&programRunsOn(program,day)));
  const legacyMonthDates=(readonlyCache?.nightCalendarDates||[]).filter(day=>String(day).startsWith(nightViewMonth));
  const firstSemesterDormDates=[];
  if(nightViewMonth>="2026-03"&&nightViewMonth<="2026-07"&&firstSemesterDormIds.size){
    const [yy,mm]=nightViewMonth.split("-").map(Number),start=new Date(yy,mm-1,1,12),end=new Date(yy,mm,0,12);
    for(const d=new Date(start);d<=end;d.setDate(d.getDate()+1)){const key=dateKey(d);if(key<="2026-07-16"&&isNightOperatingDay(key))firstSemesterDormDates.push(key);}
  }
  const dates=[...new Set([...monthRows.map(row=>row.date),...legacyMonthDates,...programDates,...firstSemesterDormDates,attendanceViewDate].filter(day=>String(day).startsWith(nightViewMonth)&&isNightOperatingDay(day)))].sort();
  const statusesFor=(student,day)=>slots.map(slot=>resolveStatus(student,day,slot));
  const classSummaries=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1)).map(no=>{
    const roster=allStudents.filter(student=>classNumberOf(student)===no);
    const statusRows=roster.map(student=>statusesFor(student,attendanceViewDate));
    const participants=statusRows.filter(items=>items.some(status=>positive.has(status.cls))).length;
    const rosterIds=new Set(roster.flatMap(student=>[String(student.id||""),String(student.studentNo||"")]).filter(Boolean));
    const checks=nightRows.filter(row=>{
      if(row.date!==attendanceViewDate || !(nightRecordIsAttendance(row)||/지각/.test(String(row.result)))) return false;
      const found=allStudents.find(s=>String(s.id||"")===String(row.studentId||"")||String(s.studentNo||"")===String(row.studentNo||""));
      const rowClass=classNumberOf(found||{}) || (String(row.className||"").match(/(?:^|\D)(\d+)\s*반?\s*$/)?.[1] || String(row.className||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0] || "");
      return rowClass===no && ([String(row.studentId||""),String(row.studentNo||"")].some(id=>id&&rosterIds.has(id)) || Boolean(found));
    }).length;
    return {classNo:no,roster:roster.length,participants,checks};
  });
  const selectedSummary=classSummaries.find(item=>item.classNo===classNo)||{classNo,roster:students.length,participants:0,checks:0};
  const classCards=`<div class="night-class-overview daily-class-overview">${classSummaries.map(item=>`<button data-night-class="${item.classNo}" class="${classNo===item.classNo?"active":""}"><small>${item.classNo}반 · 재적 ${item.roster}명</small><b>${item.participants}<em>당일 참여</em></b><span>출석체크 ${item.checks}건</span></button>`).join("")}</div>`;

  const dateHead=dates.map(day=>`<th colspan="3" class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day":""}" data-night-date="${escapeHtml(day)}"><b>${escapeHtml(day.slice(5).replace("-","/"))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(day))}</small></th>`).join("");
  const slotHead=dates.map(day=>slots.map(slot=>`<th class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day-slot":""}">${escapeHtml(slot)}</th>`).join("")).join("");
  const bodyRows=students.map(student=>`<tr><th class="student-name-cell"><button data-night-student="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(student))}"><b>${escapeHtml(String(student.number||"").padStart(2,"0"))}번</b><span>${escapeHtml(student.name)}</span></button></th>${dates.map(day=>slots.map(slot=>{const status=resolveStatus(student,day,slot);const programAttrs=status.programId?` data-night-program="${escapeHtml(status.programId)}" data-night-program-kind="${escapeHtml(status.programKind||"general")}"`:"";return `<td class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day-cell":""}"><button class="night-status-cell ${status.cls}" title="${escapeHtml(status.title)}" data-night-student="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(student))}" data-night-day="${escapeHtml(day)}" data-night-slot="${escapeHtml(slot)}"${programAttrs}><span>${escapeHtml(status.label)}</span></button></td>`;}).join("")).join("")}</tr>`).join("");
  const matrix=dates.length?`<div class="night-month-scroll-hint">선택일 열은 강조 표시됩니다. 월간 운영일은 항상 유지됩니다. ← 좌우로 스크롤하여 월 전체 확인 →</div><div class="night-month-matrix"><table><thead><tr><th rowspan="2" class="student-name-head">학생</th>${dateHead}</tr><tr>${slotHead}</tr></thead><tbody>${bodyRows}</tbody></table></div>`:`<div class="empty-state compact-empty data-card"><b>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?`${nightViewMonth} 야자출결 자료가 없습니다.`:`${attendanceViewDate} · ${escapeHtml(nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).reason)}`}</b><span>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?'운영일인데 자료가 없다면 데이터 처리시트의 30_야자출결_정규화를 확인하세요.':'미운영일은 결석·미입력으로 집계하지 않습니다.'}</span></div>`;
  const gradeSummary=classSummaries.reduce((sum,item)=>({roster:sum.roster+item.roster,participants:sum.participants+item.participants,checks:sum.checks+item.checks}),{roster:0,participants:0,checks:0});
  return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendanceDateControls("야자 조회일")}<section class="attendance-dashboard-head night"><div><small>NIGHT STUDY · ${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))}</small><h3>1학년 선택일 참여현황</h3><p>1학년 전체 재적을 기준으로, 오후자습·야자1·야자2 중 출결 기록이 확인된 학생을 참여로 집계합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-head-metrics"><span><b>${gradeSummary.roster}</b>1학년 재적인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.participants}</b>학년 참여인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.checks}</b>선택일 출석체크</span><span><b>${dates.length}</b>${nightViewMonth} 운영일</span></div></section>${classCards}<div class="section-title-row monthly-night-title"><div><small>MONTHLY · ${nightViewMonth}</small><h3>${classNo}반 월간 참여현황</h3></div><span class="state">${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))} 선택</span></div><div class="night-matrix-legend"><span class="present">출석</span><span class="dorm">학사</span><span class="after">방과후 강좌명</span><span class="program">프로그램명</span><span class="late">지각</span><span class="pending">확인</span><span class="none">기록 없음</span></div>${matrix}</div>`;
}
function openNightAttendanceDetail(studentKey, day, slot) {
  const students = readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const student = students.find(item => attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey) || {};
  const rows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];
  const row = rows.find(item => item.date === day && nightSlotName(item) === slot && (attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey || String(item.studentNo||"") === String(student.studentNo||"")));
  openDrawer(`${student.name || "학생"} · ${day} ${slot}`);
  $("#drawerTitle")?.classList.add("privacy-sensitive-text");
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>학번</small><b class="privacy-sensitive-text">${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"-")}</b></span><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</b></span><span><small>일자</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(slot)}</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>야자출결 상세</h4><dl class="detail-list"><dt>결과</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.result||"출석 기록 없음")}</dd><dt>소속·유형</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.affiliation||row?.type||"일반 야자")}</dd><dt>상세</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.detail||"-")}</dd><dt>원본 시간</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.time||row?.period||slot)}</dd></dl></div><p class="drawer-note">방과후·야간심화·추가 프로그램 상태는 해당 프로그램 상세 화면으로 바로 연결됩니다.</p>`;
}

function subjectsView() {
  return `<div class="module-page">${toolbar("학생·희망과목·오류 검색", "신청 입력")}<div class="data-card"><div class="data-head student-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>1차 신청</span><span>상담</span><span>상태</span></div>${samples.students.map((r, i) => `<div class="data-row student-grid"><b>${r[0]}</b><span>${r[1]}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "미신청" : "완료"}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "필요" : "-"}</span><span class="state ${i === 2 ? "warn" : ""}">${i === 2 ? "확인 필요" : "정상"}</span></div>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}
function schoolScheduleTimetableMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const monday=new Date(anchor); monday.setDate(anchor.getDate()-((anchor.getDay()+6)%7));
  const days=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(monday);d.setDate(monday.getDate()+i);return d;});
  const dayEvents=days.map(d=>schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).filter(calendarEventMatchesFilter).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99"))));
  const maxRows=Math.max(1,...dayEvents.map(rows=>rows.length));
  const rows=Array.from({length:maxRows},(_,rowIndex)=>`<div class="school-schedule-row"><div class="school-schedule-slot">${rowIndex+1}</div>${dayEvents.map(events=>{const event=events[rowIndex];if(!event)return '<div class="school-schedule-cell empty"><span>—</span></div>';const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);return `<button class="school-schedule-cell kind-${kind} ${calendarEventIsPending(event)?"pending":""}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(calendarShortTitle(calendarDisplayTitle(event),26))}</b><small>${escapeHtml([event.target,event.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||event.owner||"학교일정")}</small></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="school-schedule-timetable"><div class="school-schedule-head"><div class="school-schedule-corner">순서</div>${days.map(d=>`<button data-calendar-date="${dateKey(d)}" class="${dateKey(d)===dateKey(today)?"today":""}"><b>${["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][d.getDay()]}</b><span>${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}</span><em>${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).length}건</em></button>`).join("")}</div>${rows}</section>`;
}
function calendarView() {
  const hubTabs=`<div class="uep-hub-tabs calendar-hub-tabs"><button class="${calendarHubMode==="schedule"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="schedule"><b>학교일정</b><small>월간·주간 일정</small></button><button class="${calendarHubMode==="timetable"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="timetable"><b>시간표</b><small>개인·학급·학생·교사</small></button></div>`;
  if(calendarHubMode==="timetable") return `<div class="module-page calendar-timetable-hub">${hubTabs}${timetableView()}</div>`;
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  return `<div class="module-page calendar-page-v654 school-schedule-page">${hubTabs}<div class="calendar-page-toolbar"><div><small>SCHOOL SCHEDULE · 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더</small><h3>학교일정</h3><p>월간계획을 기본으로 확인하고 월간·주간 보기를 자유롭게 전환합니다.</p></div><div><button class="btn secondary" data-calendar-today>${state.calendarMode==="week"?"이번 주":"오늘"}</button><button class="btn primary" data-reference="calendar-add">＋ 개인 일정</button></div></div><div class="school-schedule-summary"><b>${anchor.getFullYear()}년 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}월</b><span>선택일 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}/${anchor.getDate()} · ${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(anchor)).length}건</span></div><div class="card calendar-page-card school-schedule-card">${calendarMarkup(true)}<div class="widget-source-footer dashboard-source">출처: 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더 · 대시보드 월간/주간과 동일 DB</div></div></div>`;
}

function inputCenterBatchStudents(){
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>!String(s.status||s.enrollmentStatus||'').includes('전출'));
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='학급') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1') && String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchClass||'1'));
  if(scope==='학년전체') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1'));
  if(scope==='개별') return students.filter(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo||'').trim());
  return students;
}
function inputCenterBatchExpectedCount(){
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='사용자지정') return Math.max(0,Number(inputCenterConfig.expectedCount||0));
  return inputCenterBatchStudents().length;
}
function inputCenterStudentMatch(textValue='',fileName=''){
  const students=inputCenterBatchStudents();
  const hay=`${fileName||''}\n${textValue||''}`;
  const numbers=[...hay.matchAll(/(?:^|[^0-9])([123][0-9]{3})(?![0-9])/g)].map(m=>m[1]);
  for(const no of numbers){const student=students.find(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===no);if(student)return {student,matchStatus:'자동매칭'};}
  const shortHay=hay.slice(0,4000);
  const matched=students.filter(s=>s.name&&shortHay.includes(String(s.name)));
  if(matched.length===1)return {student:matched[0],matchStatus:'이름매칭'};
  return {student:null,matchStatus:'확인필요'};
}
function inputCenterDraftFromPayload(payload={},textValue=''){
  const match=inputCenterStudentMatch(textValue,payload.name||payload.fileName||'');
  const s=match.student||{};
  return {draftId:`DRAFT-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`,filePath:payload.path||'',fileName:payload.name||payload.fileName||'직접입력',extension:payload.extension||'',size:Number(payload.size||0),text:String(textValue||payload.text||''),studentId:s.id||'',studentNo:s.studentNo||'',name:s.name||'',grade:s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1)||'',className:String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,''),matchStatus:match.matchStatus,selected:true};
}
function inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file={}, textValue=''){
  const name=String(file?.name||file?.fileName||'');
  const text=String(textValue||file?.text||'');
  const hay=`${name}\n${text.slice(0,12000)}`.toLowerCase();
  const rules=[
    {type:'프로그램 신청서',score:[/신청자|참가신청|신청 활동|신청 프로그램|희망 진로|희망순위|신청서/g,/연락처|학년|반|번호|이름/g],reason:'신청·희망·참가 관련 열/문구'},
    {type:'만족도·설문조사',score:[/만족도|설문|응답|매우 만족|보통|불만족|소감/g,/문항|응답자|제출시간|타임스탬프/g],reason:'설문·응답 관련 문구'},
    {type:'수행평가지',score:[/수행평가|서술형|평가지|배점|채점|문항|풀이/g,/교과|성취기준|평가요소/g],reason:'수행평가·채점 관련 문구'},
    {type:'보고서·탐구보고서',score:[/보고서|탐구|활동내용|새롭게 알게|느낀점|소감|주제/g,/책 제목|독서|진로희망|탐구내용/g],reason:'보고서·탐구·독서 관련 문구'},
    {type:'출결·참여명단',score:[/출석|결석|참여명단|참가자 명단|출결|출석부/g,/학번|이름|반/g],reason:'출결·참여명단 관련 문구'},
    {type:'성적·평가자료',score:[/성적|점수|등급|석차|평균|원점수|표준점수/g,/학번|이름|과목/g],reason:'성적·점수 관련 열/문구'},
    {type:'상담·관찰자료',score:[/상담|관찰|면담|상담내용|교사 의견|특이사항/g],reason:'상담·관찰 관련 문구'}
  ];
  let best={type:'기타',points:0,reason:'명확한 자동 분류 근거 없음'};
  for(const r of rules){let pts=0;for(const re of r.score){const m=hay.match(re);if(m)pts+=Math.min(4,m.length);}if(pts>best.points)best={type:r.type,points:pts,reason:r.reason};}
  const confidence=best.points>=6?'높음':best.points>=3?'보통':'낮음';
  return {...best,confidence};
}
function inputCenterSpreadsheetRows(file, raw){
  const ext=String(file?.extension||'').toLowerCase();
  if(!['xls','xlsx','csv','tsv'].includes(ext))return null;
  const allLines=String(raw||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n');
  const sections=[];let current={name:'',lines:[]};
  for(const line of allLines){if(line.startsWith('__UEP_SHEET__\t')){if(current.lines.length)sections.push(current);current={name:line.split('\t').slice(1).join('\t').trim(),lines:[]};}else if(line.trim())current.lines.push(line);}
  if(current.lines.length)sections.push(current);if(!sections.length)return null;
  const ordered=sections.slice().sort((a,b)=>{const score=x=>/제출내역_텍스트/.test(x.name)?100:/제출내역|응답|보고서/.test(x.name)?50:0;return score(b)-score(a);});
  const split=line=>(ext==='csv'?line.split(','):line.split('\t')).map(v=>String(v||'').replace(/\\n/g,'\n'));
  for(const section of ordered){
    const rows=section.lines.map(split);let hi=-1,idx={};
    for(let i=0;i<Math.min(rows.length,25);i++){
      const cells=rows[i].map(x=>String(x||'').trim());const find=alts=>cells.findIndex(v=>alts.some(a=>v.replace(/\s/g,'').includes(a)));
      const g=find(['학년']),c=find(['반']),n=find(['번호','번']),nm=find(['이름','성명']),sn=find(['학번']);if(nm>=0&&(sn>=0||(g>=0&&c>=0&&n>=0))){hi=i;idx={g,c,n,nm,sn};break;}
    }
    if(hi<0)continue;const headers=rows[hi].map(x=>String(x||'').trim());const out=[];
    for(let r=hi+1;r<rows.length;r++){
      const cells=rows[r].map(x=>String(x??'').trim());if(!cells.some(Boolean))continue;const name=idx.nm>=0?cells[idx.nm]:'';if(!name||/합계|계$/.test(name)||/합계|총계|인원/.test(String(cells[0]||''))||/^\d+$/.test(name))continue;
      let studentNo=idx.sn>=0?String(cells[idx.sn]||'').replace(/\D/g,''):'';
      if(studentNo.length===5&&/^[123]\d{4}$/.test(studentNo)){const g=studentNo[0],cl=String(Number(studentNo.slice(1,3))),num=studentNo.slice(-2);studentNo=`${g}${cl}${num}`;}
      if(!studentNo&&idx.g>=0&&idx.c>=0&&idx.n>=0){const g=String(cells[idx.g]||'').replace(/\D/g,'').slice(-1),cl=String(Number(String(cells[idx.c]||'').replace(/\D/g,''))),num=String(cells[idx.n]||'').replace(/\D/g,'').padStart(2,'0').slice(-2);if(g&&cl&&num)studentNo=`${g}${cl}${num}`;}
      const draft=inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},cells.join(' | '));const st=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===studentNo);
      if(st){draft.studentId=st.id||'';draft.studentNo=st.studentNo||studentNo;draft.name=st.name||name;draft.grade=st.grade||studentNo.slice(0,1);draft.className=String(st.className||classNumberOf(st)||'').replace(/반/g,'');draft.matchStatus=String(st.name||'')===String(name||'')?'표 구조 자동매칭':'학번매칭·이름확인필요';}
      else{draft.studentNo=studentNo;draft.name=name;draft.matchStatus=studentNo?'학번 인식·학생DB 확인필요':'이름 인식·학번 확인필요';}
      const submittedIdx=headers.findIndex(h=>/제출.*(일시|시간)|타임스탬프/.test(h));const identityCols=new Set([idx.g,idx.c,idx.n,idx.nm,idx.sn,submittedIdx].filter(x=>x>=0));
      draft.structuredRow=true;draft.ocrStatus='구조분석완료';draft.ocrEngine='Excel 셀 직접분석';draft.sheetName=section.name;draft.rowNo=r+1;draft.sheetHeaders=headers;draft.structuredCells=cells;draft.submittedAt=submittedIdx>=0?cells[submittedIdx]:'';draft.reportFields=headers.map((h,i)=>({label:h,value:cells[i]||'',index:i})).filter(x=>x.label&&x.value&&!identityCols.has(x.index));draft.text=draft.reportFields.map(x=>`${x.label}: ${x.value}`).join('\n');out.push(draft);
    }
    if(out.length)return out;
  }
  return null;
}
function inputCenterExpandTextFile(file){
  const raw=String(file?.text||'').replace(/\r/g,'');
  const structured=inputCenterSpreadsheetRows(file,raw);
  const classification=inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file,raw);
  if(structured){structured.forEach(d=>{const q=(d.sheetHeaders||[]).filter(h=>/^\s*\d+[.)]/.test(String(h||''))).length;const isReport=q>=3||/보고서|제출내역_텍스트/.test(String(d.sheetName||''));d.detectedType=isReport?'보고서·탐구보고서':classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=isReport?'높음':classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=isReport?`Excel 문항형 보고서 · ${q}개 문항 직접 인식`:classification.reason;});return structured;}
  if(!raw.trim()){const d=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,'');d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;return [d];}
  const lines=raw.split('\n').filter(x=>x.trim());
  const studentLines=lines.filter(line=>/(?:^|[^0-9])[123][0-9]{3}(?![0-9])/.test(line));
  const drafts=(studentLines.length>=2 && studentLines.length>=Math.min(3,Math.ceil(lines.length*.25)))?studentLines.map(line=>inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},line)):[inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,raw)];
  drafts.forEach(d=>{d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;});return drafts;
}
function inputCenterBundleDuplex(files=[]){
  if(({'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source)!=='수기 스캔'||!['양면 스캔','단면 스캔','사진(JPG/PNG)'].includes(inputCenterConfig.subtype)) return files.flatMap(file=>inputCenterExpandTextFile(file));
  const per=Math.max(1,Number(inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent|| (inputCenterConfig.subtype==='양면 스캔'?2:1)));
  const out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<files.length;i+=per){
    const pair=files.slice(i,i+per); const first=pair[0]||{};
    const text=pair.map(x=>String(x.text||'')).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    const draft=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(first,text);
    draft.bundleId=`BUNDLE-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
    draft.pages=pair.map((x,j)=>({page:j+1,filePath:x.path||'',fileName:x.name||'',extension:x.extension||'',size:Number(x.size||0),rotation:0}));
    draft.pageCount=pair.length; draft.fileName=pair.map(x=>x.name).filter(Boolean).join(' + ')||(first.name||'스캔 원본');
    draft.matchStatus=draft.studentNo?draft.matchStatus:'판독 대기';
    draft.bundleStatus=pair.length===per?`${per}페이지 묶음 · 순서/회전 확인`:`예상 ${per}페이지 중 ${pair.length}페이지 · 확인 필요`;
    draft.batchScope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope; draft.batchGrade=inputCenterConfig.batchGrade; draft.batchClass=inputCenterConfig.batchClass;
    out.push(draft);
  }
  return out;
}
function inputCenterIsCompleted(row={}){return /정규화완료|적용완료/.test(String(row.status||row.processingStatus||''));}
async function refreshInputCenterSourceSilently(){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.refreshInputCenterSource)return false;
  try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.refreshInputCenterSource();if(!result?.ok)return false;readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),externalInputs:result.externalInputs||[],inputCenterSyncedAt:result.inputCenterSyncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};return true;}catch{return false;}
}
function inputCenterAllHistory(){return (readonlyCache?.externalInputs||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||'')));}
function inputCenterHistory(){const rows=inputCenterAllHistory();return inputCenterConfig.historyMode==='completed'?rows.filter(inputCenterIsCompleted):rows.filter(x=>!inputCenterIsCompleted(x));}
function inputCenterLibraryRows(){
  const remote=inputCenterHistory().map(x=>({...x,storageMode:x.storageMode||'uep'}));
  // 02 검수·분류와 03 UEP 적용은 [UEP]입력센터 원본을 단일 기준으로 사용합니다.
  // 예전 PC 로컬 보관 이력은 새 정규화 파이프라인에 섞지 않습니다.
  if(inputCenterConfig.libraryScope!=='local') return remote.sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
  return (inputCenterLocalHistory||[]).map(x=>({...x,storageMode:'local'})).sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramSemester(p={}){
  const text=`${p.semester||''} ${p.term||''} ${p.schoolTerm||''} ${p.date||''} ${p.startDate||''}`;
  if(/2학기|(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '2';
  if(/1학기|(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '1';
  const m=text.match(/2026[-/.](\d{1,2})/); if(m) return Number(m[1])<=7?'1':'2';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramGroup(p={}){
  const text=`${p.kind||''} ${p.type||''} ${p.afterType||''} ${p.actualTitle||''} ${p.title||''}`;
  if(p.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return /야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)?'night':'after';
  if(p.kind==='common'||/공통/.test(text)) return 'common';
  if(p.kind==='selected'||/선택/.test(text)) return 'selected';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramIdentity(p={}){
  const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p);
  const title=String(p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const teacher=String(p.teacher||p.teacherName||p.instructor||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const semester=inputCenterProgramSemester(p);
  // 방과후/야간심화 readonly 데이터는 차시·출석 결합 과정에서 행별 임시 ID가 달라질 수 있습니다.
  // 연결목록은 강좌 마스터를 선택하는 곳이므로 학기+유형+강좌명+담당교사 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  if(group==='after'||group==='night') return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
  const id=String(p.id||p.programId||p.afterId||p.afterSchoolId||p.activityId||p.masterId||'').trim();
  if(id) return `${group}|${id}`;
  return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
}
function inputCenterProgramCandidates(purpose=inputCenterConfig.purpose,semester=inputCenterConfig.semester){
  const filtered=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>{
    const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); if(!group) return false;
    const sem=inputCenterProgramSemester(p); if(sem&&semester&&sem!==String(semester)) return false;
    if(purpose==='보고서 연결') return (group==='common'||group==='selected') && p.reportRequired!==false && p.reportUse!==false;
    if(purpose==='프로그램 참가신청') return group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    if(purpose==='만족도조사') return group==='common'||group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    return false;
  });
  // readonly 프로그램 데이터에는 방과후 차시/일정 결합 때문에 같은 마스터 강좌가 여러 행으로 들어올 수 있습니다.
  // 입력센터 연결목록은 차시가 아니라 '프로그램 마스터 1건'을 선택하는 곳이므로 안정 ID 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  const deduped=[]; const seen=new Set();
  filtered.forEach(p=>{const key=inputCenterProgramIdentity(p);if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);deduped.push(p);});
  const rank={common:1,selected:2,after:3,night:4};
  return deduped.sort((a,b)=>(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(a)]||9)-(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(b)]||9)||String(a.actualTitle||a.title||'').localeCompare(String(b.actualTitle||b.title||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramLabel(p={}){
  const g=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); const prefix={common:'공통',selected:'선택',after:'방과후',night:'야간심화'}[g]||'프로그램';
  const title=p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'';
  return `${prefix} · ${title}`;
}
function inputCenterView(){
  const drafts=inputCenterDrafts;
  const matched=drafts.filter(x=>x.studentNo).length, pending=drafts.filter(x=>!x.studentNo||/오류|확인필요/.test(String(x.ocrStatus||''))).length, selected=drafts.filter(x=>x.selected!==false).length;
  const ocrDone=drafts.filter(x=>x.ocrStatus==='OCR완료').length, ocrErrors=drafts.filter(x=>/오류|확인필요/.test(String(x.ocrStatus||''))).length;
  const saveLabel=inputCenterConfig.storageMode==='uep'?'선택 자료 입력센터 원본 저장':'선택 자료 로컬 보관';
  const tab=inputCenterConfig.tab||'import';
  const completedView=inputCenterConfig.historyMode==='completed';
  const library=inputCenterLibraryRows();
  const batchIds=[...new Set(library.map(x=>String(x.batchId||'')).filter(Boolean))];
  const latestBatchId=batchIds[0]||'';
  const activeBatchId=inputCenterConfig.batchFilter==='latest'?latestBatchId:inputCenterConfig.batchFilter;
  const inputStudentDigits=x=>String(x?.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  const inferredGrade=x=>{
    const no=inputStudentDigits(x);
    const fromNo=no.match(/^([123])/)?.[1];
    if(fromNo) return fromNo;
    return String(x?.grade||'').replace(/[^123]/g,'').slice(0,1);
  };
  const inferredClass=x=>{
    const no=inputStudentDigits(x);
    const fromNo=no.match(/^[123]([1-9])/)?.[1];
    if(fromNo) return fromNo;
    const raw=String(x?.className||x?.classNo||x?.class||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
    return raw&&Number(raw)>=1&&Number(raw)<=9?String(Number(raw)):'';
  };
  const filterInputRows=rows=>rows.filter(x=>{
    const storageOk=inputCenterConfig.libraryScope==='local'?x.storageMode==='local':inputCenterConfig.libraryScope==='uep'?x.storageMode!=='local':true;
    const gradeOk=inputCenterConfig.gradeFilter==='all'||inferredGrade(x)===inputCenterConfig.gradeFilter;
    const classOk=inputCenterConfig.classFilter==='all'||inferredClass(x)===inputCenterConfig.classFilter;
    const typeOk=inputCenterConfig.typeFilter==='all'||String(x.detectedType||x.type||'').includes(inputCenterConfig.typeFilter);
    const matchOk=inputCenterConfig.matchFilter==='all'||(inputCenterConfig.matchFilter==='matched'?Boolean(x.studentNo):!x.studentNo);
    const sourceOk=true;
    const batchOk=!activeBatchId||String(x.batchId||'')===String(activeBatchId);
    return storageOk&&gradeOk&&classOk&&typeOk&&matchOk&&sourceOk&&batchOk;
  });
  const filtered=filterInputRows(library);
  const gradePills=[['all','전학년'],['1','1학년'],['2','2학년'],['3','3학년']];
  const classPills=[['all','전체반'],...Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>[String(i+1),`${i+1}반`])];
  const matchPills=[['all','매칭전체'],['matched','매칭완료'],['unmatched','확인필요']];
  const pillGroup=(label,items,selected,attr)=>`<div class="input-filter-compact-group"><span class="input-filter-label">${label}</span><div class="input-filter-pills">${items.map(([v,t])=>`<button type="button" class="input-filter-pill ${selected===v?'active':''}" ${attr}="${escapeHtml(v)}">${escapeHtml(t)}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
  // 입력경로는 배치 자체가 이미 출처 정보를 가지므로 검수/적용 필터에서 중복 노출하지 않습니다.
  inputCenterConfig.sourceFilter='all';
  const selectedFilterText=[inputCenterConfig.gradeFilter==='all'?'전학년':`${inputCenterConfig.gradeFilter}학년`,inputCenterConfig.classFilter==='all'?'전체반':`${inputCenterConfig.classFilter}반`,inputCenterConfig.matchFilter==='all'?'매칭전체':inputCenterConfig.matchFilter==='matched'?'매칭완료':'확인필요'].join(' · ');
  const batchLabel=(id)=>{const rows=library.filter(x=>String(x.batchId||'')===String(id));const first=rows[0]||{};if(!id)return '배치 없음';const dt=String(first.createdAt||first.savedAt||first.registeredAt||'');const md=dt?dt.slice(5,16).replace('T',' '):'';const src=first.source||'외부자료';const kind=String(first.type||first.detectedType||'').replace('보고서·탐구보고서','보고서')||'자료';return `${md?md+' · ':''}${src} · ${kind} · ${rows.length}건`;};
  const historySwitch=`<div class="input-history-switch"><button type="button" class="input-filter-pill ${!completedView?'active':''}" data-input-history-mode="active">작업목록</button><button type="button" class="input-filter-pill ${completedView?'active':''}" data-input-history-mode="completed">처리 완료 이력</button></div>`;
  const filterBar=`<div class="input-review-filters unified"><div class="input-filter-head"><b>학생·자료 필터</b><span class="input-filter-current">${completedView?'완료 이력':'작업 중'} · ${escapeHtml(selectedFilterText)} · <strong>${filtered.length}건</strong></span>${historySwitch}</div><div class="input-filter-toolbar"><label class="input-filter-batch"><span>입력배치</span><select class="uep-filter-select" data-input-batch-filter><option value="latest" ${inputCenterConfig.batchFilter==='latest'?'selected':''}>최근 배치 · ${escapeHtml(batchLabel(latestBatchId))}</option>${batchIds.map(id=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(id)}" ${inputCenterConfig.batchFilter===id?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(batchLabel(id))}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>${pillGroup('학년',gradePills,inputCenterConfig.gradeFilter,'data-input-grade-filter')}${pillGroup('반',classPills,inputCenterConfig.classFilter,'data-input-class-filter')}${pillGroup('매칭',matchPills,inputCenterConfig.matchFilter,'data-input-match-filter')}</div></div>`;
  if(!inputCenterConfig.purpose){
    inputCenterConfig.purpose=/보고서/.test(String(inputCenterConfig.type||''))?'보고서 연결':/신청/.test(String(inputCenterConfig.type||''))?'프로그램 참가신청':/만족도|설문/.test(String(inputCenterConfig.type||''))?'만족도조사':'텍스트 추출·기타';
  }
  const purposes=['보고서 연결','프로그램 참가신청','만족도조사','텍스트 추출·기타'];
  const purposeMeta={
    '보고서 연결':['기존 보고서를 학생·프로그램에 연결','보고서·탐구보고서'],
    '프로그램 참가신청':['신청 명단·희망순위 등을 프로그램에 연결','프로그램 신청서'],
    '만족도조사':['설문 문항·응답을 프로그램과 연결','만족도·설문조사'],
    '텍스트 추출·기타':['프로그램 연결 없이 텍스트 추출·보관','기타']
  };
  const sources=['수기 스캔','리로스쿨','Excel·CSV','문서 파일','직접 입력'];
  const sourceMap={'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'};
  const sourceActual=sourceMap[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source;
  const subtypeOptions={'수기 스캔':['단면 스캔','양면 스캔','학생별 PDF','사진(JPG/PNG)'],'리로스쿨':['보고서 제출','참가신청 명단','과제 제출내역','설문·응답자료'],'교사 파일':['PDF·JPG','PPT·PPTX','Excel·CSV','DOCX','HWP·HWPX'],'직접 입력':['학생별 텍스트','여러 학생 일괄 붙여넣기','상담·관찰 기록','기타']};
  const actualSource=sourceActual;
  let subtypes=subtypeOptions[actualSource]||['기타'];
  if(inputCenterConfig.source==='Excel·CSV') subtypes=['Excel·CSV'];
  if(inputCenterConfig.source==='문서 파일') subtypes=['HWP·HWPX','DOCX','PDF·JPG','PPT·PPTX'];
  if(!subtypes.includes(inputCenterConfig.subtype)) inputCenterConfig.subtype=subtypes[0];
  const sourceHelp=actualSource==='수기 스캔'?'스캔 자료는 학생별 페이지를 묶은 뒤 로컬 OCR을 실행하고 학번·이름을 학생마스터와 연결합니다.':actualSource==='리로스쿨'?'리로스쿨 내려받기 파일은 셀 구조를 직접 읽고 학생별 행을 분리합니다.':inputCenterConfig.source==='Excel·CSV'?'Excel·CSV는 OCR 없이 시트·헤더·셀 구조를 직접 분석합니다.':inputCenterConfig.source==='문서 파일'?'HWP·DOCX·텍스트 PDF는 텍스트 추출을 우선하고 이미지형 문서에만 OCR을 사용합니다.':'학번 또는 학생 이름이 포함된 텍스트를 학생별로 분리해 분석합니다.';
  const batchTargetCount=inputCenterBatchExpectedCount();
  const pagesPer=Math.max(1,Number(inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent||1));
  const expectedFiles=actualSource==='수기 스캔'&&batchTargetCount?batchTargetCount*pagesPer:0;
  const batchScopeOptions=['개별','학급','학년전체','사용자지정'];
  const batchScopeFields=inputCenterConfig.batchScope==='개별'?`<label>학번<input data-input-batch-student value="${escapeHtml(inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo||'')}" placeholder="예: 1101"></label>`:inputCenterConfig.batchScope==='학급'?`<label>학년<select data-input-batch-grade>${['1','2','3'].map(x=>`<option ${inputCenterConfig.batchGrade===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>반<select data-input-batch-class>${Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1)).map(x=>`<option ${inputCenterConfig.batchClass===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:inputCenterConfig.batchScope==='학년전체'?`<label>학년<select data-input-batch-grade>${['1','2','3'].map(x=>`<option ${inputCenterConfig.batchGrade===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:`<label>예상 인원<input type="number" min="1" data-input-expected-count value="${escapeHtml(inputCenterConfig.expectedCount||'')}" placeholder="선택"></label>`;
  const scanBatchFields=actualSource==='수기 스캔'?`<label>페이지<select data-input-pages-per-student>${['1','2','3','4'].map(x=>`<option value="${x}" ${String(inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent)===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:'';
  const candidatePrograms=inputCenterProgramCandidates(); if(inputCenterConfig.programId&&!candidatePrograms.some(p=>String(p.id||p.programId||'')===String(inputCenterConfig.programId))) inputCenterConfig.programId='';
  const programField=inputCenterConfig.purpose!=='텍스트 추출·기타'?`<label class="grow">연결 프로그램<select data-input-program><option value="">선택하세요</option>${candidatePrograms.map(p=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(p.id||p.programId||'')}" ${inputCenterConfig.programId===String(p.id||p.programId||'')?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(inputCenterProgramLabel(p))}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:'';
  const compactSetup=`<div class="input-center-compact-setup"><label>대상<select data-input-batch-scope>${batchScopeOptions.map(x=>`<option ${inputCenterConfig.batchScope===x?'selected':''}>${x}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>${batchScopeFields}${scanBatchFields}<label>학기<select data-input-semester><option ${inputCenterConfig.semester==='1'?'selected':''}>1</option><option ${inputCenterConfig.semester==='2'?'selected':''}>2</option></select></label>${programField}</div>`;
  const purposeCards=`<div class="input-center-source-cards input-purpose-cards">${purposes.map(v=>`<button class="${inputCenterConfig.purpose===v?'active':''}" data-input-purpose="${escapeHtml(v)}"><b>${escapeHtml(v)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(purposeMeta[v][0])}</small></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  const sourceCards=`<div class="input-method-row"><b>자료 가져오기</b>${sources.map(src=>`<button class="${inputCenterConfig.source===src?'active':''}" data-input-source="${escapeHtml(src)}">${escapeHtml(src==='수기 스캔'?'수기 스캔(PDF·사진)':src==='문서 파일'?'HWP·DOCX·PDF':src)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
  const tabs=`<div class="input-center-tabs"><button class="${tab==='import'?'active':''}" data-input-tab="import"><b>01 원본 가져오기</b><small>파싱·OCR·학생매칭·원본저장</small></button><button class="${tab==='library'?'active':''}" data-input-tab="library"><b>02 검수·분류</b><small>자료성격·연결프로그램 확정</small></button><button class="${tab==='review'?'active':''}" data-input-tab="review"><b>03 UEP 적용</b><small>정규화 목적지 적용</small></button></div>`;
  if(tab==='library'){
    const reviewRows=filtered;
    const first=reviewRows[0]||{};
    const nature=String(first.type||'').includes('보고서')?'보고서':String(first.type||'').includes('신청')?'신청':String(first.type||'').includes('만족도')||String(first.type||'').includes('설문')?'만족도조사':String(first.type||'').includes('출결')||String(first.type||'').includes('명단')?'출결·명단':first.type||'기타';
    const p=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(v=>String(v.id||v.programId||'')===String(first.programId||''))||{};
    const sourceText=reviewRows.length?`${first.source||'외부자료'} · ${String(first.fileName||'').split('.').pop().toUpperCase()}`:'-';
    const reviewSemester=String(first.semester||inputCenterConfig.semester||'').replace(/[^12]/g,'').slice(0,1)||inputCenterConfig.semester; const reviewPurpose=nature==='보고서'?'보고서 연결':nature==='신청'?'프로그램 참가신청':nature==='만족도조사'?'만족도조사':'텍스트 추출·기타';
    const programOptions=inputCenterProgramCandidates(reviewPurpose,reviewSemester).map(v=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(v.id||v.programId||'')}" ${String(first.programId||'')===String(v.id||v.programId||'')?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(inputCenterProgramLabel(v))}</option>`).join('');
    return `<div class="module-page input-center-page">${tabs}<section class="input-center-hero"><div><small>REVIEW & CLASSIFY · 배치 단위 검수</small><h2>어디서 온 자료인지, 무엇인지, 어디에 연결할지를 확정합니다.</h2><p>Excel처럼 구조가 완전한 자료는 일괄 검수하고, OCR·스캔처럼 애매한 자료만 학생별로 확인합니다.</p></div></section><section class="card input-library-card"><div class="section-head"><div><small>BATCH · ${escapeHtml(activeBatchId||'선택 없음')}</small><h3>자료출처 → 자료성격 → 연결프로그램</h3></div></div>${filterBar}<div class="input-stage-cards"><article><small>자료출처</small><b>${escapeHtml(sourceText)}</b><span>원본의 출처와 파일 형식</span></article><article><small>자료성격</small><b>${escapeHtml(nature)}</b><span>UEP에서 사용할 자료 유형</span></article><article><small>연결 프로그램</small><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title||first.programId||'미지정')}</b><span>${first.programId?'프로그램 연결 확인':'연결 프로그램을 선택해 주세요'}</span></article><article><small>학생 매칭</small><b>${reviewRows.filter(r=>r.studentId||r.studentNo).length}/${reviewRows.length}</b><span>${reviewRows.every(r=>r.studentId||r.studentNo)?'모든 학생 확인 완료':'확인이 필요한 학생이 있습니다'}</span></article></div><div class="input-center-meta" style="margin:14px 0"><label>자료성격<select data-input-review-nature><option ${nature==='신청'?'selected':''}>신청</option><option ${nature==='보고서'?'selected':''}>보고서</option><option ${nature==='만족도조사'?'selected':''}>만족도조사</option><option ${nature==='출결·명단'?'selected':''}>출결·명단</option><option ${nature==='활동기록'?'selected':''}>활동기록</option><option ${nature==='기타'?'selected':''}>기타</option></select></label><label class="grow">연결프로그램<select data-input-review-program><option value="">연결 없음</option>${programOptions}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-input-confirm-classification ${(reviewRows.length&&!completedView)?'':'disabled'}>${completedView?'처리 완료':'이 배치 검수·분류 확정'}</button></div>${reviewRows.length?`<div class="input-library-list">${reviewRows.map((x,i)=>`<article class="input-library-row"><div><span class="storage-badge uep">원본 저장</span><b>${escapeHtml(x.studentNo||'학생 확인 전')} ${escapeHtml(x.name||'')}</b><small>${escapeHtml(x.source||'')} · ${escapeHtml(x.type||'')} · ${escapeHtml(x.title||x.fileName||'자료')}</small></div><div class="input-library-actions"><small>${escapeHtml(x.matchStatus||'')}</small><button class="btn secondary" data-input-view="${i}">내용 확인</button></div></article>`).join('')}</div>`:`<div class="empty-state input-center-empty"><b>${completedView?'처리 완료 이력이 없습니다.':'검수할 원본 배치가 없습니다.'}</b><span>${completedView?'UEP 적용이 끝난 배치는 여기에 보관됩니다.':'01 원본 가져오기에서 저장한 배치를 선택해 주세요.'}</span></div>`}</section></div>`;
  }
  if(tab==='review'){
    const applyRows=filtered.filter(x=>String(x.rawText||x.normalizedText||x.text||'').trim());inputCenterReviewRowsCache=applyRows;
    const first=applyRows[0]||{};const program=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(v=>String(v.id||v.programId||'')===String(first.programId||''))||{};
