    const nature=String(first.type||'').includes('보고서')?'보고서':String(first.type||'').includes('신청')?'신청':String(first.type||'').includes('만족도')||String(first.type||'').includes('설문')?'만족도조사':String(first.type||'').includes('출결')||String(first.type||'').includes('명단')?'출결·명단':first.type||'기타';
    const kind=first.programKind||program.kind||'';const dest=nature==='보고서'?(kind==='selected'?'21_선택보고서_정규화':'20_공통보고서_정규화'):nature==='신청'?'프로그램 신청·참여 정규화':nature==='만족도조사'?'프로그램 만족도 정규화':'자료유형별 표준 정규화';
    return `<div class="module-page input-center-page">${tabs}<section class="input-center-hero"><div><small>APPLY CENTER · 정규화 적용</small><h2>검수된 원본을 UEP가 실제 사용하는 표준 데이터로 반영합니다.</h2><p>[UEP]입력센터 원본은 그대로 보존하고, 자료성격과 연결프로그램에 따라 데이터처리 표준 탭으로 이관합니다.</p></div></section><section class="card input-library-card"><div class="section-head"><div><small>ROUTING · ${escapeHtml(activeBatchId||'')}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(dest)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(first.source||'외부자료')} → ${escapeHtml(nature)} → ${escapeHtml(program.actualTitle||program.title||first.programId||'연결대상 없음')}</p></div></div>${filterBar}<div class="input-stage-cards"><article><small>적용 후보</small><b>${applyRows.length}명</b><span>검수 완료 후 반영할 학생 자료</span></article><article><small>자료성격</small><b>${escapeHtml(nature)}</b><span>확정된 자료 유형</span></article><article><small>연결 프로그램</small><b>${escapeHtml(program.actualTitle||program.title||first.programId||'미지정')}</b><span>${first.programId?'프로그램 연결 완료':'02 검수·분류에서 연결 필요'}</span></article><article><small>정규화 목적지</small><b>${escapeHtml(dest)}</b><span>UEP가 실제 조회하는 표준 데이터</span></article></div><div class="input-detail-actions" style="margin:14px 0 18px;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn primary" data-input-apply-uep ${(applyRows.length&&!completedView)?'':'disabled'}>${completedView?'적용 완료':'UEP에 적용'}</button><button class="btn secondary" data-input-export-csv>검수자료 CSV</button><span style="font-size:12px;color:#687b84">적용 전 신규·중복·갱신을 검사하고 확인 후 정규화합니다.</span></div>${applyRows.length?`<div class="input-apply-ready"><b>검수·분류가 완료된 ${applyRows.length}건을 정규화 원장에 적용할 준비가 되었습니다.</b><span>개별 내용 확인·수정은 02 검수·분류에서 수행하며, 03은 신규·중복·갱신 판정 후 최종 반영만 수행합니다.</span></div>`:`<div class="empty-state input-center-empty"><b>${completedView?'처리 완료 이력이 없습니다.':'적용할 검수 배치가 없습니다.'}</b><span>${completedView?'완료된 배치는 다시 적용하지 않으며 이력만 확인합니다.':'02 검수·분류에서 배치를 먼저 확정해 주세요.'}</span></div>`}</section></div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="module-page input-center-page">${tabs}
    <section class="input-center-hero"><div><small>UEP INPUT CENTER · [UEP]입력센터 원본</small><h2>학생은 하던 방식대로, 자료는 UEP가 학생별로 정리합니다.</h2><p>종이 수행평가·스캔·리로스쿨·교사 파일·복사한 텍스트를 한 곳에서 받아 원본을 보존하고 학생ID 중심으로 연결합니다.</p></div><div class="input-center-pipeline"><span>원본 구조 확인</span><i>→</i><span>자료유형 판별</span><i>→</i><span>학생별 정리</span><i>→</i><span>검수·적용</span></div></section>
    <section class="card input-center-config">${purposeCards}${sourceCards}${compactSetup}<div class="input-center-source-help"><b>${escapeHtml(inputCenterConfig.purpose)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(sourceHelp)}</span></div><div class="input-center-actions compact-actions"><div class="input-storage-inline"><span>저장</span><label><input type="radio" name="input-storage" value="uep" ${inputCenterConfig.storageMode==='uep'?'checked':''}> 입력센터 원본</label><label><input type="radio" name="input-storage" value="local" ${inputCenterConfig.storageMode==='local'?'checked':''}> PC 임시</label></div>${inputCenterConfig.source!=='직접 입력'?`<button class="btn primary" data-input-files>＋ 파일 여러 개 가져오기</button><button class="btn secondary" data-input-open-folder>원본 보관 폴더</button>`:''}<span>01에서는 원본만 저장하고, 02에서 자료성격·연결프로그램을 최종 확정합니다.</span></div>${inputCenterConfig.source==='직접 입력'?`<div class="input-center-paste direct-only"><textarea data-input-paste rows="5" placeholder="학번 또는 학생 이름 / 활동일 또는 학기 / 학생이 작성한 내용을 붙여넣어 주세요. 여러 학생이면 각 학생 블록 앞에 학번을 넣어주세요.">${escapeHtml(inputCenterConfig.manualText||'')}</textarea><button class="btn secondary" data-input-add-text>붙여넣기 자료 분석</button></div>`:''}</section>
    <section class="input-center-summary"><article><small>가져온 자료</small><b>${drafts.length}</b><span>${inputCenterConfig.batchScope} 범위</span></article><article><small>보고서 묶음</small><b>${drafts.length}</b><span>학생별 자동 묶음</span></article><article><small>자료 분석 완료</small><b>${drafts.filter(d=>d.structuredRow||d.ocrStatus==='OCR완료').length}</b><span>Excel 직접분석 · 스캔 OCR</span></article><article><small>자동 매칭</small><b>${matched}</b><span>학생 확인 완료</span></article><article class="${ocrErrors?'warn':''}"><small>확인 필요</small><b>${ocrErrors}</b><span>학생매칭·자료분석 확인</span></article></section>
    <section class="card input-center-stage"><div class="section-head"><div><small>STAGING · 저장 전 교사 검수</small><h3>이번에 가져온 자료</h3></div><div><button class="btn secondary" data-input-clear ${drafts.length?'':'disabled'}>대기목록 비우기</button><button class="btn primary" data-input-save ${drafts.length?'':'disabled'}>${saveLabel}</button></div></div>${drafts.length?`<div class="input-center-table-wrap"><table class="input-center-table"><thead><tr><th>선택</th><th>자료</th><th>자동 판별</th><th>학생매칭</th><th>검수상태</th><th>내용 미리보기</th></tr></thead><tbody>${drafts.map(d=>`<tr data-input-draft="${escapeHtml(d.draftId)}"><td><input type="checkbox" data-input-select ${d.selected!==false?'checked':''}></td><td><b>${escapeHtml(d.pages?.length?`${d.pages.length}페이지 · ${inputCenterConfig.title||'학생 보고서'}`:d.fileName)}</b><small>${d.pages?.length?d.pages.map(p=>`${p.page}p ${escapeHtml(p.fileName)}`).join(' · '):(d.extension?`${escapeHtml(d.extension.toUpperCase())} · `:'')+(d.size?`${Math.ceil(d.size/1024)}KB`:'직접 텍스트')}</small>${d.pages?.length?`<small class="bundle-note">${escapeHtml(d.bundleStatus||'페이지 묶음')}</small>`:''}</td><td><b>${escapeHtml(d.detectedType||'판별 대기')}</b><small>신뢰도 ${escapeHtml(d.detectedConfidence||'-')} · ${escapeHtml(d.detectedReason||'')}</small></td><td>${d.studentNo?`<b>${escapeHtml(d.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(d.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(d.className||'-')}반 · ${escapeHtml(d.matchStatus||'자동매칭')}</small>`:`<b>학생 확인 전</b><small>${escapeHtml(d.ocrDetected||'OCR 결과에서 학번·이름을 찾는 중')}</small>`}</td><td><span class="input-match ${d.ocrStatus==='OCR완료'?(d.studentNo?'ok':'pending'):/오류|확인필요/.test(String(d.ocrStatus||''))?'pending':''}">${escapeHtml(d.ocrStatus||(d.structuredRow?'구조분석완료':'OCR 대기'))}</span><small>${escapeHtml(d.ocrEngine||d.ocrError||'')}</small></td><td><p>${escapeHtml((d.text||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,180)||(d.ocrStatus==='OCR처리중'?'OCR 처리 중…':'OCR 결과 대기'))}</p></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:'<div class="empty-state input-center-empty"><b>아직 가져온 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>파일을 여러 개 선택하거나 텍스트를 붙여넣으면 학생별 검수 대기자료로 들어옵니다.</span></div>'}</section></div>`;
}

function inputCenterOcrCandidates(){return inputCenterBatchStudents();}
function inputCenterApplyOcrMatch(draft){
  const text=String(draft.text||'');const candidates=inputCenterOcrCandidates();const compact=text.replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const nums=[...compact.matchAll(/(?:^|[^0-9])([123][0-9]{3})(?![0-9])/g)].map(m=>m[1]);
  for(const no of nums){const st=candidates.find(x=>String(x.studentNo||'')===no);if(st){draft.studentId=st.id||'';draft.studentNo=st.studentNo||'';draft.name=st.name||'';draft.grade=st.grade||String(st.studentNo||'').slice(0,1);draft.className=String(st.className||classNumberOf(st)||'').replace(/반/g,'');draft.matchStatus='OCR 자동매칭';draft.ocrDetected=`학번 ${no}`;return true;}}
  const names=candidates.filter(st=>st.name&&compact.includes(String(st.name)));if(names.length===1){const st=names[0];draft.studentId=st.id||'';draft.studentNo=st.studentNo||'';draft.name=st.name||'';draft.grade=st.grade||String(st.studentNo||'').slice(0,1);draft.className=String(st.className||classNumberOf(st)||'').replace(/반/g,'');draft.matchStatus='OCR 이름매칭';draft.ocrDetected=`이름 ${st.name}`;return true;}
  draft.matchStatus='확인필요';draft.ocrDetected=nums.length?`판독 학번 후보 ${nums.join(', ')}`:'학번·이름 자동검출 실패';return false;
}
async function inputCenterRunAutoOCR(drafts){
  const targets=[];for(const d of drafts){d.ocrStatus='OCR처리중';d.ocrError='';const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];pages.forEach((p,i)=>{if(p.filePath)targets.push({key:`${d.draftId}::${i}`,path:p.filePath});});}
  render('inputs');if(!targets.length)return;
  const result=await window.schoolBoard?.runInputOCR?.({files:targets});if(!result?.ok){drafts.forEach(d=>{d.ocrStatus='OCR오류';d.ocrError=result?.reason||'로컬 OCR 호출 실패';});render('inputs');return;}
  const byKey=new Map((result.results||[]).map(x=>[x.key,x]));
  for(const d of drafts){const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];const texts=[],engines=new Set(),errors=[];pages.forEach((p,i)=>{const r=byKey.get(`${d.draftId}::${i}`);if(!r)return;if(r.ok&&r.text)texts.push(`[${i+1}페이지]\n${r.text}`);if(r.engine)engines.add(r.engine);if(!r.ok)errors.push(`${i+1}p ${r.reason||r.status||'OCR 실패'}`);});d.text=texts.join('\n\n').trim();d.rawText=d.text;d.ocrEngine=[...engines].join(' + ');d.ocrError=errors.join(' / ');d.ocrStatus=errors.length?(d.text?'OCR확인필요':'OCR오류'):(d.text?'OCR완료':'OCR확인필요');inputCenterApplyOcrMatch(d);}
  render('inputs');toast(`로컬 OCR 완료 · 자동매칭 ${drafts.filter(x=>x.studentNo).length}건 · 확인필요 ${drafts.filter(x=>!x.studentNo||/오류|확인필요/.test(String(x.ocrStatus||''))).length}건`);
}
async function inputCenterSelectFiles(){
  const result=await window.schoolBoard?.selectInputFiles?.();
  if(!result?.ok){if(!result?.canceled)toast(result?.reason||'파일을 가져오지 못했습니다.');return;}
  const incoming=result.files||[]; const bundled=inputCenterBundleDuplex(incoming); const incomingNames=new Set(incoming.map(x=>String(x.name||x.fileName||''))); if(({'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source)!=='수기 스캔'&&incomingNames.size){inputCenterDrafts=inputCenterDrafts.filter(d=>!incomingNames.has(String(d.fileName||'')));} inputCenterDrafts.push(...bundled);
  if(({'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source)==='수기 스캔') inputCenterRunAutoOCR(bundled);
  const target=inputCenterBatchExpectedCount(), per=Math.max(1,Number(inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent||1));
  const expected=({'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source)==='수기 스캔'&&target?target*per:0;
  render('inputs');
  if(expected&&incoming.length!==expected) toast(`배치 예상 ${expected}파일과 실제 ${incoming.length}파일이 다릅니다. 자료는 가져왔으며 확인 필요 항목에서 검수할 수 있습니다.`);
  else toast(({'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source)==='수기 스캔'?`${incoming.length}장 → ${Math.ceil(incoming.length/per)}개 학생 보고서 묶음으로 가져왔습니다.`:`${incoming.length}개 파일을 배치로 가져왔습니다.`);
}
function inputCenterApplyStudentNo(draft,no){
  const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===String(no||'').trim());
  if(student){draft.studentId=student.id||'';draft.studentNo=student.studentNo||'';draft.name=student.name||'';draft.grade=student.grade||String(student.studentNo||'').slice(0,1);draft.className=String(student.className||classNumberOf(student)||'').replace(/반/g,'');draft.matchStatus='교사확인';return true;}
  return false;
}
async function inputCenterSave(){
  const rows=inputCenterDrafts.filter(x=>x.selected!==false); if(!rows.length)return toast('등록할 자료를 선택해 주세요.');
  const isProgramReport=inputCenterConfig.type==='보고서·탐구보고서'||inputCenterConfig.subtype==='보고서 제출'||rows.some(x=>/보고서/.test(String(x.detectedType||'')));
  const batchId=`IN-${new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:.TZ]/g,'').slice(0,14)}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6).toUpperCase()}`;
  const selectedProgram=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(p=>String(p.id||p.programId||'')===String(inputCenterConfig.programId||''));
  const resolvedType=isProgramReport?'보고서·탐구보고서':inputCenterConfig.type;
  const resolvedTitle=inputCenterConfig.title.trim()||String(rows[0]?.fileName||'입력센터 자료').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'');
  const payload={batchId,source:inputCenterConfig.source,type:resolvedType,subtype:inputCenterConfig.subtype,storageMode:inputCenterConfig.storageMode,title:resolvedTitle,semester:inputCenterConfig.semester,teacher:currentLoginTeacherName()||currentUser?.name||'',programId:inputCenterConfig.programId||'',programKind:selectedProgram?.kind||'',programName:selectedProgram?.actualTitle||selectedProgram?.title||'',batchScope:inputCenterConfig.batchScope,batchGrade:inputCenterConfig.batchGrade,batchClass:inputCenterConfig.batchClass,batchStudentNo:inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo,pagesPerStudent:Number(inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent||1),expectedCount:inputCenterBatchExpectedCount(),records:rows.map(x=>({...x,programId:inputCenterConfig.programId||'',programKind:selectedProgram?.kind||'',programName:selectedProgram?.actualTitle||selectedProgram?.title||'',reportFields:x.reportFields||[],submittedAt:x.submittedAt||''}))};
  let result=await window.schoolBoard?.saveInputRecords?.(payload); if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'입력자료를 저장하지 못했습니다.');
  if(inputCenterConfig.storageMode==='uep'&&result?.requiresConfirmation){
    const programLabel=selectedProgram?`${selectedProgram.kind==='common'?'공통활동':'선택활동'} · ${selectedProgram.actualTitle||selectedProgram.title||inputCenterConfig.programId}`:'선택 프로그램';
    const summary=`등록 대상: ${programLabel}\n\n신규 ${result.newCount||0}건 · 중복 ${result.duplicateCount||0}건 · 갱신 ${result.updateCount||0}건\n중복 자료는 저장하지 않고, 기존 학생·프로그램 보고서가 있으면서 제출시각이 달라진 자료는 갱신합니다.\n\n계속 저장할까요?`;
    if(!confirm(summary))return toast('영구 저장을 취소했습니다. 기존 자료는 변경되지 않았습니다.');
    result=await window.schoolBoard?.saveInputRecords?.({...payload,confirmed:true}); if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'입력자료를 저장하지 못했습니다.');
  }
  if(inputCenterConfig.storageMode==='local') inputCenterLocalHistory.unshift(...rows.map(r=>({...r,source:payload.source,type:payload.type,subtype:payload.subtype,title:payload.title||r.fileName,storageMode:'local',savedAt:new Date().toISOString(),createdAt:new Date().toISOString()})));
  inputCenterDrafts=[]; if(inputCenterConfig.storageMode==='uep'){if(Array.isArray(result?.externalInputs))readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),externalInputs:result.externalInputs,inputCenterSyncedAt:result.inputCenterSyncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};else await refreshInputCenterSourceSilently();inputCenterConfig.tab='library';inputCenterConfig.batchFilter=batchId;inputCenterConfig.historyMode='active';} render('inputs');
  if(inputCenterConfig.storageMode==='uep') toast(`입력센터 원본 저장 완료 · 신규 ${result.newCount||0} · 갱신 ${result.updateCount||0} · 중복 제외 ${result.duplicateCount||0} · 02 검수·분류로 이동했습니다.`);
  else toast(`${result.count||rows.length}건을 내 PC 로컬에 저장했습니다.`);
}
function inputCenterHumanReportText(value=''){
  const source=String(value||'').trim();if(!source)return '';
  try{
    const meta=JSON.parse(source);
    const fields=Array.isArray(meta?.fields)?meta.fields:Array.isArray(meta)?meta:[];
    const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
    const pairs=fields.map((f,i)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${i+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
    if(pairs.length)return pairs.map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
  }catch{}
  return source;
}
function inputCenterReviewDisplayText(row={}){
  const reviewed=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.reviewText||'');
  if(reviewed)return reviewed;
  const structured=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.normalizedText||'');
  if(structured&&!/^\s*[\[{]/.test(structured))return structured;
  if(structured&&structured!==String(row.normalizedText||'').trim())return structured;
  return String(row.rawText||row.text||'').trim();
}
async function inputCenterOpenDetail(row){
  inputCenterDetail=row;
  const rawPaths=Array.isArray(row.pages)&&row.pages.length?row.pages.map(p=>p.filePath||p.storedPath||'').filter(Boolean):String(row.storedPath||row.filePath||'').split('|').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const assets=[];
  for(const p of rawPaths){const r=await window.schoolBoard?.readInputAsset?.(p);if(r?.ok)assets.push({...r,path:p,rotation:0});}
  const text=inputCenterReviewDisplayText(row);
  const raw=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.rawText||row.text||row.normalizedText||'');
  const body=`<div class="input-detail"><div class="input-detail-original"><div class="section-head"><div><small>ORIGINAL · ${assets.length||rawPaths.length}페이지</small><h3>원본 확인</h3></div></div>${assets.length?`<div class="input-page-tabs">${assets.map((a,i)=>`<button class="${i===0?'active':''}" data-input-page="${i}">${i+1}페이지</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="input-page-view">${assets.map((a,i)=>`<div class="input-page ${i===0?'active':''}" data-input-page-panel="${i}"><img src="${a.dataUrl}" data-input-page-img="${i}" alt="${i+1}페이지 원본"><div><button class="btn secondary" data-input-rotate="${i}">↻ 90° 회전</button><button class="btn secondary" data-input-open-asset="${i}">원본 파일 열기</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:`<div class="empty-state"><b>미리 볼 수 있는 이미지 원본이 없습니다.</b><span>PDF 등은 원본 파일 열기로 확인할 수 있습니다.</span></div>`}</div><div class="input-detail-text"><small>TEXT REVIEW · 원본과 검수본 분리 보존</small><h3>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'학생 확인 전')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</h3><div class="input-ocr-meta"><span>${escapeHtml(row.ocrStatus||'OCR 상태 미확인')}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.ocrEngine||'로컬 OCR')}</span>${row.ocrError?`<span class="warn">${escapeHtml(row.ocrError)}</span>`:''}</div><label>원본에서 읽은 내용<textarea rows="12" readonly>${escapeHtml(raw||'원본에서 읽은 내용이 없습니다. OCR 또는 파일 분석 상태를 확인해 주세요.')}</textarea></label><label>확인·수정할 내용<textarea rows="12" data-input-review-text placeholder="원본과 비교해 필요한 부분만 수정해 주세요.">${escapeHtml(text)}</textarea></label><div class="input-detail-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-input-detail-save>검수 완료</button></div></div></div>`;
  $('#drawerKicker').textContent=`${row.source||'입력센터'} · ${row.title||row.fileName||'학생 자료'}`;$('#drawerTitle').textContent='자료 상세보기';$('#drawerBody').innerHTML=body;$('#drawer').classList.remove('hidden');$('#drawerBackdrop').classList.remove('hidden');
  $$('[data-input-page]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{$$('[data-input-page]').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));$$('[data-input-page-panel]').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.dataset.inputPagePanel===b.dataset.inputPage));});
  $$('[data-input-rotate]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const i=Number(b.dataset.inputRotate),img=$(`[data-input-page-img="${i}"]`);if(!img)return;assets[i].rotation=(assets[i].rotation+90)%360;img.style.transform=`rotate(${assets[i].rotation}deg)`;});
  $$('[data-input-open-asset]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>window.schoolBoard?.openInputAsset?.(assets[Number(b.dataset.inputOpenAsset)]?.path||''));
  $('[data-input-detail-save]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{row.reviewText=$('[data-input-review-text]')?.value||'';row.status=row.reviewText?'텍스트검수완료':row.status;toast('확인한 내용을 저장했습니다.');closeDrawer();render('inputs');});
}

function inputCenterSuggestTarget(rows=[]){
  const joined=rows.map(r=>`${r.type||''} ${r.subtype||''} ${r.title||''}`).join(' ');
  if(/신청서|신청자|신청명단/.test(joined)) return {type:'프로그램 신청서',label:'프로그램 > 신청·참여 대상',description:'학생별 신청내용을 프로그램 신청/참여 자료로 연결합니다. 실제 참여 확정 전에는 신청자료로 구분합니다.'};
  if(/보고서|독서|탐구/.test(joined)) return {type:'학생 보고서',label:'프로그램 > 참여이력·보고서',description:'검수 텍스트를 학생별 프로그램 참여이력의 보고서 자료로 연결합니다.'};
  if(/만족도|설문/.test(joined)) return {type:'만족도·설문',label:'프로그램 > 만족도·설문 결과',description:'학생별 응답을 프로그램 만족도/설문 자료로 연결합니다.'};
  if(/수행평가|평가지/.test(joined)) return {type:'교과 수행평가',label:'학생정보 > 교과활동 참고자료',description:'수행평가 원문·검수본을 학생별 교과활동 참고자료로 연결합니다.'};
  return {type:'외부 학생자료',label:'학생정보 > 외부자료',description:'자료유형이 명확하지 않아 학생별 외부자료로 보관합니다. 이후 용도를 다시 지정할 수 있습니다.'};
}
async function inputCenterApply(mode='uep'){
  const rows=inputCenterReviewRowsCache||[];if(!rows.length)return toast('적용할 검수 자료가 없습니다.');
  const unmatched=rows.filter(r=>!r.studentNo&&!r.studentId).length;if(unmatched)return toast(`학생 매칭이 끝나지 않은 자료 ${unmatched}건이 있습니다.`);
  const first=rows[0]||{};const program=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(v=>String(v.id||v.programId||'')===String(first.programId||''))||{};
  const nature=String(first.type||'').includes('보고서')?'보고서':String(first.type||'').includes('신청')?'신청':String(first.type||'').includes('만족도')||String(first.type||'').includes('설문')?'만족도조사':first.type||'기타';
  if(nature==='보고서'&&!first.programId)return toast('보고서는 연결프로그램을 먼저 확정해 주세요.');
  const payload={batchId:first.batchId||'',nature,programId:first.programId||'',programKind:first.programKind||program.kind||'',programName:program.actualTitle||program.title||'',programSemester:program.semester||program.term||'',records:rows.map(r=>({id:r.id,batchId:r.batchId,studentId:r.studentId,studentNo:r.studentNo,name:r.name,schoolYear:r.schoolYear,semester:r.semester,programId:r.programId,programKind:r.programKind,rawText:r.rawText,normalizedText:r.normalizedText,reviewText:r.reviewText||'',createdAt:r.createdAt}))};
  let result=await window.schoolBoard?.normalizeInputBatch?.(payload);if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'UEP 정규화 사전검사에 실패했습니다.');
  if(result.requiresConfirmation){const summary=`정규화 대상: ${result.targetSheet||''}\n${result.programName||payload.programName||''}\n\n신규 ${result.newCount||0}건 · 중복 ${result.duplicateCount||0}건 · 갱신 ${result.updateCount||0}건\n\n계속 UEP에 적용할까요?`;if(!confirm(summary))return toast('UEP 적용을 취소했습니다.');result=await window.schoolBoard?.normalizeInputBatch?.({...payload,confirmed:true});if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'UEP 정규화 적용에 실패했습니다.');}
  await refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:false});inputCenterConfig.historyMode='active';inputCenterConfig.batchFilter='latest';toast(`${result.targetSheet||'UEP'} 적용 완료 · 신규 ${result.newCount||0} · 갱신 ${result.updateCount||0} · 중복 ${result.duplicateCount||0} · 작업목록에서 완료 배치를 내렸습니다.`);render('inputs');
}

function inputCenterExportCsv(){
  const rows=inputCenterReviewRowsCache||[]; if(!rows.length)return toast('내보낼 검수 자료가 없습니다.');
  const q=v=>'"'+String(v??'').replace(/"/g,'""')+'"';
  const csv='\ufeff'+[['학번','이름','학년','반','자료유형','자료제목','검수텍스트','원본파일'].map(q).join(','),...rows.map(r=>[r.studentNo,r.name,r.grade,r.className,r.type,r.title||r.fileName,r.reviewText||r.normalizedText||r.rawText||r.text,r.fileName].map(q).join(','))].join('\r\n');
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'}));a.download=`UEP_검수자료_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.csv`;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000);toast(`${rows.length}건을 CSV로 내보냈습니다.`);
}
function bindInputCenter(){
  $$('[data-input-tab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.tab=b.dataset.inputTab;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-history-mode]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.historyMode=b.dataset.inputHistoryMode||'active';inputCenterConfig.batchFilter='latest';render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-batch-filter]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.batchFilter=e.target.value;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-scope]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.libraryScope=b.dataset.inputScope;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-grade-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.gradeFilter=b.dataset.inputGradeFilter;inputCenterConfig.classFilter='all';render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-class-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.classFilter=b.dataset.inputClassFilter;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-match-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.matchFilter=b.dataset.inputMatchFilter;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-source-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.sourceFilter=b.dataset.inputSourceFilter;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-view]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const rows=inputCenterLibraryRows().filter(x=>inputCenterConfig.libraryScope==='local'?x.storageMode==='local':inputCenterConfig.libraryScope==='uep'?x.storageMode!=='local':true);const row=rows[Number(b.dataset.inputView)];if(row)inputCenterOpenDetail(row);});

  $('[data-input-confirm-classification]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{const rows=filtered;if(!rows.length)return toast('확정할 배치가 없습니다.');const nature=$('[data-input-review-nature]')?.value||'기타';const programId=$('[data-input-review-program]')?.value||'';const program=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(v=>String(v.id||v.programId||'')===String(programId))||{};const result=await window.schoolBoard?.classifyInputBatch?.({batchId:activeBatchId,nature,programId,programKind:program.kind||'',programName:program.actualTitle||program.title||'',programSemester:program.semester||program.term||''});if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'검수·분류 저장에 실패했습니다.');if(Array.isArray(result?.externalInputs))readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),externalInputs:result.externalInputs,inputCenterSyncedAt:result.inputCenterSyncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};else await refreshInputCenterSourceSilently();inputCenterConfig.tab='review';toast(`${result.count||rows.length}건 검수·분류 확정 · 03 UEP 적용으로 이동합니다.`);render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-export-csv]')?.addEventListener('click',inputCenterExportCsv);
  $('[data-input-apply-local]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>inputCenterApply('local'));
  $('[data-input-apply-uep]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>inputCenterApply('uep'));
  $$('[data-input-review-view]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const row=inputCenterReviewRowsCache[Number(b.dataset.inputReviewView)];if(row)inputCenterOpenDetail(row);});
  $$('[data-input-purpose]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.purpose=b.dataset.inputPurpose;const map={'보고서 연결':'보고서·탐구보고서','프로그램 참가신청':'프로그램 신청서','만족도조사':'만족도·설문조사','텍스트 추출·기타':'기타'};inputCenterConfig.type=map[inputCenterConfig.purpose]||'기타';inputCenterConfig.programId='';render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-source]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.source=b.dataset.inputSource;const actual={'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source;if(actual==='리로스쿨'&&inputCenterConfig.purpose==='보고서 연결')inputCenterConfig.subtype='보고서 제출';else if(actual==='리로스쿨'&&inputCenterConfig.purpose==='프로그램 참가신청')inputCenterConfig.subtype='참가신청 명단';render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-subtype]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>inputCenterConfig.subtype=e.target.value);
  $$('input[name="input-storage"]').forEach(r=>r.addEventListener('change',e=>inputCenterConfig.storageMode=e.target.value));
  $('[data-input-semester]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.semester=e.target.value;inputCenterConfig.programId='';render('inputs');});
  const titleInput=$('[data-input-title]'); if(titleInput){titleInput.readOnly=false;titleInput.disabled=false;titleInput.addEventListener('input',e=>{inputCenterConfig.title=e.target.value;});titleInput.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.title=e.target.value;});titleInput.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{e.stopPropagation();});}
  $('[data-input-batch-scope]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.batchScope=e.target.value;render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-batch-grade]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.batchGrade=e.target.value;render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-batch-class]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.batchClass=e.target.value;render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-batch-student]')?.addEventListener('input',e=>{inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo=e.target.value;});
  $('[data-input-expected-count]')?.addEventListener('input',e=>{inputCenterConfig.expectedCount=e.target.value;render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-pages-per-student]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent=e.target.value;render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-paste]')?.addEventListener('input',e=>inputCenterConfig.manualText=e.target.value);
  $('[data-input-program]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.programId=e.target.value;const p=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(v=>String(v.id||v.programId||'')===String(e.target.value))||{};const sem=String(p.semester||p.term||'');if(/1/.test(sem))inputCenterConfig.semester='1';else if(/2/.test(sem))inputCenterConfig.semester='2';});
  $('[data-input-files]')?.addEventListener('click',inputCenterSelectFiles);
  $('[data-input-open-folder]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>window.schoolBoard?.openInputFolder?.());
  $('[data-input-add-text]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{const raw=$('[data-input-paste]')?.value||'';if(!raw.trim())return toast('붙여넣을 내용을 입력해 주세요.');const fake={name:'직접입력.txt',extension:'txt',size:new Blob([raw]).size,text:raw,path:''};inputCenterDrafts.push(...inputCenterExpandTextFile(fake));inputCenterConfig.manualText='';render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-clear]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(inputCenterDrafts.length&&confirm('현재 대기목록을 비울까요?')){inputCenterDrafts=[];render('inputs');}});
  $('[data-input-save]')?.addEventListener('click',inputCenterSave);
  $$('[data-input-draft]').forEach(row=>{const draft=inputCenterDrafts.find(d=>d.draftId===row.dataset.inputDraft);if(!draft)return;row.querySelector('[data-input-select]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>draft.selected=e.target.checked);});
}

function submissionDateText(value){
  const raw=String(value??"").trim();
  if(!raw)return "-";
  const serial=Number(raw);
  let date=null;
  if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)date=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000);
  else{
    const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
    if(Number.isFinite(parsed))date=new Date(parsed);
  }
  if(!date||Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return raw;
  return `${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}
function reportSubmissionLabel(report){
  const date=submissionDateText(report?.finalSubmittedAt||report?.submittedAt);
  const count=Math.max(1,Number(report?.submissionCount)||1);
  return `${date} (${count}차 제출)`;
}

function selectionComparisonsForStudent(student){
  return (readonlyCache?.selectionComparisons||[]).filter(x=>(x.studentId&&String(x.studentId)===String(student?.id))||(x.studentNo&&String(x.studentNo)===String(student?.studentNo)));
}
function selectionComparisonMarkup(student){
  const rows=selectionComparisonsForStudent(student);
  if(!rows.length)return `<section class="selection-compare-panel"><header><div><small>PRE-CONSULTATION ↔ MAIN APPLICATION</small><h3>사전상담 ↔ 본신청 비교</h3></div><span>비교자료 없음</span></header><p class="selection-compare-help">기존 Google Form 사전상담과 리로스쿨 본신청 자료가 함께 연결되면 학기별 변경 내용을 표시합니다.</p></section>`;
  const changed=rows.filter(x=>x.status!=='일치').length;
  return `<section class="selection-compare-panel"><header><div><small>PRE-CONSULTATION ↔ MAIN APPLICATION</small><h3>사전상담 ↔ 본신청 비교</h3><p>학생이 상담 단계에서 생각했던 선택과 실제 본신청이 어떻게 달라졌는지 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${changed?`변경 ${changed}개 학기`:'전체 일치'}</span></header><div class="selection-compare-grid">${rows.sort((a,b)=>curriculumTermOrder(a.term)-curriculumTermOrder(b.term)).map(x=>`<article class="selection-compare-card ${x.status==='일치'?'same':'changed'}"><div class="selection-compare-title"><b>${escapeHtml(x.term)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.status)}</em></div><div class="selection-compare-columns"><div><small>사전상담</small><p>${x.preSubjects.map(s=>`<span>${escapeHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')||'<i>없음</i>'}</p></div><div><small>본신청</small><p>${x.mainSubjects.map(s=>`<span>${escapeHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')||'<i>없음</i>'}</p></div></div>${x.status!=='일치'?`<div class="selection-change-summary">${x.removed.length?`<span class="removed">빠짐 · ${escapeHtml(x.removed.join(' · '))}</span>`:''}${x.added.length?`<span class="added">추가 · ${escapeHtml(x.added.join(' · '))}</span>`:''}</div>`:'<div class="selection-change-summary"><span class="same">사전상담과 본신청이 같습니다.</span></div>'}</article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
}

function selectionErrorHistoryMarkup(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.selectionErrorTransitions||[]).filter(x=>String(x.studentId||'')===String(student?.id||'')||String(x.studentNo||'')===String(student?.studentNo||''));
  if(!rows.length)return `<section class="selection-error-history"><header><div><small>VALIDATION HISTORY</small><h3>사전신청 오류 이력</h3></div><span>특이사항 없음</span></header><p>사전 Google Form 신청 당시 발견된 문·이과 혼합·과학 위계·학기간 중복 오류 이력입니다.</p></section>`;
  const bad=rows.filter(x=>/지속|신규|재검토/.test(x.status)).length;
  return `<section class="selection-error-history"><header><div><small>VALIDATION HISTORY</small><h3>사전신청 오류 이력</h3><p>상담 당시 오류가 실제 본신청에서 해결되었는지 집중 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${bad?`확인 필요 ${bad}건`:'모두 해결'}</span></header><div class="selection-error-history-grid">${rows.map(x=>`<article class="${/해결/.test(x.status)?'resolved':/본신청 전/.test(x.status)?'waiting':'warning'}"><b>${escapeHtml(x.category)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.status)}</em><p>${escapeHtml(x.mainMessage||x.preMessage||'세부내용 확인')}</p></article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
}
function selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle){
  const rows=(bundle?.selected||[]).filter(x=>x.finalSubmittedAt||x.submittedAt||x.submissionCount||x.modifiedAfterNotice);
  const stamp=x=>{const raw=x.finalSubmittedAt||x.submittedAt||"";const serial=Number(raw);if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)return Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000;const parsed=Date.parse(String(raw).replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:0;};
  const row=rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>stamp(b)-stamp(a)||(Number(b.submissionCount)||0)-(Number(a.submissionCount)||0))[0]||{};
  const count=Math.max(1,...rows.map(x=>Number(x.submissionCount)||1));
  const rawDate=row.finalSubmittedAt||row.submittedAt||"";
  const parsedText=submissionDateText(rawDate);
  const y=(new Date()).getFullYear();
  const m=String(parsedText).match(/(\d{4})[.\/-](\d{1,2})[.\/-](\d{1,2})/);
  const afterNotice=Boolean(m && `${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,"0")}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,"0")}`>`${y}-08-03`);
  return {date:parsedText,count,modifiedAfterNotice:afterNotice||rows.some(x=>Boolean(x.modifiedAfterNotice))};
}
function outputReportRows(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const studentMap=new Map();
  students.forEach(s=>{studentMap.set(String(s.id),s);studentMap.set(String(s.studentNo),s);});
  return (readonlyCache?.programReports||[]).map(report=>{
    const student=studentMap.get(String(report.studentId||report.studentNo))||{};
    const rawClass=String(student.className||String(report.studentNo||"").slice(1,2)||"");
    const classNo=(rawClass.match(/[1-9]/)||[""])[0];
    return {...report,classNo,number:String(student.number||String(report.studentNo||"").slice(-2)||"").replace(/^0/,""),studentName:report.name||student.name||"",studentRef:student};
  }).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.classNo)-Number(b.classNo)||Number(a.number)-Number(b.number)||String(a.programName).localeCompare(String(b.programName)));
}
function outputReportProgramKey(report){
  const raw=String(report?.programId||"").trim();
  const pid=raw.replace(/^(common|selected|after(?:-session|-course|-master)?)-/,'').trim();
  if(pid)return `id:${pid}`;
  const name=String(report?.reportGroup||report?.programName||"").normalize('NFKC').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  return name?`name:${name}`:'';
}
function outputReportProgramLabel(report){
  const raw=String(report?.programId||"").trim();
  const pid=raw.replace(/^(common|selected|after(?:-session|-course|-master)?)-/,'').trim();
  if(pid){
    const program=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(p=>{
      const candidate=String(p?.programId||p?.id||"").replace(/^(common|selected|after(?:-session|-course|-master)?)-/,'').trim();
      return candidate&&candidate===pid;
    });
    if(program)return String(program.actualTitle||program.title||program.recordTitle||report?.reportGroup||report?.programName||'보고서').trim();
  }
  return String(report?.reportGroup||report?.programName||'보고서').trim();
}
function outputProgramsForReports(rows){
  const map=new Map();
  rows.forEach(r=>{const key=outputReportProgramKey(r);if(key&&!map.has(key))map.set(key,{key,label:outputReportProgramLabel(r)});});
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
}
function outputSelected(id){return outputCenterState.selected.has(String(id));}
function outputCheckbox(id){return `<input type="checkbox" data-output-select="${escapeHtml(String(id))}" ${outputSelected(id)?"checked":""}>`;}

function outputClassNoOfStudent(student){return String(student?.className||student?.classNo||String(student?.studentNo||'').slice(1,2)||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'').slice(0,1);}
function outputClassStudentPicker(description){
  const students=outputStudents();
  const classes=[...new Set(students.map(outputClassNoOfStudent).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  if(!classes.includes(String(outputCenterState.classNo)))outputCenterState.classNo=classes[0]||'1';
  const scoped=students.filter(s=>outputClassNoOfStudent(s)===String(outputCenterState.classNo));
  if(!scoped.some(s=>s.id===outputCenterState.studentId))outputCenterState.studentId=scoped[0]?.id||'';
  return `<section class="output-picker-v7642"><p>${escapeHtml(description||'반과 학생을 선택하세요.')}</p><div class="output-class-cards">${classes.map(c=>`<button class="${String(outputCenterState.classNo)===c?'active':''}" data-output-class-card="${c}"><b>${c}반</b><small>${students.filter(s=>outputClassNoOfStudent(s)===c).length}명</small></button>`).join('')}</div><div class="output-student-cards">${scoped.map(s=>`<button class="${s.id===outputCenterState.studentId?'active':''}" data-output-student-card="${escapeHtml(s.id)}"><b>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(s.name)}</span></button>`).join('')||'<div class="empty-state">해당 반 학생이 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section>`;
}
function reportCategoryKey(row){
  const text=`${row?.kind||''} ${row?.type||''} ${row?.area||''} ${row?.reportGroup||''} ${row?.programName||''}`;
  const common=/공통/.test(text)||String(row?.kind||'').includes('common');
  const career=/진로/.test(text);
  return `${common?'common':'selected'}-${career?'career':'autonomy'}`;
}
function programCategoryKey(p){
  if(p?.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화/.test(`${p?.kind||''} ${p?.type||''} ${p?.title||''}`))return 'after';
  const common=p?.kind==='common'||/공통/.test(`${p?.kind||''} ${p?.type||''}`);
  const career=/진로/.test(`${p?.type||''} ${p?.area||''} ${p?.recordTitle||''}`);
  return `${common?'common':'selected'}-${career?'career':'autonomy'}`;
}
function outputCategoryCards(kind='report'){
  const isAttendance=kind==='attendance';
  const items=isAttendance?[
    ['selected-autonomy','★','선택(자율)','선택활동','자율 영역 선택프로그램 출석부'],
    ['selected-career','◎','선택(진로)','선택활동','진로 영역 선택프로그램 출석부'],
    ['after','▦','방과후학교','수업·차시 연동','강좌별 전체 차시 통합 출석부']
  ]:[
    ['common-autonomy','◉','공통(자율)','학교 창체운영','공통 자율활동 학생 보고서'],
    ['common-career','◎','공통(진로)','학교 창체운영','공통 진로활동 학생 보고서'],
    ['selected-autonomy','★','선택(자율)','학년 특색프로그램','선택 자율활동 학생 보고서'],
    ['selected-career','◇','선택(진로)','학년 특색프로그램','선택 진로활동 학생 보고서']
  ];
  const key=isAttendance?'attendanceCategory':'reportCategory';
  return `<div class="output-standard-hub ${isAttendance?'three':'four'}">${items.map(([id,icon,title,eyebrow,desc])=>`<button class="output-standard-card ${outputCenterState[key]===id?'active':''}" data-output-${kind}-category="${id}"><span class="output-standard-icon">${icon}</span><div><small>${eyebrow}</small><h3>${title}</h3><p>${desc}</p></div><footer><em>${outputCenterState[key]===id?'선택됨':'보기'} ›</em></footer></button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function outputAfterCourseKey(program){return String(program?.courseId||program?.programId||program?.id||'').replace(/^after-(?:course|master|session)-/,'');}
function afterschoolIntegratedAttendancePreview(course){
  const all=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>p?.kind==='after'&&!p.isCourseMaster&&!/일정확인필요|취소|휴강|폐강/.test(String(p.status||p.operationStatus||'')));
  const key=outputAfterCourseKey(course);
  const sessions=all.filter(s=>outputAfterCourseKey(s)===key).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||'').localeCompare(String(b.date||''))||Number(a.sessionNo||0)-Number(b.sessionNo||0));
  const rosterMap=new Map();
  [course,...sessions].forEach(p=>(p.students||[]).forEach(st=>{const k=String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number||st.name||'');if(k&&!rosterMap.has(k))rosterMap.set(k,st);}));
  const roster=[...rosterMap.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||a.number||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||b.number||'')));
  const head=sessions.map((session,i)=>`<th><b>${escapeHtml(session.sessionNo||i+1)}차시</b><small>${escapeHtml(String(session.date||'').slice(5).replace('-','/'))}</small></th>`).join('');
  const rows=roster.map((student,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||student.number||'-')}</td><td><b>${escapeHtml(student.name||'-')}</b></td>${sessions.map(session=>`<td class="attendance-mark">${escapeHtml(resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(session,student)||'-')}</td>`).join('')}<td></td></tr>`).join('');
  return `<div class="output-attendance-summary"><span><small>강좌</small><b>${escapeHtml(course.actualTitle||course.title||'-')}</b></span><span><small>수강학생</small><b>${roster.length}명</b></span><span><small>전체 차시</small><b>${sessions.length}차시</b></span><span><small>담당</small><b>${escapeHtml(course.teacher||sessions[0]?.teacher||'-')}</b></span></div><div class="output-attendance-scroll"><table class="output-attendance-table"><thead><tr><th>순번</th><th>학번</th><th>이름</th>${head}<th>비고</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows||'<tr><td colspan="99">출석부 학생 자료가 없습니다.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function reportsOutputPanel(){
  const all=outputReportRows(),students=outputStudents();
  if(outputCenterState.reportScope==='student'){
    const student=outputSelectedStudent(),reports=student?studentAllReports(student):[];
    const content=student?`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>제출 보고서 ${reports.length}건</span></div><div class="preview-list">${reports.map(r=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(r.reportGroup||r.programName||'보고서')}</b><span>${escapeHtml(r.kind||'')} · ${escapeHtml(reportSubmissionLabel(r))}</span></article>`).join('')||'<p>제출한 보고서가 없습니다.</p>'}</div>`:'<p>학생을 선택하세요.</p>';
    return `${outputStandardSection('REPORT · STUDENT','학생 개인별 보고서','반을 선택하고 학생을 누르면 해당 학생의 보고서를 모아봅니다.')}<div class="output-scope-tabs standard"><button data-output-report-scope="program">프로그램별</button><button class="active" data-output-report-scope="student">학생 개인별</button></div>${outputClassStudentPicker('반을 누른 뒤 학생을 선택하면 제출한 보고서 목록을 확인할 수 있습니다.')}${student?outputPreviewShell(`${student.studentNo} ${student.name} · 보고서`,`제출 보고서 ${reports.length}건`,content,`<button class="btn primary" data-output-student-reports-all>전체 보고서 인쇄</button>`):''}`;
  }
  const categoryRows=all.filter(r=>reportCategoryKey(r)===outputCenterState.reportCategory);
  const programs=outputProgramsForReports(categoryRows);
  const programKeys=programs.map(p=>p.key);
  if(outputCenterState.program==='all'||!programKeys.includes(outputCenterState.program))outputCenterState.program=programKeys[0]||'all';
  const filtered=categoryRows.filter(r=>(outputCenterState.program==='all'||outputReportProgramKey(r)===outputCenterState.program)&&(outputCenterState.status==='all'||(outputCenterState.status==='submitted'?r.submitted:!r.submitted)));
  const submitted=categoryRows.filter(r=>r.submitted).length, missing=categoryRows.length-submitted;
  return `${outputStandardSection('REPORT CENTER','프로그램별 보고서','활동영역 → 프로그램 → 제출학생 → 실제 보고서 순서로 확인합니다.',`<span><b>${programs.length}</b>프로그램</span><span><b>${submitted}</b>제출</span><span><b>${missing}</b>미제출</span>`)}<div class="output-scope-tabs standard"><button class="active" data-output-report-scope="program">프로그램별</button><button data-output-report-scope="student">학생 개인별</button></div>${outputCategoryCards('report')}<section class="output-standard-stage"><header><div><small>STEP 02 · PROGRAM</small><h3>프로그램 선택</h3><p>프로그램ID 기준으로 동일 프로그램의 보고서를 한 카드로 묶습니다.</p></div></header><div class="output-standard-program-grid">${programs.map(p=>{const count=categoryRows.filter(r=>outputReportProgramKey(r)===p.key).length;return `<button class="output-standard-program ${outputCenterState.program===p.key?'active':''}" data-output-program-card="${escapeHtml(p.key)}"><div><small>REPORT PROGRAM</small><h4>${escapeHtml(p.label)}</h4><p>보고서 ${count}건</p></div><em>${outputCenterState.program===p.key?'선택됨':'선택'} ›</em></button>`}).join('')||'<div class="empty-state">해당 영역의 보고서가 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section><section class="output-standard-stage"><header><div><small>STEP 03 · STUDENTS</small><h3>제출 학생</h3><p>출력할 학생을 선택한 뒤 실제 보고서를 확인합니다.</p></div><div class="output-bulk-actions"><span>${filtered.length}건</span><button class="btn secondary" data-output-select-visible>현재 목록 전체선택</button><button class="btn primary" data-output-bulk-report-print>선택 보고서 인쇄</button></div></header><div class="output-table"><div class="output-row head"><span></span><span>반</span><span>학번·이름</span><span>보고서</span><span>제출일</span><span>보기</span></div>${filtered.map(r=>`<div class="output-row"><span>${outputCheckbox(r.id)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(r.classNo||'-')}반</span><span>${escapeHtml(`${r.studentNo||''} ${r.studentName||''}`)}</span><span><b>${escapeHtml(outputReportProgramLabel(r))}</b></span><span>${escapeHtml(reportSubmissionLabel(r))}</span><span><button class="link-button" data-open-student-report="${escapeHtml(r.id)}">보기</button></span></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty-state">조건에 맞는 보고서가 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section>`;
}
function subjectsOutputPanel(){
  const errors=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[];
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const errorStudentIds=new Set(errors.map(e=>String(e.studentId||e.studentNo)));
  const rows=students.filter(s=>outputCenterState.errorFilter==="all"||(outputCenterState.errorFilter==="error"?errorStudentIds.has(String(s.id))||errorStudentIds.has(String(s.studentNo)):!errorStudentIds.has(String(s.id))&&!errorStudentIds.has(String(s.studentNo))));
  return `<div class="output-filter-bar"><label>상담 구분<select data-output-filter="errorFilter"><option value="all">전체 학생</option><option value="error" ${outputCenterState.errorFilter==="error"?"selected":""}>오류·상담 필요</option><option value="normal" ${outputCenterState.errorFilter==="normal"?"selected":""}>정상</option></select></label></div><div class="output-bulk-actions"><span>${rows.length}명</span><button class="btn primary" data-output-subject-print>선택과목 상담표 인쇄</button><button class="btn secondary" data-output-subject-excel>신청내역 Excel</button></div><div class="output-table"><div class="output-row subject head"><span>반</span><span>학번·이름</span><span>상태</span><span>오류내용</span></div>${rows.map(s=>{const es=errors.filter(e=>String(e.studentId||e.studentNo)===String(s.id)||String(e.studentNo)===String(s.studentNo));return `<div class="output-row subject"><span>${escapeHtml(s.className||"-")}반</span><span>${escapeHtml(`${s.studentNo} ${s.name}`)}</span><span class="state ${es.length?"warn":""}">${es.length?"상담 필요":"정상"}</span><span>${escapeHtml(es.map(e=>e.message||e.type).join(" · ")||"-")}</span></div>`;}).join("")}</div>`;
}
function attendanceOutputPanel(){
  const connected=readonlyCache?.programs||[];
  const isAfter=outputCenterState.attendanceCategory==='after';
  let programs;
  if(isAfter){
    const sessions=connected.filter(p=>p.kind==='after');
    programs=groupedAfterSchoolCourses(sessions);
  }else programs=connected.filter(p=>p.kind!=='after'&&programCategoryKey(p)===outputCenterState.attendanceCategory);
  if(outputCenterState.session==='all'||!programs.some(p=>String(p.id)===String(outputCenterState.session)))outputCenterState.session=programs[0]?.id||'all';
  const p=programs.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(outputCenterState.session));
  const participantCount=p?(p.students||[]).length:0;
  const preview=p?(isAfter?afterschoolIntegratedAttendancePreview(p):`<div class="output-attendance-summary"><span><small>프로그램</small><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title||'-')}</b></span><span><small>대상</small><b>${participantCount}명</b></span><span><small>운영일</small><b>${escapeHtml(p.date||'-')}</b></span><span><small>담당</small><b>${escapeHtml(p.teacher||'-')}</b></span></div><table class="output-attendance-table simple"><thead><tr><th>순번</th><th>학번</th><th>이름</th><th>출석</th><th>비고</th></tr></thead><tbody>${(p.students||[]).map((st,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${escapeHtml(st.studentNo||st.number||'-')}</td><td><b>${escapeHtml(st.name||'-')}</b></td><td>${escapeHtml(st.attendance||st.status||'')}</td><td></td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="5">명단 자료가 없습니다.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table>`):'';
  return `${outputStandardSection('ATTENDANCE · PRINT','출석부','선택활동 또는 방과후 강좌를 고르면 실제 출석 결과를 출력형태로 확인합니다.',`<span><b>${programs.length}</b>프로그램</span><span><b>${p?(p.students||[]).length:0}</b>대상</span>`)}${outputCategoryCards('attendance')}<section class="output-standard-stage"><header><div><small>STEP 02 · PROGRAM</small><h3>${isAfter?'방과후 강좌':'프로그램'} 선택</h3><p>${isAfter?'강좌를 선택하면 프로그램 메뉴에서 기록한 전체 차시 출석결과를 한 출석부에 모읍니다.':'프로그램을 선택하면 해당 참여학생의 출석부를 표시합니다.'}</p></div></header><div class="output-standard-program-grid">${programs.map(item=>`<button class="output-standard-program ${String(item.id)===String(outputCenterState.session)?'active':''}" data-output-session-card="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"><div><small>${isAfter?'AFTER SCHOOL COURSE':'SELECTED PROGRAM'}</small><h4>${escapeHtml(item.actualTitle||item.title||item.recordTitle||'프로그램')}</h4><p>${escapeHtml(isAfter?`${item.sessionCount||item.dates?.length||0}차시 · ${(item.students||[]).length}명`:[item.date,item.time,(item.students||[]).length&&`${(item.students||[]).length}명`].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p></div><em>${String(item.id)===String(outputCenterState.session)?'선택됨':'선택'} ›</em></button>`).join('')||'<div class="empty-state">해당 영역의 프로그램이 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section>${p?outputPreviewShell(p.actualTitle||p.title,isAfter?'전체 차시 통합 출석부 · 프로그램 메뉴 출석결과 연동':'프로그램 출석부',preview,`<button class="btn primary" data-output-roster-print="${escapeHtml(p.id)}">출석부 인쇄</button>`):''}`;
}

function outputStudents(){
  return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>String(s.status||"재학")==="재학").slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)));
}
function outputSelectedStudent(){
  const students=outputStudents();
  if(!outputCenterState.studentId||!students.some(s=>s.id===outputCenterState.studentId))outputCenterState.studentId=students[0]?.id||"";
  return students.find(s=>s.id===outputCenterState.studentId)||null;
}
function outputStudentPicker(description){return outputClassStudentPicker(description);}

  return studentCanonicalReports(student);
}
