function printStudentReportPackage(student,reports){
  if(!student||!reports.length)return toast("출력할 보고서가 없습니다.");
  const body=reports.map(r=>`<article class="report-page"><header class="print-head"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM · 학생 제출 보고서</small><h1>${escapeHtml(r.reportGroup||r.programName||"활동 보고서")}</h1><p>${escapeHtml(`${student.studentNo} ${student.name} · ${r.submittedAt||""}`)}</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header><section class="print-section">${(r.responses||[]).map((v,i)=>v?`<div class="report-qa"><h3>Q${i+1}. ${escapeHtml(r.questions?.[i]||`${i+1}번 문항`)}</h3><div class="record-box">${escapeHtml(v)}</div></div>`:"").join("")}</section></article>`).join("");
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_전체보고서`,body);
}
function curriculumTermsForStudent(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.selectedSubjects||[]).filter(x=>studentMatches(x,student));
  return rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.term||a.semester||"").localeCompare(String(b.term||b.semester||"")));
}
function allCurriculumSubjects(){
  const rows=readonlyCache?.selectedSubjects||[];
  const map=new Map();
  rows.forEach(r=>{const key=`${r.term||r.semester}|${r.group||""}|${r.subject||r.courseName||r.name||""}`;if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,r);});
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.term||a.semester||"").localeCompare(String(b.term||b.semester||""))||String(a.subject||a.name||"").localeCompare(String(b.subject||b.name||""),"ko"));
}
function subjectPersonalOutputPanel(){
  const student=outputSelectedStudent(),chosen=student?curriculumTermsForStudent(student):[];
  const errors=(readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[]).filter(e=>student&&studentMatches(e,student));
  const byGroup={};chosen.forEach(r=>{const g=r.group||r.category||'선택묶음';(byGroup[g]??=[]).push(`${r.term||r.semester||''} · ${r.subject||r.courseName||r.name||''}`)});
  const preview=student?`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>진로희망 ${escapeHtml(student.career||'-')}</span></div><div class="preview-choice-groups">${Object.entries(byGroup).map(([g,items])=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(g)}</b>${items.map(x=>`<span>✓ ${escapeHtml(x)}</span>`).join('')}</article>`).join('')||'<p>선택과목 자료가 없습니다.</p>'}${errors.length?`<article class="warn"><b>선택 검토 ${errors.length}건</b><span>${escapeHtml(errors.map(e=>e.message||e.type).join(' · '))}</span></article>`:''}</div>`:'';
  return `${outputClassStudentPicker('1~9반을 선택한 뒤 학생을 골라 교육과정 선택 상담자료를 확인합니다.')}${student?outputPreviewShell(`${student.studentNo} ${student.name} · 선택과목`,`학교 교육과정 선택묶음 기준`,preview,`<button class="btn primary" data-output-subject-personal>선택과목 상담표 인쇄</button>`):''}`;
}
function curriculumTermOrder(term){
  const order=["2학년 1학기","2학년 2학기","3학년 1학기","3학년 2학기"];const i=order.indexOf(String(term));return i<0?99:i;
}
function printSubjectPersonal(student){
  if(!student)return;
  const chosen=curriculumTermsForStudent(student),selectedKeys=new Set(chosen.map(r=>`${r.term||r.semester}|${r.subject||r.courseName||r.name}`));
  const all=allCurriculumSubjects().filter(r=>curriculumTermOrder(r.term||r.semester)<99);
  const terms=["2학년 1학기","2학년 2학기","3학년 1학기","3학년 2학기"];
  const errors=(readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[]).filter(e=>studentMatches(e,student));
  const groups=[...new Set(all.map(r=>r.group||r.category||"선택과목"))];
  const matrixRows=groups.flatMap(group=>{
    const subjects=[...new Set(all.filter(r=>(r.group||r.category||"선택과목")===group).map(r=>r.subject||r.courseName||r.name||"").filter(Boolean))];
    return subjects.map((subject,index)=>`<tr><td>${index===0?escapeHtml(group):""}</td><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${terms.map(term=>{const exists=all.some(r=>(r.term||r.semester)===term&&(r.subject||r.courseName||r.name)===subject);const selected=selectedKeys.has(`${term}|${subject}`);return `<td class="${selected?"selected-course":exists?"offered-course":"not-offered"}">${selected?"● 선택":exists?"○ 개설":""}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`);
  }).join("");
  const chosenByTerm=terms.map(term=>`<div class="term-choice-card"><b>${term}</b><span>${chosen.filter(r=>(r.term||r.semester)===term).map(r=>escapeHtml(r.subject||r.courseName||r.name||"")).join(" · ")||"선택내역 없음"}</span></div>`).join("");
  const body=`<header class="print-head"><small>운호고등학교 2026학년도 입학생 교육과정 선택 상담자료</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||"-")}</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header>
  <section class="print-section"><div class="curriculum-legend"><span class="legend-selected">● 학생 선택</span><span>○ 학교 개설</span><span>학교 교육과정 편성표 기준</span></div><div class="term-choice-grid">${chosenByTerm}</div></section>
  <section class="print-section"><h2>선택 검토</h2><div class="record-box ${errors.length?"draft":"final"}">${errors.length?errors.map(e=>escapeHtml(e.message||e.type)).join("<br>"):"확인된 선택 오류가 없습니다."}</div></section>
  <section class="print-section"><h2>전체 교육과정 선택과목 편성표</h2><table class="curriculum-matrix"><thead><tr><th>선택영역</th><th>개설과목</th>${terms.map(t=>`<th>${t}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${matrixRows}</tbody></table></section>`;
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_선택과목`,body,"portrait");
}
function recordOutputPanel(){
  const student=outputSelectedStudent();
  const bundle=student?studentRecordBundle(student):null;
  let preview='';
  if(student&&bundle){
    preview=outputCenterState.recordPackage==='evidence'?`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>활동 ${bundle.activities.length}건 · 프로그램 ${bundle.programs.length}건 · 보고서 ${(bundle.reports||[]).length}건</span></div><div class="preview-list">${bundle.activities.slice(0,8).map(a=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(a.title||a.type||'활동')}</b><span>${escapeHtml([a.area,a.date,a.role].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span></article>`).join('')||'<p>활동 근거자료가 없습니다.</p>'}`:`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>교사용 작성본</span></div><div class="preview-list">${[['autonomy','자율활동'],['career','진로활동'],['behavior','종합의견']].map(([area,label])=>`<article><b>${label}</b><span>${escapeHtml((localStorage.getItem(recordDraftKey(student.id,area))||existingFinalRecord(bundle,area)?.text||'저장된 내용 없음').slice(0,160))}</span></article>`).join('')}</div>`;
  }
  return `${outputClassStudentPicker('반과 학생을 선택하면 실제 생활기록부 출력 내용을 미리 확인할 수 있습니다.')}<div class="output-choice-cards two"><button class="${outputCenterState.recordPackage==='evidence'?'active':''}" data-output-record-package="evidence"><b>활동 근거자료</b><small>활동·프로그램·보고서·SDGs</small></button><button class="${outputCenterState.recordPackage==='teacher'?'active':''}" data-output-record-package="teacher"><b>교사용 작성본</b><small>자율·진로·종합의견</small></button></div>${student?outputPreviewShell(`${student.studentNo} ${student.name} · 생활기록부`,outputCenterState.recordPackage==='evidence'?'활동 근거자료':'교사용 작성본',preview,`<button class="btn primary" data-output-record-print>생활기록부 자료 인쇄</button>`):''}`;
}
function printRecordPackage(student,type){
  if(!student)return;
  const bundle=studentRecordBundle(student);
  let body=`<header class="print-head"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM · 생활기록부 교사용 자료</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||"-")}</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header>`;
  if(type==="evidence"){
    const selectedPrograms=bundle.programs.filter(p=>p.kind==="selected");
    const reportCommon=bundle.programs.filter(p=>p.kind==="common"&&(bundle.reports||[]).some(r=>String(r.programId||"")===String(p.programId||p.id).replace(/^common-/,"")));
    body+=`<section class="print-section"><h2>학생 활동현황</h2><table><thead><tr><th>일자</th><th>영역</th><th>활동</th><th>역할·근거</th></tr></thead><tbody>${bundle.activities.map(a=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(a.date||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(a.area||a.type||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(a.title||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(a.role||a.evidence||a.note||"")}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section><section class="print-section"><h2>선택활동 참여 프로그램</h2>${selectedPrograms.map(p=>`<div class="record-box"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.title)}</b> ${escapeHtml(recordDateText(p))}<br>${escapeHtml(p.memo||"")}</div>`).join("")||"자료 없음"}</section><section class="print-section"><h2>보고서가 있는 공통활동</h2>${reportCommon.map(p=>`<div class="record-box"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.title)}</b> ${escapeHtml(recordDateText(p))}</div>`).join("")||"자료 없음"}</section><section class="print-section"><h2>SDGs 연계 성장 프로파일</h2>${bundle.programs.filter(p=>p.sdgs).map(p=>`<div class="record-box"><b>SDGs ${escapeHtml(p.sdgs)}</b> · ${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.title)}<br>${escapeHtml(p.sdgsEvidence||"")}</div>`).join("")||"확정된 SDGs 자료 없음"}</section>`;
  }else{
    const areas=[["autonomy","자율활동 특기사항"],["career","진로활동 특기사항"],["behavior","종합의견(행동발달 특기사항)"]];
    body+=areas.map(([area,label])=>{const draft=localStorage.getItem(recordDraftKey(student.id,area))||"";const final=existingFinalRecord(bundle,area);return `<section class="print-section"><h2>${label}</h2><h3>초안 · ${draft.length}자</h3><div class="record-box draft">${escapeHtml(draft||"저장된 초안 없음")}</div><h3>최종본 · ${(final?.text||"").length}자</h3><div class="record-box final">${escapeHtml(final?.text||"확정된 최종본 없음")}</div></section>`}).join("");
  }
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_생활기록부`,body);
}
function scoresOutputPanel(){
  const student=outputSelectedStudent();
  const mode=outputCenterState.scoreMode;
  let preview='';
  if(student){
    if(mode==='admission-summary'){
      const base=admissionBase(student.id);
      preview=`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>입시 종합</span></div><div class="preview-metrics"><span><small>내신 5등급 평균</small><b>${base?.avg5==null?'-':Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2)}</b></span><span><small>실제 9등급 참고</small><b>${base?.actual9==null?'-':Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2)}</b></span><span><small>진로희망</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.career||'-')}</b></span></div>`;
    }else{
      const all=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.studentId===student.id);const internal=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==='내신');const mock=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==='모의고사');
      preview=`<div class="preview-student-head"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><span>성적 종합</span></div><div class="preview-metrics"><span><small>내신 기록</small><b>${internal.length}</b></span><span><small>모의고사 기록</small><b>${mock.length}</b></span><span><small>진로희망</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.career||'-')}</b></span></div>`;
    }
  }
  return `${outputClassStudentPicker('반과 학생을 선택한 뒤 성적메뉴 또는 입시메뉴의 종합화면을 출력합니다.')}<div class="output-choice-cards two"><button class="${mode==='score-summary'?'active':''}" data-output-score-mode="score-summary"><b>성적 종합</b><small>성적 메뉴의 학생별 종합화면</small></button><button class="${mode==='admission-summary'?'active':''}" data-output-score-mode="admission-summary"><b>입시 종합</b><small>입시 메뉴의 학생별 종합화면</small></button></div>${student?outputPreviewShell(`${student.studentNo} ${student.name} · ${mode==='admission-summary'?'입시 종합':'성적 종합'}`,'출력 전 미리보기',preview,`<button class="btn primary" data-output-score-print>${mode==='admission-summary'?'입시 종합':'성적 종합'} 인쇄</button>`):''}`;
}
function printScorePackage(student,mode){
  if(!student)return;
  const all=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(r=>r.studentId===student.id);
  const internal=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="내신");
  const mock=all.filter(r=>r.scoreType==="모의고사");
  const gradeRows=internal.filter(r=>Number.isFinite(Number(r.level)));
  const printWeightedAverage=uepCommonWeightedAverage(gradeRows,r=>Number(r.level));
  const avg=Number.isFinite(printWeightedAverage)?printWeightedAverage.toFixed(2):"-";
  const mockExams=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const latestMock=mockExams.at(-1)||"-";
  const subjects=[...new Set(mock.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const summary=`<div class="score-print-summary"><div><small>최근 내신 평균</small><b>${avg}</b><span>등급 평균</span></div><div><small>내신 자료</small><b>${internal.length}</b><span>과목·시험 기록</span></div><div><small>최근 모의고사</small><b>${escapeHtml(latestMock)}</b><span>${mock.length}개 성적</span></div><div><small>조회범위</small><b>${mode==="combined"?"종합":mode==="mock"?"모의":"내신"}</b><span>개인별 누적</span></div></div>`;
  const internalExams=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b));
  const internalSubjects=[...new Set(internal.map(r=>r.subject).filter(Boolean))];
  const internalTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>내신 누적 성적</h2><table class="score-wide"><thead><tr><th>과목</th>${internalExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${internalSubjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${internalExams.map(exam=>{const r=internal.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · ${escapeHtml(r.achievement||"")}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const mockTable=`<section class="print-section"><h2>모의고사 비교</h2><table class="score-wide mock"><thead><tr><th>영역</th>${mockExams.map(e=>`<th>${escapeHtml(e)}<small>등급 / 원점수 / 표준</small></th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${subjects.map(subject=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(subject)}</td>${mockExams.map(exam=>{const r=mock.find(x=>x.subject===subject&&x.exam===exam);return `<td>${r?`<b>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.level))}등급</b><small>${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.rawScore))}점 · 표준 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.standardScore))}${r.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${escapeHtml(scoreDisplay(r.percentile))}`:""}</small>`:"-"}</td>`}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`;
  const body=`<header class="print-head score-title"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM · 학생상담 성적 종합</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||"-")} · 전체 누적 성적</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header>${summary}${mode!=="mock"?internalTable:""}${mode!=="internal"?mockTable:""}`;
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_성적상담자료`,body,"portrait");
}


function printAdmissionSummaryPackage(student){
  if(!student)return;
  const base=admissionBase(student.id);
  const body=`<header class="print-head score-title"><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM</small><h1>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(student.name)} · 입시 종합</h1><p>진로희망: ${escapeHtml(student.career||'-')}</p>${studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student)}</header><section class="print-section"><h2>입시 종합 요약</h2><div class="score-print-summary"><div><small>내신 5등급 평균</small><b>${base?.avg5==null?'-':Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2)}</b></div><div><small>실제 9등급 참고</small><b>${base?.actual9==null?'-':Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2)}</b></div><div><small>최근 모의</small><b>${escapeHtml(base?.mockExam||'-')}</b></div><div><small>관리 구분</small><b>${studentAdmissionPrintLabel(student)||'일반'}</b></div></div></section><section class="print-section"><h2>상담 메모</h2><div class="record-box">UEP 입시 메뉴의 종합 분석을 상담자료로 활용하세요.</div></section>`;
  openPrintDocument(`${student.studentNo}_${student.name}_입시종합`,body,'portrait');
}
function outputHubTabsMarkup(){
  return `<div class="uep-hub-tabs output-hub-tabs"><button class="${outputHubMode==="alerts"?"active":""}" data-output-hub="alerts"><b>알림</b><small>학생 안내·제출·출결</small></button><button class="${outputHubMode==="print"?"active":""}" data-output-hub="print"><b>출력</b><small>보고서·출석부·상담자료</small></button></div>`;
}
function communicationView() {
  const items=[
    ["01","학부모 안내","프로그램·학교일정·활동 결과를 학부모용 안내자료로 구성합니다."],
    ["02","가정통신문","UEP의 일정과 프로그램 정보를 바탕으로 가정통신문을 구성합니다."],
    ["03","학교 홍보","프로그램 운영성과와 활동사진을 학교 홍보자료로 연결합니다."],
    ["04","학교 사진함","학교전경·수업·진로·실험·행사·학생활동 사진을 분류해 활용합니다."]
  ];
  return `<section class="communication-hub"><div class="communication-hero"><small>COMMUNICATION & PROMOTION</small><h2>UEP의 교육활동을 학부모와 학교 밖으로 연결합니다.</h2><p>현재는 배포 기준 골격입니다. 실제 데이터 연결과 문서 생성 기능은 이후 업데이트에서 순차적으로 제공합니다.</p></div><div class="communication-grid">${items.map(([no,title,desc])=>`<article class="communication-card"><span>${no}</span><h3>${title}</h3><p>${desc}</p><em>준비 중 · 업데이트 예정</em></article>`).join("")}</div></section>`;
}

function outputsView() {
  const hub=outputHubTabsMarkup();
  if(outputHubMode==="alerts") return `<div class="module-page output-alert-hub">${hub}${studentAlertsView()}</div>`;
  if(outputCenterState.home){
    const cards=[
      ["reports","▤","보고서","학생 보고서","프로그램별·학생별 보고서를 선택하고 미리보기 후 출력합니다."],
      ["attendance","✓","출석부","프로그램 출결","선택활동·방과후학교 출석결과를 실제 출석부로 출력합니다."],
      ["subjects","◇","선택과목","교육과정 상담","반·학생을 선택해 교육과정 선택 상담자료를 확인합니다."],
      ["records","▦","생활기록부","활동·기록 근거","활동 근거자료와 교사용 작성본을 학생별로 확인합니다."],
      ["scores","◎","성적·입시","종합 상담자료","성적 종합과 입시 종합 화면을 상담자료로 출력합니다."],
    ];
    return `<div class="module-page output-standard-page">${hub}${outputStandardSection('UEP PRINT CENTER','출력','필요한 자료를 선택하고 결과를 확인한 뒤 인쇄·PDF·Excel로 저장합니다.','')}<div class="output-home-standard-grid output-print-five">${cards.map(([id,icon,title,eyebrow,desc])=>`<button class="output-home-standard-card" data-output-home-card="${id}"><span class="output-standard-icon">${icon}</span><div><small>${eyebrow}</small><h3>${title}</h3><p>${desc}</p></div><footer><em>열기 ›</em></footer></button>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
  }
  const titles={reports:"보고서",subjects:"선택과목",attendance:"출석부",records:"생활기록부",scores:"성적·입시"};
  let panel=reportsOutputPanel();
  if(outputCenterState.tab==="subjects")panel=subjectPersonalOutputPanel();
  else if(outputCenterState.tab==="attendance")panel=attendanceOutputPanel();
  else if(outputCenterState.tab==="records")panel=recordOutputPanel();
  else if(outputCenterState.tab==="scores")panel=scoresOutputPanel();
  return `<div class="module-page output-standard-page">${hub}<div class="output-section-head standard"><button class="btn secondary" data-output-home-back>← 출력</button><div><small>UEP PRINT CENTER</small><h2>${titles[outputCenterState.tab]||"출력자료"}</h2><p>위에서 선택하고 아래에서 결과를 확인합니다.</p></div></div>${panel}</div>`;
}

function selectedOutputReports(){
  const ids=outputCenterState.selected;
  return outputReportRows().filter(r=>ids.has(String(r.id)));
}
function printMultipleReports(reports){
  if(!reports.length)return toast("출력할 보고서를 선택하세요.");
  const popup=window.open("","_blank","width=1100,height=900");if(!popup)return toast("인쇄 창을 열 수 없습니다.");
  const pages=reports.map(report=>{const responses=(report.responses||[]).filter(v=>String(v||"").trim());return `<section class="report-page"><header><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM</small><h1>${escapeHtml(report.reportGroup||report.programName||"학생 보고서")}</h1></header><div class="meta"><span><b>학생</b>${escapeHtml(`${report.studentNo||""} ${report.studentName||report.name||""}`)}</span><span><b>최종 제출</b>${escapeHtml(reportSubmissionLabel(report))}</span><span><b>구분</b>${escapeHtml(report.kind||"-")}</span><span><b>매칭</b>${escapeHtml(report.matchStatus||"-")}</span></div>${responses.map((v,i)=>`<article><h3>${escapeHtml((report.questions?.[i]||reportQuestionLabel(i)))}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(v)}</p></article>`).join("")}</section>`;}).join("");
  popup.document.write(`<!doctype html><html lang="ko"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP_선택보고서_${reports.length}명</title><style>@page{size:A4 portrait;margin:13mm}*{box-sizing:border-box}body{font-family:"Malgun Gothic",sans-serif;color:#233b4d;margin:0}.report-page{page-break-after:always}.report-page:last-child{page-break-after:auto}header{border-bottom:3px solid #65b7a8;padding-bottom:7mm;margin-bottom:6mm}h1{font-size:19pt;margin:2mm 0}.meta{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:2mm 7mm;background:#f4f8fa;padding:4mm;margin-bottom:5mm}article{border:1px solid #d4e1e7;border-radius:3mm;padding:4mm;margin-bottom:3mm;break-inside:avoid}article h3{font-size:10.5pt;color:#178e7c;margin:0 0 2mm}article p{white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.55;margin:0;font-size:9.5pt}</style></head><body>${pages}<script>window.onload=()=>window.print();<\/script></body></html>`);popup.document.close();
}
function exportReportStatusExcel(reports){
  const rows=[["반","학번","이름","보고서그룹","프로그램명","구분","제출일시","매칭상태"],...reports.map(r=>[r.classNo||"",r.studentNo||"",r.studentName||r.name||"",r.reportGroup||"",r.programName||"",r.kind||"",r.submittedAt||"",r.matchStatus||""])];
  const html=`<table>${rows.map(row=>`<tr>${row.map(c=>`<td>${xmlEscape(c)}</td>`).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</table>`;const blob=new Blob([`\ufeff<html><meta charset="utf-8"><body>${html}</body></html>`],{type:"application/vnd.ms-excel;charset=utf-8"});const a=document.createElement("a");a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download="UEP_보고서_제출현황.xls";a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000);
}


function selectionOutputRows(){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const errors=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[];
  const subjects=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjects||readonlyCache?.studentSubjects||[];
  return students.map(student=>{
    const studentErrors=errors.filter(error=>
      String(error.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"")
      || String(error.studentNo||"")===String(student.studentNo||"")
    );
    const studentSubjects=subjects.filter(row=>
      String(row.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"")
      || String(row.studentNo||"")===String(student.studentNo||"")
    );
    return {student,errors:studentErrors,subjects:studentSubjects,status:studentErrors.length?"상담 필요":"정상"};
  }).filter(row=>{
    if(outputCenterState.errorFilter==="error")return row.errors.length>0;
    if(outputCenterState.errorFilter==="normal")return row.errors.length===0;
    return true;
  });
}
function printSelectionCounseling(){
  const rows=selectionOutputRows();
  if(!rows.length)return toast("출력할 학생이 없습니다.");
  const popup=window.open("","_blank","width=1150,height=900");
  if(!popup)return toast("인쇄 창을 열 수 없습니다.");
  const pages=rows.map(({student,errors,subjects})=>{
    const grouped={};
    subjects.forEach(item=>{
      const term=String(item.term||item.semester||item["이수학기"]||"학기 미확인");
      if(!grouped[term])grouped[term]=[];
      grouped[term].push(item);
    });
    const subjectHtml=Object.entries(grouped).map(([term,list])=>`
      <section><h3>${escapeHtml(term)}</h3>
      <table><thead><tr><th>과목명</th><th>계열</th><th>분류</th><th>상태</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>${list.map(item=>`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(item.subject||item.courseName||item.name||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(item.track||item.desiredTrack||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(item.category||item.area||"")}</td><td>${escapeHtml(item.status||"")}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></section>`).join("");
    return `<article class="page">
      <header><small>운호고등학교 UEP 선택과목 상담표</small><h1>${escapeHtml(`${student.studentNo||""} ${student.name||""}`)}</h1></header>
      <div class="meta"><span><b>반</b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</span><span><b>상태</b>${errors.length?"상담 필요":"정상"}</span></div>
      <section class="errors"><h3>오류·상담 내용</h3>${errors.length?`<ul>${errors.map(e=>`<li>${escapeHtml(e.message||e.type||"확인 필요")}</li>`).join("")}</ul>`:"<p>확인된 오류가 없습니다.</p>"}</section>
      ${subjectHtml||"<p>연결된 선택과목 신청내역이 없습니다.</p>"}
    </article>`;
  }).join("");
  popup.document.write(`<!doctype html><html lang="ko"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP_선택과목_상담표</title><style>
  @page{size:A4 landscape;margin:12mm}*{box-sizing:border-box}body{font-family:"Malgun Gothic",sans-serif;color:#203b4c;margin:0}.page{page-break-after:always}.page:last-child{page-break-after:auto}header{border-bottom:3px solid #64b7a8;padding-bottom:5mm;margin-bottom:5mm}h1{font-size:19pt;margin:2mm 0}.meta{display:flex;gap:12mm;background:#f1f7f9;padding:4mm;margin-bottom:5mm}section{margin-bottom:5mm}h3{font-size:12pt;color:#168e7b;margin:0 0 2mm}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:9pt}th,td{border:1px solid #cbdce4;padding:2.5mm;text-align:left}th{background:#eaf4f6}.errors{border:1px solid #efd6a5;background:#fff9ed;padding:4mm}</style></head><body>${pages}<script>window.onload=()=>window.print();<\/script></body></html>`);
  popup.document.close();
}
function exportSelectionSubjectsExcel(){
  const rows=selectionOutputRows();
  if(!rows.length)return toast("저장할 학생이 없습니다.");
  const table=[["반","학번","이름","상태","오류내용","학기","과목명","계열","분류"]];
  rows.forEach(({student,errors,subjects})=>{
    if(subjects.length){
      subjects.forEach(item=>table.push([
        student.className||"",student.studentNo||"",student.name||"",
        errors.length?"상담 필요":"정상",
        errors.map(e=>e.message||e.type).join(" · "),
        item.term||item.semester||item["이수학기"]||"",
        item.subject||item.courseName||item.name||"",
        item.track||item.desiredTrack||"",
        item.category||item.area||""
      ]));
    }else{
      table.push([student.className||"",student.studentNo||"",student.name||"",errors.length?"상담 필요":"정상",errors.map(e=>e.message||e.type).join(" · "),"","","",""]);
    }
  });
  const html=`<table>${table.map(row=>`<tr>${row.map(cell=>`<td>${xmlEscape(cell)}</td>`).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</table>`;
  const blob=new Blob([`\ufeff<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"></head><body>${html}</body></html>`],{type:"application/vnd.ms-excel;charset=utf-8"});
  const a=document.createElement("a");
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download="UEP_선택과목_신청내역.xls";
  a.click();
  setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1000);
}

const UEP_ROLES=[
  {id:"admin",name:"관리자",scope:"전체 설정·자료연결·권한·업데이트·감사로그"},
  {id:"grade_head",name:"학년부장",scope:"해당 학년 전체 학생·프로그램·출결·성적·생활기록부"},
  {id:"grade_manager",name:"학년총무",scope:"해당 학년 업무공지·프로그램·출결·자료정리"},
  {id:"homeroom",name:"담임교사",scope:"학년 학생자료 조회 · 담임반 업무수정 · 타반 개인정보 보호"},
  {id:"subject",name:"교과담당",scope:"담당 교과·수업·선택과목·프로그램 출석"},
  {id:"vice_principal",name:"교감",scope:"학교 전체 현황 열람·승인·감사 확인"},
  {id:"principal",name:"교장",scope:"학교 전체 요약 현황·통계·최종 승인 열람"}
];

function uep08125ValidTeacherName(value){
  const v=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').trim();
  if(!v)return '';
  if(/^(?:\?+|사용자|담당교사|unknown|null|undefined)$/i.test(v))return '';
  return v;
}
function uep08125PickTeacherName(...values){for(const value of values){const v=uep08125ValidTeacherName(value);if(v)return v;}return '';}

function currentUserProfile(){
  const raw=state?.settings?.userProfile||{}, session=state?.auth?.user||{}, current=state?.currentUser||{}, remembered=state?.auth?.rememberedUser||{};
  return {
    id:String(session.id||raw.id||""),
    email:String(session.email||current.email||remembered.email||raw.email||""),
    name:uep08125PickTeacherName(session.name,current.name,remembered.name,raw.name,state?.settings?.loginUserName,state?.settings?.teacherName),
    primaryRole:String(session.primaryRole||raw.primaryRole||state?.settings?.currentRole||"admin"),
    secondaryRole:String(session.secondaryRole||raw.secondaryRole||""),
    grade:String(session.grade||raw.grade||state?.settings?.neis?.grade||"1"),
    classNo:String(session.classNo||raw.classNo||""),
    department:String(session.department||raw.department||"1학년부")
  };
}
function currentRoleId(){return currentUserProfile().primaryRole||"admin";}
function roleName(id){return UEP_ROLES.find(role=>role.id===id)?.name||"관리자";}
function currentRoleDisplay(){
  const p=currentUserProfile();
  if(p.primaryRole==="admin"&&p.secondaryRole==="grade_head") return "관리자 · 학년부장";
  return roleName(p.primaryRole);
}
function refreshHeaderRoleBadge(){
  const roleChip=document.getElementById("headerRoleChip");
  const versionChip=document.getElementById("headerVersionChip");
  const profileName=document.querySelector(".profile b");
  const profileMeta=document.querySelector(".profile small");
  const profileAvatar=document.querySelector(".profile > div");
  const p=currentUserProfile();
  const role=currentRoleId();
  if(roleChip){
    roleChip.textContent=currentRoleDisplay();
    roleChip.dataset.role=role;
  }
  if(versionChip)versionChip.textContent=`v${APP_VERSION}`;
  const displayTeacherName=p.name||currentLoginTeacherName()||"사용자"; if(profileName) profileName.textContent=displayTeacherName;
  if(profileMeta) profileMeta.textContent=[p.department,currentRoleDisplay()].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
  if(profileAvatar) profileAvatar.textContent=(displayTeacherName||"U").slice(0,1);
  const profileBox=document.querySelector('.profile');
  if(profileBox){profileBox.setAttribute('role','button');profileBox.setAttribute('tabindex','0');profileBox.title='계정 메뉴';}
}

let authSessionReady=false;
function roleFromAccount(account){
  const role=String(account?.role||'').trim(), admin=String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y';
  if(admin)return {primaryRole:'admin',secondaryRole:/학년부장/.test(role)?'grade_head':''};
  if(/학년부장/.test(role))return {primaryRole:'grade_head',secondaryRole:''};
  if(/학년총무/.test(role))return {primaryRole:'grade_manager',secondaryRole:'homeroom'};
  if(/담임/.test(role))return {primaryRole:'homeroom',secondaryRole:''};
  if(/교감/.test(role))return {primaryRole:'vice_principal',secondaryRole:''};
  if(/교장/.test(role))return {primaryRole:'principal',secondaryRole:''};
  return {primaryRole:'subject',secondaryRole:''};
}
function accountToSession(account){
  const roles=roleFromAccount(account);
  return {id:String(account.id||''),email:String(account.email||'').trim(),name:String(account.name||'').trim(),grade:String(account.grade||'1'),classNo:String(account.classNo||''),department:String(account.department||(`${account.grade||1}학년부`)),...roles};
}

function activeUserAccounts(){
  const sheetAccounts=(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[]).filter(a=>a.active!==false&&String(a.activeRaw||'Y').toUpperCase()!=='N');
  // 정상 동기화된 계정표가 있으면 반드시 시트 값을 우선합니다.
  if(sheetAccounts.length) return sheetAccounts;
  // 신규 PC/초기 동기화 지연 때 로그인 화면이 막히지 않도록 배포 당시 승인 계정만 fallback으로 사용합니다.
  return UEP_DISTRIBUTION_USER_FALLBACK.filter(a=>a.active!==false&&String(a.activeRaw||'Y').toUpperCase()!=='N');
}
const UEP_BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME='홍석민';
function bootstrapAdminAccount(name,email){
  const n=String(name||'').trim(),e=String(email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  if(n!==UEP_BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME||!e||!e.includes('@'))return null;
  const known=(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[]).find(a=>String(a.name||'').trim()===UEP_BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME);
  return {id:String(known?.id||'bootstrap-admin'),name:UEP_BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME,email:e,grade:String(known?.grade||'1'),classNo:String(known?.classNo||''),department:String(known?.department||'1학년부'),role:String(known?.role||'학년부장'),admin:'Y',active:true,activeRaw:'Y',bootstrap:true};
}
function findUserAccount(name,email){
  const n=String(name||'').trim(),e=String(email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const active=activeUserAccounts();
  const byEmail=active.find(a=>String(a.email||'').trim().toLowerCase()===e);
  if(byEmail)return byEmail;
  const exact=active.find(a=>String(a.name||'').trim()===n&&String(a.email||'').trim().toLowerCase()===e);
  if(exact)return exact;
  return bootstrapAdminAccount(n,e);
}
function userAuthFailureMessage(name,email){
  const n=String(name||'').trim(),e=String(email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const all=readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[];
  const byEmail=all.find(a=>String(a.email||'').trim().toLowerCase()===e);
  if(byEmail&&(byEmail.active===false||String(byEmail.activeRaw||'Y').toUpperCase()==='N'))return '사용 중지된 계정입니다. 관리자에게 사용 승인을 요청해 주세요.';
  if(!all.length)return '사용자계정 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다. 데이터 연결 상태를 확인해 주세요.';
  if(n===UEP_BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME)return '최고관리자 확인에 실패했습니다. 이메일 형식을 확인한 뒤 다시 시도해 주세요.';
  return '등록되거나 승인된 교사 이메일을 확인해 주세요.';
}
function maskEmail(email){const [local,domain='']=String(email||'').split('@');if(!local)return '';return `${local.slice(0,1)}${'*'.repeat(Math.max(3,local.length-1))}${domain?'@'+domain:''}`;}
function hideUserAuthGate(){document.getElementById('userAuthGate')?.classList.add('hidden');document.body.classList.remove('uep-auth-locked');authSessionReady=true;state.auth.locked=false;refreshHeaderRoleBadge();}
function authGateMessage(text){const node=document.getElementById('userAuthMessage');if(node)node.textContent=text||'';}
function renderUserAuthGate({locked=false,switchUser=false}={}){
  const gate=document.getElementById('userAuthGate');if(!gate)return;document.body.classList.add('uep-auth-locked');gate.classList.remove('hidden');authSessionReady=false;
  const remembered=state?.auth?.rememberedUser||state?.auth?.user;const canContinue=remembered&&uep08125ValidTeacherName(remembered.name)&&remembered.email&&!switchUser;
  gate.innerHTML=`<section class="user-auth-card"><div class="user-auth-brand"><span>U</span><div><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM</small><h2>${locked?'UEP 화면 잠금':'사용자 확인'}</h2><p>${locked?'학생정보 보호를 위해 현재 화면을 잠갔습니다.':'학교에 등록된 이메일을 기준으로 사용자를 확인합니다. 최고관리자는 신규 PC에서도 안전하게 진입할 수 있습니다.'}</p></div></div>${canContinue?`<button class="user-auth-remembered" id="rememberedUserContinue"><b>${escapeHtml(remembered.name)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(maskEmail(remembered.email))}</span><small>${locked?'계속 사용':'자동 로그인 사용자'}</small></button><button class="user-auth-text" id="useDifferentUser">다른 사용자로 로그인</button>`:`<form id="userAuthForm"><label>교사 이름<input id="userAuthName" autocomplete="name" required placeholder="예: 홍석민"></label><label>교사 이메일<input id="userAuthEmail" type="email" autocomplete="email" required placeholder="학교에 등록된 이메일"></label><label class="user-auth-check"><input id="userAuthRemember" type="checkbox"> 이 PC에서 자동 로그인</label><p id="userAuthMessage" class="user-auth-message"></p><button class="btn primary" type="submit">UEP 시작</button></form>`}<div class="user-auth-exit-row"><button type="button" class="user-auth-text danger" id="userAuthExit">UEP 종료</button></div><footer>PC 인증과 사용자 확인은 별도입니다. 상담 메모와 개인 권한은 현재 로그인 교사를 기준으로 표시됩니다.</footer></section>`;
  document.getElementById('useDifferentUser')?.addEventListener('click',()=>renderUserAuthGate({locked:false,switchUser:true}));
  document.getElementById('userAuthExit')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(confirm('UEP를 종료할까요?'))await window.schoolBoard?.close?.();});
  document.getElementById('rememberedUserContinue')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{const status=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});if(!status?.authenticated)return renderUserAuthGate({switchUser:true});const a=status.user||{},account={id:String(a.userId||remembered.id||''),email:String(a.email||remembered.email||'').trim(),name:uep08125PickTeacherName(a.name,remembered.name),role:String(a.role||''),admin:a.isAdmin?'Y':'N',grade:String(a.grade||'1'),classNo:String(a.homeroom||''),department:String(a.department||((a.grade||1)+'학년부')),active:true,activeRaw:'Y'};state.auth.user=accountToSession(account);googleConnectionStatus={ok:true,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};await save();hideUserAuthGate();refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:false}).catch(()=>{});navigate(state.activePage||'dashboard');});
  /* UEP_08250_STAGED_DATA_LOADING */
const UEP_08250_LOAD={core:'ready',academic:'waiting',admission:'waiting',running:false};
function uep08250Emit(){try{window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('uep:data-stage',{detail:{...UEP_08250_LOAD}}));}catch{}}
async function uep08250RefreshStage(stage){
  if(UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]==='loading')return; UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]='loading';uep08250Emit();
  try{const r=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.();if(r?.ok){const c=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(c?.ok&&c.data){readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(c.data);googleConnectionError='';}}else if(r)googleConnectionError=r.reason||'';UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]='ready';}
  catch(e){UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]='error';googleConnectionError=e?.message||String(e);}uep08250Emit();
}
async function uep08250StartBackgroundStages(){if(UEP_08250_LOAD.running)return;UEP_08250_LOAD.running=true;uep08250Emit();try{await uep08250RefreshStage('academic');await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,250));await uep08250RefreshStage('admission');}finally{UEP_08250_LOAD.running=false;uep08250Emit();}}
async function uep08250PrioritizeAdmission(){if(UEP_08250_LOAD.admission==='ready')return true;await uep08250RefreshStage('admission');return UEP_08250_LOAD.admission==='ready';}
document.getElementById('userAuthForm')?.addEventListener('submit',async event=>{event.preventDefault();const name=document.getElementById('userAuthName')?.value,email=document.getElementById('userAuthEmail')?.value;const submit=event.submitter||document.querySelector('#userAuthForm button[type="submit"]');if(submit){submit.disabled=true;submit.textContent='확인 중…';}try{if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin)throw new Error('이 UEP 빌드에는 School Read API 로그인이 없습니다.');const result=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadLogin({name,email});if(!result?.ok){authGateMessage(result?.message||'UEP 로그인에 실패했습니다.');return;}const a=result.user||{};const account={id:String(a.userId||''),email:String(a.email||email||'').trim(),name:uep08125PickTeacherName(a.name,name),role:String(a.role||''),admin:a.isAdmin?'Y':'N',grade:String(a.grade||'1'),classNo:String(a.homeroom||''),department:String(a.department||((a.grade||1)+'학년부')),active:true,activeRaw:'Y'};const user=accountToSession(account),remember=Boolean(document.getElementById('userAuthRemember')?.checked);state.auth.user=user;state.auth.rememberUser=remember;state.auth.rememberedUser=remember?{id:user.id,name:user.name,email:user.email}:null;state.settings.userProfile={...state.settings.userProfile,...user};state.settings.loginUserName=user.name;state.settings.teacherName=user.name;googleConnectionStatus={ok:true,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};await save();hideUserAuthGate();const cached=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(cached?.ok&&cached.data){readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);googleConnectionError='';}startReadonlyAutoRefresh();navigate(state.activePage||'dashboard');setTimeout(()=>uep08250StartBackgroundStages(),80);}catch(error){authGateMessage(error?.message||'UEP 로그인에 실패했습니다.');}finally{if(submit){submit.disabled=false;submit.textContent='UEP 시작';}}});
}
async function restoreRememberedSessionImmediately(){
  const remembered=state?.auth?.rememberUser?state?.auth?.rememberedUser:null;
  const savedUser=state?.auth?.user;
  if(!uep08125ValidTeacherName(remembered?.name)||!remembered?.email||!uep08125ValidTeacherName(savedUser?.name)||!savedUser?.email)return false;
  const sameName=String(savedUser.name).trim()===String(remembered.name).trim();
  const sameEmail=String(savedUser.email).trim().toLowerCase()===String(remembered.email).trim().toLowerCase();
  if(!sameName||!sameEmail)return false;
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus)return false;
  const status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});
  if(!status?.authenticated)return false;
  const a=status.user||{};const account={id:String(a.userId||remembered?.id||savedUser?.id||''),email:String(a.email||remembered?.email||savedUser?.email||'').trim(),name:uep08125PickTeacherName(a.name,remembered?.name,savedUser?.name),role:String(a.role||''),admin:a.isAdmin?'Y':'N',grade:String(a.grade||'1'),classNo:String(a.homeroom||''),department:String(a.department||((a.grade||1)+'학년부')),active:true,activeRaw:'Y'};state.auth.user=accountToSession(account);state.settings.userProfile={...state.settings.userProfile,...state.auth.user};state.settings.loginUserName=state.auth.user.name;state.settings.teacherName=state.auth.user.name;googleConnectionStatus={ok:true,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};
  const gate=document.getElementById('userAuthGate');
  if(gate){
    document.body.classList.add('uep-auth-locked');gate.classList.remove('hidden');
    gate.innerHTML=`<section class="user-auth-card auto-login-transition"><div class="user-auth-brand"><span>U</span><div><small>UNHO EDUCATION PLATFORM</small><h2>자동 로그인 중…</h2><p>${escapeHtml(state.auth.user.name)} · ${escapeHtml(maskEmail(state.auth.user.email))}</p></div></div><div class="auto-login-progress"><i></i></div><footer>승인된 UEP 세션을 확인했습니다.</footer></section>`;
    await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,220));
  }
  await save();
  hideUserAuthGate();
  return true;
}
async function initializeUserSessionGate(){
  const remembered=state?.auth?.rememberUser?state?.auth?.rememberedUser:null;
  const savedUser=state?.auth?.user;
  if(uep08125ValidTeacherName(remembered?.name)&&remembered?.email&&window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus){
    try{
      const status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});
      if(status?.authenticated){
        const a=status.user||{};const account={id:String(a.userId||remembered?.id||savedUser?.id||''),email:String(a.email||remembered?.email||savedUser?.email||'').trim(),name:uep08125PickTeacherName(a.name,remembered?.name,savedUser?.name),role:String(a.role||''),admin:a.isAdmin?'Y':'N',grade:String(a.grade||'1'),classNo:String(a.homeroom||''),department:String(a.department||((a.grade||1)+'학년부')),active:true,activeRaw:'Y'};state.auth.user=accountToSession(account);state.settings.userProfile={...state.settings.userProfile,...state.auth.user};state.settings.loginUserName=state.auth.user.name;state.settings.teacherName=state.auth.user.name;googleConnectionStatus={ok:true,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};
        await save();
        hideUserAuthGate();
        return true;
      }
    }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP] 저장된 School Read 세션 확인 실패',error);}
  }
  state.auth.user=null;
  authSessionReady=false;
  state.auth.locked=true;
  renderUserAuthGate({switchUser:true});
  authGateMessage('교사 이름과 이메일로 UEP를 시작하세요.');
  return false;
}
async function lockCurrentUser(){state.auth.locked=true;await save();closeAccountPopover();renderUserAuthGate({locked:true});}
async function switchCurrentUser(){await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogout?.();googleConnectionStatus={ok:false,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};state.auth.user=null;state.auth.locked=true;await save();closeAccountPopover();renderUserAuthGate({switchUser:true});}
async function logoutCurrentUser(){await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogout?.();googleConnectionStatus={ok:false,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};state.auth.user=null;state.auth.rememberUser=false;state.auth.rememberedUser=null;state.auth.locked=true;await save();closeAccountPopover();renderUserAuthGate({switchUser:true});}
function closeAccountPopover(){document.querySelector('.account-popover')?.remove();}
function openAccountPopover(){
  closeAccountPopover();const p=currentUserProfile(),profile=document.querySelector('.profile');if(!profile||!authSessionReady)return;const pop=document.createElement('div');pop.className='account-popover';pop.innerHTML=`<div class="account-popover-head"><span>${escapeHtml((p.name||'U').slice(0,1))}</span><div><b>${escapeHtml(p.name||'사용자')}</b><small>${escapeHtml([p.department,currentRoleDisplay()].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small><em>${escapeHtml(maskEmail(p.email))}</em></div></div><button data-account-lock>화면 잠금<small>현재 화면을 가립니다</small></button><button data-account-switch>다른 사용자로 전환<small>교사명·이메일 다시 입력</small></button><button class="danger" data-account-logout>로그아웃<small>자동 로그인도 해제</small></button>`;document.body.appendChild(pop);const r=profile.getBoundingClientRect();pop.style.left=`${Math.max(12,r.left)}px`;pop.style.bottom=`${Math.max(12,window.innerHeight-r.top+8)}px`;pop.querySelector('[data-account-lock]').onclick=lockCurrentUser;pop.querySelector('[data-account-switch]').onclick=switchCurrentUser;pop.querySelector('[data-account-logout]').onclick=logoutCurrentUser;setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener('pointerdown',accountPopoverOutside,{once:true}),0);
}
function accountPopoverOutside(event){if(!event.target.closest('.account-popover')&&!event.target.closest('.profile'))closeAccountPopover();}

function setupWizardScopeMarkup(){
  const p=currentUserProfile();
  const isHomeroom=p.primaryRole==="homeroom"||p.secondaryRole==="homeroom";
  const isGradeWide=p.primaryRole==="admin"||p.primaryRole==="grade_head"||p.secondaryRole==="grade_head";
  const classMarkup=isHomeroom?`<label>담임 반<select id="wizardHomeClass"><option value="">반 선택</option>${Array.from({length:14},(_,i)=>i+1).map(v=>`<option value="${v}" ${String(v)===p.classNo?'selected':''}>${v}반</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:`<div class="setup-scope-readonly"><small>담당 반</small><b>${isGradeWide?'반 없음 · 학년 전체':'담임 아님'}</b><span>현재 역할에서는 담임반을 지정하지 않습니다.</span></div>`;
  return `<div class="setup-profile-grid">
    <label>학교<input id="wizardSchoolName" value="${escapeHtml(state.settings.neis?.schoolName||'운호고등학교')}" readonly></label>
    <label>학년도<input id="wizardSchoolYear" value="2026" readonly></label>
    <label>담당 학년<select id="wizardGrade">${[1,2,3].map(v=>`<option value="${v}" ${String(v)===p.grade?'selected':''}>${v}학년</option>`).join('')}</select></label>
    ${classMarkup}
    <label class="wide">부서<input id="wizardDepartment" value="${escapeHtml(p.department)}" placeholder="예: 1학년부"></label>
  </div>`;
}

  <div class="setup-wizard-actions">
    <button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-wizard-google>${credentialReady?'서비스 계정 JSON 변경':'서비스 계정 JSON 등록'}</button>
    <button class="btn primary" type="button" data-wizard-sync>지금 새로고침</button>

}
function setupWizardNeisMarkup(){
  const p=currentUserProfile();

  const classOptions=`<option value="">${isHomeroom?'반 선택':'반 선택 안 함'}</option>`+Array.from({length:14},(_,i)=>i+1).map(v=>`<option value="${v}" ${String(v)===classValue?'selected':''}>${v}</option>`).join('');
  if(setupWizardPreview==="timetable"){
    const previewTitle=isHomeroom?"담임반 시간표 미리보기":"내 교사시간표 미리보기";

  }
  return `<div class="setup-neis-form">
    <label>학교명<input id="wizardNeisSchool" value="${escapeHtml(state.settings.neis.schoolName||'운호고등학교')}"></label>

  </div>
  <div class="setup-status-grid"><span><b>현재 상태</b>${neisData?.ok?(neisData.offline?'마지막 저장자료':'NEIS 실시간 연결'):'조회 전'}</span><span><b>사용 기준</b>${isHomeroom?'담임반':'1학년 전체 · 반 없음'}</span><span><b>API 키</b>기본 불필요</span></div>
  <div class="setup-wizard-actions"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-wizard-neis-load>선택한 반 시간표 가져오기</button><button class="btn secondary" type="button" data-wizard-neis-open>마법사 안에서 시간표 확인</button></div>`;
}

function saveWizardCurrentStep(){
  const p=currentUserProfile();
  if(setupWizardStep===1){
    const name=String($("#wizardUserName")?.value||p.name||"").trim();
    let primaryRole=String($("#wizardRole")?.value||p.primaryRole||"admin");
    let secondaryRole=String($("#wizardSecondaryRole")?.value||"");
    if(name==="홍석민"){ primaryRole="admin"; secondaryRole="grade_head"; }
    state.settings.userProfile={...p,name,primaryRole,secondaryRole};
    state.settings.currentRole=primaryRole;
    state.settings.loginUserName=name;
    state.settings.teacherName=name;
  }
  if(setupWizardStep===2){
    const grade=String($("#wizardGrade")?.value||p.grade||"1");
    const activeProfile=currentUserProfile();
    const isHomeroom=activeProfile.primaryRole==="homeroom"||activeProfile.secondaryRole==="homeroom";
    const classNo=isHomeroom?String($("#wizardHomeClass")?.value||""):"";
    const department=String($("#wizardDepartment")?.value||p.department||"").trim();
    state.settings.userProfile={...activeProfile,grade,classNo,department};
    state.settings.grade=grade;
    state.settings.neis={...state.settings.neis,grade,...(classNo?{classNo}:{})};
  }
  if(setupWizardStep===5){
    $$("[data-wizard-widget]").forEach(input=>{
      const key=input.dataset.wizardWidget;
      state.settings.widgetSettings[key]={...(state.settings.widgetSettings[key]||{}),visible:Boolean(input.checked)};
    });
  }
  save();
  refreshHeaderRoleBadge();
  try {
    const bootContent=document.getElementById('pageContent');
    if(bootContent && !String(bootContent.innerHTML||'').trim()){
      state.activePage='dashboard';
      navigate('dashboard');
    }
  } catch (bootRenderError) {
    console.error('[UEP] 부팅 대시보드 강제 복구 실패',bootRenderError);
  }
}

function setSetupWizardLock(active){
  setupWizardActive=Boolean(active);
  document.querySelector('.app')?.classList.toggle('setup-wizard-active',setupWizardActive);
  document.body.classList.toggle('setup-wizard-locked',setupWizardActive);
  if(setupWizardActive){
    closeDrawer();
    document.getElementById('modalBackdrop')?.classList.add('hidden');
  }
}
function openSetupWizard(step=0){
  setupWizardStep=Math.max(0,Math.min(SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS.length-1,Number(step)||0));
  setupWizardPreview="";
  setSetupWizardLock(true);
  renderSetupWizard();
}
function renderSetupWizard(){
  if(!setupWizardActive) return;
  const item=SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS[setupWizardStep];
  const pageContent=$("#pageContent");
  if(!pageContent) return;
  $("#pageKicker").textContent="UEP FIRST SETUP · 승인 전 보호 모드";
  $("#pageTitle").textContent="초기 설정 마법사";
  $(".page-title").classList.remove("dashboard-title-hidden");
  $("#pageActions").classList.add("hidden");
  const progress=SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS.map((_,i)=>`<span class="${i<setupWizardStep?'done':i===setupWizardStep?'active':''}">${i<setupWizardStep?'✓':i+1}</span>`).join('<i></i>');
  let extra="";
  if(setupWizardStep===1) extra=setupWizardProfileMarkup();
  if(setupWizardStep===2) extra=setupWizardScopeMarkup();
  if(setupWizardStep===3) extra=setupWizardConnectionMarkup();
  if(setupWizardStep===4) extra=setupWizardNeisMarkup();
  if(setupWizardStep===5) extra=setupWizardWidgetsMarkup();
  if(setupWizardStep===6) extra=setupWizardEnvironmentMarkup();
  if(setupWizardStep===7) extra=setupWizardFinishMarkup();
  pageContent.innerHTML=`<div class="setup-main-shell setup-wizard-shell setup-wizard-v07639"><header class="setup-main-header"><div><small>UEP SECURE ONBOARDING</small><h2>처음부터 끝까지 이 화면 안에서만 설정합니다</h2><p>설정이 완료되기 전에는 학생정보·성적·출결·생활기록부 등 다른 UEP 화면으로 이동할 수 없습니다.</p></div><span>STEP ${setupWizardStep+1} / ${SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS.length}</span></header><div class="setup-wizard-progress">${progress}</div><section class="setup-wizard-stage ${setupWizardPreview?'preview-mode':''}"><div class="setup-wizard-icon">${item.icon}</div><small>STEP ${setupWizardStep+1} / ${SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS.length}</small><h2>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</h2><p>${escapeHtml(item.desc)}</p><div class="setup-wizard-summary">${escapeHtml(item.body)}</div>${extra}</section><footer><button class="btn secondary" data-setup-prev ${setupWizardStep===0?'disabled':''}>이전</button>${setupWizardStep<SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS.length-1?'<button class="btn primary" data-setup-next>다음</button>':'<button class="btn primary" data-setup-finish>설정 저장 후 승인 단계로</button>'}</footer></div>`;

  $("[data-wizard-hong]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    $("#wizardUserName").value="홍석민";
    $("#wizardRole").value="admin";
    $("#wizardSecondaryRole").value="grade_head";
  });
  $("[data-wizard-google]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{await selectGoogleCredential();renderSetupWizard();});
  $("[data-wizard-sync]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{const button=$("[data-wizard-sync]");if(button){button.disabled=true;button.textContent="새로고침 중…";}const result=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.();if(result?.ok){const cached=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(cached?.ok){readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);googleConnectionError="";}}else if(result){googleConnectionError=result.reason||"학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";}renderSetupWizard();});
  $("[data-wizard-neis-load]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{
    const school=$("#wizardNeisSchool")?.value.trim()||"운호고등학교";
    const grade=$("#wizardNeisGrade")?.value||currentUserProfile().grade||"1";
    const classNo=$("#wizardNeisClass")?.value||"";
    const date=$("#wizardNeisDate")?.value||dateKey(today);
    if(!classNo) return toast("학년부장·관리자는 반 선택이 필수가 아닙니다. 시간표 미리보기가 필요할 때만 반을 선택하세요.");
    state.settings.neis={...state.settings.neis,schoolName:school,grade,classNo,date};
    save();
    const button=$("[data-wizard-neis-load]");
    if(button){button.disabled=true;button.textContent="NEIS 조회 중…";}
    await loadNeisData(true,date);
    renderSetupWizard();
    toast(neisData?.ok?"마법사 내부 미리보기용 시간표를 가져왔습니다.":"NEIS 조회 결과를 확인하세요.");
  });
  $("[data-wizard-neis-open]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const date=$("#wizardNeisDate")?.value||dateKey(today);
    const grade=$("#wizardNeisGrade")?.value||currentUserProfile().grade||"1";
    const classNo=$("#wizardNeisClass")?.value||"";
    state.settings.neis={...state.settings.neis,date,grade,...(classNo?{classNo}:{})};
    save();
    setupWizardPreview="timetable";
    renderSetupWizard();
  });
  $("[data-wizard-preview-back]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{setupWizardPreview="";renderSetupWizard();});
  $("[data-setup-prev]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{saveWizardCurrentStep();setupWizardPreview="";if(setupWizardStep>0){setupWizardStep--;renderSetupWizard();}});
  $("[data-setup-next]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(setupWizardStep===1&&!String($("#wizardUserName")?.value||"").trim()) return toast("사용자 이름을 입력해 주세요.");
    if(setupWizardStep===2){
      const p=currentUserProfile();
      const isHomeroom=p.primaryRole==="homeroom"||p.secondaryRole==="homeroom";
      if(isHomeroom&&!String($("#wizardHomeClass")?.value||"").trim()) return toast("담임교사는 담당 반을 선택해 주세요.");
    }
    saveWizardCurrentStep();
    setupWizardPreview="";
    setupWizardStep++;
    renderSetupWizard();
  });
  $("[data-setup-finish]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    saveWizardCurrentStep();
    state.settings.setupCompleted=true;
    state.settings.setupCompletedVersion=APP_VERSION;
    save();
    refreshHeaderRoleBadge();
  try {
    const bootContent=document.getElementById('pageContent');
    if(bootContent && !String(bootContent.innerHTML||'').trim()){
      state.activePage='dashboard';
      navigate('dashboard');
    }
  } catch (bootRenderError) {
    console.error('[UEP] 부팅 대시보드 강제 복구 실패',bootRenderError);
  }
    setSetupWizardLock(false);
    navigate("dashboard");
    toast(`${currentUserProfile().name||"사용자"} 초기 설정을 저장했습니다.`);
  });
}

function connectionHealthRows(){
  const required=[
    ["02_학생마스터",(readonlyCache?.students||[]).length>0],
    ["04_학생연락식별정보",(readonlyCache?.students||[]).some(s=>s.phone&&s.phone!=="-")],
    ["05_대학적성데이터",!String(googleConnectionError||"").includes("05_대학적성데이터")&&!String(googleConnectionError||"").includes("05_진로희망이력")],
    ["06_선택과목이력",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.subjectSelections)],
    ["20_학사생마스터",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormStudents)],
    ["41_학교캘린더",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.calendarEvents)],
    ["42_급식지도계획",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.lunchDuties)],
    ["43_야자감독계획",Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors)]
  ];
  return required.map(([name,ok])=>({name,ok:Boolean(ok)}));
}
function issueStatusLabel(value){return ({new:"접수",checking:"확인 중",fixed:"수정 완료",hold:"보류"})[value]||"접수";}

const PRIVACY_MODE_STORE='uep-privacy-demo-mode-v1';
function privacyModeEnabled(){try{return localStorage.getItem(PRIVACY_MODE_STORE)==='1';}catch{return false;}}
function applyPrivacyMode(){
  const enabled=privacyModeEnabled();
  document.body.classList.toggle('privacy-demo-mode',enabled);
  const button=document.getElementById('privacyModeButton');
  if(button){
    button.classList.toggle('active',enabled);
    const b=button.querySelector('b'); if(b)b.textContent=enabled?'개인정보 보호 중':'개인정보 가리기';
    button.title=enabled?'개인정보 보호 모드 해제':'학생·연수 시연용 개인정보 가리기';
  }
  const signal=document.getElementById('studentSignalButton');
  if(signal&&enabled)signal.title='선택 학생 정보 보호 중';
  else if(signal)updateStudentSignalIndicator();
}
function togglePrivacyMode(){
  const next=!privacyModeEnabled();
  try{localStorage.setItem(PRIVACY_MODE_STORE,next?'1':'0');}catch{}
  applyPrivacyMode();
  toast(next?'개인정보 보호 모드를 켰습니다. 기능은 유지하고 주요 개인정보만 가립니다.':'개인정보 보호 모드를 해제했습니다.');
}

function issueReportButtonMarkup(){return `<button class="nav" id="studentGuidanceButton"><span>✎</span><b>학생지도</b></button>`;}
function openStudentGuidanceDialog(){
  const old=document.getElementById('studentGuidanceLayer');if(old)old.remove();
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>String(s.status||'재학')==='재학').sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||''),'ko'));
  const classes=[...new Set(students.map(s=>String(classNumberOf(s)||'')).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  const todayValue=dateKey(new Date());
  const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.id='studentGuidanceLayer';layer.className='issue-layer';
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog student-guidance-dialog"><header><div><small>STUDENT GUIDANCE</small><h3>학생지도 기록</h3><p>반과 학생을 선택하고 날짜와 지도내용을 기록합니다.</p></div><button type="button" data-guidance-close>×</button></header><form id="studentGuidanceForm"><div class="student-guidance-grid"><label>반 선택<select id="studentGuidanceClass" required><option value="">반 선택</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(c)}">${escapeHtml(c)}반</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>학생 선택<select id="studentGuidanceStudent" required disabled><option value="">학생 선택</option></select></label></div><label>지도 날짜<input id="studentGuidanceDate" type="date" value="${escapeHtml(todayValue)}" required></label><label>지도 내용<textarea id="studentGuidanceMemo" rows="7" required placeholder="학생에게 지도한 내용, 약속사항, 추후 확인할 내용을 기록해 주세요."></textarea></label><div class="issue-meta">지도교사 ${escapeHtml(currentLoginTeacherName()||currentUser?.name||roleName(currentRoleId())||'')} · 기본정보 연결시트 46_학생지도기록 저장</div><div class="modal-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-guidance-close>취소</button><button class="btn primary" type="submit">지도 기록 저장</button></div></form></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(layer);
  const classSelect=layer.querySelector('#studentGuidanceClass'),studentSelect=layer.querySelector('#studentGuidanceStudent');
  const fillStudents=()=>{const c=String(classSelect.value||'');const rows=students.filter(st=>String(classNumberOf(st))===c);studentSelect.innerHTML=`<option value="">학생 선택</option>${rows.map(st=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(st.id||st.studentNo||'')}">${escapeHtml(`${st.studentNo||''} ${st.name||''}`)}</option>`).join('')}`;studentSelect.disabled=!c;};
  classSelect.onchange=fillStudents;
  studentSelect.onchange=()=>{selectedStudentId=studentSelect.value||"";updateStudentSignalIndicator();};
  const closeGuidance=()=>{layer.remove();updateStudentSignalIndicator();};
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-guidance-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=closeGuidance);layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)closeGuidance();};
  layer.querySelector('#studentGuidanceForm').onsubmit=async e=>{e.preventDefault();const id=studentSelect.value,student=students.find(st=>String(st.id||st.studentNo||'')===String(id));if(!student)return toast('학생을 선택해 주세요.');const memo=layer.querySelector('#studentGuidanceMemo').value.trim();if(!memo)return toast('지도 내용을 입력해 주세요.');const payload={studentId:String(student.id||''),studentNo:String(student.studentNo||''),name:String(student.name||''),grade:String(student.grade||'1'),className:String(classNumberOf(student)||''),number:String(student.number||''),guidanceDate:String(layer.querySelector('#studentGuidanceDate').value||todayValue),memo,writer:String(currentLoginTeacherName()||currentUser?.name||roleName(currentRoleId())||''),status:'기록'};const saveBtn=layer.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');saveBtn.disabled=true;saveBtn.textContent='저장 중…';try{const result=await window.schoolBoard?.saveStudentGuidanceRecord?.(payload);if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'학생지도 기록 저장 실패');readonlyCache.studentGuidanceRecords=Array.isArray(readonlyCache.studentGuidanceRecords)?[result.record,...readonlyCache.studentGuidanceRecords]:[result.record];layer.remove();toast(`${student.studentNo} ${student.name} 학생지도 기록을 저장했습니다.`);}catch(error){saveBtn.disabled=false;saveBtn.textContent='지도 기록 저장';toast(error?.message||'학생지도 기록 저장에 실패했습니다.');}};
  setTimeout(()=>classSelect?.focus(),30);
}
function openIssueReportDialog(){

  const layer=document.createElement("div");layer.id="issueReportLayer";layer.className="issue-layer";
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog"><header><div><small>UEP FEEDBACK</small><h3>오류 신고</h3></div><button type="button" data-issue-close>×</button></header><form id="issueReportForm"><label>발견한 화면<input id="issuePage" value="${escapeHtml(pageTitles[state.activePage]?.[0]||state.activePage||'')}" required></label><label>오류 유형<select id="issueType"><option>화면 표시</option><option>데이터 연결</option><option>기능 작동</option><option>속도·안정성</option><option>개선 제안</option></select></label><label>오류 내용<textarea id="issueDescription" rows="5" required placeholder="어떤 동작을 했을 때 무엇이 잘못 보였는지 적어주세요."></textarea></label><label>재현 방법<textarea id="issueSteps" rows="3" placeholder="예: 학생정보 → 1반 → 학생 클릭"></textarea></label><div class="issue-meta">버전 v${APP_VERSION} · ${escapeHtml(roleName(currentRoleId()))} · ${new Date().toLocaleString('ko-KR')}</div><div class="modal-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-issue-close>취소</button><button class="btn primary">신고 저장</button></div></form></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(layer);
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-issue-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>layer.remove());
  layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)layer.remove();};

  setTimeout(()=>layer.querySelector('#issueDescription')?.focus(),30);
}
function openIssueManager(){
  const old=document.getElementById("issueReportLayer");if(old)old.remove();
  const reports=state.issueReports||[];const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.id='issueReportLayer';layer.className='issue-layer';
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog issue-manager"><header><div><small>ADMIN CONSOLE</small><h3>오류 신고 관리</h3></div><button type="button" data-issue-close>×</button></header><div class="issue-summary"><b>전체 ${reports.length}건</b><span>접수 ${reports.filter(r=>r.status==='new').length} · 확인 중 ${reports.filter(r=>r.status==='checking').length} · 완료 ${reports.filter(r=>r.status==='fixed').length}</span></div><div class="issue-list">${reports.length?reports.map(r=>`<article data-issue-id="${escapeHtml(r.id)}"><div class="issue-item-head"><span class="issue-state ${escapeHtml(r.status)}">${issueStatusLabel(r.status)}</span><b>${escapeHtml(r.page)} · ${escapeHtml(r.type)}</b><small>v${escapeHtml(r.version||'')} · ${escapeHtml(formatSyncTime(r.createdAt))}</small></div><p>${escapeHtml(r.description)}</p>${r.steps?`<small>재현: ${escapeHtml(r.steps)}</small>`:''}<div class="issue-admin-row"><select data-issue-status><option value="new" ${r.status==='new'?'selected':''}>접수</option><option value="checking" ${r.status==='checking'?'selected':''}>확인 중</option><option value="fixed" ${r.status==='fixed'?'selected':''}>수정 완료</option><option value="hold" ${r.status==='hold'?'selected':''}>보류</option></select><input data-issue-note value="${escapeHtml(r.adminNote||'')}" placeholder="관리자 메모·수정 버전"><button class="btn primary" data-issue-save>저장</button></div></article>`).join(''):`<div class="empty-box">접수된 오류 신고가 없습니다.</div>`}</div><div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-issue-close>닫기</button></div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(layer);layer.querySelectorAll('[data-issue-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>layer.remove());
  layer.querySelectorAll('[data-issue-save]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{const card=button.closest('[data-issue-id]');const item=reports.find(r=>r.id===card.dataset.issueId);if(!item)return;item.status=card.querySelector('[data-issue-status]').value;item.adminNote=card.querySelector('[data-issue-note]').value.trim();item.updatedAt=new Date().toISOString();await save();toast('신고 처리 상태를 저장했습니다.');layer.remove();openIssueManager();});
}

function accessAccountRole(account){
  if(String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y')return '관리자';
  const role=String(account?.role||'').trim();
  if(/학년부장/.test(role))return '학년부장';
  if(/학년총무/.test(role))return '학년총무';
  if(/담임/.test(role))return '담임교사';
  if(/교감/.test(role))return '교감';
  if(/교장/.test(role))return '교장';
  return '교과담당';
}
function openAccessControlPanel(){
  const old=document.getElementById("accessControlLayer");if(old)old.remove();
  const p=currentUserProfile(), isAdmin=currentRoleId()==="admin";
  const accounts=[...(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[])].sort((a,b)=>String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''),'ko'));
  const activeCount=accounts.filter(a=>a.active!==false).length, blockedCount=accounts.length-activeCount;
  const roleOptions=["관리자","학년부장","학년총무","담임교사","교과담당","교감","교장"];
  const rows=accounts.length?accounts.map(a=>`<tr data-access-user="${escapeHtml(a.id)}"><td><b>${escapeHtml(a.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(maskEmail(a.email))}</small></td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-access-role>${roleOptions.map(r=>`<option ${accessAccountRole(a)===r?'selected':''}>${r}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.admin==='Y'?'관리자':a.role||'교과담당')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-access-grade>${[1,2,3].map(g=>`<option ${String(a.grade)==String(g)?'selected':''}>${g}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.grade||'-')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-access-class><option value="">-</option>${Array.from({length:14},(_,i)=>i+1).map(c=>`<option value="${c}" ${String(a.classNo)==String(c)?'selected':''}>${c}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.classNo||'-')}</td><td><span class="state ${a.active!==false?'connected':'error'}">${a.active!==false?'승인':'차단'}</span></td><td>${isAdmin?`<button class="btn secondary" data-access-toggle>${a.active!==false?'사용 중지':'사용 승인'}</button><button class="btn primary" data-access-save>권한 저장</button>`:'-'}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6">01_사용자계정에 등록된 사용자가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
  const layer=document.createElement("div");layer.id="accessControlLayer";layer.className="issue-layer";
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog issue-manager access-control-dialog"><header><div><small>UEP RELEASE SECURITY</small><h3>사용자·권한 승인</h3></div><button type="button" data-access-close>×</button></header>
  <div class="issue-summary"><b>${isAdmin?'관리자 권한 제어판':'내 권한 상태'}</b><span>${escapeHtml(p.name||'미설정')} · ${escapeHtml(currentRoleDisplay())}</span></div>
  <div class="security-control-grid release-security-grid"><article><small>등록 사용자</small><b>${accounts.length}명</b><p>01_사용자계정 기준</p></article><article><small>사용 승인</small><b>${activeCount}명</b><p>로그인 가능</p></article><article><small>사용 중지</small><b>${blockedCount}명</b><p>로그인 차단</p></article><article><small>권한 원본</small><b>구글시트</b><p>역할·학년·담임반</p></article></div>
  <div class="security-gate-warning release-ready"><b>배포 승인 방식</b><p>UEP는 <b>01_사용자계정</b>에 등록되고 사용여부가 Y인 교사를 이메일 기준으로 확인합니다. 최고관리자는 신규 PC·계정표 일시 오류에서도 부트스트랩 진입이 가능하며, 일반 사용자의 역할과 담당 학년·반은 계정표 기준을 유지합니다.</p></div>
  <div class="access-user-table-wrap"><table class="access-user-table"><thead><tr><th>사용자</th><th>역할</th><th>학년</th><th>담임반</th><th>상태</th><th>관리</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>
  <div class="readonly-rule"><b>배포 전 확인</b><span>관리자 계정 최소 1명 유지 · 퇴직/전보/미사용 교사는 사용 중지 · 담임은 담당반 확인 · 관리자 권한은 필요한 사용자에게만 부여</span></div>
  <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-access-refresh>계정표 새로고침</button><button class="btn secondary" data-access-close>닫기</button></div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(layer);
  const close=()=>layer.remove(); layer.querySelectorAll('[data-access-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=close); layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)close();};
  layer.querySelector('[data-access-refresh]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{const result=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();if(result?.ok){readonlyCache=result.data;layer.remove();openAccessControlPanel();}else toast(result?.reason||'사용자 계정표를 새로고침하지 못했습니다.');});
  if(isAdmin){
    layer.querySelectorAll('[data-access-user]').forEach(row=>{
      const id=row.dataset.accessUser, account=accounts.find(a=>String(a.id)===String(id)); if(!account)return;
      row.querySelector('[data-access-toggle]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
        if(account.active!==false && String(account.id)===String(p.id)) return toast('현재 로그인한 관리자 계정은 자기 자신을 중지할 수 없습니다.');
        const active=account.active===false; const result=await window.schoolBoard?.updateUserAccount?.({id,active});
        if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'사용자 승인 상태를 저장하지 못했습니다.');
        const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.(); if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data; layer.remove();openAccessControlPanel();toast(active?'사용 승인을 완료했습니다.':'사용을 중지했습니다.');
      });
      row.querySelector('[data-access-save]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
        const role=row.querySelector('[data-access-role]')?.value||'', grade=row.querySelector('[data-access-grade]')?.value||'1', classNo=row.querySelector('[data-access-class]')?.value||'';
        const admin=role==='관리자'; const storedRole=admin?'관리자':role;
        const result=await window.schoolBoard?.updateUserAccount?.({id,role:storedRole,admin,grade,classNo,active:account.active!==false});
        if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'권한을 저장하지 못했습니다.');
        const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.(); if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data; layer.remove();openAccessControlPanel();toast('사용자 권한을 저장했습니다.');
      });
    });
  }
}

function releaseReadinessRows(){
  const p=currentUserProfile(), account=findUserAccount(p.name,p.email);
  return [
    {name:'UEP 표준 구글시트',ok:Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length&&!googleConnectionError),note:readonlyCache?.students?.length?`${readonlyCache.students.length}명 학생 인식`:'연결 확인 필요'},
    {name:'사용자 계정 승인표',ok:Boolean((readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[]).length),note:`${(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[]).filter(a=>a.active!==false).length}명 승인`},
    {name:'현재 사용자 권한',ok:Boolean(account&&account.active!==false),note:account?`${account.name} · ${account.admin==='Y'?'관리자':account.role||'교과담당'}`:'계정표 일치 확인 필요'},
    {name:'권한별 조회범위',ok:Boolean(currentTeacherAccessScope()?.label),note:currentTeacherAccessScope()?.label||'확인 필요'},
    {name:'업데이트 정보',ok:Boolean(state.settings.updateManifestUrl),note:state.settings.updateManifestUrl?'업데이트 확인 주소 등록':'manifest 주소 등록 필요'},
    {name:'Google API 보호',ok:true,note:'120초 캐시 · 429 재시도 · 순차 batch 조회'},
    {name:'런타임 진단',ok:true,note:'오류·중복 DOM·리소스 점검 활성'}
  ];
}

function settingsTabsMarkup(){
  const tabs=[
    ["basic","01 기본·연결"],
    ["security","02 사용자·보안"],
    ["operations","03 알림·운영"],
    ["system","04 시스템 관리"]
  ];
  return `<div class="settings-tabs settings-final-tabs">${tabs.map(([id,label])=>`<button class="${settingsPanel===id?'active':''}" data-settings-panel="${id}">${label}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

function accessControlInlineMarkup(){
  const p=currentUserProfile(), isAdmin=currentRoleId()==="admin";
  const accounts=[...(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[])].sort((a,b)=>String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''),'ko'));
  const activeCount=accounts.filter(a=>a.active!==false).length, blockedCount=accounts.length-activeCount;
  const roleOptions=["관리자","학년부장","학년총무","담임교사","교과담당","교감","교장"];
  const rows=accounts.length?accounts.map(a=>`<tr data-inline-access-user="${escapeHtml(a.id)}"><td><b>${escapeHtml(a.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(maskEmail(a.email))}</small></td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-role>${roleOptions.map(r=>`<option ${accessAccountRole(a)===r?'selected':''}>${r}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.admin==='Y'?'관리자':a.role||'교과담당')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-grade>${[1,2,3].map(g=>`<option ${String(a.grade)==String(g)?'selected':''}>${g}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.grade||'-')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-class><option value="">-</option>${Array.from({length:14},(_,i)=>i+1).map(c=>`<option value="${c}" ${String(a.classNo)==String(c)?'selected':''}>${c}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.classNo||'-')}</td><td><span class="state ${a.active!==false?'connected':'error'}">${a.active!==false?'승인':'차단'}</span></td><td>${isAdmin?`<button class="btn secondary" data-inline-access-toggle>${a.active!==false?'사용 중지':'사용 승인'}</button><button class="btn primary" data-inline-access-save>권한 저장</button>`:'-'}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6">01_사용자계정에 등록된 사용자가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
  return `<article class="setting-card access-control-inline"><div class="connection-title"><div><small>UEP RELEASE SECURITY</small><h3>사용자·권한 승인</h3><p>01_사용자계정의 사용여부·역할·담당학년·담임반을 이 화면에서 바로 관리합니다.</p></div><span class="state connected">계정표 승인</span></div><div class="security-control-grid release-security-grid"><article><small>등록 사용자</small><b>${accounts.length}명</b><p>01_사용자계정 기준</p></article><article><small>사용 승인</small><b>${activeCount}명</b><p>로그인 가능</p></article><article><small>사용 중지</small><b>${blockedCount}명</b><p>로그인 차단</p></article><article><small>현재 관리자</small><b>${escapeHtml(p.name||'-')}</b><p>${escapeHtml(currentRoleDisplay())}</p></article></div><div class="access-user-table-wrap"><table class="access-user-table"><thead><tr><th>사용자</th><th>역할</th><th>학년</th><th>담임반</th><th>상태</th><th>관리</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><div class="connection-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-inline-access-refresh>계정표 새로고침</button></div><p class="safe-note">현재 로그인한 관리자 자기 계정은 사용 중지할 수 없습니다.</p></article>`;
}

const UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY='uep_subject_confidential_pin_hash_v3';
const UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY='uep_subject_confidential_unlocked_v3';
function subjectConfidentialAllowed(){
  const role=String(currentRoleId?.()||'');
  return ['admin','grade_head'].includes(role)||['관리자','학년부장'].includes(String(currentRoleDisplay?.()||''));
}
function subjectConfidentialSharedHash(){
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.settings)?readonlyCache.settings:[];
  const hit=rows.find(r=>String(r?.key||r?.settingKey||r?.['설정키']||'').trim()==='SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH');
  return String(hit?.value||hit?.settingValue||hit?.['설정값']||'').trim();
}
function subjectConfidentialPasswordHash(){const central=typeof uepSharedSettingValue08166==='function'?String(uepSharedSettingValue08166('SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH')||'').trim():'';return central||subjectConfidentialSharedHash()||String(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY)||'');}
function subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured(){return !!subjectConfidentialPasswordHash();}
async function subjectConfidentialDigest(value){const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(String(value||''));const hash=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');}
async function setSubjectConfidentialPassword(){
  // 0.81.46: Electron에서 차단될 수 있는 prompt 대신 검증된 보안 전용 모달을 사용합니다.
  if(!subjectConfidentialAllowed()){toast('관리자·학년부장 권한에서 설정할 수 있습니다.');return false;}
  const configured=subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});
  if(!result)return false;
  if(configured&&await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.current)!==subjectConfidentialPasswordHash()){
    toast('현재 선택과목 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');return false;
  }
  localStorage.setItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY,await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.first));
  lockSubjectConfidential();
  toast('선택과목 신청현황 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');
  if(state.activePage==='settings')render('settings');
  return true;
}
function lockSubjectConfidential(){sessionStorage.removeItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY);}
async function unlockSubjectConfidential(){
  if(!subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured()&&typeof uepLoadSharedSecurity08168==='function')await uepLoadSharedSecurity08168(true);
  // __UEP_08159_HOMEROOM_SUBJECT_GATE__
  const scope=typeof currentTeacherAccessScope==='function'?currentTeacherAccessScope():null;
  const role=String(currentRoleId?.()||'');
  const homeroomAllowed=role==='homeroom'||Boolean(scope?.teacher&&scope?.classNo);
  if(!subjectConfidentialAllowed()&&!homeroomAllowed){toast('담임·학년부장·관리자는 비밀번호 인증 후 과목별 신청현황을 열람할 수 있습니다.');return false;}
  if(sessionStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY)==='1')return true;
  if(!subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured()){toast(subjectConfidentialAllowed()?'설정 → 사용자·보안에서 선택과목 대외비 비밀번호를 먼저 설정해 주세요.':'관리자가 아직 선택과목 열람 비밀번호를 공용 설정하지 않았습니다.');return false;}
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'unlock',configured:true,kind:'subject'});
  if(!result)return false;
  if(await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.first)!==subjectConfidentialPasswordHash()){toast('선택과목 대외비 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');return false;}
  sessionStorage.setItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY,'1');
  return true;
}
function settingsView() {
  const connected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const credentialReady = Boolean(googleConnectionStatus?.ok);
  const googleStatus = connected ? ["구글시트 연결됨", ""] : googleConnectionError ? ["권한·연결 오류", "error"] : credentialReady ? ["권한 확인 필요", "warn"] : ["인증 필요", "warn"];
  const connectionRows = [
    ["학생정보", readonlyCache?.students?.length], ["학사생", readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length], ["학사외출", readonlyCache?.dormOutings?.length],
    ["성적", readonlyCache?.scoreRecords?.length], ["수능최저", readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums?.length], ["대학입결", readonlyCache?.admissionCuts?.length],
  ];
  const neisStatus = neisData?.ok ? (neisData.offline ? ["저장자료 표시", "warn"] : ["실시간 연결됨", ""]) : neisLoading ? ["불러오는 중", "warn"] : ["연결 확인", "error"];
  const profile=currentUserProfile(), access=currentTeacherAccessScope();

  const header=(title,desc,badge)=>`${settingsTabsMarkup()}<section class="settings-section-head"><div><small>UEP SETTINGS</small><h2>${title}</h2><p>${desc}</p></div><span>${badge}</span></section>`;

  if(settingsPanel==="security") return `<div class="module-page settings-v064 settings-final">
    ${header("사용자·보안","계정 승인과 역할, 민감정보 보호를 관리합니다.","관리자·학년부장")}
    <article class="setting-card sensitive-security-settings"><div><small>PRIVACY · SECURITY</small><h3>🔒 민감정보 보안</h3><p>교육비지원 · 요보호학생 · 위기학생 상세정보의 열람 비밀번호를 관리합니다.</p><p class="settings-help">상세정보는 1차 블라인드 후 비밀번호 인증을 거쳐 확인하며 인증은 10분 동안 유지됩니다.</p></div><div class="sensitive-security-status"><b>${sensitivePasswordConfigured()?'비밀번호 설정됨':'비밀번호 설정 필요'}</b><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-sensitive-password-set>${sensitivePasswordConfigured()?'비밀번호 변경':'비밀번호 설정'}</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-sensitive-lock>즉시 잠금</button></div></article>
    <article class="setting-card subject-confidential-security-settings"><div><small>CONFIDENTIAL · CURRICULUM</small><h3>🔐 선택과목 대외비 보안</h3><p>과목별 신청현황 전용 비밀번호를 민감정보 비밀번호와 별도로 관리합니다.</p><p class="settings-help">관리자·학년부장만 설정 및 열람할 수 있으며 담임은 비활성화됩니다.</p></div><div class="sensitive-security-status"><b>${subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured()?'비밀번호 설정됨':'비밀번호 설정 필요'}</b><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-subject-confidential-password-set ${subjectConfidentialAllowed()?'':'disabled'}>${subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured()?'비밀번호 변경':'비밀번호 설정'}</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-subject-confidential-lock ${subjectConfidentialAllowed()?'':'disabled'}>즉시 잠금</button></div></article>
