    <article class="setting-card access-control-card"><div class="connection-title"><div><h3>사용자·권한 승인</h3><p>01_사용자계정의 사용여부·역할·담당학년·담임반을 기준으로 로그인과 조회범위를 제어합니다.</p></div><span class="state connected">계정표 승인</span></div><div class="connection-actions"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-access-control>권한 승인 제어판</button></div><p class="safe-note">신규 사용자 등록은 표준 구글시트에서 관리하고 UEP에서는 승인상태와 역할을 조정합니다.</p></article>
  </div>`;

  if(settingsPanel==="operations") return `<div class="module-page settings-v064 settings-final">
    ${header("알림·운영","실제 운영 중 필요한 알림과 오류 신고만 관리합니다.","운영 설정")}
    <article class="setting-card mail-alert-settings"><h3>메일 알림</h3><p>필요한 운영 알림만 선택합니다. 발송 기준 시각은 일과운영시간과 연동합니다.</p><div class="mail-alert-grid">${[["dormOuting","학사외출"],["afternoonStudy","오후자습 현황"],["night1","야자1 현황"],["night2","야자2 현황"],["nightException","야간 이상상황"],["nightSummary","야간 종료 종합"]].map(([key,label])=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-mail-alert="${key}" ${state.settings.mailAlerts?.[key]?"checked":""}> ${label}</label>`).join("")}</div><small>※ 실제 자동메일 발송은 연결된 UEP 데이터처리 메일 트리거가 이 설정값을 읽도록 연동됩니다.</small></article>
    <article class="setting-card issue-admin-card"><div class="connection-title"><div><h3>오류 신고 관리</h3><p>사용자가 저장한 오류 신고를 확인하고 처리 상태와 수정 버전을 기록합니다.</p></div><span class="state">${(state.issueReports||[]).filter(r=>r.status!=='fixed').length}건 처리 중</span></div><div class="connection-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-open-issue-report>새 신고 작성</button><button class="btn primary" data-open-issue-manager>신고 관리</button></div><p class="safe-note">현재는 PC 로컬 저장 방식이며 중앙 수집은 관리자 서버 또는 관리 시트 연결 단계에서 확장합니다.</p></article>
  </div>`;

  if(settingsPanel==="system") return `<div class="module-page settings-v064 settings-final">
    ${header("시스템 관리","연결상태·배포 준비·업데이트처럼 평소에는 건드리지 않는 관리 도구입니다.","관리자 점검")}
    <article class="setting-card health-check-card"><div class="connection-title"><div><h3>연결 진단</h3><p>필수 탭과 주요 데이터가 정상적으로 읽히는지 한 번에 확인합니다.</p></div><span class="state ${googleConnectionError?'error':'connected'}">${googleConnectionError?'확인 필요':'정상'}</span></div><div class="health-list">${connectionHealthRows().map(row=>`<div><span>${row.ok?'✓':'!'}</span><b>${row.name}</b><small>${row.ok?'인식':'확인 필요'}</small></div>`).join('')}</div>${googleConnectionError?`<div class="connection-error-detail"><b>최근 오류</b><span>${escapeHtml(googleConnectionError)}</span></div>`:''}<div class="connection-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-run-health-check>다시 진단</button></div></article>
    <article class="setting-card"><h3>조회 연결 상태</h3><p>UEP 기준 구글시트에 실제 자료가 있는 항목을 확인합니다.</p>${connectionRows.map(([label,count]) => `<div class="setting-row"><span><b>${label}</b><small>${Number(count || 0).toLocaleString()}건 인식</small></span><span>${count ?     <article class="setting-card release-readiness-card"><div class="connection-title"><div><h3>배포 준비 점검</h3><p>교사용 배포 전에 필수 연결·계정·권한·업데이트·API 보호 상태를 확인합니다.</p></div><span class="state ${releaseReadinessRows().every(row=>row.ok)?'connected':'warn'}">${releaseReadinessRows().every(row=>row.ok)?'배포 준비':'확인 필요'}</span></div><div class="health-list release-health-list">${releaseReadinessRows().map(row=>`<div><span>${row.ok?'✓':'!'}</span><b>${escapeHtml(row.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(row.note)}</small></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="connection-actions"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-access-control>권한 점검</button><button class="btn secondary" data-run-health-check>연결 다시 진단</button></div><p class="safe-note">자동 설치·롤백은 현재 비활성화되어 있습니다.</p></article>
    <article class="setting-card update-settings-card"><div class="connection-title"><div><h3>UEP 업데이트</h3><p>현재 버전 ${APP_VERSION} · 담임 배포판부터는 앱 내부 업데이트 채널을 사용합니다.</p></div><span class="state connected">업데이트 확인 작동</span></div><form id="updateSettingsForm" class="update-settings-form"><input id="updateManifestUrl" type="url" value="${escapeHtml(state.settings.updateManifestUrl||'')}" placeholder="https://.../uep-update.json"><button class="btn secondary" type="submit">주소 저장</button><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-check-update>업데이트 확인</button></form><div class="update-roadmap"><span class="done">① 동일 빌드</span><span class="done">② 버전 확인</span><span class="done">③ 업데이트 채널</span><span class="done">④ 앱 내부 적용·롤백</span></div><div id="updateCheckResult" class="update-check-result"><small>새 버전 확인 → 무결성 검증 → 앱 내부 적용 → 실패 시 이전판 복구 방식입니다.</small></div></article>

  </div>`;

  </div>`;

function helpView() {
  return `<div class="module-page help-manual-page"><div class="setting-card help-intro"><div><h3>UEP 사용 설명서</h3><p>각 메뉴의 자료 출처와 실제 업무 흐름을 기준으로 설명합니다.</p></div><span>${Object.keys(helpItems).length}개 메뉴</span></div><div class="help-grid">${Object.entries(helpItems).map(([key,item])=>`<article class="help-item"><button class="help-button" data-help="${key}"><span><b>${item.title}</b><small>${item.summary}</small></span><span>${helpOpen===key?"⌃":"⌄"}</span></button>${helpOpen===key?`<div class="help-content"><h4>${item.title}</h4><ol>${item.steps.map(step=>`<li>${step}</li>`).join("")}</ol><p><b>자료 출처:</b> ${item.source}</p><p><b>문제 확인 순서:</b> 원본시트 → 데이터처리시트 → UEP 화면 구현</p></div>`:""}</article>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}

function classNumberOf(student) {
  return String(student?.className || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
}
function availableClasses() {
  const found = [...new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).map(classNumberOf).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  return found.length ? found : Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1));
}
function optionStudents(classNo, selectedId = "") {
  return (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => !classNo || classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id === selectedId ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
}

function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
    const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
    semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  }
  return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
}
function scorePeriodLabel(key) {
  if (!key || key === "all") return "전체 누적";
  const [year, semester] = String(key).split("-");
  return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
}
function availableScorePeriods(mode = scoreMode) {
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => mode === "combined" || row.scoreType === (mode === "mock" ? "모의고사" : "내신"));
  return [...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
}
function scoreExamOrder(exam) {
  const order = ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사","3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const index = order.indexOf(exam);
  return index < 0 ? 999 : index;
}
function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
}
function gradeToneClass(value) {
  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}
function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  });
  return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
function scoreTableMarkup(rows, mode = scoreMode) {
  const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
    const key=`${row.studentId}|${period}|${row.exam}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{studentId:row.studentId,studentNo:row.studentNo,name:row.name,period,exam:row.exam,items:new Map()});
    groups.get(key).items.set(row.subject,row);
  });
  const grouped=[...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)) || a.period.localeCompare(b.period) || scoreExamOrder(a.exam)-scoreExamOrder(b.exam) || String(a.exam).localeCompare(String(b.exam),"ko"));
  const cell=(row)=>{
    if(!row) return `<td class="empty-score-cell">-</td>`;
    const trend=scoreTrend(row,readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]);
    const trendMarkup=trend.text!=="-"?`<em class="trend ${trend.cls}">${trend.text}</em>`:"";
    if(row.scoreType==="모의고사") return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 표준 ${scoreDisplay(row.standardScore)}${row.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${scoreDisplay(row.percentile)}`:""}</small></td>`;
    const gradeValue = row.level === "산출제외" ? (row.achievement || "산출제외") : row.level;
    return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(gradeValue, Number.isFinite(Number(gradeValue))?"등급":"")}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 석차 ${escapeHtml(String(row.rank||"-"))}/${escapeHtml(String(row.enrollment||"-"))}${row.achievement?` · ${escapeHtml(row.achievement)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  };
  const title = mode === "mock" ? "모의고사 과목별 등급" : "내신 과목별 등급";
  return `<div class="data-card score-matrix-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${title}</strong></div><small>전체 누적 또는 선택 학기를 표시합니다.</small></div><div class="score-matrix-scroll"><table class="score-matrix-table"><thead><tr><th class="student-col">학생</th><th class="exam-col">학기·시험</th>${subjects.map(subject=>`<th>${escapeHtml(subject)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${grouped.map(group=>`<tr><th class="student-col"><b>${escapeHtml(group.studentNo)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.name)}</small></th><th class="exam-col"><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(group.period).replace("학년도 ","· "))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.exam)}</small></th>${subjects.map(subject=>cell(group.items.get(subject))).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
}

function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
}
function scoreAverage(rows) {
  return uepCommonWeightedAverage(rows,row=>Number(row.level));
}
function combinedScoreDashboardMarkup(student, rows) {
  const allRows = readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || [];
  const scopedRows = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows.filter(row=>row.studentId===student.id) : allRows.filter(row=>row.studentId===student.id);
  const internal = scopedRows.filter((row) => row.scoreType === "내신");
  const mock = scopedRows.filter((row) => row.scoreType === "모의고사");
  const internalPeriods = [...new Set(internal.map(scorePeriodKey))].sort();
  const latestInternalPeriod = internalPeriods.at(-1) || "";
  const summaryInternal = scorePeriod === "all" && latestInternalPeriod ? internal.filter(row=>scorePeriodKey(row)===latestInternalPeriod) : internal;
  const periodLabel = scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod);
  const coreInternalOrder = ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  const internalSubjects = [...new Set(summaryInternal.map((row) => row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a, b) => {
    const ai = coreInternalOrder.indexOf(a), bi = coreInternalOrder.indexOf(b);
    if (ai >= 0 || bi >= 0) return (ai < 0 ? 999 : ai) - (bi < 0 ? 999 : bi);
    return a.localeCompare(b, "ko");
  });
  const byInternal = (subject, exam) => summaryInternal.find((row) => row.subject === subject && row.exam === exam);
  const internalRows = internalSubjects.map((subject) => {
    const first = byInternal(subject, "1차고사"), second = byInternal(subject, "2차고사"), final = byInternal(subject, "학기말고사");
    const finalGrade = final?.level === "산출제외" ? (final?.achievement || "산출제외") : final?.level;
    return `<div class="consult-score-row consult-internal-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(subject)}</b><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(first?.level)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(first?.rawScore)}</span><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(second?.level)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(second?.rawScore)}</span><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(finalGrade,Number.isFinite(Number(finalGrade))?"등급":"")}${final?.achievement&&Number.isFinite(Number(finalGrade))?`<small>${escapeHtml(final.achievement)}</small>`:""}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(final?.rawScore)}</span></div>`;
  }).join("") || `<div class="consult-empty">선택 범위의 내신 성적이 없습니다.</div>`;

  const mockExamList = [...new Set(mock.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
  const summaryMockExams = mockExamList.slice(-2);
  const firstMockExam = summaryMockExams[0] || "3월모의고사";
  const secondMockExam = summaryMockExams[1] || summaryMockExams[0] || "6월모의고사";
  const coreMockOrder = ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"];
  const mockSubjects = [...new Set(mock.filter(row=>summaryMockExams.includes(row.exam)).map((row) => row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=coreMockOrder.indexOf(a),bi=coreMockOrder.indexOf(b); return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
  const byMock = (subject, exam) => mock.find(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam);
  const mockRows = mockSubjects.map(subject=>{
    const first=byMock(subject,firstMockExam), second=byMock(subject,secondMockExam);
    const trend = first && second && Number.isFinite(Number(first.level)) && Number.isFinite(Number(second.level)) ? Number(first.level)-Number(second.level) : null;
    const trendText = trend == null ? "" : trend > 0 ? `<small class="trend good">▲${Math.abs(trend)}등급</small>` : trend < 0 ? `<small class="trend bad">▼${Math.abs(trend)}등급</small>` : `<small class="trend">유지</small>`;
    return `<div class="consult-score-row consult-mock-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(subject)}</b><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(first?.level)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(first?.rawScore)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(first?.standardScore)}</span><span>${gradeBadgeMarkup(second?.level)}${trendText}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(second?.rawScore)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(second?.standardScore)}</span></div>`;
  }).join("") || `<div class="consult-empty">선택 범위의 모의고사 성적이 없습니다.</div>`;

  const finalInternal = internal.filter(row=>row.exam==="학기말고사");
  const semesterSource = finalInternal.length ? finalInternal : internal;
  const yearValues = semesterSource.map(row=>Number(row.schoolYear)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const firstYear = yearValues.length ? Math.min(...yearValues) : Number(String(today.getFullYear()));
  const semesterSlots=["1-1","1-2","2-1","2-2","3-1","3-2"], semesterAverages=new Map();
  semesterSource.forEach(row=>{const year=Number(row.schoolYear);const grade=Number.isFinite(year)?Math.max(1,Math.min(3,year-firstYear+1)):1;const sem=String(row.semester||"1").match(/[12]/)?.[0]||"1";const key=`${grade}-${sem}`;if(!semesterAverages.has(key))semesterAverages.set(key,[]);if(Number.isFinite(Number(row.level)))semesterAverages.get(key).push(Number(row.level));});
  const semesterCells=semesterSlots.map(slot=>{const rowsForSlot=semesterSource.filter(row=>{const year=Number(row.schoolYear);const grade=Number.isFinite(year)?Math.max(1,Math.min(3,year-firstYear+1)):1;const sem=String(row.semester||"1").match(/[12]/)?.[0]||"1";return `${grade}-${sem}`===slot;});const weighted=uepCommonWeightedAverage(rowsForSlot,row=>Number(row.level));const average=Number.isFinite(weighted)?weighted.toFixed(2):"-";return `<span><b>${slot}</b><em>${average}</em></span>`;}).join("");
  const cumulative=scoreAverage(semesterSource);
  const latestInternalExam = ["학기말고사","2차고사","1차고사"].find(exam=>summaryInternal.some(row=>row.exam===exam)) || "내신";
  const latestInternalRows=summaryInternal.filter(row=>row.exam===latestInternalExam);
  const latestInternalAverage=scoreAverage(latestInternalRows.length?latestInternalRows:summaryInternal);
  const mockGradeCard=(exam)=>{const examRows=mock.filter(row=>row.exam===exam).sort((a,b)=>{const ai=coreMockOrder.indexOf(a.subject),bi=coreMockOrder.indexOf(b.subject);return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");});return `<span class="mock-grade-metric"><small>${escapeHtml(exam)}</small><div>${examRows.map(row=>`<i title="${escapeHtml(row.subject)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.subject).replace("통합","").slice(0,2))}${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}</i>`).join("")||"<em>-</em>"}</div><em>과목별 등급</em></span>`;};

  const baseFirst=admissionBase(student.id,firstMockExam), baseSecond=admissionBase(student.id,secondMockExam);
  const rules=readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums||[];
  const cutRows=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).map(cut=>({...cut,diff:baseSecond.actual9==null?null:baseSecond.actual9-Number(cut.cut)})).filter(cut=>cut.diff==null||Number.isFinite(cut.diff)).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.diff??99)-Math.abs(b.diff??99));
  const uniqueCuts=[],seenCuts=new Set();for(const cut of cutRows){const key=`${cut.university}|${cut.unit}|${cut.name}`;if(seenCuts.has(key))continue;seenCuts.add(key);uniqueCuts.push(cut);if(uniqueCuts.length>=7)break;}
  const targetRows=uniqueCuts.map(cut=>{const rule=rules.find(item=>item.university===cut.university&&(!item.unit||!cut.unit||item.unit===cut.unit))||rules.find(item=>item.university===cut.university);const firstCheck=rule?minimumCheck(rule,baseFirst.mock):null;const secondCheck=rule?minimumCheck(rule,baseSecond.mock):null;const internalOk=cut.diff==null?"판단대기":cut.diff<=0?"충족":cut.diff<=.7?"도전":"상향";const internalClass=internalOk==="충족"?"good":internalOk==="도전"?"warn":"bad";return `<div class="consult-target-row consult-target-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(cut.university)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(cut.unit||"-")}</span><span>${escapeHtml(cut.name||"-")}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(cut.cut,2)}</span><span class="consult-status ${internalClass}">${internalOk}</span><span>${escapeHtml(rule?.minimum||"최저자료 없음")}</span><span class="consult-status ${firstCheck?.result==="충족"?"good":firstCheck?.result==="미충족"?"bad":"warn"}">${escapeHtml(firstCheck?.result||"-")}</span><span class="consult-status ${secondCheck?.result==="충족"?"good":secondCheck?.result==="미충족"?"bad":"warn"}">${escapeHtml(secondCheck?.result||"-")}</span></div>`;}).join("")||`<div class="consult-empty">연결된 대학 입결 자료가 없습니다.</div>`;

  const cumulativeTables = `<section class="consult-cumulative-scores"><header><div><small>ALL RECORDS · ${escapeHtml(periodLabel)}</small><h4>누적 성적 전체보기</h4></div><span>학기 전환 버튼으로 범위를 바꿀 수 있습니다.</span></header>${internal.length?scoreTableMarkup(internal,"internal"):""}${mock.length?scoreTableMarkup(mock,"mock"):""}</section>`;
  const actualNineText=baseSecond.actual9==null?"산출 자료 없음":`${baseSecond.actual9.toFixed(2)}등급`;
  const targetRuleMarkup=`<div class="consult-admission-rule"><span><small>현재 9등급 환산 내신</small><b>${escapeHtml(actualNineText)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(baseSecond.preferred||"최근 내신")} 기준</em></span><p><b>판정 규칙</b> 과목별 석차÷수강자 백분위를 9등급 경계(4·11·23·40·60·77·89·96%)로 환산해 평균합니다. 대학 목표 내신 이하이면 ‘충족’, 목표보다 0.7등급 이내 높으면 ‘도전’, 그보다 높으면 ‘상향’으로 표시합니다.</p></div>`;
  return `<section class="consult-dashboard"><header class="consult-student-head"><div><small>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} · ${escapeHtml(student.className)} ${escapeHtml(student.number||"")}번 · ${escapeHtml(periodLabel)}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(student.name)} 학사상담 대시보드</h3><p>상단은 선택 범위의 최근 학기 요약, 하단은 해당 범위의 모든 성적입니다.</p></div><span>종합성적</span></header><div class="consult-metrics consult-grade-metrics"><span><small>${escapeHtml(latestInternalPeriod?scorePeriodLabel(latestInternalPeriod):periodLabel)} · ${escapeHtml(latestInternalExam)}</small><b>${latestInternalAverage==null?"-":latestInternalAverage.toFixed(2)}</b><em>5등급제 평균</em></span>${mockGradeCard(firstMockExam)}${mockGradeCard(secondMockExam)}<span><small>조회범위</small><b>${escapeHtml(periodLabel)}</b><em>전체 누적·학기별 전환</em></span></div><div class="consult-score-columns"><section class="consult-panel"><header><h4>최근 학기 내신 요약</h4><small>등급 우선 · 원점수 참고</small></header><div class="consult-table-scroll"><div class="consult-score-head consult-internal-grid"><span>과목</span><span>1차 등급</span><span>점수</span><span>2차 등급</span><span>점수</span><span>학기말 등급·성취도</span><span>점수</span></div>${internalRows}</div></section><section class="consult-panel"><header><h4>최근 모의고사 비교</h4><small>${escapeHtml(firstMockExam)} ↔ ${escapeHtml(secondMockExam)}</small></header><div class="consult-table-scroll"><div class="consult-score-head consult-mock-grid"><span>과목</span><span>${escapeHtml(firstMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))} 등급</span><span>원점수</span><span>표준</span><span>${escapeHtml(secondMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))} 등급</span><span>원점수</span><span>표준</span></div>${mockRows}</div></section></div><section class="consult-panel consult-semester"><header><h4>학기별 누적 평균 등급</h4><small>학기말고사 · 5등급제 기준</small></header><div class="consult-semester-grid">${semesterCells}<span class="total"><b>누적</b><em>${cumulative==null?"-":cumulative.toFixed(2)}</em></span></div></section>${cumulativeTables}<section class="consult-panel consult-targets"><header><h4>현재 성적 기준 대학 지원 가능성</h4><small>9등급 환산 내신과 대학별 목표 내신을 비교한 참고 판정</small></header>${targetRuleMarkup}<div class="consult-table-scroll"><div class="consult-target-head consult-target-grid"><span>대학명</span><span>모집단위</span><span>전형명</span><span>목표 내신</span><span>내신 판단</span><span>수능최저</span><span>${escapeHtml(firstMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))}</span><span>${escapeHtml(secondMockExam.replace("모의고사",""))}</span></div>${targetRows}</div></section></section>`;
}
function runScoreQuery() {
  const scope = $("#scoreScope")?.value || "class";
  const classNo = $("#scoreClass")?.value || "";
  const studentId = $("#scoreStudent")?.value || "";
  const exam = $("#scoreExam")?.value || "";
  const target = $("#scoreQueryResult");
  let rows = readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || [];
  if (scoreMode === "internal") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "내신");
  if (scoreMode === "mock") rows = rows.filter(x => x.scoreType === "모의고사");
  if (scorePeriod !== "all") rows = rows.filter(x=>scorePeriodKey(x)===scorePeriod);
  if (scope === "individual") {
    if (!studentId) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 먼저 선택한 뒤 학생을 고르고 조회하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    rows = rows.filter(x => x.studentId === studentId);
  } else {
    if (!classNo) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>반을 선택하세요.</b><span>반을 선택한 뒤 조회 버튼을 눌러야 성적이 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
    const ids = new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s=>s.id));
    rows = rows.filter(x => ids.has(x.studentId));
  }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope !== "individual") { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>종합성적은 개인별로 조회하세요.</b><span>반과 학생을 선택하면 학사상담 대시보드가 표시됩니다.</span></div>`; return; }
  if (scoreMode === "combined" && scope === "individual") {
    const student = (readonlyCache?.students || []).find(item=>item.id===studentId);
    if (!student) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>학생자료를 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>학생정보 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
    target.innerHTML = combinedScoreDashboardMarkup(student, rows); return;
  }
  if (exam) rows = rows.filter(x=>x.exam===exam);
  if (!rows.length) { target.innerHTML = `<div class="query-empty"><b>조회 결과가 없습니다.</b><span>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}의 반·시험 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`; return; }
  let averageRows=rows, averageLabel='5등급제 평균';
  if(scoreMode==='internal'&&scope==='individual'){
    const periods=[...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort();
    const latestPeriod=periods.at(-1)||'';
    const periodRows=latestPeriod?rows.filter(row=>scorePeriodKey(row)===latestPeriod):rows;
    const preferredExam=['학기말고사','2차고사','1차고사'].find(name=>periodRows.some(row=>row.exam===name));
    averageRows=preferredExam?periodRows.filter(row=>row.exam===preferredExam):periodRows;
    averageLabel=preferredExam==='학기말고사'?'최근 학기말 평균':preferredExam?`최근 ${preferredExam} 임시평균`:'5등급제 평균';
  }
  const gradeValues=averageRows.map(x=>Number(x.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const weightedAverage=uepCommonWeightedAverage(averageRows,x=>Number(x.level));
  const average=Number.isFinite(weightedAverage)?weightedAverage.toFixed(2):"-";
  const persons=new Set(rows.map(x=>x.studentId)).size, subjects=new Set(rows.map(x=>x.subject).filter(Boolean)).size;
  const modeLabel={internal:"내신",mock:"모의고사",combined:"종합"}[scoreMode]||"종합";
  const third=scoreMode==="mock"?`<span><b>${subjects}</b>과목별 등급</span>`:`<span><b>${average}</b>${escapeHtml(averageLabel)}</span>`;
  target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${persons}</b>명</span><span><b>${rows.length}</b>성적행</span>${third}<span><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))}</b>조회범위</span></div>${scoreTableMarkup(rows,scoreMode)}`;
}


function uepCommonCourseKey(value){
  return String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/[ⅠⅡⅢ]/g,ch=>({'Ⅰ':'I','Ⅱ':'II','Ⅲ':'III'}[ch]||ch)).replace(/[*＊]/g,'');
}
function uepCommonCreditOf(row){
  const key=uepCommonCourseKey(row?.subject||row?.course||row?.courseName||row?.['과목명']);
  const curriculumRows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.curriculumDb)?readonlyCache.curriculumDb:[];
  const credits=[...new Set(curriculumRows.filter(c=>uepCommonCourseKey(c?.['과목명'])===key).map(c=>Number(c?.['이수단위'])).filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0))];
  if(credits.length===1)return credits[0];
  const direct=[row?.credit,row?.credits,row?.courseCredit,row?.unitCredit,row?.['이수학점'],row?.['이수단위'],row?.['학점']].map(Number).find(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0);
  if(direct)return direct;
  console.warn('[UEP common weighted grade] credit unresolved',{subject:row?.subject||row?.['과목명'],row});
  return null;
}
function uepCommonWeightedAverage(items,valueFn,rowFn=(x=>x)){
  const weighted=(items||[]).map(item=>{const value=Number(valueFn(item));const row=rowFn(item);const credit=uepCommonCreditOf(row);return {value,credit};}).filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x.value)&&Number.isFinite(x.credit)&&x.credit>0);
  const credits=weighted.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.credit,0);
  return credits?weighted.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.value*x.credit,0)/credits:null;
}
function scoreStudentInfo(studentId){
  return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(student=>student.id===studentId)||{};
}
function scoreNumeric(value){
  const number=Number(String(value??"").replace(/[^0-9.\-]/g,""));
  return Number.isFinite(number)?number:null;
}
function scoreStatisticsRows(type,exam,classNo="all"){
  const wanted=type==="mock"?"모의고사":"내신";
  let rows=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.scoreType===wanted&&(!exam||row.exam===exam));
  if(classNo!=="all"){
    const ids=new Set((readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(student=>classNumberOf(student)===String(classNo)).map(student=>student.id));
    rows=rows.filter(row=>ids.has(row.studentId));
  }
  return rows;
}
function scoreStatisticsStudentButton(studentId,content){
  return `<button class="score-stat-student" data-score-stat-student="${escapeHtml(studentId)}">${content}</button>`;
}
function mockStatisticsMarkup(exam,classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("mock",exam,classNo);
  if(!exam)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>모의고사를 선택하세요.</b><span>시험별 등급 분포와 원점수 합 랭킹을 조회합니다.</span></div>`;
  if(!rows.length)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>${escapeHtml(exam)} 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>선택한 반 또는 성적 원본 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`;
  const subjectOrder=["국어","수학","영어","한국사"];
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=subjectOrder.indexOf(a),bi=subjectOrder.indexOf(b);
    if(ai>=0||bi>=0)return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
    return String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko");
  });
  const distribution=subjects.map(subject=>{
    const subjectRows=rows.filter(row=>row.subject===subject);
    const counts=Array.from({length:9},(_,index)=>subjectRows.filter(row=>Number(row.level)===index+1).length);
    const top2=counts[0]+counts[1],top4=top2+counts[2]+counts[3];
    return `<div class="score-grade-row"><b>${escapeHtml(subject)}</b>${counts.map((count,index)=>`<span><small>${index+1}등급</small><strong>${count}</strong></span>`).join("")}<em><small>1~2등급</small><strong>${top2}</strong></em><em><small>1~4등급</small><strong>${top4}</strong></em></div>`;
  }).join("");
  const groups=new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    if(!groups.has(row.studentId))groups.set(row.studentId,[]);
    groups.get(row.studentId).push(row);
  });
  const rankings=[...groups.entries()].map(([studentId,items])=>{
    const student=scoreStudentInfo(studentId);
    const find=subject=>items.find(row=>row.subject===subject);
    const korean=scoreNumeric(find("국어")?.rawScore);
    const math=scoreNumeric(find("수학")?.rawScore);
    const inquiryRows=items.filter(row=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(String(row.subject||"")));
    const inquiryScores=inquiryRows.map(row=>scoreNumeric(row.rawScore)).filter(value=>value!==null);
    const inquiry=inquiryScores.length?inquiryScores.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0):0;
    const total=[korean,math].filter(value=>value!==null).reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0)+inquiry;
    return {studentId,student,korean,math,inquiry,total,english:find("영어")?.level??"-",history:find("한국사")?.level??"-"};
  }).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total||String(a.student.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo||"")));
  const rankingRows=rankings.map((item,index)=>`<div class="score-ranking-row"><strong>${index+1}</strong>${scoreStatisticsStudentButton(item.studentId,`<b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name||"")}</span>`)}<span>${item.korean??"-"}</span><span>${item.math??"-"}</span><span>${item.inquiry||"-"}</span><em>${item.total||"-"}</em><span>${escapeHtml(String(item.english))}등급</span><span>${escapeHtml(String(item.history))}등급</span></div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 과목별 등급 분포</h3><p>1~2등급은 인서울 수능최저 참고, 1~4등급은 지역거점국립대 수능최저 참고 인원입니다.</p></div><span>${new Set(rows.map(row=>row.studentId)).size}명</span></div><div class="score-grade-head"><span>과목</span>${Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>`<span>${i+1}등급</span>`).join("")}<span>1~2</span><span>1~4</span></div>${distribution}</section>
  <section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 원점수 합 랭킹</h3><p>국어·수학·탐구 원점수 합으로 정렬하며 영어와 한국사는 등급만 표시합니다.</p></div></div><div class="score-ranking-head"><span>순위</span><span>학생</span><span>국어</span><span>수학</span><span>탐구 합</span><span>총합</span><span>영어</span><span>한국사</span></div>${rankingRows}</section>`;
}


function mockAllExamComparisonMarkup(classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("mock","",classNo);
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),'ko'));
  const preferred=['국어','수학','영어','한국사','통합사회','통합과학'];
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=preferred.indexOf(a),bi=preferred.indexOf(b);return(ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),'ko');});
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="query-empty"><b>모의고사 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  const head='<div class="score-grade-head"><span>과목·시험</span>'+Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>'<span>'+(i+1)+'등급</span>').join('')+'<span>1~2</span><span>1~4</span></div>';
  const body=subjects.map(subject=>'<section class="mock-compare-subject"><h4>'+escapeHtml(subject)+'</h4>'+exams.map(exam=>{const scope=rows.filter(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam),counts=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>scope.filter(row=>Number(row.level)===i+1).length),values=[...counts,counts[0]+counts[1],counts.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];return '<div class="score-grade-row mock-compare-row"><b>'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</b>'+values.slice(0,9).map((v,i)=>'<span><small>'+(i+1)+'등급</small><strong>'+v+'</strong></span>').join('')+'<em><small>1~2등급</small><strong>'+values[9]+'</strong></em><em><small>1~4등급</small><strong>'+values[10]+'</strong></em></div>';}).join('')+'</section>').join('');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>전체 모의고사 과목별 등급 비교</h3><p>왼쪽은 과목, 과목 안의 행은 3월·6월·9월·11월 시험이며 위쪽 등급별 인원을 동시에 비교합니다.</p></div><span>'+exams.length+'회</span></div>'+head+body+'</section>';
}

function mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects){
  const grades=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1),categories=[...grades.map(x=>x+'등급'),'1~2','1~4'];
  const values=(subject,exam)=>{const basic=grades.map(grade=>rows.filter(row=>row.subject===subject&&row.exam===exam&&Number(row.level)===grade).length);return[...basic,basic[0]+basic[1],basic.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];};
  const matrix=new Map(subjects.flatMap(subject=>exams.map(exam=>[subject+'|'+exam,values(subject,exam)])));
  const max=Math.max(1,...[...matrix.values()].flat()),chartMax=Math.max(max+4,Math.ceil(max*1.12)),W=1180,H=410,left=52,right=18,top=42,bottom=86,plotW=W-left-right,plotH=H-top-bottom,groupW=plotW/categories.length;
  const colors=['#0b8a78','#2f80ed','#f2994a','#9b51e0','#eb5757','#219653'],pairCount=Math.max(1,subjects.length*exams.length),gap=1,barW=Math.max(2,Math.min(8,(groupW-10-gap*(pairCount-1))/pairCount)),y=v=>top+plotH-(v/chartMax)*plotH;
  const grid=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const value=Math.round(chartMax*(4-i)/4),yy=top+plotH*i/4;return '<line x1="'+left+'" y1="'+yy+'" x2="'+(W-right)+'" y2="'+yy+'" stroke="#dfe9ec"/><text x="'+(left-8)+'" y="'+(yy+4)+'" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#718690">'+value+'</text>';}).join('');
  const bars=categories.map((category,ci)=>{const used=barW*pairCount+gap*(pairCount-1),start=left+ci*groupW+(groupW-used)/2;let n=0,markup='';subjects.forEach((subject,si)=>exams.forEach((exam,ei)=>{const v=matrix.get(subject+'|'+exam)[ci],x=start+n*(barW+gap),yy=y(v),opacity=Math.max(.32,1-ei*.18);markup+='<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+yy+'" width="'+barW+'" height="'+(top+plotH-yy)+'" rx="1.5" fill="'+colors[si%colors.length]+'" opacity="'+opacity+'"><title>'+escapeHtml(subject)+' · '+escapeHtml(exam)+' · '+category+' '+v+'명</title></rect>';n++;}));return markup+'<text x="'+(left+ci*groupW+groupW/2)+'" y="'+(H-42)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#315667">'+category+'</text>';}).join('');
  const subjectLegend=subjects.map((s,i)=>'<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px"><i style="width:11px;height:11px;border-radius:2px;background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+escapeHtml(s)+'</span>').join('');
  const examLegend=exams.map((e,i)=>'<span>'+escapeHtml(e)+(i===0?' 진하게':'')+'</span>').join(' · ');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>전 과목 시험별 등급 인원 통합 비교</h3><p>등급마다 과목별·시험별 막대를 쌓지 않고 각각 나란히 표시합니다.</p></div><span>'+subjects.length+'과목 · '+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:14px;margin-bottom:6px;font-weight:700">'+subjectLegend+'</div><small>'+examLegend+'</small><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:1050px">'+grid+bars+'</svg></div></section>';
}
function mockTrendStatisticsMarkup(classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("mock","",classNo);
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  const preferred=["국어","수학","영어","한국사"];
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=preferred.indexOf(a),bi=preferred.indexOf(b);return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko");});
  if(!scoreStatisticsTrendSubject||(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject!=="__all__"&&!subjects.includes(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject)))scoreStatisticsTrendSubject="__all__";
  const subject=scoreStatisticsTrendSubject;
  if(exams.length<2)return '<div class="query-empty"><b>비교할 모의고사가 부족합니다.</b><span>서로 다른 시험 자료가 2회 이상 연결되면 추이를 표시합니다.</span></div>';
  const renderSubject=subject=>{
  const grades=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1),categories=[...grades.map(x=>x+'등급'),'1~2등급','1~4등급'];
  const counts=exams.map(exam=>{const basic=grades.map(grade=>rows.filter(row=>row.exam===exam&&row.subject===subject&&Number(row.level)===grade).length);return [...basic,basic[0]+basic[1],basic.slice(0,4).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)];});
  const colors=['#0b8a78','#2f80ed','#f2994a','#9b51e0','#eb5757','#219653'];
  const rawMax=Math.max(1,...counts.flat()),chartMax=Math.max(rawMax+4,Math.ceil(rawMax*1.12)),W=1120,H=380,left=52,right=22,top=38,bottom=72,plotW=W-left-right,plotH=H-top-bottom;
  const groupW=plotW/categories.length,gap=Math.max(2,Math.min(7,groupW*.05)),barW=Math.max(5,Math.min(28,(groupW-gap*(exams.length+1))/exams.length));
  const y=v=>top+plotH-(v/chartMax)*plotH;
  const grid=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const value=Math.round(chartMax*(4-i)/4),yy=top+plotH*i/4;return '<line x1="'+left+'" y1="'+yy+'" x2="'+(W-right)+'" y2="'+yy+'" stroke="#dfe9ec"/><text x="'+(left-9)+'" y="'+(yy+4)+'" text-anchor="end" font-size="11" fill="#718690">'+value+'</text>';}).join('');
  const bars=categories.map((category,gi)=>{const used=barW*exams.length+gap*(exams.length-1),start=left+gi*groupW+(groupW-used)/2;return exams.map((exam,ei)=>{const v=counts[ei][gi],x=start+ei*(barW+gap),yy=y(v),h=top+plotH-yy,color=colors[ei%colors.length];return '<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+yy+'" width="'+barW+'" height="'+h+'" rx="3" fill="'+color+'"><title>'+escapeHtml(exam)+' · '+category+' '+v+'명</title></rect><text x="'+(x+barW/2)+'" y="'+Math.max(top+10,yy-5)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="'+color+'">'+v+'</text>';}).join('')+'<text x="'+(left+gi*groupW+groupW/2)+'" y="'+(H-27)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="800" fill="#315667">'+category+'</text>';}).join('');
  const legend=exams.map((exam,i)=>'<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-weight:800"><i style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</span>').join('');
  const tableHead='<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px repeat(11,minmax(70px,1fr));background:#28566f;color:white;font-weight:800"><span style="padding:11px">시험</span>'+categories.map(c=>'<span style="padding:11px;text-align:center">'+c+'</span>').join('')+'</div>';
  const tableRows=exams.map((exam,i)=>'<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:140px repeat(11,minmax(70px,1fr));border-top:1px solid #e2ebee"><b style="padding:11px;color:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'">'+escapeHtml(exam)+'</b>'+counts[i].map(v=>'<span style="padding:11px;text-align:center">'+v+'명</span>').join('')+'</div>').join('');
  return '<section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>'+escapeHtml(subject)+' 시험별 등급 분포 비교</h3><p>X축은 1~9등급과 누적 1~2·1~4등급, Y축은 인원입니다. 각 항목에서 시험별 막대를 나란히 비교합니다.</p></div><span>'+exams.length+'회</span></div><div style="padding:16px;overflow-x:auto"><div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:16px;margin:0 0 8px">'+legend+'</div><svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;min-width:980px">'+grid+bars+'</svg><div style="min-width:940px">'+tableHead+tableRows+'</div></div></section>';
  };
  const visibleSubjects=subject==="__all__"?subjects:[subject];
  const detail=visibleSubjects.map(renderSubject).join('')||'<div class="query-empty"><b>표시할 과목 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  return (subject==='__all__'?mockTrendOverviewMarkup(rows,exams,subjects):'')+detail;
}
function internalNineGradeCut(percentile){
  if(percentile<=4)return 1;
  if(percentile<=11)return 2;
  if(percentile<=23)return 3;
  if(percentile<=40)return 4;
  if(percentile<=60)return 5;
  if(percentile<=77)return 6;
  if(percentile<=89)return 7;
  if(percentile<=96)return 8;
  return 9;
}
function internalAchievementSummary(items){
  const achievements=items
    .map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||"").replace(/\d+$/,"").trim(),achievement:String(row.achievement||row.performance||row.level||"").trim()}))
    .filter(item=>item.subject&&item.achievement&&/^[A-E]$/i.test(item.achievement));
  if(!achievements.length)return {summary:"-",detail:""};
  const unique=[...new Map(achievements.map(item=>[`${item.subject}|${item.achievement.toUpperCase()}`,{subject:item.subject,achievement:item.achievement.toUpperCase()}])).values()];
  const detail=unique.map(item=>`${item.subject} ${item.achievement}`).join(" · ");
  return {summary:detail,detail};
}
function internalStatisticsMarkup(exam,classNo){
  const rows=scoreStatisticsRows("internal",exam,classNo);
  if(!exam)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>내신 시험을 선택하세요.</b><span>총점과 평균등급 기준 랭킹을 조회합니다.</span></div>`;
  if(!rows.length)return `<div class="query-empty"><b>${escapeHtml(exam)} 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>선택한 반 또는 성적 원본 자료를 확인하세요.</span></div>`;

  const groups=new Map();
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    if(!groups.has(row.studentId))groups.set(row.studentId,[]);
    groups.get(row.studentId).push(row);
  });
  const results=[...groups.entries()].map(([studentId,items])=>{
    const student=scoreStudentInfo(studentId);
    const gradeRows=items.filter(row=>{
      const level=scoreNumeric(row.level);
      return level!==null&&level>=1&&level<=5;
    });
    const scores=gradeRows.map(row=>scoreNumeric(row.rawScore)).filter(value=>value!==null);
    const levels5=gradeRows.map(row=>scoreNumeric(row.level)).filter(value=>value!==null);
    const actualNine=actualNineAverage(gradeRows);
    const levels9=actualNine.details.map(item=>item.result.grade).filter(value=>Number.isFinite(value));
    const achievement=internalAchievementSummary(items.filter(row=>{
      const level=scoreNumeric(row.level);
      return !(level!==null&&level>=1&&level<=5);
    }));
    return {
      studentId,
      student,
      total:scores.reduce((sum,value)=>sum+value,0),
      average5:uepCommonWeightedAverage(gradeRows,row=>scoreNumeric(row.level)),
      average9:uepCommonWeightedAverage(actualNine.details,item=>Number(item.result?.grade),item=>item.row||{}),
      gradeSubjects:levels5.length,
      allSubjects:gradeRows.length,
      achievement
    };
  });
  const totalRank=results.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total||((a.average5??99)-(b.average5??99)));
  const averageRank=results.filter(item=>item.average5!==null).slice().sort((a,b)=>a.average5-b.average5||b.total-a.total);
  const totalList=totalRank.map((item,index)=>`<div class="internal-ranking-row total"><strong>${index+1}</strong>${scoreStatisticsStudentButton(item.studentId,`<b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name||"")}</span>`)}<span>${item.allSubjects}과목</span><em>${scoreDisplay(item.total,1)}</em><small>평균등급 ${item.average5===null?"-":scoreDisplay(item.average5,2)}(5등급)</small></div>`).join("");
  const averageList=averageRank.map((item,index)=>`<div class="internal-ranking-row average"><strong>${index+1}</strong>${scoreStatisticsStudentButton(item.studentId,`<b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name||"")}</span>`)}<span>${item.gradeSubjects}과목</span><em>${scoreDisplay(item.average5,2)}<small>(5등급)</small></em><span class="nine-grade-ref">${item.average9===null?"-":scoreDisplay(item.average9,2)}<small>(9등급 참고)</small></span><span class="achievement-ref" title="${escapeHtml(item.achievement.detail)}">${escapeHtml(item.achievement.summary)}</span><small class="total-ref">${scoreDisplay(item.total,1)}</small></div>`).join("");
  return `<div class="internal-ranking-columns"><section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 총점 랭킹</h3><p>등급이 산출되는 과목의 원점수 합만 기준으로 정렬합니다. 성취도 A/B/C 과목은 총점에서 제외합니다.</p></div></div><div class="internal-ranking-head total"><span>순위</span><span>학생</span><span>등급산출 과목</span><span>총점</span><span>평균등급<br><small>(5등급)</small></span></div>${totalList}</section><section class="score-stat-section"><div class="score-stat-title"><div><h3>${escapeHtml(exam)} 평균등급 랭킹</h3><p>실제 5등급 평균만 랭킹에 사용합니다. 9등급 참고는 입시 등급환산과 동일하게 과목별 석차/수강자수로 계산합니다.</p></div></div><div class="internal-ranking-head average"><span>순위</span><span>학생</span><span>등급산출 과목</span><span>평균등급<br><small>(5등급)</small></span><span>평균등급<br><small>(9등급 참고)</small></span><span>성취도 과목</span><span>총점</span></div>${averageList}</section></div>`;
}
function scoreStatisticsView(){
  const rows=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const isTrend=scoreStatisticsType==="mocktrend",isCompare=scoreStatisticsType==="mockcompare", wanted=scoreStatisticsType==="internal"?"내신":"모의고사";
  const exams=[...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType===wanted).map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  const subjects=[...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType==="모의고사").map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
  if(!scoreStatisticsExam||!exams.includes(scoreStatisticsExam))scoreStatisticsExam=exams.at(-1)||"";
  if(!scoreStatisticsTrendSubject||(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject!=="__all__"&&!subjects.includes(scoreStatisticsTrendSubject)))scoreStatisticsTrendSubject="__all__";
  const selector=isTrend?`<label>과목<select id="scoreStatisticsTrendSubject"><option value="__all__" ${scoreStatisticsTrendSubject==="__all__"?"selected":""}>전체 과목</option>${subjects.map(subject=>`<option ${subject===scoreStatisticsTrendSubject?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(subject)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>`:isCompare?'':`<label>시험<select id="scoreStatisticsExam">${exams.map(exam=>`<option ${exam===scoreStatisticsExam?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(exam)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>`;
  const result=isTrend?mockTrendStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsClass):isCompare?mockAllExamComparisonMarkup(scoreStatisticsClass):(scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?mockStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsExam,scoreStatisticsClass):internalStatisticsMarkup(scoreStatisticsExam,scoreStatisticsClass));
  const label=isTrend?"시험별 추이":isCompare?"전체 시험 비교":scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?"모의고사 통계":"내신 통계";
  return `<div class="score-statistics-shell"><div class="score-stat-controls"><div class="score-stat-type"><button data-score-stat-type="mock" class="${scoreStatisticsType==="mock"?"active":""}">모의고사 통계</button><button data-score-stat-type="mockcompare" class="${isCompare?"active":""}">전체 시험 비교</button><button data-score-stat-type="mocktrend" class="${isTrend?"active":""}">시험별 추이</button><button data-score-stat-type="internal" class="${scoreStatisticsType==="internal"?"active":""}">내신 통계</button></div>${selector}<label>범위<select id="scoreStatisticsClass"><option value="all">1학년 전체</option>${availableClasses().map(no=>`<option value="${no}" ${scoreStatisticsClass===no?"selected":""}>${no}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-score-stat-run>통계 조회</button></div><div class="score-stat-guide"><b>${label}</b><span>기존 시험별 분포·랭킹은 유지하며, 추이 화면은 모든 모의고사를 시간순으로 비교합니다.</span></div><div id="scoreStatisticsResult">${result}</div></div>`;
}
function openStudentCombinedScore(studentId){
  const student=scoreStudentInfo(studentId);
  if(!student)return toast("학생정보를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  scoreMode="combined";
  scorePeriod="all";
  render("scores");
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const scope=$("#scoreScope"),classSelect=$("#scoreClass"),studentSelect=$("#scoreStudent");
    if(scope)scope.value="individual";
    if(classSelect)classSelect.value=classNumberOf(student);
    if(studentSelect)studentSelect.innerHTML=`<option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents(classNumberOf(student),studentId)}`;
    if(studentSelect)studentSelect.value=studentId;
    $("#scoreStudentLabel")?.classList.remove("hidden");
    runScoreQuery();
  },0);
}

function fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment() {
  const internal = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.scoreType === "내신");
  const counts = new Map();
  internal.forEach(row => {
    const value = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""));
    if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) counts.set(value, (counts.get(value) || 0) + 1);
  });
  const frequent = [...counts.entries()].sort((a,b) => b[1]-a[1] || b[0]-a[0])[0]?.[0];
  return frequent || (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(student => !/전출|자퇴|퇴학/.test(String(student.status || "재학"))).length || 259;
}
function fiveGradeBoundaryData(enrollment) {
  const total = Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(enrollment) || 1));
  const cumulativeRates = [0.10, 0.34, 0.66, 0.90, 1];
  const cumulative = cumulativeRates.map(rate => Math.round(total * rate));
  cumulative[cumulative.length - 1] = total;
  const counts = cumulative.map((value, index) => value - (index ? cumulative[index - 1] : 0));
  return { total, cumulative, counts };
}
function fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup() {
  if (scoreMode === "mock") return "";
  const data = fiveGradeBoundaryData(fiveGradeBoundaryEnrollment());
  const grades = data.counts.map((count, index) => `<span><small>${index + 1}등급</small><b>${count}명</b><em>${index ? data.cumulative[index - 1] + 1 : 1}~${data.cumulative[index]}등</em></span>`).join("");
  return `<details class="five-grade-boundary"><summary><div><b>5등급 경계 보정</b><span>수강자 ${data.total}명 기준 · 1등급 ${data.cumulative[0]}등까지</span></div><em>기준 보기</em></summary><div class="five-grade-boundary-body"><div class="five-grade-boundary-control"><label>수강자 수<input id="fiveGradeEnrollment" type="number" min="1" max="999" value="${data.total}"></label><button class="btn secondary" id="fiveGradeApply" type="button">경계 다시 계산</button><p><b>나이스 등급을 본 정보로 사용</b><span>아래 경계표는 수강자 수에 따른 확인용 보조정보입니다. 과목별 수강자 수가 다르면 해당 인원으로 다시 계산하세요.</span></p></div><div class="five-grade-boundary-grid" id="fiveGradeBoundaryGrid">${grades}</div><small>누적 비율 10%·34%·66%·90%에 수강자 수를 곱한 뒤 반올림하여 경계를 산출합니다.</small></div></details>`;
}

function scoresView() {
  const classes = availableClasses();
  const periods = availableScorePeriods(scoreMode);
  if (scorePeriod !== "all" && !periods.includes(scorePeriod)) scorePeriod = "all";
  const modeRows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row=>scoreMode==="combined" || row.scoreType===(scoreMode==="mock"?"모의고사":"내신")).filter(row=>scorePeriod==="all"||scorePeriodKey(row)===scorePeriod);
  const exams = [...new Set(modeRows.map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>scoreExamOrder(a)-scoreExamOrder(b)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
  const label={internal:"내신",mock:"모의고사",combined:"종합",statistics:"통계"}[scoreMode];
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs"><button data-score-mode="internal" class="${scoreMode==="internal"?"active":""}">내신</button><button data-score-mode="mock" class="${scoreMode==="mock"?"active":""}">모의고사</button><button data-score-mode="combined" class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"active":""}">종합성적</button><button data-score-mode="statistics" class="${scoreMode==="statistics"?"active":""}">통계</button></div>`;
  if(scoreMode==="statistics")return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-blue"><span>02</span><div><h3>성적 통계</h3><p>시험별 등급 분포와 학생 랭킹을 조회합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${scoreStatisticsView()}</div>`;
  const guide = scoreMode === "combined" ? "개인별 조회 시 최신 요약과 전체 누적 성적을 함께 표시합니다." : "전체 누적 또는 학기별로 범위를 바꿔 조회합니다.";
  const periodTabs = `<div class="score-period-tabs"><button data-score-period="all" class="${scorePeriod==="all"?"active":""}">전체 누적</button>${periods.map(key=>`<button data-score-period="${escapeHtml(key)}" class="${scorePeriod===key?"active":""}">${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(key))}</button>`).join("")}</div>`;
  const gradeBoundary = fiveGradeBoundaryMarkup();
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-blue"><span>01</span><div><h3>성적 조회</h3><p>내신·모의고사·종합을 나누어 조회합니다. ${guide}</p></div></div>${tabs}${periodTabs}${gradeBoundary}<div class="query-panel"><label>조회단위<select class="filter" id="scoreScope">${scoreMode==="combined"?`<option value="individual" selected>개인별</option>`:`<option value="class">반별</option><option value="individual">개인별</option>`}</select></label><label>반<select class="filter" id="scoreClass"><option value="">반 선택</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label id="scoreStudentLabel" class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"":"hidden"}">학생<select class="filter" id="scoreStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option></select></label><label class="${scoreMode==="combined"?"score-exam-muted":""}">시험<select class="filter" id="scoreExam" ${scoreMode==="combined"?"disabled":""}><option value="">${scoreMode==="combined"?"선택 범위 전체 자동 통합":`${label} 전체`}</option>${exams.map(x=>`<option>${escapeHtml(x)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" id="scoreQueryButton">조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="scoreCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="scoreQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="scoreQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>⌁</span><b>${label} 성적 조회 대상을 선택하세요.</b><small>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(scorePeriod))} 기준 · 조회 버튼을 눌렀을 때만 표시됩니다.</small></div></div></div>`;
}

function actualNineGrade(row) {
  let rank = Number(String(row?.rank ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  let enrollment = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  let derived=false;
  if((!Number.isFinite(rank)||rank<=0)&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(row?.rawScore))){
    const raw=scoreNumeric(row.rawScore);
    const peers=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(peer=>
      peer?.scoreType==="내신"&&String(peer.exam||"")===String(row?.exam||"")&&String(peer.subject||"")===String(row?.subject||"")&&
      String(peer.year||"")===String(row?.year||"")&&String(peer.semester||"")===String(row?.semester||"")&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore))
    );
    if(peers.length){
      rank=peers.filter(peer=>scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore)>raw).length+1;
      if(!Number.isFinite(enrollment)||enrollment<=0)enrollment=peers.length;
      derived=true;
    }
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(rank) || !Number.isFinite(enrollment) || rank <= 0 || enrollment <= 0) return null;
  const percentile = rank / enrollment * 100;
  const grade = percentile <= 4 ? 1 : percentile <= 11 ? 2 : percentile <= 23 ? 3 : percentile <= 40 ? 4 : percentile <= 60 ? 5 : percentile <= 77 ? 6 : percentile <= 89 ? 7 : percentile <= 96 ? 8 : 9;
  return { grade, percentile, rank, enrollment, derived, detail:`${rank}/${enrollment}명 = 상위 ${percentile.toFixed(1)}%${derived?' · 원점수 기준 추정석차':''}` };
}
function actualNineAverage(rows) {
  const details = rows.map(row=>({row,result:actualNineGrade(row)})).filter(item=>item.result);
  const average = details.length ? details.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item.result.grade,0)/details.length : null;
  return { average, details };
}
// v0.76.73 dashboard performance indexes: build once per connected-data generation.
let __uepStudentPerfIndex=null;
function uepStudentPerfIndex(){
  const scores=readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[];
  const programs=readonlyCache?.programs||state.programs||[];
  const reports=readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[];
  const night=readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||readonlyCache?.nightRecords||[];
  const selected=readonlyCache?.selectedSubjects||[];
  const signature=[scores,programs,reports,night,selected, scores.length,programs.length,reports.length,night.length,selected.length];
  const cached=__uepStudentPerfIndex;
  if(cached&&signature.every((v,i)=>cached.signature[i]===v))return cached;
  const scoreByStudent=new Map(), programByStudent=new Map(), reportByStudent=new Map(), nightByStudent=new Map(), selectedByStudent=new Map();
  const add=(map,key,value)=>{key=String(key||'').trim();if(!key)return;const list=map.get(key);if(list)list.push(value);else map.set(key,[value]);};
  scores.forEach(row=>add(scoreByStudent,row.studentId||row.studentNo,row));
  programs.forEach(program=>(program.students||[]).forEach(st=>{add(programByStudent,st.studentId||st.id,program);add(programByStudent,st.studentNo||st.number,program);}));
  reports.forEach(row=>{add(reportByStudent,row.studentId,row);add(reportByStudent,row.studentNo,row);});
  night.forEach(row=>{add(nightByStudent,row.studentId,row);add(nightByStudent,row.studentNo,row);});
  selected.forEach(row=>{add(selectedByStudent,row.studentId,row);add(selectedByStudent,row.studentNo,row);});
  __uepStudentPerfIndex={signature,scoreByStudent,programByStudent,reportByStudent,nightByStudent,selectedByStudent};
  return __uepStudentPerfIndex;
}
function uepRowsForStudent(map,student){
  const id=String(student?.id||student||'').trim(), no=String(student?.studentNo||'').trim();
  const a=id?(map.get(id)||[]):[], b=no?(map.get(no)||[]):[];
  if(!a.length)return b;if(!b.length||a===b)return a;
  return [...new Set([...a,...b])];
}
let __uepDashboardCounselCache=null;
function dashboardCounselCacheSignature(){
  const c=readonlyCache||{};
  return [c.students,c.scoreRecords,c.programs,c.programReports,c.reports,c.nightAttendance,c.nightRecords,c.selectedSubjects,c.counselRecords,
    c.students?.length||0,c.scoreRecords?.length||0,c.programs?.length||0,c.programReports?.length||0,c.counselRecords?.length||0,
    state?.growthCounselRecords?.length||0,Object.keys(state?.autoNoticeReceipts||{}).length];
}
function mockExamNames(studentId) {
  const order=["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const rows=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(studentId));
  return [...new Set(rows.filter(row=>row.scoreType==="모의고사").map(row=>row.exam).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
    const ai=order.indexOf(a),bi=order.indexOf(b); return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi)||a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  });
}

function admissionBase(studentId, mockExam = "6월모의고사") {
  const scores=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(studentId));
  const internal=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="내신");
  const preferred=["학기말고사","2차고사","1차고사"].find(exam=>internal.some(x=>x.exam===exam));
  const currentInternal=preferred?internal.filter(x=>x.exam===preferred):internal;
  const gradeRows=currentInternal.filter(x=>{const level=Number(x.level);return Number.isFinite(level)&&level>=1&&level<=5;});
  const curriculumRows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.curriculumDb)?readonlyCache.curriculumDb:[];
  const normCourse=s=>String(s||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/[ⅠⅡⅢ]/g,ch=>({'Ⅰ':'I','Ⅱ':'II','Ⅲ':'III'}[ch]||ch)).replace(/[*＊]/g,'');
  const creditOf=row=>{
    const course=normCourse(row.subject||row.course||row.courseName||row['과목명']);
    const matches=curriculumRows.filter(c=>normCourse(c['과목명'])===course);
    const credits=[...new Set(matches.map(c=>Number(c['이수단위'])).filter(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0))];
    if(credits.length===1)return credits[0];
    const direct=[row.credit,row.credits,row.courseCredit,row.unitCredit,row['이수학점'],row['이수단위'],row['학점']].map(Number).find(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0);
    if(direct)return direct;
    console.warn('[UEP grade weight] curriculum credit unresolved', {course,row});
    return null;
  };
  const weightedFive=gradeRows.map(row=>({level:Number(row.level),credit:creditOf(row)})).filter(row=>Number.isFinite(row.credit)&&row.credit>0);
  const fiveCredits=weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.credit,0);
  const avg5=fiveCredits?weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+(row.level*row.credit),0)/fiveCredits:null;
  const actual=actualNineAverage(gradeRows);
  const actualDetails=actual.details||[];
  const weightedActual=actualDetails.map(item=>({item,credit:creditOf(item.row||{})})).filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x.credit)&&x.credit>0&&Number.isFinite(Number(x.item.result?.grade)));
  const actualCredits=weightedActual.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.credit,0);
  const actual9=actualCredits?weightedActual.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+(Number(x.item.result.grade)*x.credit),0)/actualCredits:null;
  const mock=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="모의고사"&&x.exam===mockExam);
  return {preferred,currentInternal,avg5,actual9,actualDetails,mock};
}

function minimumCheck(rule, mockRows) {
  const minimumText=String(rule.minimum||"").trim();
  if (minimumText.includes("미적용")) return { result:"미적용", detail:"수능최저 미적용", cls:"" };
  const all=mockRows.filter(row=>row.level!==""&&row.level!==null&&row.level!==undefined).map(row=>({subject:String(row.subject||""),level:Number(row.level)})).filter(item=>Number.isFinite(item.level));
  const english=all.find(item=>item.subject==="영어");
  const history=all.find(item=>item.subject==="한국사");
  const englishLimit=Number(String(rule.english||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  const historyLimit=Number(String(rule.history||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||0);
  if(englishLimit && (!english||english.level>englishLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`영어(${english?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${englishLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  if(historyLimit && (!history||history.level>historyLimit)) return {result:"미충족",detail:`한국사(${history?.level??"-"}) > 별도기준 ${historyLimit}`,cls:"bad"};
  let count=Number(rule.areaCount||0);
  let sumLimit=Number(rule.sumLimit||0);
  const parsed=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:개\s*)?(?:영역\s*)?(?:등급\s*)?합\s*(\d+)/)
    || minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*합\s*(\d+)/);
  if(!count&&parsed) count=Number(parsed[1]);
  if(!sumLimit&&parsed) sumLimit=Number(parsed[2]);
  const eachRule=minimumText.match(/(\d+)\s*등급\s*(\d+)\s*개/);
  const inquiry=all.filter(item=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(item.subject)).sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  const core=all.filter(item=>["국어","수학","영어"].includes(item.subject));
  const inquiryCount=Number(rule.inquiryCount||0);
  let candidates=[...core];
  if(inquiryCount===1 && inquiry.length) candidates.push({...inquiry[0],subject:`탐구(${inquiry[0].subject})`});
  else if(inquiryCount>=2) candidates.push(...inquiry.slice(0,inquiryCount).map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  else candidates.push(...inquiry.map(item=>({...item,subject:`탐구(${item.subject})`})));
  candidates=candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.level-b.level);
  if(eachRule){
    const eachLimit=Number(eachRule[1]), eachCount=Number(eachRule[2]);
    const chosen=candidates.slice(0,eachCount);
    if(chosen.length<eachCount) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${eachCount}영역`,cls:"warn"};
    const detail=chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" · ")+` / 각 ${eachLimit}등급 이내`;
    const met=chosen.every(item=>item.level<=eachLimit);
    return {result:met?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:met?"good":"bad"};
  }
  if(!count||!sumLimit) return {result:"판단보류",detail:minimumText||"세부기준 확인",cls:"warn"};
  const chosen=candidates.slice(0,count);
  if(chosen.length<count) return {result:"자료부족",detail:`${chosen.length}/${count}영역`,cls:"warn"};
  const sum=chosen.reduce((total,item)=>total+item.level,0);
  const detail=`${chosen.map(item=>`${item.subject}(${item.level})`).join(" + ")} = ${sum} / 기준 ${count}합${sumLimit}${englishLimit?` · 영어≤${englishLimit}`:""}`;
  return {result:sum<=sumLimit?"충족":"미충족",detail,cls:sum<=sumLimit?"good":"bad"};
}

function universityNameKey(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .normalize("NFKC")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)|\[[^\]]*\]/g,"")
    .replace(/대학교/g,"대")
    .replace(/캠퍼스/g,"")
    .replace(/\s+/g,"")
    .replace(/[()·.\-_/]/g,"");
}
function universityMatches(value, query) {
  if (!query) return true;
  const a=universityNameKey(value), b=universityNameKey(query);
  return a.includes(b) || b.includes(a);
}
function admissionUniversities() {
  return [...new Set([...(readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums||[]).map(x=>x.university),...(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).map(x=>x.university)].filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>String(a).localeCompare(String(b),"ko"));
}
function minimumAllExamsMarkup(student, rules) {
  const exams=mockExamNames(student.id);
  const mockRows=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.studentId===student.id&&row.scoreType==="모의고사");
  if(!exams.length) return `<div class="query-empty data-card"><b>모의고사 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>개인별 수능최저 비교를 위해 모의고사 성적을 연결하세요.</span></div>`;
  return `<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${rules.length}</b>대학·전형</span><span><b>${exams.length}</b>전체 모의고사</span><span><b>과목별 등급</b>평균등급 미사용</span><span><b>계산식 표시</b>검증 가능</span></div><div class="data-card minimum-matrix-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>전체 모의고사 × 수능최저</strong></div><small>각 칸에 사용한 과목별 등급과 계산식을 표시합니다.</small></div><div class="minimum-matrix-scroll"><table class="minimum-matrix"><thead><tr><th>대학·전형</th><th>수능최저 기준</th>${exams.map(name=>`<th>${escapeHtml(name)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${rules.map(rule=>`<tr><th><b>${escapeHtml(rule.university)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(rule.type)} · ${escapeHtml(rule.name)} ${escapeHtml(rule.unit||"")}</small></th><td class="rule-cell">${escapeHtml(rule.minimum||"-")}<small>${escapeHtml([rule.inquiryCount?`탐구 ${rule.inquiryCount}과목`:"",rule.english?`영어 ${rule.english}`:"",rule.history?`한국사 ${rule.history}`:""].filter(Boolean).join(" · "))}</small></td>${exams.map(name=>{const check=minimumCheck(rule,mockRows.filter(row=>row.exam===name));return `<td><span class="minimum-result ${check.cls}">${check.result}</span><small>${escapeHtml(check.detail)}</small></td>`;}).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
}
function universityCohortMarkup(university, mode, rules, cuts) {
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const rows=students.map(student=>{
    const base=admissionBase(student.id);
    const exams=mockExamNames(student.id);
    const mockRows=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>row.studentId===student.id&&row.scoreType==="모의고사");
    const minMatches=[];
    rules.forEach(rule=>exams.forEach(exam=>{const check=minimumCheck(rule,mockRows.filter(row=>row.exam===exam));if(check.result==="충족")minMatches.push({rule,exam,check});}));
    const cutMatches=cuts.filter(cut=>base.actual9!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(cut.cut))&&base.actual9<=Number(cut.cut)).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.cut)-Number(b.cut));
    return {student,base,minMatches,cutMatches};
  });
  const internalRows=rows.filter(item=>item.cutMatches.length).sort((a,b)=>(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo)));
  const minimumRows=rows.filter(item=>item.minMatches.length).sort((a,b)=>b.minMatches.length-a.minMatches.length||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo)));
  const combinedRows=rows.filter(item=>item.cutMatches.length&&item.minMatches.length).sort((a,b)=>(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99));
  const internalTable=`<div class="data-card cohort-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${escapeHtml(university)} 내신 기준 충족 학생</strong></div><small>석차 기반 실제 9등급이 입결 이내인 학생</small></div><div class="data-head cohort-internal-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>실제 9등급</span><span>충족 모집단위·전형</span><span>입결</span></div>${internalRows.map(item=>{const cut=item.cutMatches[0];return `<div class="data-row cohort-internal-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.className)}반</span><strong>${item.base.actual9?.toFixed(2)||"-"}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(`${cut.unit||"-"} · ${cut.name||"-"}`)}</span><span>${Number(cut.cut).toFixed(2)}</span></div>`;}).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>현재 내신 기준 충족 학생이 없습니다.</b></div>'}</div>`;
  const minimumTable=`<div class="data-card cohort-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${escapeHtml(university)} 수능최저 충족 학생</strong></div><small>한 번 이상 충족한 모의고사·전형을 모아 표시</small></div><div class="data-head cohort-minimum-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>충족 모의고사</span><span>충족 전형 수</span><span>대표 계산식</span></div>${minimumRows.map(item=>{const exams=[...new Set(item.minMatches.map(x=>x.exam))];const first=item.minMatches[0];return `<div class="data-row cohort-minimum-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.className)}반</span><span>${escapeHtml(exams.join(" · "))}</span><strong>${item.minMatches.length}</strong><span><b>${escapeHtml(first.rule.name||first.rule.type||"")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(first.check.detail)}</small></span></div>`;}).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>현재 수능최저 충족 학생이 없습니다.</b></div>'}</div>`;
  const combinedTable=`<div class="data-card cohort-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${escapeHtml(university)} 내신·수능최저 동시 충족</strong></div><small>현재 자료에서 두 조건을 모두 충족한 학생</small></div><div class="data-head cohort-combined-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>실제 9등급</span><span>내신 충족</span><span>최저 충족 모의고사</span></div>${combinedRows.map(item=>`<div class="data-row cohort-combined-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(item.student.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(item.student.className)}반</span><strong>${item.base.actual9?.toFixed(2)||"-"}</strong><span>${item.cutMatches.length}개 모집단위</span><span>${escapeHtml([...new Set(item.minMatches.map(x=>x.exam))].join(" · "))}</span></div>`).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>동시 충족 학생이 없습니다.</b></div>'}</div>`;
  const summary=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${escapeHtml(university)}</b>대학 기준</span><span><b>${internalRows.length}</b>내신 충족</span><span><b>${minimumRows.length}</b>최저 충족</span><span><b>${combinedRows.length}</b>동시 충족</span></div>`;
  if(mode==="minimum") return summary+minimumTable;
  if(mode==="cuts") return summary+internalTable;
  return summary+combinedTable+internalTable+minimumTable;
}

function runAdmissionQuery() {
  const scope=$("#admissionScope")?.value || "individual";
  const studentId=$("#admissionStudent")?.value || "";
  const universityInput=($("#admissionUniversity")?.value || "").trim();
  const target=$("#admissionQueryResult");
  const students=readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const rules=(readonlyCache?.admissionMinimums || []).filter(x=>universityMatches(x.university,universityInput));
  const cuts=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts || []).filter(x=>universityMatches(x.university,universityInput));
  if(scope==="university") {
    if(!universityInput){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>대학을 선택하세요.</b><span>대학명을 입력하거나 자동완성 목록에서 선택하면 충족 학생을 모아 보여줍니다.</span></div>`;return;}
    if(!rules.length&&!cuts.length){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>연결 자료에서 대학을 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>자동완성 목록의 대학명을 선택하세요.</span></div>`;return;}
    target.innerHTML=universityCohortMarkup(universityInput,admissionMode,rules,cuts);
    return;
  }
  if(!studentId){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택하세요.</b><span>반 필터는 선택 사항이며, 학생을 고른 뒤 조회하세요.</span></div>`;return;}
  const student=students.find(x=>x.id===studentId);
  if(!student){target.innerHTML=`<div class="query-empty"><b>학생자료를 찾지 못했습니다.</b><span>학생정보 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>`;return;}
  const base=admissionBase(student.id);
  const head=`<div class="student-admission-head"><div><small>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)} · ${escapeHtml(student.className)}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(student.name)} 입시 조회</h3></div><span class="state">${escapeHtml(base.preferred || "내신 자료")} · 5등급제/실제9등급</span></div>`;
  const actualDetailTable=`<div class="data-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>석차 기반 실제 9등급</strong></div><small>석차 ÷ 수강자수 누적비율 기준</small></div><div class="data-head actual-nine-grid"><span>과목</span><span>원점수</span><span>5등급</span><span>석차/수강자</span><span>상위비율</span><span>실제 9등급</span></div>${base.actualDetails.map(({row,result})=>`<div class="data-row actual-nine-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</b><span>${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)}</span><span>${scoreDisplay(row.level,2)}</span><span>${result.rank}/${result.enrollment}</span><span>${result.percentile.toFixed(1)}%</span><span class="state">${result.grade}등급</span></div>`).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>석차·수강자수 자료가 없습니다.</b><span>산출제외 과목은 실제 9등급 계산에서 제외합니다.</span></div>'}</div>`;
  const conversion=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${base.avg5==null?"-":base.avg5.toFixed(2)}</b>5등급제 평균</span><span><b>${base.actual9==null?"-":base.actual9.toFixed(2)}</b>실제 9등급 평균</span><span><b>${base.actualDetails.length}</b>산출과목</span><span><b>${escapeHtml(base.preferred||"-")}</b>기준고사</span></div>${actualDetailTable}`;
  const cutRows=cuts.map(cut=>({...cut,diff:base.actual9==null?null:base.actual9-Number(cut.cut)})).filter(item=>item.diff==null||Number.isFinite(item.diff)).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.diff??99)-Math.abs(b.diff??99)).slice(0,100);
  const cutsHtml=`<div class="query-summary"><span><b>${base.actual9==null?"-":base.actual9.toFixed(2)}</b>실제 9등급 평균</span><span><b>${cuts.length}</b>입결자료</span><span><b>${universityInput?escapeHtml(universityInput):"전체"}</b>대학범위</span><span><b>${cutRows.length}</b>표시자료</span></div><div class="data-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>대학별 입결 비교</strong></div><small>실제 9등급 평균과 가까운 순</small></div><div class="data-head admission-cut-grid"><span>대학</span><span>모집단위</span><span>전형</span><span>입결</span><span>차이</span><span>판정</span></div>${cutRows.map(row=>`<div class="data-row admission-cut-grid"><b>${escapeHtml(row.university)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row.unit)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.name)}</span><span>${Number(row.cut).toFixed(2)}</span><span>${row.diff==null?"-":row.diff.toFixed(2)}</span><span class="state ${row.diff!=null&&row.diff>0.7?"warn":""}">${row.diff==null?"판단대기":row.diff<=0?"범위내":row.diff<=0.7?"도전":"상향"}</span></div>`).join("")||'<div class="query-empty"><b>입결 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>'}</div>`;
  const minimum=minimumAllExamsMarkup(student,rules);
  if(admissionMode==="conversion") target.innerHTML=head+conversion;
  else if(admissionMode==="minimum") target.innerHTML=head+minimum;
  else if(admissionMode==="cuts") target.innerHTML=head+cutsHtml;
  else if(admissionMode==="mock") target.innerHTML=head+minimum+cutsHtml;
  else target.innerHTML=head+conversion+minimum+cutsHtml;
}


function latestMockSnapshot(studentId){
  const names=mockExamNames(studentId);
  const exam=names[names.length-1]||"";
  const rows=uepRowsForStudent(uepStudentPerfIndex().scoreByStudent,String(studentId)).filter(r=>r.scoreType==="모의고사"&&(!exam||r.exam===exam));
  const main=rows.filter(r=>["국어","수학","영어"].includes(String(r.subject||"")));
  const inquiry=rows.filter(r=>!["국어","수학","영어","한국사"].includes(String(r.subject||""))).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.level||99)-Number(b.level||99)).slice(0,2);
  const mainLevels=main.map(r=>Number(r.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const inquiryLevels=inquiry.map(r=>Number(r.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const levels=[...mainLevels,...inquiryLevels];
  return {exam,rows,levels,mainLevels,inquiryLevels,max:levels.length?Math.max(...levels):null,best:levels.length?Math.min(...levels):null,
    mainWithin2:mainLevels.filter(v=>v<=2).length,mainWithin3:mainLevels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,mainWithin4:mainLevels.filter(v=>v<=4).length,
    allWithin4:levels.length>0&&levels.every(v=>v<=4),allWithin5:levels.length>0&&levels.every(v=>v<=5),detail:[...main,...inquiry].map(r=>`${r.subject} ${r.level}등급`).join(" · ")};
}
function studentPreparationStats(student){
  const idx=uepStudentPerfIndex();
  const programs=uepRowsForStudent(idx.programByStudent,student);
  const after=programs.filter(p=>/방과후/.test(`${p.kind||''} ${p.afterType||''} ${p.title||''}`)).length;
  const night=programs.filter(p=>/야간심화|심화수업/.test(`${p.afterType||''} ${p.title||''}`)).length;
  const selected=programs.filter(p=>/선택/.test(`${p.kind||''}`)).length;
  const reports=uepRowsForStudent(idx.reportByStudent,student);
  const nightAttendance=uepRowsForStudent(idx.nightByStudent,student);
  const subjectRows=uepRowsForStudent(idx.selectedByStudent,student);
  return {after,night,selected,reports:reports.length,nightStudy:nightAttendance.length,subjects:subjectRows.length};
}
function admissionManagementDataset(group){
  const rule=uepManagementRule(group);
  const standard={internal:rule.internal,name:rule.label,mockText:rule.mockText};
  const dataset=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).map(student=>{
    const base=admissionBase(student.id),mock=latestMockSnapshot(student.id),prep=studentPreparationStats(student);
    const directInternalOk=base.actual9!=null&&base.actual9<=standard.internal;
    const fiveKey=base.avg5==null?null:Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2);
    return {student,base,mock,prep,directInternalOk,fiveKey,standard};
  });
  const qualifyingFiveGroups=new Set(dataset.filter(x=>x.directInternalOk&&x.fiveKey!=null).map(x=>x.fiveKey));
  return dataset.map(x=>{
    const internalOk=x.directInternalOk||(x.fiveKey!=null&&qualifyingFiveGroups.has(x.fiveKey));
    const groupExpanded=internalOk&&!x.directInternalOk;
    const mockCore=uepMockCoreFor(group,x.mock);
    // 성장관리는 양쪽 축 모두 의미 있는 근접 상태일 때만 사용합니다. 관심관리는 한 축의 완전충족만 허용합니다.
    const mockPotential=uepMockPotentialFor(group,x.mock);
    const mockAny=mockCore||mockPotential;
    const participation=Boolean(x.prep.after||x.prep.night||x.prep.nightStudy||x.prep.selected||x.prep.reports);
    // v0.76.49 관리단계: 성적 기준만 사용합니다. 참여이력만으로 관리대상에 넣지 않습니다.
    // 핵심=내신+모의 핵심기준 모두 충족 / 성장=내신 충족+모의 일부 충족 / 관심=내신 또는 모의 한 종류만 충족 / 그 외=일반
    const stage=uepManagementStage(internalOk,mockCore,mockPotential);
    const reasons=[];
    if(x.directInternalOk)reasons.push(`9등급 평균 ${standard.internal} 이내`);
    else if(groupExpanded)reasons.push('기준 충족 학생과 동일한 5등급 평균 그룹');
    if(mockCore)reasons.push(standard.mockText);
    else if(mockPotential)reasons.push('모의고사 핵심기준 근접');
    if(participation)reasons.push('학습·프로그램 참여 이력');
    if(!reasons.length)reasons.push('현재 관리 기준 미충족');
    const managePoints=[];
    if(!mockCore)managePoints.push('취약 모의고사 영역 보완');
    if(!x.prep.after&&!x.prep.night)managePoints.push('방과후·야간심화 추천 검토');
    if(!x.prep.subjects)managePoints.push('선택과목·진로 연계 확인');
