    if(!x.prep.selected)managePoints.push('계열 연계 프로그램 참여 권장');
    if(!managePoints.length)managePoints.push('상승 추이와 수능최저 준비 지속 확인');
    return {...x,internalOk,groupExpanded,mockCore,mockPotential,stage,reasons,managePoints};
  });
}
function admissionManagementRows(group,filter=admissionManagementFilter){
  const order={core:0,growth:1,interest:2,general:3};
  return admissionManagementDataset(group)
    .filter(x=>x.stage!=='general'&&(filter==='all'||x.stage===filter))
    .sort((a,b)=>order[a.stage]-order[b.stage]||(a.base.actual9??99)-(b.base.actual9??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo)));
}
function admissionManagementMarkup(group){
  const fallbackRule=uepManagementRule(group);
  const dataset=admissionManagementDataset(group),standard=dataset[0]?.standard||{name:fallbackRule.label,internal:fallbackRule.internal,mockText:fallbackRule.mockText};
  const managed=dataset.filter(x=>x.stage!=='general');
  const counts={all:managed.length,core:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='core').length,growth:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='growth').length,interest:dataset.filter(x=>x.stage==='interest').length};
  const rows=admissionManagementRows(group,admissionManagementFilter);
  const stageLabel={all:'전체 관리 대상',core:'핵심관리',growth:'성장관리',interest:'관심관리'}[admissionManagementFilter]||'전체 관리 대상';
  const filters=`<div class="admission-manage-filters growth-filters"><button data-admission-manage-filter="all" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='all'?'active':''}">전체 <b>${counts.all}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="core" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='core'?'active':''}">핵심관리 <b>${counts.core}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="growth" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='growth'?'active':''}">성장관리 <b>${counts.growth}</b></button><button data-admission-manage-filter="interest" class="${admissionManagementFilter==='interest'?'active':''}">관심관리 <b>${counts.interest}</b></button></div>`;
  const stageName={core:'핵심관리',growth:'성장관리',interest:'관심관리'};
  return `<div class="admission-manage-note growth-note"><b>${standard.name} 성장관리 기준</b> · 내신과 모의고사 기준을 함께 충족하면 핵심관리, 양쪽에 근거가 있으나 모의 일부기준만 충족하면 성장관리, 내신 또는 모의 한 종류만 충족하면 관심관리로 분류합니다. 내신은 실제 9등급 평균 ${standard.internal} 이내와 동일한 5등급 평균 그룹을 보호대상으로 포함합니다.</div>${filters}<div class="admission-manage-summary"><span><b>${rows.length}</b><small>${stageLabel}</small></span><span><b>${rows.filter(x=>x.groupExpanded).length}</b><small>동일 5등급 그룹 포함</small></span><span><b>${rows.filter(x=>x.mockCore).length}</b><small>모의 핵심기준 충족</small></span><span><b>${rows.filter(x=>x.prep.after||x.prep.night||x.prep.selected).length}</b><small>학습·프로그램 이력</small></span><span><b>${rows.filter(x=>!x.prep.night&&!x.prep.after).length}</b><small>심화학습 추천 검토</small></span></div><div class="data-card"><div class="data-head admission-manage-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>반</span><span>관리단계</span><span>내신(5/9등급)</span><span>최근 모의고사</span><span>선정 이유</span><span>관리</span></div>${rows.map((x,i)=>`<div class="data-row admission-manage-grid growth-row" data-growth-student-id="${escapeHtml(x.student.id||'')}"><b>${escapeHtml(x.student.studentNo)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(x.student.name)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(/반$/.test(String(x.student.className||''))?x.student.className:`${x.student.className||''}반`)}</span><span><em class="management-stage stage-${x.stage}">${stageName[x.stage]}</em></span><span class="admission-grade-pair"><b>${x.base.avg5==null?'-':x.base.avg5.toFixed(2)} <small>5등급</small></b><strong>${x.base.actual9==null?'-':x.base.actual9.toFixed(2)} <small>9등급</small></strong><em class="${x.directInternalOk?'direct':x.groupExpanded?'expanded':'not-met'}">${x.directInternalOk?'직접 기준':x.groupExpanded?'동일그룹':'추이관찰'}</em></span><span><b>${escapeHtml(x.mock.exam||'-')}</b><small>${escapeHtml(x.mock.detail||'자료 없음')}</small><em class="${x.mockCore?'mock-ok':'mock-no'}">${x.mockCore?'핵심기준':'성장 가능성 확인'}</em></span><span class="management-reasons">${x.reasons.slice(0,2).map(r=>`<small>✓ ${escapeHtml(r)}</small>`).join('')}</span><button class="btn secondary" data-admission-manage-detail="${i}" data-admission-group="${group}">상세</button></div><div class="admission-manage-detail hidden" id="admissionManageDetail-${group}-${i}"><b>추천 이유</b> · ${x.reasons.map(escapeHtml).join(' · ')}<br><b>관리 포인트</b> · ${x.managePoints.map(escapeHtml).join(' · ')}<br><b>운영 원칙</b> · 이 단계는 합격 예측이 아니라 상담·프로그램 추천·성적 추적의 우선순위입니다.</div>`).join('')||'<div class="query-empty"><b>해당 관리단계의 학생이 없습니다.</b><span>다른 관리단계를 선택하거나 성적 연결 상태를 확인하세요.</span></div>'}</div>`;
}

function counselRecordLabel(row){
  return '상담 메모';
}
function studentCounselHistoryMarkup(student,limit=5){
  const rows=growthCounselRecords(student?.id).slice(0,limit);
  return `<section class="student-counsel-history"><div class="student-section-title"><h4>내 상담메모</h4><button class="link-button no-print" data-student-counsel-add="${escapeHtml(student?.id||'')}">상담 기록</button></div>${rows.length?`<div class="student-counsel-list">${rows.map(row=>`<article><div><b>${escapeHtml((row.createdAt||'').replace('T',' ').slice(0,16))}</b><small>내가 작성한 메모</small></div><p>${escapeHtml(row.memo||'')}</p><footer><button class="link-button no-print" data-student-counsel-edit="${escapeHtml(row.id||'')}">수정</button></footer></article>`).join('')}</div>`:'<p class="empty">내가 저장한 상담 메모가 없습니다.</p>'}</section>`;
}
function currentCounselStudent(studentId){
  const key=String(studentId||'').trim();
  return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(row=>String(row.id||'')===key||String(row.studentNo||'')===key)||null;
}
function closeCounselSidePanel(){document.querySelector('.counsel-side-panel')?.remove();document.body.classList.remove('counsel-panel-open');updateStudentSignalIndicator();}
function openCounselSidePanel(studentId,editId=''){
  const student=currentCounselStudent(studentId);if(!student)return toast('학생정보를 찾지 못했습니다.');
  closeCounselSidePanel();
  const records=growthCounselRecords(student.id), edit=editId?records.find(row=>String(row.id)===String(editId)):null;
  const teacherKey=String(currentNoticeUserId()||currentLoginTeacherName()||'user').replace(/[^0-9a-z가-힣_.@-]/gi,'_');
  const draftKey=`uep:counsel:draft:${teacherKey}:${student.id||student.studentNo}`;
  let draft='';try{draft=localStorage.getItem(draftKey)||'';}catch{}
  const candidate=findDashboardCounselCandidate(student.id);
  const panel=document.createElement('aside');panel.className='counsel-side-panel';panel.dataset.studentSignalId=String(student.id||student.studentNo||'');
  panel.innerHTML=`<header><div><small>PRIVATE COUNSEL MEMO</small><h3>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(student.name||'')}</h3><p>내 상담메모 · 작성자 본인만 표시</p></div><button type="button" data-counsel-panel-close>×</button></header><div class="counsel-side-body">${candidate?`<section class="counsel-recommend-context"><h4>UEP 추천 근거</h4>${(candidate.reasons||[]).slice(0,3).map(x=>`<p>• ${escapeHtml(x)}</p>`).join('')}</section>`:''}<section class="counsel-compose"><h4>${edit?'메모 수정':'새 상담 메모'}</h4><textarea rows="8" data-counsel-panel-memo placeholder="학생을 보면서 필요한 상담 메모를 남겨주세요.">${escapeHtml(edit?.memo||(edit?'':draft)||'')}</textarea><div class="counsel-side-actions">${edit?'<button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-counsel-panel-cancel-edit>새 메모로 전환</button>':''}<button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-counsel-panel-draft>임시저장</button><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-counsel-panel-save>${edit?'수정 저장':'저장'}</button></div><small><b>임시저장</b>은 이 PC에만 저장되고 상담완료로 처리되지 않습니다. <b>저장</b>은 상담기록 시트에 확정 저장됩니다.</small></section><section class="counsel-my-history"><h4>내 이전 상담메모</h4>${records.length?records.map(row=>`<button type="button" data-counsel-panel-edit="${escapeHtml(row.id||'')}"><span><b>${escapeHtml((row.createdAt||'').replace('T',' ').slice(0,16))}</b><small>${escapeHtml((row.modifiedAt&&row.modifiedAt!==row.createdAt)?'수정됨':'')}</small></span><p>${escapeHtml(row.memo||'')}</p></button>`).join(''):'<p class="empty">이 학생에게 남긴 상담 메모가 없습니다.</p>'}</section></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(panel);document.body.classList.add('counsel-panel-open');updateStudentSignalIndicator();
  panel.querySelector('[data-counsel-panel-close]').onclick=closeCounselSidePanel;
  panel.querySelector('[data-counsel-panel-cancel-edit]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>openCounselSidePanel(student.id));
  panel.querySelectorAll('[data-counsel-panel-edit]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>openCounselSidePanel(student.id,btn.dataset.counselPanelEdit));
  panel.querySelector('[data-counsel-panel-draft]').onclick=()=>{
    const memo=panel.querySelector('[data-counsel-panel-memo]')?.value||'';
    try{localStorage.setItem(draftKey,memo);}catch{return toast('이 PC에 임시저장하지 못했습니다.');}
    toast('상담 메모를 이 PC에 임시저장했습니다.');
  };
  panel.querySelector('[data-counsel-panel-save]').onclick=async()=>{
    const memo=panel.querySelector('[data-counsel-panel-memo]')?.value?.trim();if(!memo)return toast('상담 메모를 입력해 주세요.');
    const now=new Date().toISOString(), payload={id:edit?.id||'',studentId:student.id,studentNo:student.studentNo,name:student.name,grade:student.grade||'1',className:student.className||'',createdAt:edit?.createdAt||now,area:'상담메모',type:'교사 메모',memo,action:'',nextDate:'',status:'완료',group:candidate?.group||'',stage:candidate?.stage||'',discoveryReason:(candidate?.reasons||[]).join(' · '),writer:currentLoginTeacherName()||'사용자'};
    const saveBtn=panel.querySelector('[data-counsel-panel-save]');saveBtn.disabled=true;saveBtn.textContent='저장 중…';
    try{const result=await window.schoolBoard?.saveCounselRecord?.(payload);if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'상담메모 저장 실패');const list=Array.isArray(readonlyCache.counselRecords)?[...readonlyCache.counselRecords]:[];const ix=list.findIndex(x=>String(x.id)===String(result.record?.id||result.id));if(ix>=0)list[ix]=result.record;else list.unshift(result.record);readonlyCache.counselRecords=list;try{localStorage.removeItem(draftKey);}catch{}__uepDashboardCounselCache=null;openCounselSidePanel(student.id);toast('상담 메모를 시트에 저장했습니다.');}catch(error){saveBtn.disabled=false;saveBtn.textContent=edit?'수정 저장':'저장';toast(error?.message||'상담 메모 저장에 실패했습니다.');}
  };
}
function growthCenterMarkup(){
  const candidates=dashboardCounselCandidates();
  const candidateMap=new Map(candidates.map(row=>[String(classNumberOf(row.student)),row]));
  const teacher=String(currentLoginTeacherName()||'').trim();const connected=teacher?(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.counselRecords)?readonlyCache.counselRecords:[]).filter(row=>String(row.writer||'').trim()===teacher):[];const local=teacher?(Array.isArray(state.growthCounselRecords)?state.growthCounselRecords:[]).filter(row=>String(row.writer||'').trim()===teacher):[];const records=[...connected,...local.filter(x=>!connected.some(y=>String(y.id)===String(x.id)))].sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||'')));
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const followups=records.filter(row=>row.status!=='완료'&&row.nextDate&&row.nextDate<=todayKey);
  const admissionRecords=records.filter(row=>row.area==='입시');
  const classCards=Array.from({length:9},(_,index)=>{
    const cls=String(index+1),x=candidateMap.get(cls);
    if(!x)return `<article class="growth-class-candidate empty"><header><b>${cls}반</b><small>현재 추천 없음</small></header><p>현재 우선순위 기준에 해당하는 학생이 없습니다.</p></article>`;
    return `<button class="growth-class-candidate" data-counsel-student="${escapeHtml(x.student.id||'')}"><header><b>${cls}반</b><small>현재 1순위</small></header><strong>${escapeHtml(x.student.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(x.student.name)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml((x.tags||[]).slice(0,2).join(' · ')||'상담 추천')}</span><p>${escapeHtml(x.reasons?.[0]||'상담 추천')}</p><em>상담 완료/제외 시 다음 학생 자동 승계</em></button>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="growth-center-overview"><section class="growth-center-kpis"><span><b>${candidates.length}</b><small>반별 현재 추천</small></span><span><b>${followups.length}</b><small>확인일 도래</small></span><span><b>${admissionRecords.length}</b><small>입시 상담이력</small></span><span><b>${records.filter(x=>x.status!=='완료').length}</b><small>진행중 상담</small></span></section><section class="growth-class-queue"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>오늘의 발굴학생 · 반별 상담 우선순위 큐</strong></div><small>각 반 현재 1순위 · 완료/제외 시 다음 학생 자동 승계</small></div><div class="growth-class-grid">${classCards}</div></section><div class="growth-center-grid"><section class="data-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>선정 기준</strong></div><small>번호순이 아닌 상담 필요도 우선</small></div><div class="growth-rule-summary"><span><b>①</b> 9등급 2.5 경계</span><span><b>②</b> 9등급 4.0 경계</span><span><b>③</b> 내신↔모의 불균형</span><span><b>④</b> 프로그램 참여 부족</span></div></section><section class="data-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>입시 상담이력</strong></div><small>작성자 본인 상담 기록</small></div>${admissionRecords.length?admissionRecords.slice(0,8).map(row=>`<article class="growth-counsel-row"><div><b>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</b><small>${escapeHtml((row.createdAt||'').slice(0,10))}</small></div><p>${escapeHtml(row.memo||'')}</p><span>${escapeHtml(row.status||'진행중')}</span></article>`).join(''):'<div class="query-empty"><b>입시 상담이력이 없습니다.</b><span>상담하기에서 기록하면 작성자 본인에게만 표시됩니다.</span></div>'}</section></div></div>`;
}
function admissionsView() {
  const classes=availableClasses();
  const growthMode=admissionMode==='growth';
  const managementMode=["inseoul","regional"].includes(admissionMode);
  const mockMode=admissionMode==="mock";
  const label={growth:"성장관리센터",conversion:"등급환산",minimum:"수능최저",cuts:"대학별 입결",combined:"종합분석",mock:"모의고사 입시분석",inseoul:"인서울 관리",regional:"지거국 관리"}[admissionMode]||'성장관리센터';
  const needsUniversity=["minimum","cuts","combined"].includes(admissionMode);
  const allowUniversityScope=!growthMode&&!managementMode&&admissionMode!=="conversion";
  const universities=admissionUniversities();
  const tabs=`<div class="module-tabs admission-growth-tabs"><button data-admission-mode="growth" class="${growthMode?"active":""}">01 성장관리센터</button><button data-admission-mode="inseoul" class="${admissionMode==="inseoul"?"active":""}">02 인서울관리</button><button data-admission-mode="regional" class="${admissionMode==="regional"?"active":""}">03 지거국관리</button><button data-admission-mode="combined" class="${admissionMode==="combined"?"active":""}">04 종합분석</button><button data-admission-mode="mock" class="${mockMode?"active":""}">05 모의고사</button><button data-admission-mode="minimum" class="${admissionMode==="minimum"?"active":""}">06 수능최저</button><button data-admission-mode="cuts" class="${admissionMode==="cuts"?"active":""}">대학별 입결</button><button data-admission-mode="conversion" class="${admissionMode==="conversion"?"active":""}">등급환산</button></div>`;
  if(mockMode){
    return `<div class="module-page admission-mock-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>05</span><div><h3>모의고사 입시분석</h3><p>학생의 누적 모의고사를 내신·수능최저·대학입결과 연결해 입시 상담 기준으로 분석합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label>학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label><button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">입시 모의분석 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="admissionQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>◎</span><b>입시 분석할 학생을 선택하세요.</b><small>누적 모의고사와 내신·수능최저·대학입결을 함께 연결합니다.</small></div></div></div>`;
  }
  if(growthMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>01</span><div><h3>성장관리센터</h3><p>오늘 새롭게 발굴된 학생, 입시 상담이력, 다음 확인일을 한곳에서 관리합니다. 상담 원본은 학생정보 대시보드에 저장되고 이곳에서는 입시 유형만 활용합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${growthCenterMarkup()}</div>`;
  if(managementMode)return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='inseoul'?'02':'03'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>내신과 모의고사 기준 충족 정도에 따라 핵심·성장·관심 관리군을 구성하고 이력을 지속 관리합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}${admissionManagementMarkup(admissionMode==="inseoul"?"inseoul":"regional")}</div>`;
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="module-intro pastel-lilac"><span>${admissionMode==='combined'?'04':admissionMode==='minimum'?'06':'·'}</span><div><h3>${label}</h3><p>학생별 내신·모의고사·수능최저·대학입결을 연결해 상담 근거를 확인합니다.</p></div></div>${tabs}<div class="query-panel admission-query-panel admission-query-v418"><label>조회방식<select class="filter" id="admissionScope"><option value="individual">개인 학생 조회</option>${allowUniversityScope?'<option value="university">대학 기준 학생 찾기</option>':''}</select></label><label id="admissionClassLabel">반 필터<select class="filter" id="admissionClass"><option value="">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${c}">${c}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label id="admissionStudentLabel">학생<select class="filter" id="admissionStudent"><option value="">학생 선택</option>${optionStudents("")}</select></label>${needsUniversity?`<label id="admissionUniversityLabel">대학 선택<input class="search compact" id="admissionUniversity" list="admissionUniversityOptions" placeholder="대학명 입력 또는 선택"><datalist id="admissionUniversityOptions">${universities.map(name=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}"></option>`).join("")}</datalist><small>연결된 수능최저·입결 DB 기준 자동완성</small></label>`:""}<button class="btn primary" id="admissionQueryButton">${label} 조회</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionCounselButton">상담 기록</button><button class="btn secondary" id="admissionQueryReset">초기화</button></div><div id="admissionQueryResult"><div class="query-empty"><span>◎</span><b>${label} 조회 대상을 선택하세요.</b><small>${allowUniversityScope?"개인 학생 또는 대학 기준 학생 찾기를 선택할 수 있습니다.":"학생을 선택하면 석차 기반 실제 9등급을 표시합니다."}</small></div></div></div>`;
}

function studentMatches(item, student) {
  const studentId=String(student?.id||"").trim();
  const itemStudentId=String(item?.studentId||item?.학생ID||"").trim();
  if(studentId && itemStudentId && itemStudentId===studentId)return true;
  
  const no=String(student?.studentNo||"").replace(/\\.0$/,"" ).trim();
  const itemNo=String(item?.studentNo||item?.학번||item?.number||"").replace(/\\.0$/,"" ).trim(); return Boolean(no)&&Boolean(itemNo)&&itemNo===no;
}
function studentEntryDate(student){
  const raw=student?.entryDate||student?.statusDate||student?.moveInDate||student?.admissionDate||student?.enrollmentDate||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentLinkedRecordDate(item){
  const raw=item?.date||item?.day||item?.activityDate||item?.attendanceDate||item?.submittedAt||item?.finalSubmittedAt||item?.startDate||item?.endDate||item?.recordDate||item?.createdAt||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student){
  const entry=studentEntryDate(student);
  if(!entry||!/전입/.test(String(student?.status||student?.enrollmentStatus||student?.enrollmentReason||"")))return true;
  const recordDate=studentLinkedRecordDate(item);
  return !recordDate||recordDate>=entry;
}
function normalizedVolunteerTitle(value){
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/^\s*\((?:자율[·ㆍ.\s-]*자치(?:활동)?|진로[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동|동아리[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동)\)\s*/,"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(/중식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "중식도우미";
  if(/석식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "석식도우미";
  return text.replace(/급식/g,"");
}
function volunteerIdentity(item){
  return [String(item?.date||item?.endDate||item?.startDate||""),normalizedVolunteerTitle(item?.title||item?.type||"")].join("|");
}
function isVolunteerActivity(item){
  return /봉사|도우미|필사집|교과도우미|수업\s*지원/.test(`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`);
}
function isTrueClubActivity(item){
  const text=`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`;
  return /동아리/.test(text)&&!isVolunteerActivity(item)&&!/\(동아리활동\)/.test(text);
}

function studentRecordBundle(student) {
  // 07_학생활동이력은 마스터·봉사 체계에 넣기 어려운 기타 활동만 담당합니다.
  // 14_봉사활동확정에서 복제되어 들어온 행은 중복 표시하지 않습니다.
  const rawActivities=(readonlyCache?.activityHistory||[]).filter(item=>studentMatches(item,student)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student)&&!/14_봉사활동확정/.test(String(item.source||""))).map(item=>{
    if(!isVolunteerActivity(item))return item;
    const inside=/^\s*\((?:자율[·ㆍ.\s-]*자치(?:활동)?|진로[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동|동아리[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동)\)/.test(String(item.title||""));
    return {...item,area:inside?"교육과정 내 봉사":"교육과정 외 봉사"};
  });
  const selected=(()=>{
  const out=[],seen=new Set();
  for(const raw of (readonlyCache?.subjectSelections||[])){
    if(!studentMatches(raw,student)||!studentRecordWithinEnrollment(raw,student)) continue;
    const item={...raw,
      term:raw.term||raw.semester||raw.학기||raw.이수학기||'',
      semester:raw.semester||raw.term||raw.학기||raw.이수학기||'',
      subject:raw.subject||raw.standardSubject||raw.course||raw.과목명||raw.선택과목||'',
      status:raw.status||raw.신청상태||'현재 선택'
    };
    const key=[item.term,item.subject].join('|');
    if(!item.subject||seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);out.push(item);
  }
  return out;
})();
  const finalRecords=(readonlyCache?.finalRecordRows||[]).filter(item=>studentMatches(item,student)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student));
  const programs=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>studentRecordWithinEnrollment(program,student)&&(program.students||[]).some(item=>studentMatches(item,student)));
  const reports=studentCanonicalReports(student);
  const volunteer=(readonlyCache?.volunteerActivities||[])
    .filter(item=>studentMatches(item,student)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student))
    .map(item=>{
      const title=String(item.title||item.type||"");
      const inside=/^\s*\((?:자율[·ㆍ.\s-]*자치(?:활동)?|진로[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동|동아리[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동)\)/.test(title);
      return {...item,area:inside?"교육과정 내 봉사":"교육과정 외 봉사"};
    });
  // 봉사활동확정이 최우선입니다. 같은 날짜·활동·시간의 계획/이력 자료는 제거합니다.
  const confirmedVolunteerKeys=new Set(volunteer.map(volunteerIdentity));
  const activities=rawActivities.filter(item=>!isVolunteerActivity(item)||!confirmedVolunteerKeys.has(volunteerIdentity(item)));
  // 프로그램 메뉴의 공통·선택 마스터는 그대로 유지하고, 생활기록부 활동내역에서는
  // 진로활동만 학생 기록 카드에 보조 근거로 합칩니다. 봉사는 14_봉사활동확정을 직접 사용합니다.
  const careerPrograms=programs.filter(program=>/진로/.test(String(program.type||""))).map(program=>({
    id:`record-program-${program.id}`,studentId:student.id,studentNo:student.studentNo,
    date:program.date,area:"진로활동",type:program.type,title:program.recordTitle||program.title,
    role:[program.kind==="common"?"공통활동":"선택활동",program.teacher].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),
    organization:program.department||program.place||"",evidence:program.source||"프로그램 마스터",
    status:program.status||"",source:program.source||"프로그램 마스터",note:program.memo||""
  }));
  const mergedActivities=[...activities,...volunteer,...careerPrograms];
  return {activities:mergedActivities,selected,finalRecords,programs,reports,volunteer};
}



function reportTimestampValue(report){
  const raw=String(report?.finalSubmittedAt||report?.submittedAt||report?.date||'').trim();
  const serial=Number(raw);
  if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)return Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000;
  const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,'AM').replace(/오후/g,'PM'));
  return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:0;
}
function canonicalReportKey(report){
  const pid=unifiedProgramId(reportProgramId(report));
  if(pid)return `id:${pid}`;
  const name=[report?.reportGroup,report?.programName,report?.programTitle,report?.activityTitle,report?.title]
    .map(recordProgramMatchName).find(Boolean)||'';
  return name?`name:${name}`:`report:${String(report?.id||report?.reportId||'')}`;
}
function studentCanonicalReports(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[])
    .filter(r=>r&&r.submitted!==false&&studentMatches(r,student)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(r,student))
    .slice().sort((a,b)=>reportTimestampValue(b)-reportTimestampValue(a)||Number(b?.submissionCount||1)-Number(a?.submissionCount||1));
  const map=new Map();
  for(const row of rows){const key=canonicalReportKey(row);if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,row);}
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>reportTimestampValue(a)-reportTimestampValue(b)||String(a.reportGroup||a.programName||'').localeCompare(String(b.reportGroup||b.programName||''),'ko'));
}
function programRequiresStudentReport(program,student,reports){
  if(!program||program.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화/.test(`${program.afterType||''} ${program.title||''}`))return false;
  if(program.reportRequired===false)return false;
  if(program.reportRequired===true||program.reportDataConnected===true||String(program.reportFormUrl||'').trim())return true;
  return Boolean(student&&resolveStudentProgramReport(program,student,reports||studentCanonicalReports(student)));
}
function studentActivityView(student){
  const bundle=studentRecordBundle(student);
  const submittedReports=studentCanonicalReports(student);
  const programs=(bundle.programs||[]).map(p=>{
    const report=resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,submittedReports);
    return {...p,report,reportSubmitted:Boolean(report),reportSubmittedAt:report?.submittedAt||''};
  });
  const selectedPrograms=programs.filter(p=>p.kind==='selected');
  const commonReportPrograms=programs.filter(p=>p.kind==='common'&&p.reportRequired===true);
  const afterPrograms=programs.filter(p=>p.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(`${p.afterType||''} ${p.title||''}`));
  return {
    ...bundle,
    programs,
    submittedReports,
    holisticPrograms:[...selectedPrograms,...commonReportPrograms],
    selectedPrograms,
    commonReportPrograms,
    afterPrograms,
    career:studentCareerSupportBase_(student,bundle)
  };
}
function studentCareerSupportBase_(student,bundle){
  const profile=student?.careerProfile||{};
  const rows=(bundle?.selected||[]);
  const first=(...values)=>values.map(v=>String(v??"").trim()).find(Boolean)||"-";
  const hopeTrack=first(profile.hopeTrack,rows[0]?.track,rows[0]?.hopeTrack,rows[0]?.careerTrack,rows[0]?.trackHope,profile.track,student?.hopeTrack,student?.careerTrack,student?.desiredTrack);
  const hopeMajor=first(profile.hopeMajor,rows[0]?.major,rows[0]?.hopeMajor,rows[0]?.careerMajor,rows[0]?.majorHope,profile.major,student?.hopeMajor,student?.careerMajor,student?.desiredMajor);
  const aptitude=first(profile.universityAptitude,student?.universityAptitude,student?.aptitudeData,profile.aptitude,student?.careerAptitude,student?.career,"-");
  const text=`${hopeTrack} ${hopeMajor}`.replace(/-/g,"");
  const scores={"문과":0,"이과":0,"예체능":0};
  const add=(key,rx,w=1)=>{const m=text.match(rx);if(m)scores[key]+=m.length*w;};
  add("문과",/인문|국문|영문|언어|문학|사학|역사|철학|사회|정치|외교|행정|법학|경제|경영|회계|무역|교육|심리|언론|미디어|관광|사회복지/g);
  add("이과",/자연|수학|통계|물리|화학|생명|지구과학|공학|컴퓨터|소프트웨어|인공지능|데이터|의학|의예|치의|한의|약학|간호|보건|건축|전자|전기|기계|반도체|환경|식품/g);
  add("예체능",/미술|디자인|음악|성악|작곡|악기|체육|스포츠|무용|연기|연극|영화|영상|애니|만화|공예|사진|실용음악/g);
  const ranked=Object.entries(scores).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  let recommendation="혼합";
  if(ranked[0][1]>0 && (ranked[1][1]===0 || ranked[0][1]>=ranked[1][1]*2)) recommendation=ranked[0][0];
  return {hopeTrack,hopeMajor,aptitude,recommendation};
}

function studentCareerSupport(student,bundle){
  return studentCareerSupportBase_(student,bundle||studentRecordBundle(student));
}

function careerSupportMarkup(student,bundle,compact=false){
  const c=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
  return `<div class="career-support-strip ${compact?'compact':''}"><span><small>진로희망계열</small><b>${escapeHtml(c.hopeTrack)}</b></span><span><small>진로희망전공</small><b>${escapeHtml(c.hopeMajor)}</b></span><span class="career-recommend"><small>추천 계열</small><b>${escapeHtml(c.recommendation)}</b><em>참고</em></span><span><small>대학적성데이터</small><b>${escapeHtml(c.aptitude)}</b></span></div>`;
}

function recordAreaKey(value){
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  // 공통활동마스터의 교육과정 내 봉사는 진로 이수시간에 포함하지 않습니다.
  // 활동영역 문자열에 진로가 함께 들어 있어도 봉사활동을 우선 분리합니다.
  if(/봉사/.test(text))return "autonomy";
  if(/진로/.test(text))return "career";
  if(/자율|공통|선택/.test(text))return "autonomy";
  return "other";
}
function recordProgramMatchName(value){
  return String(value||"").normalize("NFKC")
    .replace(/\s*[（(\[]\s*(?:20\d{2}[.\-/년]\s*)?\d{1,2}[.\-/월]\s*\d{1,2}(?:[.\-/일]\s*)?(?:[~～\-–—]\s*(?:20\d{2}[.\-/년]\s*)?\d{1,2}[.\-/월]\s*\d{1,2}(?:[.\-/일]\s*)?)?\s*[）)\]]\s*$/u, "")
    .replace(/\s*[·|]\s*(?:20\d{2})?[.\-/년\s]*\d{1,2}[.\-/월\s]+\d{1,2}(?:\s*일)?\s*$/u, "")
    .replace(/[\s·ㆍ.,:;()\[\]{}'"`~!@#$%^&*+=_|\\/<>?–—-]+/g, "")
    .toLowerCase();
}
function unifiedProgramId(value){
  return String(value||"").trim().replace(/^(?:common-|selected-|after-(?:session|master|course)-)+/,"");
}
function unifiedProgramSemester(program){
  const explicit=String(program?.semester||program?.term||"").trim();
  if(/1/.test(explicit))return "1학기";
  if(/2/.test(explicit))return "2학기";
  const month=Number(String(program?.date||program?.startDate||"").slice(5,7));
  return month&&month<=7?"1학기":month?"2학기":"";
}
function resolveStudentProgramReport(program,student,reports){
  const pool=(reports||readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[]).filter(r=>r&&r.submitted!==false);
  const sid=String(student?.studentId||student?.id||"").trim();
  const sno=String(student?.studentNo||student?.number||"").replace(/\D/g,"").slice(-4);
  const studentPool=pool.filter(report=>{
    const rsid=String(report.studentId||"").trim();
    const rsno=String(report.studentNo||report.number||"").replace(/\D/g,"").slice(-4);
    return (sid&&rsid===sid)||(sno&&rsno===sno);
  });
  if(!studentPool.length)return null;

  // 공통활동은 마스터에서 보고서사용=Y인 활동만 보고서 연결 대상입니다.
  // 같은 이름의 후속 학기 활동에 이전 학기 보고서가 붙는 것을 원천 차단합니다.
  const kind=String(program?.kind||program?.programType||program?.activityType||"").toLowerCase();
  const isCommon=kind==="common"||String(program?.id||"").startsWith("common-");
  if(isCommon && program?.reportRequired===false)return null;

  const semester=unifiedProgramSemester(program);
  const semesterPool=studentPool.filter(report=>{
    const rSemester=String(report.semester||"").trim();
    return !(semester&&rSemester&&semester!==rSemester);
  });
  const preferredPool=semesterPool.length?semesterPool:studentPool;
  const newest=items=>items.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.finalSubmittedAt||b.submittedAt||b.date||'').localeCompare(String(a.finalSubmittedAt||a.submittedAt||a.date||'')))[0]||null;

  const pid=unifiedProgramId(program?.programId||program?.activityId||program?.id||"");
  if(pid){
    const exact=studentPool.filter(report=>unifiedProgramId(report.programId||report.activityId||report.sourceProgramId||report.linkedProgramId||"")===pid);
    if(exact.length)return newest(exact);
  }

  const pnames=[program?.recordTitle,program?.actualTitle,program?.title,program?.programName].map(recordProgramMatchName).filter(Boolean);
  const nameMatches=pool=>pool.filter(report=>{
    const rnames=[report.reportGroup,report.programName,report.programTitle,report.activityTitle,report.title].map(recordProgramMatchName).filter(Boolean);
    return pnames.some(name=>rnames.includes(name));
  });
  const preferredNames=nameMatches(preferredPool);
  if(preferredNames.length)return newest(preferredNames);
  const fallbackNames=nameMatches(studentPool);
  if(fallbackNames.length)return newest(fallbackNames);

  return semesterPool.find(report=>{
    const rnames=[report.reportGroup,report.programName,report.programTitle,report.activityTitle,report.title].map(recordProgramMatchName).filter(Boolean);
    return pnames.some(name=>rnames.some(rname=>name&&rname&&(name.includes(rname)||rname.includes(name))));
  })||null;
}

function reportProgramForRecord(report,bundle){
  return (bundle.programs||[]).find(program=>{
    const pid=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-|^selected-|^after-session-/,"");
    if(report.programId && String(report.programId)===pid)return true;
    const names=[program.actualTitle,program.title].filter(Boolean).map(recordProgramMatchName);
    const reportName=recordProgramMatchName(report.programName||report.reportGroup||"");
    return reportName&&names.some(name=>name===reportName||name.includes(reportName)||reportName.includes(name));
  })||null;
}
function recordProgramArea(program){
  return recordAreaKey([program?.type,program?.programType,program?.activityType,program?.recordTitle].filter(Boolean).join(" "));
}
function recordReportArea(report,bundle){
  const program=reportProgramForRecord(report,bundle);
  return recordProgramArea(program)!=="other"?recordProgramArea(program):recordAreaKey(report.kind);
}
function recordProgramReportCard(program,reports){
  const participant=(program.students||[])[0]||{};
  const report=(reports||[]).find(r=>{
    const pid=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-|^selected-|^after-session-/,"");
    return (r.programId&&String(r.programId)===pid)
      || recordProgramMatchName(r.programName||"")===recordProgramMatchName(program.actualTitle||program.title||"");
  });
  return `<article class="record-program-report-card">
    <div><b>${escapeHtml(program.recordTitle||program.actualTitle||program.title||"프로그램")}</b>
    <small>${escapeHtml([program.date,program.time,program.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||"운영정보 확인")}</small></div>
    <span class="state ${report?.submitted?"":"warn"}">${report?.submitted?"보고서 제출":"보고서 미제출"}</span>
    ${report?.id?`<button class="link-button no-print" data-open-student-report="${escapeHtml(report.id)}">보고서 보기</button>`:""}
  </article>`;
}
function recordAreaCollection(student,bundle,area){
  const programs=(bundle.programs||[]).filter(program=>program.kind!=="after"&&recordProgramArea(program)===area);
  const reports=(bundle.reports||[]).filter(report=>recordReportArea(report,bundle)===area);
  return {programs,reports};
}
function recordAreaPanel(student,bundle,area,label){
  const collection=recordAreaCollection(student,bundle,area);
  const programIds=new Set(collection.programs.map(p=>String(p.programId||p.id||"").replace(/^common-|^selected-|^after-session-/,"")));
  const orphanReports=collection.reports.filter(report=>!programIds.has(String(report.programId||"")));
  return `<section class="record-area-panel ${area}">
    <header><div><small>${area==="autonomy"?"AUTONOMY":"CAREER"}</small><h3>${label}</h3></div>
    <span>${collection.programs.length}개 프로그램 · ${collection.reports.filter(r=>r.submitted).length}개 보고서</span></header>
    <div class="record-program-report-list">
      ${collection.programs.map(program=>recordProgramReportCard(program,collection.reports)).join("")}
      ${orphanReports.map(report=>`<article class="record-program-report-card orphan"><div><b>${escapeHtml(report.reportGroup||report.programName||"보고서")}</b><small>${escapeHtml([report.submittedAt,report.matchStatus].filter(Boolean).join(" · "))}</small></div><span class="state">보고서 제출</span><button class="link-button no-print" data-open-student-report="${escapeHtml(report.id)}">보고서 보기</button></article>`).join("")}
      ${!collection.programs.length&&!orphanReports.length?'<div class="empty-inline">연결된 참여 프로그램·보고서가 없습니다.</div>':""}
    </div>
  </section>`;
}


function recordAreaLabel(area){return area==="autonomy"?"자율활동 특기사항":area==="career"?"진로활동 특기사항":"종합의견(행동발달 특기사항)";}
function recordAreaLimit(area){
  const rules=readonlyCache?.recordRuleRows||[];
  for(const row of rules){
    const text=Object.values(row).join(" ");
    if((area==="autonomy"&&/자율/.test(text))||(area==="career"&&/진로/.test(text))||(area==="behavior"&&/행동|종합의견/.test(text))){
      const numbers=[...text.matchAll(/(\d{3,4})\s*(?:자|글자|byte|바이트)/gi)].map(m=>Number(m[1]));
      if(numbers.length)return numbers[0];
    }
  }
  return area==="career"?700:500;
}
function recordRuleSummary(area){
  return (readonlyCache?.recordRuleRows||[]).filter(row=>{
    const text=Object.values(row).join(" ");
    return /생기부|생활기록부|특기사항|금지|관찰|문장/.test(text)
      && ((area==="autonomy"&&/자율|공통/.test(text))||(area==="career"&&/진로/.test(text))||(area==="behavior"&&/행동|종합|공통/.test(text))||!/자율|진로|행동|종합/.test(text));
  }).slice(0,8).map(row=>({source:row.__source||"규칙",text:Object.entries(row).filter(([k,v])=>k!=="__source"&&String(v||"").trim()).map(([,v])=>String(v).trim()).slice(0,4).join(" · ")}));
}
function recordEvidenceRows(student,bundle,area){
  const programs=(bundle.programs||[]).filter(p=>p.kind!=="after"&&(area==="behavior"||recordProgramArea(p)===area));
  const reports=(bundle.reports||[]).filter(r=>r.submitted&&(area==="behavior"||recordReportArea(r,bundle)===area));
  const reportByProgram=new Map();reports.forEach(r=>{if(r.programId)reportByProgram.set(String(r.programId),r);});
  const rows=programs.map(program=>{
    const pid=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-|^selected-|^after-session-/,"");
    const report=reportByProgram.get(pid)||reports.find(r=>recordProgramMatchName(r.programName||"")===recordProgramMatchName(program.actualTitle||program.title||""));
    return {id:`program:${pid}`,kind:"program",title:program.recordTitle||program.actualTitle||program.title,date:program.date||"",program,report};
  });
  reports.forEach(report=>{if(!rows.some(row=>row.report?.id===report.id))rows.push({id:`report:${report.id}`,kind:"report",title:report.reportGroup||report.programName||"학생 보고서",date:report.submittedAt||"",report});});
  if(area==="behavior")(bundle.activities||[]).forEach(a=>rows.push({id:`activity:${a.id}`,kind:"activity",title:a.title||a.type||"활동",date:a.date||"",activity:a}));
  return rows;
}
function recordEvidenceText(row){
  if(row.report)return [row.title,...(row.report.responses||[]).filter(Boolean)].join(" ");
  if(row.activity)return [row.activity.title,row.activity.role,row.activity.note,row.activity.evidence].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  return [row.title,row.program?.memo,row.program?.recordTitle].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}
function recordBehaviorCategory(row){
  if(row.program)return recordProgramArea(row.program)==="career"?"진로활동":"자율활동";
  const text=[row.activity?.area,row.activity?.type,row.activity?.title,row.activity?.role,row.title].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  const inside=/교육과정\s*내|창체\s*봉사|교내봉사/.test(text)&&!/교육과정\s*외/.test(text);
  if(inside)return "교육과정 내 봉사";
  if(/교육과정\s*외|1인\s*1역할|환경도우미|중식|석식|필사집|교과도우미|수업\s*지원|봉사/.test(text))return "교육과정 외 봉사";
  if(/진로/.test(text))return "진로활동";
  return "자율활동";
}
function recordEvidenceArticle(row){
  return `<article><input type="checkbox" data-record-evidence="${escapeHtml(row.id)}" ${recordWriterSelected.has(row.id)?"checked":""}><div><b>${escapeHtml(row.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([row.kind==="activity"?"활동":row.report?"보고서 제출":"프로그램 참여",row.program&&row.program.kind==="common"&&recordProgramArea(row.program)==="career"?recordDateText({...row.program,endDate:row.program.endDate||weekdaysBetween(row.program.date,"").at(-1)}):row.date].filter(Boolean).join(" · "))}</small></div>${row.report?.id?`<button class="link-button" data-open-student-report="${escapeHtml(row.report.id)}">보고서 보기</button><button class="link-button adopt" data-record-adopt-report="${escapeHtml(row.report.id)}">최종 보고서 채택</button>`:""}</article>`;
}
function recordEvidenceListMarkup(rows,area){
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="empty-inline">선택할 근거자료가 없습니다.</div>';
  if(area!=="behavior")return rows.map(recordEvidenceArticle).join("");
  const order=["자율활동","진로활동","교육과정 내 봉사","교육과정 외 봉사"];
  return order.map(label=>{const items=rows.filter(row=>recordBehaviorCategory(row)===label);return `<section class="record-evidence-group behavior-${label.includes("진로")?"career":label.includes("봉사")?"volunteer":"autonomy"}"><header><b>${label}</b><span>${items.length}건</span></header>${items.map(recordEvidenceArticle).join("")||'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;}).join("");
}


function currentRecordStudent(){
  return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>s.id===recordStudentId)||null;
}
function careerDateOverride(program,student=currentRecordStudent()){
  if(!program||!student||recordProgramArea(program)!=="career"||program.kind!=="common")return null;
  const activityId=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,"");
  const classNo=recordStudentClass(student);
  const key=recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo);
  try{
    const value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||"null");
    if(!value)return null;
    const range=semesterRangeForProgram(program);
    if(value.date&&(value.date<range.start||value.date>range.end)){
      if(value.confirmed||value.status==="edited"||value.status==="confirmed"){
        return {...value,rangeWarning:true,source:`${value.source||"담임 저장"} · 현재 프로그램 학기범위와 다름`};
      }
      localStorage.removeItem(key);
      return null;
    }
    return value;
  }catch{
    localStorage.removeItem(key);
    return null;
  }
}
function recordDateStatus(program,student=currentRecordStudent()){
  const override=careerDateOverride(program,student);
  if(override)return override;
  return {status:"range",date:"",period:"",source:"주간 운영기간",confirmed:false};
}
function dateKeyLocal(value){
  const d=new Date(value);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return "";
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}
function weekdaysBetween(start,end){
  let a=new Date(`${start}T12:00:00`),b=new Date(`${end||start}T12:00:00`),out=[];
  if(Number.isNaN(a.getTime())||Number.isNaN(b.getTime()))return out;
  // 공통 진로활동의 종료일이 없더라도 해당 날짜가 속한 월~금 전체를 조회합니다.
  if(!end||start===end){
    const day=a.getDay();
    const offset=day===0?-6:1-day;
    a=new Date(a);a.setDate(a.getDate()+offset);
    b=new Date(a);b.setDate(b.getDate()+4);
  }
  for(const d=new Date(a);d<=b;d.setDate(d.getDate()+1)){if(d.getDay()>=1&&d.getDay()<=5)out.push(dateKeyLocal(d));}
  return out;
}
async function inferCareerDateFromNeis(program,student,options={}){
  const classNo=recordStudentClass(student),activityId=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,"");
  const dates=weekdaysBetween(program.date,program.endDate||"");
  if(!dates.length)return {status:"error",date:"",period:"",source:"활동 주간 확인 필요",confirmed:false};
  const weekKey=`${classNo}:${dates[0]}`;
  const busyKey=`${activityId}:${classNo}`;
  if(!options.silent){recordCareerDateBusy=busyKey;render("records");}
  try{
    let result=recordCareerWeekCache.get(weekKey);
    if(!result){
      result=await window.schoolBoard?.loadNeisDashboard?.({
        schoolName:state.settings.neis.schoolName,
        officeCode:state.settings.neis.officeCode,
        schoolCode:state.settings.neis.schoolCode,
        grade:String(student.grade||state.settings.neis.grade||"1"),
        classNo:String(classNo),
        date:dates[0],
        force:true
      });
      if(result?.ok)recordCareerWeekCache.set(weekKey,result);
    }
    if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||result?.staleReason||"나이스 시간표를 불러오지 못했습니다.");
    const days=Array.isArray(result?.timetable?.days)?result.timetable.days:[];
    const normalizeYmd=value=>{const v=String(value||"").replace(/\D/g,"");return v.length===8?`${v.slice(0,4)}-${v.slice(4,6)}-${v.slice(6,8)}`:String(value||"").slice(0,10);};
    const candidates=[];
    days.forEach(day=>(Array.isArray(day.lessons)?day.lessons:[]).forEach(lesson=>{
      const subject=String(lesson.subject||lesson.itrtCntnt||lesson.name||"");
      const dayDate=normalizeYmd(day.date||day.ymd||"");
      if(/진로|진로와직업|창체.?진로/.test(subject.replace(/\s/g,""))&&dates.includes(dayDate))candidates.push({date:dayDate,period:String(lesson.period||""),subject});
    }));
    const byDate=[...new Map(candidates.map(x=>[x.date,[]])).entries()].map(([date])=>({date,slots:candidates.filter(x=>x.date===date)}));
    let value;
    if(byDate.length===1)value={status:"estimated",date:byDate[0].date,hours:byDate[0].slots.length,source:"NEIS 학반시간표 자동 추정",confirmed:false,weekStart:dates[0],weekEnd:dates.at(-1)};
    else if(byDate.length>1)value={status:"error",date:byDate[0].date,hours:byDate[0].slots.length,source:"해당 주간에 진로활동 날짜가 여러 건 확인됨",confirmed:false,candidates:byDate,weekStart:dates[0],weekEnd:dates.at(-1)};
    else value={status:"error",date:"",hours:"",source:`${dates[0]}~${dates.at(-1)} 시간표에서 진로를 찾지 못함`,confirmed:false,weekStart:dates[0],weekEnd:dates.at(-1)};
    localStorage.setItem(recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo),JSON.stringify(value));
    if(!options.silent)toast(value.status==="estimated"?`${classNo}반 진로활동: ${value.date} ${value.hours}시간으로 추정했습니다.`:value.source);
    return value;
  }catch(error){
    const value={status:"error",date:"",hours:"",source:`NEIS 조회 실패: ${String(error?.message||error)}`,confirmed:false,weekStart:dates[0],weekEnd:dates.at(-1)};
    localStorage.setItem(recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo),JSON.stringify(value));
    if(!options.silent)toast(value.source);
    return value;
  }finally{
    if(!options.silent){recordCareerDateBusy="";render("records");}
  }
}
async function autoInferCareerDatesForClass(student,bundle){
  if(recordCareerAutoRunning||!student||recordWriterArea!=="career")return;
  const programs=(bundle?.programs||[]).filter(p=>p.kind==="common"&&recordProgramArea(p)==="career");
  const unresolved=programs.filter(p=>{
    const value=careerDateOverride(p,student);
    return !value;
  });
  if(!unresolved.length)return;
  recordCareerAutoRunning=true;
  toast(`${recordStudentClass(student)}반 진로활동 날짜를 나이스 시간표에서 자동 조회합니다.`);
  try{
    for(const program of unresolved)await inferCareerDateFromNeis(program,student,{silent:true});
  }finally{
    recordCareerAutoRunning=false;
    render("records");
    toast(`${recordStudentClass(student)}반 진로활동 주간 조회를 완료했습니다. 노란색 날짜를 담임이 확인해 주세요.`);
  }
}

function academicTermRange(year,label){
  const y=Number(year||new Date().getFullYear());
  if(label==="여름방학")return {label,start:`${y}-07-22`,end:`${y}-08-17`};
  if(label==="2학기")return {label,start:`${y}-08-18`,end:`${y+1}-02-28`};
  return {label:"1학기",start:`${y}-03-01`,end:`${y}-07-21`};
}
function semesterRangeForProgram(program){
  const d=String(program?.date||"");
  const year=Number(d.slice(0,4)||new Date().getFullYear());
  if(d>=`${year}-07-22`&&d<=`${year}-08-17`)return academicTermRange(year,"여름방학");
  if(d>=`${year}-08-18`)return academicTermRange(year,"2학기");
  if(Number(d.slice(5,7))<=2)return academicTermRange(year-1,"2학기");
  return academicTermRange(year,"1학기");
}
function mondayKeysBetween(start,end){
  const a=new Date(`${start}T12:00:00`),b=new Date(`${end}T12:00:00`),out=[];
  const offset=(a.getDay()+6)%7;a.setDate(a.getDate()-offset);
  for(const d=new Date(a);d<=b;d.setDate(d.getDate()+7))out.push(dateKey(d));
  return out;
}
function careerSlotKind(subject){
  const s=String(subject||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  return /진로|진로와직업/.test(s)?"career":"";
}
async function scanSemesterCareerSlots(student,programs,forcedRange=null){
  if(recordCareerSemesterScanRunning||!student||!programs.length)return;
  const range=forcedRange||semesterRangeForProgram(programs[0]);
  programs=programs.filter(p=>String(p.date||"")>=range.start&&String(p.date||"")<=range.end);
  const classNo=recordStudentClass(student);
  const mondays=mondayKeysBetween(range.start,range.end);
  recordCareerSemesterScanRunning=true;
  recordCareerSemesterScanProgress=`${range.label} 시간표 0/${mondays.length}주`;
  render(state.activePage||"programs");
  const slots=[],sourceIssues=[];
  try{
    for(let i=0;i<mondays.length;i++){
      recordCareerSemesterScanProgress=`${range.label} 시간표 ${i+1}/${mondays.length}주`;
      render(state.activePage||"programs");
      const weekKey=`${classNo}:${mondays[i]}`;
      let result=recordCareerWeekCache.get(weekKey);
      if(!result){
        result=await window.schoolBoard?.loadNeisDashboard?.({
          schoolName:state.settings.neis.schoolName,
          officeCode:state.settings.neis.officeCode,
          schoolCode:state.settings.neis.schoolCode,
          grade:String(student.grade||state.settings.neis.grade||"1"),
          classNo:String(classNo),
          date:mondays[i],
          force:false
        });
        if(result?.ok)recordCareerWeekCache.set(weekKey,result);
      }
      if(!result?.ok){sourceIssues.push(`${mondays[i]} 주간 조회 실패`);continue;}
      const diagnostics=Array.isArray(result?.timetable?.diagnostics)?result.timetable.diagnostics:[];
      diagnostics.filter(d=>d.rowCount>0&&!d.complete).forEach(d=>sourceIssues.push(`${d.date} ${d.weekday}: ${d.maxPeriod}/${d.expected}교시`));
      const days=Array.isArray(result?.timetable?.days)?result.timetable.days:[];
      days.forEach(day=>(day.lessons||[]).forEach(lesson=>{
        const date=String(day.date||day.ymd||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
        const ymd=date.length===8?`${date.slice(0,4)}-${date.slice(4,6)}-${date.slice(6,8)}`:String(day.date||"").slice(0,10);
        const subject=String(lesson.subject||lesson.itrtCntnt||lesson.name||"");
        if(careerSlotKind(subject)&&ymd>=range.start&&ymd<=range.end)slots.push({date:ymd,period:String(lesson.period||""),subject});
      }));
    }
    const uniqueSlots=[...new Map(slots.map(x=>[`${x.date}|${x.period}`,x])).values()].sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date)||Number(a.period)-Number(b.period));
    const unique=[...new Map(uniqueSlots.map(x=>[x.date,[]])).entries()].map(([date])=>{const daySlots=uniqueSlots.filter(x=>x.date===date);return {date,hours:daySlots.length,subjects:[...new Set(daySlots.map(x=>x.subject))],periods:daySlots.map(x=>x.period)};});
    const sortedPrograms=programs.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||"").localeCompare(String(b.date||""))||String(a.actualTitle||a.title||"").localeCompare(String(b.actualTitle||b.title||"")));
    const diagnostic={range,classNo,programCount:sortedPrograms.length,activityDateCount:unique.length,totalCareerHours:unique.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.hours,0),sourceIssues,programs:sortedPrograms.map(p=>({id:p.id,date:p.date,title:p.actualTitle||p.title})),slots:unique};
    localStorage.setItem(`uep-career-semester-diagnostic:${classNo}:${range.start}`,JSON.stringify(diagnostic));

    // Every run clears previous automatic mappings in the selected term first.
    sortedPrograms.forEach(program=>{
      const activityId=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,"");
      const key=recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo);
      try{
        const old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||"null");
        if(old&&!old.confirmed)localStorage.removeItem(key);
      }catch{localStorage.removeItem(key);}
    });

    if(sourceIssues.length){
      toast(`NEIS 시간표 원본에 누락 의심 ${sourceIssues.length}건이 있어 자동매칭을 중단했습니다. 학급시간표 진단을 먼저 확인하세요.`);
      return;
    }
    if(sortedPrograms.length!==unique.length){
      toast(`${range.label} 공통 진로활동 ${sortedPrograms.length}개와 실제 진로활동 날짜 ${unique.length}개의 수가 달라 자동매칭하지 않았습니다. 기본연결시트 활동 수 또는 NEIS 시간표를 확인하세요.`);
      return;
    }

    sortedPrograms.forEach((program,index)=>{
      const slot=unique[index];
      const activityId=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,"");
      const value={status:"estimated",date:slot.date,hours:slot.hours,source:`${range.label} 날짜순 1:1 매칭 · ${slot.hours}시간 · ${slot.subjects.join(", ")} · ${index+1}/${unique.length}`,confirmed:false,semesterScan:true,plannedDate:String(program.date||""),matchedSubject:slot.subjects.join(", "),sequence:index+1,confidence:"순서매칭"};
      localStorage.setItem(recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo),JSON.stringify(value));
    });
    toast(`${classNo}반 ${range.label}: 공통 진로활동 ${sortedPrograms.length}개와 실제 진로활동 날짜 ${unique.length}개를 매칭하고 이수시간을 합산했습니다.`);
  }catch(error){
    toast(`학기 시간표 분석 실패: ${String(error?.message||error)}`);
  }finally{
    recordCareerSemesterScanRunning=false;
    recordCareerSemesterScanProgress="";
    render(state.activePage||"programs");
  }
}
function careerMatchedHours(value){
  if(Number(value?.hours)>0)return Number(value.hours);
  const periods=String(value?.period||"").split(",").map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return periods.length||"";
}
function saveCareerDate(program,student,date,hours,status){
  const activityId=String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,""),classNo=recordStudentClass(student);
  const value={status,date,hours:Number(hours)||"",source:status==="confirmed"?"담임 확인":"담임 수정",confirmed:true,manualLocked:true,updatedAt:new Date().toLocaleString("ko-KR")};
  localStorage.setItem(recordCareerDateKey(activityId,classNo),JSON.stringify(value));return value;
}
function careerDateEditor(program,student){
  if(recordProgramArea(program)!=="career"||program.kind!=="common")return "";
  const value=recordDateStatus(program,student);
  const key=`${String(program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^common-/,"")}:${recordStudentClass(student)}`;
  const cls=value.status||"range";
  return `<div class="career-date-editor ${cls}">
    <span class="career-date-state">${value.rangeWarning?"범위 확인":cls==="confirmed"?"확인":cls==="edited"?"수정":cls==="estimated"?(value.semesterScan?"학기 매칭":"자동 추정"):cls==="error"?"확인 필요":"주간 기간"}</span>
    <input type="date" data-career-date-input="${escapeHtml(key)}" value="${escapeHtml(value.date||"")}">
    <input type="number" min="1" max="20" step="1" placeholder="이수시간" data-career-hours-input="${escapeHtml(key)}" value="${escapeHtml(careerMatchedHours(value))}">
    <button class="link-button" data-career-date-infer="${escapeHtml(program.id)}" ${recordCareerDateBusy===key?"disabled":""}>${recordCareerDateBusy===key?"조회 중":"NEIS 조회"}</button>
    ${value.status==="estimated"?`<button class="link-button confirm" data-career-date-confirm="${escapeHtml(program.id)}">담임 확인</button>`:""}
    <button class="link-button" data-career-date-save="${escapeHtml(program.id)}">수정 저장</button>
    <small>운영기간 ${escapeHtml(recordDateText({...program,date:program.date,endDate:program.endDate||weekdaysBetween(program.date,"").at(-1)}))} · ${escapeHtml(value.source||"")} ${careerMatchedHours(value)?`· ${escapeHtml(careerMatchedHours(value))}시간`:""}</small>
  </div>`;
}
function enforceTeacherEnding(text){
  const teacherEnding=/함\.?$|됨\.?$|임\.?$|보임\.?$|돋보임\.?$|기여함\.?$|발휘함\.?$|확장함\.?$|심화함\.?$|이해함\.?$|수행함\.?$|참여함\.?$/;
  const convertSentence=sentence=>{
    let s=String(sentence||"").replace(/…+$/,"").trim();if(!s)return "";
    const replacements=[
      [/하였습니다?\.?$/,"함."],[/했습니다?\.?$/,"함."],[/하였다\.?$/,"함."],[/했다\.?$/,"함."],
      [/되었다\.?$/,"됨."],[/이었다\.?$/,"임."],[/알았다\.?$/,"이해함."],[/느꼈다\.?$/,"관심을 보임."],
      [/생각했다\.?$/,"자신의 생각을 구체화함."],[/노력했다\.?$/,"꾸준히 노력하는 모습을 보임."],
      [/참여하여\.?$/,"참여함."],[/활동하여\.?$/,"활동을 수행함."],[/관찰하여\.?$/,"관찰 내용을 정리함."]
    ];
    for(const [rx,repl] of replacements){if(rx.test(s)){s=s.replace(rx,repl);break;}}
    if(!teacherEnding.test(s)){
      s=s.replace(/[,.·\s]+$/,"");
      if(/(?:캠페인|활동|실험|특강|프로그램|수련활동|방학식|종업식|입학식)$/.test(s))s+="에 성실히 참여함.";
      else if(/(?:참여하여|수행하여|탐구하여|분석하여)$/.test(s))s=s.replace(/하여$/,"함.");
      else s+="을 바탕으로 활동 내용을 정리함.";
    }
    return s;
  };
  return String(text||"").split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+|(?<=…)\s+/).map(convertSentence).filter(Boolean).join(" ");
}
function normalizedSentenceTokens(text){
  return new Set(String(text||"").replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu," ").split(/\s+/).filter(x=>x.length>=2));
}
function jaccardSimilarity(a,b){
  const A=normalizedSentenceTokens(a),B=normalizedSentenceTokens(b);
  if(!A.size||!B.size)return 0;
  let both=0;A.forEach(x=>{if(B.has(x))both++;});
  return both/(A.size+B.size-both);
}
function savedDraftsForArea(area,excludeStudentId){
  const rows=[];
  for(let i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){
    const key=localStorage.key(i)||"";
    if(!key.startsWith("uep-record-draft:"))continue;
    const parts=key.split(":");const studentId=parts[1],draftArea=parts[2];
    if(draftArea===area&&studentId!==excludeStudentId)rows.push({studentId,text:localStorage.getItem(key)||""});
  }
  return rows;
}
const OBSERVATION_TAGS=[
  ["faithful","활동 안내를 성실히 따르며 주어진 과정에 참여함"],
  ["listening","다른 학생의 의견과 설명을 경청함"],
  ["role","맡은 역할을 책임감 있게 수행함"],
  ["question","궁금한 내용을 질문하거나 추가 자료를 찾아봄"],
  ["cooperate","구성원과 협력하여 활동을 수행함"],
  ["organize","활동 후 핵심 내용을 정리함"],
  ["persistence","과제를 끝까지 수행하려는 태도를 보임"],
  ["consideration","주변 학생을 배려하며 공동 활동에 참여함"]
];
function selectedObservationTags(student,area){
  try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(recordObservationKey(student.id,area))||"[]");}catch{return [];}
}
function observationText(student,area){
  const selected=new Set(selectedObservationTags(student,area));
  return OBSERVATION_TAGS.filter(([id])=>selected.has(id)).map(([,text])=>text).join(" ");
}
function lowEvidencePanel(student,bundle,area){
  const evidence=recordEvidenceRows(student,bundle,area);
  const reports=evidence.filter(x=>x.report).length;
  const low=evidence.length<2||reports===0;
  const selected=new Set(selectedObservationTags(student,area));
  return `<section class="low-evidence-panel ${low?"attention":""}">
    <header><div><small>TEACHER OBSERVATION</small><h3>${low?"기록 근거 보완 필요":"담임 관찰 근거 추가"}</h3></div><span>${reports}개 보고서 · ${evidence.length}개 활동</span></header>
    <p>${low?"보고서가 부족하므로 실제 관찰한 항목만 선택하세요. 선택하지 않은 내용을 AI가 임의로 만들지 않습니다.":"활동자료와 함께 반영할 실제 관찰내용이 있을 때만 선택하세요."}</p>
    <div class="observation-tags">${OBSERVATION_TAGS.map(([id,label])=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-observation-tag="${id}" ${selected.has(id)?"checked":""}><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span></label>`).join("")}</div>
  </section>`;
}
function recordDateText(program){
  const override=careerDateOverride(program);
  const selectedDate=override?.confirmed&&override.date?override.date:"";
  const start=String(selectedDate||program?.date||"").trim(),end=selectedDate?"":String(program?.endDate||"").trim();
  const fmt=value=>{const m=String(value||"").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);return m?`${Number(m[1])}.${Number(m[2])}.${Number(m[3])}.`:String(value||"");};
  if(start&&end&&start!==end)return `${fmt(start)}-${fmt(end)}`;
  return fmt(start||end);
}
function recordProgramHeading(row){
  const p=row.program||reportProgramForRecord(row.report,{programs:readonlyCache?.programs||[]})||{};
  // 운호고 생활기록부 문장에는 프로그램 운영일을 기재하지 않습니다.
  return p.recordTitle||p.actualTitle||p.title||row.title||row.report?.programName||"프로그램";
}
function recordEvidenceSignals(row,area){
  const responses=(row.report?.responses||[]).map(v=>String(v||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const text=responses.join(" ");
  const signals=[];
  if(/탐구|조사|자료|분석|비교|원리|실험|관찰|질문|추가/.test(text))signals.push("탐구");
  if(/협력|협업|의견|조율|모둠|팀|경청|역할/.test(text))signals.push("협업");
  if(/문제|어려움|해결|개선|대안|방법/.test(text))signals.push("문제해결");
  if(/진로|전공|학과|직업|관심 분야|관심분야/.test(text))signals.push("진로연계");
  if(/성장|변화|성찰|돌아보|배운|알게/.test(text))signals.push("성찰");
  if(/실천|적용|계획|이어|앞으로|후속/.test(text))signals.push("후속실천");
  if(area==="behavior"&&/봉사|도우미|공동체|배려|책임|임원|회장|반장|실장/.test(`${row.title||""} ${text}`))signals.push("공동체");
  return [...new Set(signals)];
}
function recordEvidenceTopic(row){
  if(!row.report)return "";
  const a=analyzeReportTags(row.report);
  return a.majors?.[0]?.label||a.careers?.[0]?.label||a.sdgs?.[0]?.label?.replace(/^SDG\s*\d+\s*/,"")||"";
}
function reportSpecificTerms(row){
  const text=(row.report?.responses||[]).join(" ");
  const catalog=[
    ["유전자 재조합",/유전자\s*재조합/],["인슐린",/인슐린/],["항생물질",/항생/],["박테리아",/박테리아|세균/],["인공지능",/인공지능|\bAI\b/i],["생명공학",/생명공학|바이오/],["반도체",/반도체|웨이퍼|집적회로/],["데이터 분석",/데이터\s*분석|통계|그래프/],["환경",/환경|기후|탄소/],["생태계",/생태계|생물다양성/],["식량 문제",/식량/],["자원 문제",/자원/],["에너지 문제",/에너지/],["경제·금융",/경제|금융/],["법·정의",/법|정의|인권/],["의생명",/의생명|의학|의료/],["질병 치료",/질병|치료/],["지속가능성",/지속가능/]
  ];
  return catalog.filter(([,rx])=>rx.test(text)).map(([label])=>label).slice(0,7);
}
function reportAnswerByLabel(report,rx,fallbackIndex=-1){
  const qs=report?.questions||[], rs=report?.responses||[];
  const idx=qs.findIndex(q=>rx.test(String(q||""))); if(idx>=0&&rs[idx])return String(rs[idx]).trim();
  return fallbackIndex>=0&&rs[fallbackIndex]?String(rs[fallbackIndex]).trim():"";
}
function reportInterpretationClauses(row,area){
  const report=row.report||{}, responses=(report.responses||[]).map(x=>String(x||"").trim());
  const full=responses.join(" "), terms=reportSpecificTerms(row), topic=recordEvidenceTopic(row);
  const career=reportAnswerByLabel(report,/진로|관심 분야|전공/,2);
  const follow=reportAnswerByLabel(report,/추가|더 알아|후속|탐구/,3);
  const growth=reportAnswerByLabel(report,/성장|변화|어려움|노력|해결/,responses.length-1);
  const clauses=[];
  const termText=terms.slice(0,4).join("·");
  if(termText){
    let clause=`${termText}을 중심으로 관련 원리와 활용 사례를 탐색함`;
    if(/식량/.test(full)&&/자원/.test(full)&&/에너지/.test(full))clause=`인공지능과 생명공학 기술의 융합을 식량·자원·에너지 문제 해결과 연결하여 살펴보며 기술의 사회적 활용 가능성을 탐색함`;
    if(/유전자\s*재조합/.test(full)&&/인슐린/.test(full))clause=`유전자 재조합 기술을 활용한 인슐린 생산 원리를 추가로 조사하며 생명공학 기술의 실제 활용 과정을 구체적으로 이해함`;
    clauses.push(clause);
  }
  if(career&&/진로|전공|의학|생명|공학|환경|AI|인공지능|경제|법|교육/.test(career)){
    const label=topic||terms.find(x=>/의생명|생명공학|인공지능|반도체|환경|경제|법/.test(x))||"관련 분야";
    clauses.push(`${label}에 대한 탐구 내용을 자신의 관심 분야와 연결하여 진로 탐색의 방향을 구체화함`);
  }
  if(follow){
    if(/도움|질병|치료|해결|피해|개선/.test(follow))clauses.push(`활동에서 생긴 질문을 바탕으로 기술이 실제 문제 해결에 활용되는 조건과 한계를 더 알아보고자 탐구 범위를 확장함`);
    else if(/찾|검색|조사|알아/.test(follow))clauses.push(`활동 후 궁금한 내용을 추가로 조사하며 탐구를 이어감`);
  }
  if(growth&&/이해|알게|처음|어려|검색|찾아|해결|성장|변화/.test(growth))clauses.push(`이해가 부족했던 내용을 이전 학습과 추가 자료 탐색을 통해 보완하며 자신의 이해 과정을 구체화함`);
  if(area==="behavior"&&/봉사|임원|회장|반장|실장|도우미/.test(`${row.title||""} ${full}`))clauses.push("공동체 활동에서 맡은 역할을 수행하며 구성원과 함께 활동하는 모습을 보임");
  return [...new Set(clauses)].slice(0,3);
}
function studentCenteredRecordPiece(row,area){
  const heading=recordProgramHeading(row);
  if(row.report){
    const clauses=reportInterpretationClauses(row,area);
    if(clauses.length)return enforceTeacherEnding(`${heading}에 참여하여 ${clauses.join(". ")}`);
  }
  if(row.kind==="activity")return enforceTeacherEnding(`${heading}에서 공동체의 일원으로 확인되는 활동과 역할을 수행함`);
  return enforceTeacherEnding(`${heading}에 참여하여 확인 가능한 활동 내용을 바탕으로 교육활동에 참여함`);
}

function buildMultiRecordDraft(rows,area,limit){
  if(!rows.length)return "";
  const pieces=rows.map(row=>studentCenteredRecordPiece(row,area));
  let result=pieces.join(" ");
  result=enforceTeacherEnding(result);
  if(result.length<=limit)return result;
  const per=Math.max(60,Math.floor((limit-(rows.length-1))/rows.length));
  result=pieces.map(piece=>piece.length>per?enforceTeacherEnding(piece.slice(0,Math.max(20,per-16)).replace(/\s+\S*$/, "")):piece).join(" ");
  if(result.length>limit)result=result.slice(0,Math.max(20,limit-16)).replace(/\s+\S*$/, "");
  return enforceTeacherEnding(result).slice(0,limit);
}

const SDG_CATALOG=[
  [1,"빈곤 종식",/빈곤|소득격차|취약계층/],[2,"기아 종식",/기아|식량|영양|농업/],[3,"건강과 웰빙",/건강|의학|약학|보건|운동|수면|질병/],
  [4,"양질의 교육",/교육|학습|멘토링|교육격차|진로교육/],[5,"성평등",/성평등|젠더|여성|차별/],[6,"깨끗한 물과 위생",/물|수질|위생/],
  [7,"깨끗한 에너지",/에너지|태양광|재생에너지|전력/],[8,"양질의 일자리와 경제성장",/경제|금융|일자리|창업|노동/],
  [9,"산업·혁신·인프라",/산업|반도체|인공지능|AI|기술|혁신|인프라|공학/],[10,"불평등 감소",/불평등|격차|차별|포용/],
  [11,"지속가능한 도시와 공동체",/지역사회|도시|교통|안전|공동체/],[12,"책임 있는 소비와 생산",/소비|재활용|분리수거|자원순환|폐기물/],
  [13,"기후행동",/기후|탄소|온난화|환경/],[14,"해양생태계",/해양|바다|수산|해양생물/],[15,"육상생태계",/숲|생태|생물다양성|육상|자연환경/],
  [16,"평화·정의·제도",/평화|법|정의|인권|공공행정|제도/],[17,"목표를 위한 파트너십",/국제협력|ODA|협력|파트너십|세계시민/]
];

const CAREER_TAG_CATALOG=[
  ["인문·어문","인문",/문학|언어|국어|영어|외국어|철학|역사|문화|번역/],
  ["사회·법정","사회",/사회|법|정치|행정|언론|미디어|심리|복지|국제|외교/],
  ["경영·경제","경영경제",/경영|경제|금융|회계|무역|마케팅|창업|기업/],
  ["교육","교육",/교육|교사|수업|학습|상담|교육공학/],
  ["자연과학","자연",/수학|물리|화학|생명|지구과학|천문|과학|통계/],
  ["공학·IT","공학",/공학|반도체|전자|전기|기계|로봇|컴퓨터|소프트웨어|인공지능|AI|건축|화학공학|재료/],
  ["의약·보건","보건",/의학|의사|간호|약학|보건|재활|치료|생명공학/],
  ["예술·체육","예체능",/미술|디자인|음악|공연|영상|체육|스포츠|무용/],
  ["환경·농생명","환경농생명",/환경|생태|기후|농업|식품|산림|해양|동물|수산/]
];
const MAJOR_TAG_CATALOG=[
  ["반도체·전자",/반도체|웨이퍼|집적회로|회로|전자|칩/],
  ["AI·컴퓨터",/인공지능|AI|알고리즘|코딩|프로그래밍|소프트웨어|컴퓨터|데이터/],
  ["기계·로봇",/기계|로봇|자동차|모빌리티|제어/],
  ["건축·도시",/건축|도시|공간|토목|교통/],
  ["화학·재료",/화학|재료|신소재|고분자|화학공학/],
  ["의학·간호·약학",/의학|의사|간호|약학|의료|보건/],
  ["경영·경제·금융",/경영|경제|금융|회계|무역|마케팅|창업/],
  ["법·행정·정치",/법|정치|행정|외교|공공정책/],
  ["교육·상담",/교육|교사|상담|학습|교육공학/],
  ["언어·문학·문화",/문학|언어|국어|영어|외국어|문화|역사|철학/],
  ["환경·생태·에너지",/환경|생태|기후|에너지|탄소|해양|산림/],
  ["예술·디자인·체육",/예술|미술|디자인|음악|영상|체육|스포츠/]
];
function tagMatches(text,rx){
  const re=new RegExp(rx.source,'gi'), matches=[]; let m;
  while((m=re.exec(text))!==null){matches.push(m[0]); if(m.index===re.lastIndex)re.lastIndex++;}
  return [...new Set(matches)].slice(0,8);
}
function evidenceSentence(text,words){
  if(!words.length)return '';
  const parts=String(text||'').split(/(?<=[.!?。]|다\.)\s+|\n+/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return parts.find(x=>words.some(w=>x.includes(w)))||parts[0]||'';
}
function analyzeReportTags(report){
  const text=[report?.programName,report?.reportGroup,...(report?.responses||[])].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  const sdgs=SDG_CATALOG.map(([no,name,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'SDGs',key:String(no),label:`SDG ${no} ${name}`,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||Number(a.key)-Number(b.key)).slice(0,3);
  const careers=CAREER_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,key,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'진로계열',key,label,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,3);
  const majors=MAJOR_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'전공군',key:label,label,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,3);
  return {sdgs,careers,majors,text};
}
function reportTagMarkup(report){
  const a=analyzeReportTags(report);
  const group=(title,rows)=>`<div class="report-auto-tag-group"><b>${title}</b><div>${rows.length?rows.map((x,i)=>`<button type="button" class="report-auto-tag" data-report-tag-detail="${escapeHtml(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(x)))}"><span>${escapeHtml(x.label)}</span><small>근거 ${x.score}개 · ${i===0?'우선':'후보'}</small></button>`).join(''):'<span class="report-tag-empty">분석 가능한 근거가 부족합니다.</span>'}</div></div>`;
  return `<section class="report-auto-tags"><header><div><small>AUTO TAGGING</small><h3>보고서 자동 태그</h3></div><span>내용 기반 자동분석</span></header><p>교사가 직접 입력하지 않고 보고서의 단어와 문장을 근거로 SDGs·진로계열·전공군 후보를 표시합니다.</p>${group('SDGs 태그',a.sdgs)}${group('진로계열 태그',a.careers)}${group('전공군 태그',a.majors)}</section>`;
}
function openReportTagDetail(encoded){
  let tag;try{tag=JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(encoded));}catch(e){return;}
  const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.className='modal-backdrop';
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-card report-tag-detail"><header><div><small>${escapeHtml(tag.type||'자동 태그')}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(tag.label||'태그 근거')}</h3></div><button type="button" data-tag-close>×</button></header><section><h4>판정 핵심 단어</h4><div class="tag-evidence-words">${(tag.words||[]).map(w=>`<span>${escapeHtml(w)}</span>`).join('')||'<span>직접 일치 단어 없음</span>'}</div></section><section><h4>관련 문장</h4><p>${escapeHtml(tag.sentence||'관련 문장을 찾지 못했습니다.')}</p></section><section><h4>판정 설명</h4><p>보고서에서 위 표현이 반복되거나 핵심 주제로 확인되어 ${escapeHtml(tag.label||'해당 태그')} 후보로 분류되었습니다. 최종 활용 시 원문을 함께 확인합니다.</p></section><div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-tag-close>확인</button></div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(layer);layer.querySelectorAll('[data-tag-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>layer.remove());
}

function suggestSdgsForRows(rows){
  const text=rows.map(recordEvidenceText).join(" ");
  return SDG_CATALOG.map(([no,name,rx])=>({no,name,score:(text.match(new RegExp(rx.source,"gi"))||[]).length}))
    .filter(x=>x.score>0).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||a.no-b.no).slice(0,4);
}
function reportImprovementAdvice(row){
  const responses=(row.report?.responses||[]).filter(Boolean);
  const joined=responses.join(" ");
  const advice=[];
  if(joined.length<180)advice.push("활동 과정과 탐구 내용을 조금 더 구체적으로 작성");
  if(!/역할|담당|협력|질문|발표|기록|실험|조사/.test(joined))advice.push("본인의 역할이나 실제 행동을 추가");
  if(!/진로|관심|후속|탐구|계획|실천/.test(joined))advice.push("진로 연계 또는 후속 실천 계획을 추가");
  if(!/문제|해결|분석|비교|근거/.test(joined))advice.push("문제 인식과 해결·분석 과정을 보강");
  return advice.length?advice:["현재 보고서에 활동 과정·역할·성장이 비교적 잘 드러남"];
}
function careerPortfolioDashboard(student,bundle){
  // 진로 포트폴리오는 \"진로활동 + 실제 제출 보고서\"만 사용한다.
  const rawRows=recordEvidenceRows(student,bundle,"career").filter(r=>r.report);
  const seenCareerReports=new Set();
  const rows=rawRows.filter(r=>{const key=String(r.report?.id||[r.report?.submittedAt,r.title,(r.report?.responses||[]).join("|")].join("::"));if(seenCareerReports.has(key))return false;seenCareerReports.add(key);return true;});
  const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
  const hope=[career.hopeTrack,career.hopeMajor].filter(v=>v&&v!=="-").join(" · ");
  const fields=CAREER_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,key])=>({label,key,reports:[],score:0,majors:new Set()}));
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const a=analyzeReportTags(row.report);
    a.careers.forEach(tag=>{const f=fields.find(x=>x.key===tag.key);if(f){f.reports.push(row);f.score+=tag.score;}});
    a.majors.forEach(tag=>{a.careers.slice(0,1).forEach(c=>{const f=fields.find(x=>x.key===c.key);if(f)f.majors.add(tag.label);});});
  });
  const active=fields.filter(f=>f.reports.length);
  const diversity=active.length;
  const maxRepeat=Math.max(0,...active.map(f=>new Set(f.reports.map(r=>r.program?.id||r.title)).size));
  const depth=maxRepeat>=4?"심화":maxRepeat>=2?"연계":maxRepeat===1?"탐색":"근거 부족";
  const hopeText=String(hope||"");
  const linked=active.filter(f=>hopeText&&new RegExp(f.label.split('·').join('|')).test(hopeText)).length;
  const cards=fields.map(f=>{
    const count=new Set(f.reports.map(r=>r.program?.id||r.title)).size;
    const level=count>=4?"심화":count>=2?"연계":count===1?"탐색":"미탐색";
    const starred=hopeText&&new RegExp(f.label.split('·').join('|')).test(hopeText);
    return `<article class="career-field-card ${count?'active':''} ${starred?'hope':''}"><header><b>${starred?'★ ':''}${escapeHtml(f.label)}</b><span>${count}건</span></header><small>${level}${f.majors.size?` · ${escapeHtml([...f.majors].slice(0,2).join(' · '))}`:''}</small></article>`;
  }).join('');
  const evidence=rows.map(row=>{const a=analyzeReportTags(row.report);return `<article class="career-report-row"><div><small>${escapeHtml(row.report?.submittedAt||row.date||'')}</small><b>${escapeHtml(row.title||row.program?.recordTitle||row.program?.title||'진로활동')}</b><div>${a.careers.slice(0,2).map(x=>`<span>${escapeHtml(x.label)}</span>`).join('')}${a.majors.slice(0,2).map(x=>`<span class="major">${escapeHtml(x.label)}</span>`).join('')}</div></div><button class="link-button" data-open-student-report="${escapeHtml(row.report?.id||'')}">보고서 보기</button></article>`;}).join('')||'<div class="empty-inline">제출된 진로활동 보고서가 없습니다.</div>';
  return `<section class="career-portfolio"><header><div><small>CAREER EXPLORATION PORTFOLIO</small><h3>${escapeHtml(student.name)} 진로탐색 포트폴리오</h3><p>진로활동 보고서를 바탕으로 전공분야의 탐색 폭과 반복·심화 흐름을 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${rows.length}개 보고서</span></header><div class="career-portfolio-metrics"><article><small>분야 다양성</small><b>${diversity}개 분야</b></article><article><small>탐구 심화도</small><b>${depth}</b></article><article><small>희망전공 연계</small><b>${hope?`${linked?'연계 발견':'탐색 중'}`:'희망 미등록'}</b><em>${escapeHtml(hope||'희망계열·전공을 등록하면 ★로 표시')}</em></article></div><div class="career-field-grid">${cards}</div><details class="career-report-evidence"><summary>진로활동 보고서 근거 ${rows.length}건 보기</summary><div>${evidence}</div></details></section>`;
}
function sdgsDashboard(student,bundle){
  const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
  const programs=(bundle.programs||[]).filter(p=>p.kind!=="after");
  const activityRows=bundle.activities||[];
  const reportRows=[...recordEvidenceRows(student,bundle,"autonomy"),...recordEvidenceRows(student,bundle,"career")].filter(r=>r.report);
  const uniqueReports=[]; const seenReports=new Set();
  reportRows.forEach(r=>{const k=String(r.report?.id||[r.report?.submittedAt,r.title,(r.report?.responses||[]).join('|')].join('::'));if(!seenReports.has(k)){seenReports.add(k);uniqueReports.push(r);}});

  const sdgMap=new Map(SDG_CATALOG.map(([no,name])=>[no,{no,name,evidence:[],programs:[],reports:[]} ]));
  programs.forEach(p=>String(p.sdgs||'').split(/[,;/\s]+/).map(Number).filter(n=>n>=1&&n<=17).forEach(n=>{const row=sdgMap.get(n);if(row){row.programs.push(p);row.evidence.push({type:'program',title:p.recordTitle||p.title||'프로그램'});}}));
  uniqueReports.forEach(row=>suggestSdgsForRows([row]).slice(0,3).forEach(x=>{const item=sdgMap.get(Number(x.no));if(item){item.reports.push(row);item.evidence.push({type:'report',title:row.title||row.program?.recordTitle||row.program?.title||'보고서'});}}));
  const ranked=[...sdgMap.values()].filter(x=>x.evidence.length).sort((a,b)=>b.evidence.length-a.evidence.length||a.no-b.no);
  const topSdgs=ranked.slice(0,4);

  const careerCounts=new Map();
  uniqueReports.forEach(row=>{const a=analyzeReportTags(row.report);a.careers.forEach(tag=>careerCounts.set(tag.label,(careerCounts.get(tag.label)||0)+tag.score));});
  const topCareers=[...careerCounts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,4).map(([label])=>label);
  const hope=[career.hopeTrack,career.hopeMajor].filter(v=>v&&v!=='-');
  const selected=(bundle.selected||[]).slice(0,8).map(x=>x.subject).filter(Boolean);
  const areaCounts=new Map();
  activityRows.forEach(x=>{const area=String(x.area||x.type||'기타').trim()||'기타';areaCounts.set(area,(areaCounts.get(area)||0)+1);});
  const areaSummary=[...areaCounts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,5);

  const story=[];
  if(hope.length)story.push(`${hope.join(' · ')}에 관심을 두고`);
  if(topCareers.length)story.push(`${topCareers.slice(0,3).join('·')} 분야의 탐구가 반복되고 있으며`);
  if(topSdgs.length)story.push(`${topSdgs.slice(0,3).map(x=>`SDG ${x.no} ${x.name}`).join(', ')}와 연결되는 사회적 가치가 활동 속에서 나타납니다.`);
  if(!story.length)story.push('현재 연결된 학교활동과 보고서를 바탕으로 학생의 관심 주제와 가치의 흐름을 만들어 가는 단계입니다.');
  const storyText=story.join(' ');

  const valueCards=topSdgs.length?topSdgs.map(x=>`<article><b>SDG ${x.no}</b><span>${escapeHtml(x.name)}</span><small>연결 근거 ${x.evidence.length}건</small></article>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-inline">아직 충분한 SDGs 연결 근거가 없습니다.</div>';
  const topicText=topCareers.length?topCareers.join(' · '):uniqueReports.slice(0,3).map(r=>r.title).filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'탐구주제 형성 중';
  const evidenceText=[...areaSummary.map(([k,v])=>`${k} ${v}건`),`프로그램 ${programs.length}건`,`보고서 ${uniqueReports.length}건`].join(' · ');

  const details=[...sdgMap.values()].map(x=>`<button class="growth-sdg-chip ${x.evidence.length?'active':''}" data-sdgs-detail="${x.no}"><b>${x.no}</b><span>${escapeHtml(x.name)}</span><small>${x.evidence.length?`${x.evidence.length}건 연결`:'현재 근거 없음'}</small></button>`).join('');
  const detailNo=Number(recordSdgsDetail||0); const detail=detailNo?sdgMap.get(detailNo):null;

  return `<div class="growth-profile-v081">
    <section class="growth-profile-intro"><div><small>STUDENT GROWTH STORY · UNESCO SCHOOL</small><h2>${escapeHtml(student.name)} 생활기록부 핵심 성장 프로파일</h2><p><b>활동의 나열이 아니라 학생의 스토리를 봅니다.</b> 우리 학교의 유네스코 교육 방향과 학교교육과정(자율·진로·동아리·봉사), 선택활동, 교과·탐구·보고서를 진로·전공과 SDGs의 관점에서 함께 읽어 학생의 관심·문제의식·성장 흐름을 확인합니다.</p></div><span>17개 목표를 채우는 것이 목적이 아닙니다.</span></section>

    <section class="growth-story-card"><header><div><small>CORE STORY</small><h3>이 학생을 설명하는 현재의 성장 스토리</h3></div><span>생활기록부 전체 연결</span></header><p>${escapeHtml(storyText)}</p><div class="growth-story-source">${escapeHtml(evidenceText)}</div></section>

    <div class="growth-axis-grid">
      <section><small>01 CAREER · MAJOR</small><h3>진로·전공 관심</h3><b>${escapeHtml(hope.join(' · ')||'진로 관심 형성 중')}</b><p>${selected.length?`선택과목 · ${escapeHtml(selected.slice(0,6).join(' · '))}`:'선택과목 연결 자료를 확인합니다.'}</p></section>
      <section><small>02 EXPLORATION</small><h3>핵심 탐구주제</h3><b>${escapeHtml(topicText)}</b><p>보고서와 진로활동에서 반복·심화되는 주제를 중심으로 봅니다.</p></section>
      <section><small>03 VALUE · SDGs</small><h3>사회적 가치·SDGs</h3><div class="growth-value-mini">${valueCards}</div></section>
      <section><small>04 EVIDENCE FLOW</small><h3>학교교육과정 속 근거</h3><b>${activityRows.length+programs.length+uniqueReports.length}건 연결</b><p>${escapeHtml(areaSummary.map(([k,v])=>`${k} ${v}`).join(' · ')||'활동 근거 연결 중')}</p></section>
    </div>

    <section class="growth-teacher-guide"><header><div><small>HOMEROOM GUIDE</small><h3>담임은 이렇게 지도해 주세요</h3></div><span>상담·보고서·기록 연계</span></header><div><p><b>① 진로 연결</b> 이 활동이 자신의 관심 전공과 어떻게 이어지는가?</p><p><b>② 문제 발견</b> 사회·공동체·환경·미래의 어떤 문제를 발견했는가?</p><p><b>③ 관점 변화</b> 활동 전후 생각이나 판단이 어떻게 달라졌는가?</p><p><b>④ 다음 탐구</b> 다음 교과·진로·선택활동에서 무엇을 더 깊게 보고 싶은가?</p></div><em>학생 보고서는 활동 요약보다 ‘무엇을 바라보게 되었는지’를 표현할 때 생활기록부 전체의 스토리가 살아납니다.</em></section>

    <section class="growth-sdg-lens"><header><div><small>SDGs VALUE LENS</small><h3>17개 목표는 학생을 읽는 ‘가치의 렌즈’입니다</h3><p>미이수·충족을 판단하지 않고 현재 활동에서 확인되는 연결 근거를 보여줍니다.</p></div><span>${ranked.length}개 목표에서 근거 발견</span></header><div class="growth-sdg-grid">${details}</div></section>

    ${detail?`<section class="growth-sdg-detail"><header><h3>SDG ${detail.no} ${escapeHtml(detail.name)} · 연결 근거</h3><span>${detail.evidence.length}건</span></header>${detail.evidence.map(e=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(e.title)}</b><small>${e.type==='report'?'학생 보고서':'학교 프로그램'}</small></article>`).join('')||'<div class="empty-inline">현재 연결 근거가 없습니다.</div>'}</section>`:''}

    ${careerPortfolioDashboard(student,bundle)}
  </div>`;
}
function existingFinalRecord(bundle,area){
  return (bundle.finalRecords||[]).find(row=>area==="behavior"?/행동|종합/.test(String(row.area||"")):recordAreaKey(row.area)===area)||null;
}

function recordRuleRowsBySource(source){
  return (readonlyCache?.recordRuleRows||[]).filter(row=>String(row.__source||"")===source);
}
function recordRuleField(row,names){
  for(const name of names){
    const key=Object.keys(row||{}).find(k=>String(k).replace(/\s/g,"").includes(name));
    const value=key?String(row[key]??"").trim():"";
    if(value)return value;
  }
  return "";
}
function applySchoolRecordGuardrails(text){
  let out=String(text||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
  // 33_생기부작성절대규칙과 35_금지표현DB는 학교 검증 규칙이므로 완화하지 않고 최종 강제 적용한다.
  recordRuleRowsBySource("35_금지표현DB").forEach(row=>{
    const forbidden=recordRuleField(row,["금지표현","금지어"]);
    if(!forbidden||forbidden.length>60)return;
    const enabled=recordRuleField(row,["사용여부","활성화여부"]);
    if(enabled&&/^N$/i.test(enabled))return;
    if(!out.includes(forbidden))return;
    const replacement=recordRuleField(row,["수정추천표현","추천표현","대체표현"]);
    const auto=recordRuleField(row,["자동치환여부","자동치환"]);
    if(replacement&&(!auto||/^Y$/i.test(auto)))out=out.split(forbidden).join(replacement);
  });
  return enforceTeacherEnding(out);
}
function recordAdmissionSignal(student){
  const result=[];
  try{
    for(const [group,label] of [["inseoul","인서울"],["regional","지거국"]]){
      const row=admissionManagementDataset(group).find(x=>String(x.student?.id)===String(student?.id));
      if(row&&["core","growth","interest"].includes(row.stage))result.push({group,label,stage:row.stage,reasons:row.reasons||[],managePoints:row.managePoints||[]});
    }
  }catch(e){}
  return result;
}
function recordStudentInterpretation(student,bundle){
  const allRows=recordEvidenceRows(student,bundle,"behavior");
