  const reportRows=allRows.filter(r=>r.report);
  const tagText=reportRows.map(r=>recordEvidenceText(r)).join(" ");
  const sdgs=suggestSdgsForRows(reportRows).slice(0,3);
  const careers=CAREER_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,key,rx])=>({label,key,score:tagMatches(tagText,rx).length})).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,2);
  const majors=MAJOR_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,rx])=>({label,score:tagMatches(tagText,rx).length})).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,2);
  const activities=bundle.activities||[];
  const insideService=activities.filter(a=>/교육과정\s*내|창체\s*봉사|교내봉사/.test([a.area,a.type,a.title,a.role].filter(Boolean).join(" "))&&!/교육과정\s*외/.test([a.area,a.type,a.title,a.role].filter(Boolean).join(" ")));
  const leadership=activities.filter(a=>/임원|학생회|회장|부회장|실장|부실장|반장|부반장/.test([a.area,a.type,a.title,a.role].filter(Boolean).join(" ")));
  const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
  const signals=recordAdmissionSignal(student);
  const reportCount=reportRows.length, programCount=(bundle.programs||[]).filter(p=>p.kind!=="after").length;
  const evidenceLevel=reportCount>=3&&programCount>=3?"충분":reportCount||programCount>=2?"보통":"부족";
  const flow=[];
  if(insideService.length)flow.push("교육과정내 봉사");
  if(leadership.length)flow.push("공동체 역할");
  if(programCount)flow.push("프로그램 참여");
  if(reportCount)flow.push("보고서 성찰·탐구");
  if(careers.length||career.hopeMajor!=="-")flow.push("진로·전공 연결");
  return {sdgs,careers,majors,insideService,leadership,career,signals,reportCount,programCount,evidenceLevel,flow};
}
function recordInterpretationMarkup(student,bundle){
  const a=recordStudentInterpretation(student,bundle);
  const signal=a.signals.map(x=>`<span class="record-admission-badge ${x.stage}">${escapeHtml(x.label)} · ${x.stage==="core"?"핵심관리":x.stage==="growth"?"성장관리":"관심관리"}</span>`).join("");
  const gaps=[];
  if(a.reportCount===0)gaps.push("보고서 근거 없음");
  if(!a.careers.length&&a.career.hopeMajor==="-")gaps.push("진로·전공 연결 근거 약함");
  if(!a.insideService.length&&!a.leadership.length)gaps.push("종합의견 공동체 근거 확인 필요");
  if(a.signals.some(x=>x.stage==="core")&&a.reportCount<2)gaps.push("핵심관리 학생: 탐구·성찰 근거 우선 점검");
  return `<section class="record-interpretation-card"><header><div><small>STUDENT INTERPRETATION</small><h3>학생 해석 · 생활기록부 근거 점검</h3></div><span class="state ${a.evidenceLevel==="충분"?"positive":a.evidenceLevel==="부족"?"warn":""}">근거 ${a.evidenceLevel}</span></header>
    ${signal?`<div class="record-admission-signals">${signal}</div>`:""}
    <div class="record-interpretation-grid">
      <article><small>활동·보고서</small><b>${a.programCount}개 · ${a.reportCount}건</b><span>${escapeHtml(a.flow.join(" → ")||"연결 근거 확인 필요")}</span></article>
      <article><small>SDGs 흐름</small><b>${a.sdgs.map(x=>`SDG ${x.no}`).join(" · ")||"분석 근거 부족"}</b><span>${escapeHtml(a.sdgs.map(x=>x.name).join(" · ")||"보고서 내용에서 자동 분석")}</span></article>
      <article><small>진로·전공</small><b>${escapeHtml(a.careers.map(x=>x.label).join(" · ")||a.career.hopeTrack||"-")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(a.majors.map(x=>x.label).join(" · ")||a.career.hopeMajor||"-")}</span></article>
      <article><small>종합의견 근거</small><b>봉사 ${a.insideService.length} · 임원/역할 ${a.leadership.length}</b><span>${escapeHtml(gaps.join(" · ")||"현재 연결자료에서 주요 누락 없음")}</span></article>
    </div>
    <p class="safe-note">입시 관리 신호는 문장을 과장하는 조건으로 사용하지 않고, 누락된 진로·탐구·성장 근거를 더 먼저 점검하는 데만 사용합니다.</p>
  </section>`;
}
function buildIntegratedRecordDraft(student,bundle,rows,area,limit){
  let result=buildMultiRecordDraft(rows,area,limit);
  const interp=recordStudentInterpretation(student,bundle);
  const selectedReports=rows.filter(row=>row.report);
  if(selectedReports.length>=2){
    const focus=interp.majors?.[0]?.label||interp.careers?.[0]?.label||interp.sdgs?.[0]?.name||"관련 주제";
    const continuity=`여러 활동에서 ${focus}에 대한 관심을 이어가며 탐구와 성찰의 내용을 연결하는 모습을 보임.`;
    if(!result.includes(continuity)&&result.length+continuity.length+1<=limit)result=`${result} ${continuity}`.trim();
  }
  if(area==="behavior"&&rows.length===0){
    const facts=[];
    if(interp.insideService.length)facts.push("교육과정 내 봉사활동에 참여하며 공동체의 일원으로 주어진 활동을 수행함.");
    if(interp.leadership.length)facts.push("학급 및 학교 공동체에서 맡은 역할을 수행하며 구성원과 함께 활동함.");
    result=facts.join(" ");
  }
  const obs=observationText(student,area);if(obs)result=`${result} ${obs}`.trim();
  result=applySchoolRecordGuardrails(result);
  if(result.length>limit)result=applySchoolRecordGuardrails(result.slice(0,Math.max(20,limit-12)).replace(/\s+\S*$/,""));
  return result.slice(0,limit);
}

function recordWriterWorkspace(student,bundle){
  const area=recordWriterArea,rows=recordEvidenceRows(student,bundle,area),limit=recordAreaLimit(area);
  const saved=localStorage.getItem(recordDraftKey(student.id,area))||existingFinalRecord(bundle,area)?.text||"";
  const rules=recordRuleSummary(area),finalRows=bundle.finalRecords||[];
  return `<div class="record-writer">
    ${recordInterpretationMarkup(student,bundle)}
    <div class="record-student-management-row">${studentAdmissionBadgesMarkup(student)}</div>
    <div class="record-writer-tabs">${[["autonomy","자율활동 특기사항"],["career","진로활동 특기사항"],["behavior","종합의견"]].map(([id,label])=>`<button data-record-writer-area="${id}" class="${area===id?"active":""}">${label}</button>`).join("")}</div>
    <div class="record-writer-layout">
      <section class="record-evidence-panel"><header><div><small>EVIDENCE</small><h3>${recordAreaLabel(area)} 근거자료</h3></div><span>${rows.length}건</span></header>
        <div class="record-evidence-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-record-evidence-all>전체 선택</button><button class="btn secondary" data-record-evidence-clear>선택 해제</button><span>${recordWriterSelected.size}건 선택</span></div>
        <div class="record-evidence-list">${recordEvidenceListMarkup(rows,area)}</div>
      </section>
      <section class="record-compose-panel"><header><div><small>RECORD COMPOSER</small><h3>최종 문장 작성</h3></div><span id="recordCharCount">${saved.length}/${limit}자</span></header>
        <div class="record-compose-tools"><button class="btn primary" data-record-generate>규칙 기반 초안</button><button class="btn ai-button" data-record-ai-generate>AI 초안 생성</button><button class="btn secondary" data-record-rule-check>규칙 검토</button><button class="btn secondary" data-record-save-draft>초안 저장</button><button class="btn secondary" data-record-copy>복사</button></div>
        <textarea id="recordDraftText" maxlength="${limit}" placeholder="근거 프로그램과 보고서를 선택한 뒤 ‘규칙 기반 초안’을 누르세요. 프로그램 소개 요약이 아니라 학생의 탐구·행동·성장 흐름을 중심으로 생성합니다.">${escapeHtml(saved)}</textarea>
        <div class="record-rule-result" id="recordRuleResult"><b>작성 기준</b><span>${limit}자 이내 · 학생 자기평가를 그대로 옮기지 않고 교사 관찰형 문장으로 정리</span></div>
        <details class="record-used-evidence" id="recordUsedEvidence"><summary>초안에 사용한 근거 다시보기 · ${rows.filter(r=>recordWriterSelected.has(r.id)).length}건</summary><div>${rows.filter(r=>recordWriterSelected.has(r.id)).map(r=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(r.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(r.report?'보고서':'활동·프로그램')}</small>${r.report?.id?`<button type="button" class="link-button" data-open-student-report="${escapeHtml(r.report.id)}">원문 보기</button>`:''}</article>`).join('')||'<p>아직 선택한 근거가 없습니다.</p>'}</div></details>
        <details class="record-rules"><summary>생활기록부 작성규칙 보기 · ${rules.length}건</summary>${rules.map(rule=>`<p><b>${escapeHtml(rule.source)}</b>${escapeHtml(rule.text)}</p>`).join("")||'<p>연결된 작성규칙이 없습니다.</p>'}</details>
      </section>
    </div>
    ${lowEvidencePanel(student,bundle,area)}
    <section class="record-analysis-panel">
      <header><div><small>AI ASSIST</small><h3>SDGs 추천·보고서 보완</h3></div><span>${recordAiConfigured?"AI 연결됨":"AI 연결 전"}</span></header>
      <div class="record-analysis-grid">
        ${suggestSdgsForRows(rows.filter(r=>recordWriterSelected.has(r.id))).map(x=>`<article><b>SDGs ${x.no} ${escapeHtml(x.name)}</b><small>추천 근거어 ${x.score}회</small></article>`).join("")||'<div class="empty-inline">근거자료를 선택하면 SDGs 후보를 추천합니다.</div>'}
      </div>
      <button class="btn secondary" data-record-feedback-message>학생 보완요청 문구 생성</button>
      <textarea id="recordFeedbackText" placeholder="선택 보고서의 보완요청 문구가 여기에 생성됩니다."></textarea>
    </section>
    <section class="final-record-summary"><header><div><small>FINAL RECORDS</small><h3>최종 생활기록부 요약본</h3></div><span>${finalRows.length}건</span></header><div>${finalRows.map(row=>`<article><div><b>${escapeHtml(row.area||"생활기록부")}</b><small>${escapeHtml([row.status,row.updated,row.editor].filter(Boolean).join(" · "))}</small></div><p>${escapeHtml(row.text||"")}</p></article>`).join("")||'<div class="empty-inline">08_생활기록부최종기록에 확정된 요약본이 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section>
  </div>`;
}
function stripProgramDatesFromRecordText(value){
  return String(value||"")
    .replace(/\s*[（(]?(?:20\d{2}[.\-/년]\s*)?\d{1,2}[.\-/월]\s*\d{1,2}(?:일)?[）)]?/g," ")
    .replace(/\s{2,}/g," ").replace(/\s+([,.)])/g,"$1").trim();
}

function runRecordRuleCheck(text,area){
  const forbidden=recordRuleRowsBySource("35_금지표현DB").map(r=>recordRuleField(r,["금지표현","금지어"])).filter(v=>v&&v.length<60);
  const hits=[...new Set(forbidden.filter(word=>text.includes(word)))].slice(0,10),issues=[];
  if(!text.trim())issues.push("작성된 문장이 없습니다.");
  if(/나는|제가|저는/.test(text))issues.push("학생 자기서술형 표현이 포함되어 있습니다.");
  if(/느꼈|생각했|알게 되었/.test(text))issues.push("단순 소감 표현을 관찰·탐구·성장 중심으로 바꾸는 것이 좋습니다.");
  if(hits.length)issues.push(`금지표현 확인: ${hits.join(", ")}`);
  if(text.length>recordAreaLimit(area))issues.push("영역별 글자 수 제한을 초과했습니다.");
  if(/(?:했다|하였다|했습니다|하였습니다|되었다|이었다)\.?\s*$/.test(text))issues.push("최종 문장이 학생 서술형 ‘~했다’로 끝납니다. 교사 관찰형 종결로 수정해야 합니다.");
  const current=currentRecordStudent();
  const similar=savedDraftsForArea(area,current?.id).map(x=>({...x,score:jaccardSimilarity(text,x.text)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score)[0];
  if(similar?.score>=0.62){
    const other=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>s.id===similar.studentId);
    issues.push(`저장된 ${other?.name||"다른 학생"} 문장과 유사도 ${Math.round(similar.score*100)}%입니다. 활동·역할·강조점을 더 개별화하세요.`);
  }
  return issues;
}

function activityTypeLabel(item){return [item.area,item.type].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||"활동";}
function recordStudentClass(student){return String(student?.className||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]||"";}
function activityCountBy(student,rx){return studentRecordBundle(student).activities.filter(x=>rx.test(`${x.area} ${x.type} ${x.title} ${x.role}`)).length;}
function recordClassTable(students){
  const rows=students.map(student=>{const b=studentRecordBundle(student);if(recordMode==='activities'){const office=activityCountBy(student,/임원|학생회|회장|부회장|실장|부실장|반장|부반장/);const service=activityCountBy(student,/봉사|도우미|필사집/);const club=studentRecordBundle(student).activities.filter(isTrueClubActivity).length;const career=activityCountBy(student,/진로/);return `<button class="record-class-row" data-record-student="${escapeHtml(student.id)}"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)}</b><strong>${escapeHtml(student.name)}</strong><span>${office||'-'}</span><span>${service||'-'}</span><span>${club||'-'}</span><span>${career||'-'}</span><span>${b.programs.length||'-'}</span><em>보기 ›</em></button>`;}if(recordMode==='curriculum'){const terms=['2학년 1학기','2학년 2학기','3학년 1학기','3학년 2학기'];const counts=terms.map(term=>b.selected.filter(x=>String(x.term||x.semester||'')===term).length);const errorCount=selectionErrorsForStudent(student).length;const submit=selectionSubmissionInfo(b);return `<button class="record-class-row curriculum-row" data-record-student="${escapeHtml(student.id)}"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)}</b><strong>${escapeHtml(student.name)}${errorCount?`<i class="selection-error-dot">${errorCount}</i>`:''}<small class="selection-submit-state ${submit.modifiedAfterNotice?'changed':''}">${escapeHtml(`${submit.date} · ${submit.count}차`)}</small></strong>${counts.map(c=>`<span>${c||'-'}</span>`).join('')}<span>${b.selected.length||'-'}</span><em>보기 ›</em></button>`;}const status=b.finalRecords.filter(x=>/완료|확정|확인/.test(String(x.status))).length;return `<button class="record-class-row final-row" data-record-student="${escapeHtml(student.id)}"><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)}</b><strong>${escapeHtml(student.name)}</strong><span>${b.finalRecords.length}</span><span>${status}</span><span>${b.finalRecords.length-status}</span><em>보기 ›</em></button>`;}).join('');
  const head=recordMode==='activities'?'<span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>임원</span><span>봉사</span><span>동아리</span><span>진로</span><span>프로그램</span><span></span>':recordMode==='curriculum'?'<span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>2-1</span><span>2-2</span><span>3-1</span><span>3-2</span><span>전체</span><span></span>':'<span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>기록</span><span>확인</span><span>대기</span><span></span>';
  return `<section class="record-class-table ${recordMode}"><div class="record-class-head">${head}</div>${rows||'<div class="query-empty"><b>해당 반 학생 자료가 없습니다.</b></div>'}</section>`;
}
function selectionErrorsForStudent(student){
  const allowed=new Set(['문·이과 혼합 선택','문이과 혼합 선택','문이과 교차지원','문·이과 교차지원','과학계열 과목 위계 위반','과학과목 위계오류','학기간 중복 신청','학기간 중복과목 오류']);
  const rows=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[];
  return rows.filter(x=>((x.studentId&&x.studentId===student?.id)||(x.studentNo&&String(x.studentNo)===String(student?.studentNo)))&&allowed.has(String(x.type||'').trim()));
}
function curriculumTermSubjects(bundle,term){
  return (bundle?.selected||[]).filter(x=>String(x.term||x.semester||'')===term).map(x=>String(x.subject||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
}
function curriculumErrorText(row){
  return String(row?.detail||[row?.type,row?.subject,row?.terms,row?.status].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'선택과목 신청 내용을 다시 확인해 주세요.').trim();
}
function curriculumErrorCategory(row){
  const text=`${row?.type||''} ${row?.detail||''} ${row?.subject||''} ${row?.terms||''} ${row?.status||''}`;
  if(/문.?이과|교차|혼합|사회.*과학|과학.*사회/.test(text))return '문·이과 탐구 혼합';
  if(/위계|선이수|후속|Ⅰ.*Ⅱ|1.*2/.test(text))return '과목 위계 오류';
  if(/중복|동일.?과목|학기간/.test(text))return '학기간 중복 신청';
  if(/개수|과목.?수|부족|초과/.test(text))return '선택 과목 수 확인 필요';
  return '기타 확인사항';
}
function groupedCurriculumErrors(errors){
  const order=['문·이과 탐구 혼합','과목 위계 오류','학기간 중복 신청','선택 과목 수 확인 필요','기타 확인사항'];
  const map=new Map(order.map(x=>[x,[]]));
  (errors||[]).forEach(row=>map.get(curriculumErrorCategory(row)).push(curriculumErrorText(row)));
  return order.map(title=>({title,items:[...new Set(map.get(title))]})).filter(group=>group.items.length);
}
function buildCurriculumReapplicationMessage(student,bundle){
  const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
  const errors=selectionErrorsForStudent(student);
  const terms=['2학년 1학기','2학년 2학기','3학년 1학기','3학년 2학기'];
  const termBlocks=terms.map(term=>`▶ ${term}\n${curriculumTermSubjects(bundle,term).join(', ')||'신청 없음'}`).join('\n\n');
  const groups=groupedCurriculumErrors(errors);
  const submit=selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle);
  const errorBlock=groups.length?groups.map((group,index)=>`${['①','②','③','④','⑤'][index]||`${index+1}.`} ${group.title}\n${group.items.map(item=>`- ${item}`).join('\n')}`).join('\n\n'):'① 신청 내용 재확인\n- 현재 자동 검증에서 오류가 확인되지 않았습니다. 신청 내용을 다시 한 번 확인해 주세요.';
  return `[운호고 선택과목 사전점검 안내]\n\n안녕하세요. 운호고등학교 1학년부입니다.\n\n${student?.name||'학생'} 학생의 선택과목 신청 내역을 점검한 결과, 확인이 필요한 사항이 있어 안내드립니다.\n\n■ 신청 현황\n· 최종 신청일 : ${submit.date}\n· 신청 차수 : ${submit.count}차 신청\n· 8월 3일 안내 후 수정 : ${submit.modifiedAfterNotice?'수정함':'추가 수정 없음'}\n\n■ 진로희망\n· 희망계열 : ${career.hopeTrack||'-'}\n· 희망전공 : ${career.hopeMajor||'-'}\n· 추천계열 : ${career.recommendation||'-'}\n\n■ 현재 신청 내역\n\n${termBlocks}\n\n■ 확인이 필요한 사항\n\n${errorBlock}\n\n개학 후 교육과정부의 공식 수강신청이 예정되어 있으므로, 아래 구글폼에서 방학 중 신청 내용을 확인하시고 수정하여 제출하여 주시기 바랍니다.\n\n▶ 선택과목 수정 구글폼\n${SUBJECT_REAPPLICATION_FORM_URL}\n\n※ 본 내용은 사전 상담 및 오류 점검을 위한 안내이며, 최종 선택과목은 개학 후 교육과정부의 공식 수강신청 결과로 확정됩니다.\n\n감사합니다.\n운호고등학교 1학년부`;
}
function buildBulkCurriculumMessages(students){
  return (students||[]).map(student=>{
    const bundle=studentRecordBundle(student);
    return `====================\n${student.studentNo||''} ${student.name||''}\n====================\n\n${buildCurriculumReapplicationMessage(student,bundle)}`;
  }).join('\n\n\n');
}
function curriculumMessagePanel(student,bundle){
  const message=buildCurriculumReapplicationMessage(student,bundle);
  const errors=selectionErrorsForStudent(student);
  return `<section class="curriculum-message-panel"><header><div><small>STUDENT MESSAGE</small><h3>선택과목 재신청 안내문</h3></div><span>${errors.length?`${errors.length}건 오류 반영`:'신청 확인 안내'}</span></header><textarea id="curriculumMessageText" readonly>${escapeHtml(message)}</textarea><div class="curriculum-message-actions"><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-copy-curriculum-message>안내문 복사</button><a class="btn secondary" href="${escapeHtml(SUBJECT_REAPPLICATION_FORM_URL)}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">수강신청 구글폼 열기</a></div><p>2학년 1학기부터 3학년 2학기까지의 신청내역과 진로희망·추천계열·오류현황을 포함합니다.</p></section>`;
}
function recordPersonalContent(student,bundle){
  if(recordMode==='activities'){
    const activitySearchText=x=>`${x.area||""} ${x.type||""} ${x.title||""} ${x.role||""} ${x.source||""} ${x.note||""}`;
    const isInsideVolunteer=x=>String(x.area||"")==="교육과정 내 봉사";
    const isOutsideVolunteer=x=>String(x.area||"")==="교육과정 외 봉사"||(!isInsideVolunteer(x)&&isVolunteerActivity(x));
    const groupDefs=[
      ["학생회·임원",x=>/임원|학생회|회장|부회장|실장|부실장|반장|부반장/.test(activitySearchText(x)),"compact"],
      ["동아리",isTrueClubActivity,"compact"],
      ["장학금 수혜",x=>/장학/.test(activitySearchText(x)),"compact"],
      ["교육과정 외 봉사",isOutsideVolunteer,"volunteer-outside"],
      ["교육과정 내 봉사",isInsideVolunteer,"volunteer-inside"]
    ];
    const insideVolunteerSeries=x=>{
      const title=String(x.title||x.type||"");
      if(/^\s*\(자율[·ㆍ.\s-]*자치(?:활동)?\)/.test(title))return "자율·자치활동";
      if(/^\s*\(진로[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동\)/.test(title))return "진로활동";
      if(/^\s*\(동아리[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동\)/.test(title))return "동아리활동";
      return "기타";
    };
    const activityName=x=>String(x.title||x.type||'활동').replace(/^\s*\([^)]*\)\s*/,'').trim()||'활동';
    const groupedVolunteerMarkup=(rows,label)=>{
      const groups=new Map();
      rows.forEach(x=>{const key=activityName(x);if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);groups.get(key).push(x);});
      return [...groups.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0],'ko')).map(([name,items])=>{const hours=items.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.hours)||0),0);const dates=[...new Set(items.map(x=>x.date).filter(Boolean))].sort();return `<article><b>${escapeHtml(name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([`${items.length}회`,hours?`${hours}시간`:"",dates.length?dates.join(' · '):""].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small><p>${escapeHtml([...new Set(items.map(x=>x.organization||x.role||x.note).filter(Boolean))].join(' · ')||label)}</p></article>`;}).join('');
    };
    const makeActivityCard=([label,test,sizeClass])=>{
      const seen=new Set();
      const rows=bundle.activities.filter(test).filter(x=>{const key=x.id||[x.studentNo,x.title,x.date,x.hours].join("|");if(seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;});
      const totalHours=rows.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+(Number(x.hours)||0),0);
      let body='';
      if(label==="교육과정 내 봉사"&&rows.length){
        const series=["자율·자치활동","진로활동","동아리활동"];
        body=`<div class="inside-volunteer-three-columns">${series.map(seriesName=>{const items=rows.filter(x=>insideVolunteerSeries(x)===seriesName);return `<section><header><b>${seriesName}</b><span>${items.length}건</span></header>${items.length?groupedVolunteerMarkup(items,seriesName):'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;}).join('')}</div>`;
      }else if(/봉사/.test(label)){
        body=groupedVolunteerMarkup(rows,label);
      }else{
        body=rows.map(x=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(x.title||x.type||label)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([x.date,x.role,x.organization,Number(x.hours)?`${Number(x.hours)}시간`:""].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||activityTypeLabel(x))}</small><p>${escapeHtml(x.note||x.evidence||x.status||'근거자료 확인')}</p></article>`).join('');
      }
      return `<section class="record-history-group ${sizeClass||""}"><header><h3>${label}</h3><span>${rows.length}건${/봉사/.test(label)&&totalHours?` · ${totalHours}시간`:""}</span></header>${rows.length?body:'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;
    };
    const officeCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[0]);
    const clubCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[1]);
    const scholarshipCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[2]);
    const outsideCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[3]);
    const insideCard=makeActivityCard(groupDefs[4]);
    const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
    const careerValues=[career.hopeTrack,career.hopeMajor,career.aptitude].filter(v=>v&&v!=="-");
    const careerCard=`<section class="record-history-group record-career-profile compact"><header><h3>진로희망</h3><span>${careerValues.length?"등록":"0건"}</span></header>${careerValues.length?`<div class="career-support-strip compact record-career-strip"><span><small>진로희망계열</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.hopeTrack)}</b></span><span><small>진로희망전공</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.hopeMajor)}</b></span><span><small>대학적성데이터</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.aptitude)}</b></span></div>`:'<div class="empty-inline">연결 자료 없음</div>'}</section>`;
    return `<div class="record-history-grid record-history-three-columns record-history-layout-v700"><div class="record-history-small-stack">${officeCard}${careerCard}</div><div class="record-history-small-stack">${clubCard}${scholarshipCard}</div><div class="record-history-volunteer-stack">${outsideCard}</div><div class="record-history-inside-full">${insideCard}</div></div>`
  }
  if(recordMode==='sdgs') return sdgsDashboard(student,bundle);
  if(recordMode==='curriculum'){
    const byTerm=new Map();bundle.selected.forEach(x=>{const key=x.term||x.semester||'학기 미분류';if(!byTerm.has(key))byTerm.set(key,[]);byTerm.get(key).push(x);});
    const errors=selectionErrorsForStudent(student);const errorPanel=errors.length?`<section class="selection-error-panel"><header><div><small>ERROR REVIEW</small><h3>선택과목 오류검토</h3></div><span>${errors.length}건 확인 필요</span></header>${errors.map(x=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(x.type)}</b><em>${escapeHtml(x.severity)}</em><p>${escapeHtml(x.detail||[x.subject,x.terms].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p><small>${escapeHtml([x.subject,x.terms,x.status].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small></article>`).join('')}</section>`:`<section class="selection-safe-panel"><b>✓ 선택과목 검증 정상</b><span>현재 선택과목 기준 문·이과 교차지원·과학과목 위계·학기간 중복과목 오류가 없습니다.</span></section>`;
    const profilePanel=`<section class="curriculum-career-profile subtle"><header><div><small>CAREER REFERENCE</small><h3>선택과목 상담 참고정보</h3></div><span>05_대학적성데이터</span></header>${careerSupportMarkup(student,bundle,true)}</section>`;
    const submit=selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle);
    return `<div class="curriculum-summary"><b>${escapeHtml(student?.name||'학생')} 선택과목</b><span>${bundle.selected.length}과목</span><small>최종 신청 ${escapeHtml(submit.date)} · ${submit.count}차 신청 · 8/3 안내 후 ${submit.modifiedAfterNotice?'수정함':'추가 수정 없음'}</small></div>${profilePanel}${errorPanel}<div class="curriculum-term-grid">${['2학년 1학기','2학년 2학기','3학년 1학기','3학년 2학기'].map(term=>{const rows=byTerm.get(term)||[];return `<section><header><h3>${escapeHtml(term)}</h3><span>${rows.length}과목</span></header>${rows.map(x=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(x.subject)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([x.group,x.track,x.major].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small><em>${escapeHtml(x.status||'상담용 신청')}</em></article>`).join('')||'<div class="empty-inline">신청 없음</div>'}</section>`;}).join('')}</div>${curriculumMessagePanel(student,bundle)}`;
  }
  return recordWriterWorkspace(student,bundle);
}
function recordsView() {
  const allStudents=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const classStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
  if(!recordStudentId||!allStudents.some(s=>s.id===recordStudentId))recordStudentId=(classStudents[0]||allStudents[0])?.id||"";
  let student=allStudents.find(s=>s.id===recordStudentId)||classStudents[0]||allStudents[0];
  if(student&&recordStudentClass(student)!==String(recordClassNo))recordClassNo=recordStudentClass(student)||recordClassNo;
  const visibleClassStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).filter(s=>!(recordMode==='curriculum'&&recordCurriculumErrorOnly)||selectionErrorsForStudent(s).length>0).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
  if(!visibleClassStudents.some(s=>s.id===student?.id))student=visibleClassStudents[0]||student;
  const bundle=student?studentRecordBundle(student):{activities:[],selected:[],finalRecords:[],programs:[],reports:[]};
  const tabs=[["activities","활동내역"],["sdgs","SDGs 연계 성장 프로파일"],["curriculum","교육과정"],["final","기록"]];
  const classCards=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1)).map(no=>`<button data-record-class="${no}" class="${String(recordClassNo)===no?"active":""}"><b>${no}반</b><span>${allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===no).length}명</span></button>`).join("");
  const options=visibleClassStudents.map(s=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id===student?.id?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
  const personalToolbar=`<div class="record-student-toolbar personal-only"><div class="record-class-cards">${classCards}</div><label>${recordClassNo}반 학생<select id="recordStudentSelect">${options}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-open-student-dashboard="${escapeHtml(student?.id||"")}">학생 개인 대시보드</button></div>`;
  let content="";
  if(recordMode==="sdgs"){
    content=personalToolbar+recordPersonalContent(student,bundle);
  }else if(recordQueryMode==="class"){
    content=`<div class="record-query-bar"><div class="record-query-switch"><button data-record-query="class" class="active">반별 조회</button><button data-record-query="student">개인별 조회</button></div><div class="record-class-cards">${classCards}</div>${recordMode==='curriculum'?`<button type="button" class="btn ${recordCurriculumErrorOnly?'primary':'secondary'}" data-curriculum-error-only>${recordCurriculumErrorOnly?'오류학생만 보는 중':'오류학생만 보기'}</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-generate-bulk-curriculum-message>오류학생 일괄 문구 생성</button>`:''}</div><div class="record-class-summary"><div><small>${recordMode==="activities"?"ACTIVITY":recordMode==="curriculum"?"CURRICULUM":"FINAL RECORD"}</small><h3>${recordClassNo}반 한 번에 보기</h3><p>${recordMode==='curriculum'?'오류 배지를 누르면 해당 학생의 신청내역과 안내문을 확인할 수 있습니다.':'학생을 누르면 개인 상세로 전환됩니다.'}</p></div><span>${visibleClassStudents.length}명</span></div>${recordClassTable(visibleClassStudents)}${recordMode==='curriculum'?`<section class="curriculum-message-panel curriculum-bulk-panel" id="curriculumBulkPanel" hidden><header><div><small>BULK MESSAGE</small><h3>${recordClassNo}반 오류학생 일괄 안내문</h3></div><span>${visibleClassStudents.filter(s=>selectionErrorsForStudent(s).length).length}명</span></header><textarea id="curriculumBulkMessageText" readonly></textarea><div class="curriculum-message-actions"><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-copy-bulk-curriculum-message>전체 복사</button><a class="btn secondary" href="${escapeHtml(SUBJECT_REAPPLICATION_FORM_URL)}" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">수강신청 구글폼 열기</a></div><p>현재 반의 오류학생 안내문을 학번·이름 구분선과 함께 한 번에 생성합니다.</p></section>`:''}`;
  }else{
    content=`<div class="record-query-bar"><div class="record-query-switch"><button data-record-query="class">반별 조회</button><button data-record-query="student" class="active">개인별 조회</button></div></div>${personalToolbar}${recordPersonalContent(student,bundle)}`;
  }
  return `<div class="module-page records-v0601"><div class="record-main-tabs">${tabs.map(([id,label])=>`<button data-record-mode="${id}" class="${recordMode===id?"active":""}"><b>${label}</b><small>${id==="activities"?"학생 활동 사실 확인":id==="sdgs"?"진로·전공·탐구주제·사회적 가치 연계":id==="curriculum"?"학기별 선택과목":"학생 성장 자료"}</small></button>`).join("")}</div>${content}</div>`;
}
function afterSchoolProgramGroups() {
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).map(programWithOverride).filter((program) => program.kind === "after").map((program) => ({...program, reportRequired:false}));
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.afterSchoolAttendance || []).slice();
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach((row) => {
    const type = String(row.type || "방과후 프로그램").trim() || "방과후 프로그램";
    const time = String(row.time || row.period || "").trim();
    const key = [row.date || "", type, time].join("|");
    if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, { key, id: `after-${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, date: row.date || "", title: type, actualTitle: type, recordTitle: type, time: time || "시간 미등록", place: row.detail || "운영정보 확인", teacher: "", records: [], kind: "after", reportRequired: false, affectsAttendance: /오후자습/.test(type), source:"30_야자출결_정규화" });
    groups.get(key).records.push(row);
  });
  const derived = [...groups.values()].map((program) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    program.students = program.records.filter((row) => {
      const key = attendanceStudentKey(row);
      if (seen.has(key)) return false;
      seen.add(key);
      return true;
    });
    program.attendedCount = program.students.filter(nightRecordIsAttendance).length;
    return program;
  });

  const cleanId=value=>String(value||"").replace(/^after-(?:course|master|session)-/,"").trim();
  const courseKey=program=>cleanId(program?.courseId||program?.programId||program?.id||"");
  const slotKey=program=>{
    const named=typeof nightSlotName==="function"?nightSlotName(program):"";
    if(["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(named)) return named;
    return String(program?.time||program?.period||"").replace(/\s/g,"").trim()||"시간미등록";
  };
  const eventKey=program=>{
    const sid=cleanId(program?.sessionId||"");
    if(sid) return `session|${sid}`;
    const course=courseKey(program);
    const date=String(program?.date||"").slice(0,10);
    const slot=slotKey(program);
    const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||program?.type||"").replace(/\s/g,"").trim();
    return ["event",course,date,slot,title].join("|");
  };

  // 강좌 마스터와 실제 차시가 함께 들어온 경우 차시가 일정의 원본이다.
  const sessionCourseKeys=new Set(connected.filter(program=>!program.isCourseMaster).map(courseKey).filter(Boolean));
  const canonicalConnected=[];
  const seenConnected=new Set();
  connected.forEach(program=>{
    if(program.isCourseMaster&&courseKey(program)&&sessionCourseKeys.has(courseKey(program))) return;
    const key=eventKey(program);
    if(seenConnected.has(key)) return;
    seenConnected.add(key);
    canonicalConnected.push(program);
  });

  // 실제 차시가 존재하는 날짜·타임에서는 출결 정규화 행으로 두 번째 일정 카드를 만들지 않는다.
  const connectedCoverage=new Set(canonicalConnected.filter(program=>!program.isCourseMaster&&program.date).map(program=>`${String(program.date).slice(0,10)}|${slotKey(program)}`));
  const canonicalDerived=derived.filter(program=>!connectedCoverage.has(`${String(program.date||"").slice(0,10)}|${slotKey(program)}`));

  return [...canonicalConnected, ...canonicalDerived].sort((a,b) => String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)));
}
function groupedAfterSchoolCourses(items) {
  const groups=new Map();
  (items||[]).filter(p=>p&&p.kind==="after").forEach(program=>{
    const courseId=String(program.courseId||program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^after-session-/,"");
    const key=courseId||`${program.actualTitle||program.title}|${program.type||""}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{...program,id:`after-course-${key}`,sessionIds:[],dates:[],students:[],attendedCount:0,sessionCount:0});
    const group=groups.get(key);
    if(!program.isCourseMaster) group.sessionIds.push(program.sessionId||program.id);
    if(program.date) group.dates.push(program.date);
    if(program.endDate) group.dates.push(program.endDate);
    if(!program.isCourseMaster) group.sessionCount+=1;
    group.affectsAttendance=Boolean(group.affectsAttendance||programHasNightStudyImpact(program));
    if((group.dates||[]).length&&(group.dates||[]).every(isWednesdayKey)) group.affectsAttendance=false;
    const studentMap=new Map((group.students||[]).map(st=>[String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number),st]));
    (program.students||[]).forEach(st=>{const sk=String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number);if(sk&&!studentMap.has(sk))studentMap.set(sk,st);});
    group.students=[...studentMap.values()];
  });
  return [...groups.values()].map(group=>{
    const dates=[...new Set(group.dates)].sort();
    const first=dates[0]||group.date||"", last=dates.at(-1)||first;
    group.date=first;
    group.endDate=last;
    group.scheduleText=first&&last&&first!==last?`${first} ~ ${last}`:first;
    group.time=group.type==="여름방학"||/방학/.test(`${group.type||""} ${group.afterType||""}`)?group.time:(group.time||"시간 확인");
    group.attendedCount=group.students.filter(nightRecordIsAttendance).length;
    group.sessionNo=`${group.sessionCount}차시`;
    group.recordTitle="생활기록부 기록 대상 아님";
    return group;
  }).sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.date||""))||String(a.title||"").localeCompare(String(b.title||"")));
}

function programWithOverride(program) {
  if (!program || typeof program !== "object") return program;
  const override = state.programOverrides?.[program.id] || {};
  const merged = {...program, ...override, students: program.students || []};
  // 보고서 폼 URL은 프로그램별 로컬 override가 아니라 00_UEP설정의 공통값이 원본입니다.
  // 과거 로컬 빈값/구버전 override가 공통 URL을 가리지 않도록 화면 직전에도 표준 설정값을 우선 적용합니다.
  const uepSettings = readonlyCache?.uepSettings || {};
  if (program.kind === "selected") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.SELECTED_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  if (program.kind === "common") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.COMMON_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  return merged;
}
function programAreaKind(program) {
  if (program?.kind === "after" || /방과후/.test(String(program?.type || ""))) return "after";
  const text = `${program?.type || ""} ${program?.recordTitle || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || program?.title || ""}`;
  if (/진로/.test(text)) return "career";
  return "autonomy";
}
function programAreaLabel(program) {
  const kind = programAreaKind(program);
  if (kind === "career") return "진로";
  if (kind === "after") {
    const text = `${program?.afterType || ""} ${program?.type || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || program?.title || ""}`;
    if (/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return "야간심화수업";
    return "방과후";
  }
  return "자율";
}

function programMonthKey(program) {
  const direct=String(program?.date||"").match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2})/);
  if(direct) return direct[1];
  const fallback=String(program?.scheduleText||program?.originalSchedule||"");
  const year=fallback.match(/(20\d{2})/)?.[1]||String(new Date().getFullYear());
  const month=fallback.match(/(?:^|\D)(1[0-2]|0?[1-9])\s*월/)?.[1];
  return month?`${year}-${String(month).padStart(2,"0")}`:"";
}
function programTopTabs() {
  const tab=(mode,label)=>`<button data-program-mode="${mode}" class="${programMode===mode?"active":""}">${label}</button>`;
  return `<div class="program-sticky-tabs">${tab("common","공통활동")}${tab("selected","선택활동")}${tab("after","방과후학교")}${tab("planning","프로그램 기획센터")}</div>`;
}
function programMonthPicker() {
  const [year,month]=programMonth.split("-").map(Number);
  return `<div class="program-month-picker"><button data-program-month-shift="-1">‹</button><label><span>월별 조회</span><input id="programMonthInput" type="month" value="${escapeHtml(programMonth)}"></label><strong>${year}년 ${month}월</strong><button data-program-month-shift="1">›</button></div>`;
}
function commonProgramClassStudent(){
  return (readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(commonProgramClassNo))||null;
}
function commonProgramAreaPanel(commonItems){
  const autonomy=commonItems.filter(p=>recordProgramArea(p)!=="career");
  const career=commonItems.filter(p=>recordProgramArea(p)==="career");
  const careerVisible=career.filter(p=>programMonthKey(p)===programMonth);
  const autonomyVisible=autonomy.filter(p=>programMonthKey(p)===programMonth);
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const unifiedRow=program=>{
    const studentCount=Array.isArray(program.students)?program.students.length:0;
    const attended=Number(program.attendedCount||0);
    const actualTitle=program.actualTitle||program.title||"-";
    const recordTitle=program.recordTitle||program.type||actualTitle;
    const place=program.place||program.scheduleText||"운영정보 확인";
    const evidence=state.programEvidence?.[program.id]||[];
    const reportText=program.reportRequired===false?"보고서 미사용":Number.isFinite(Number(program.reportSubmittedCount))?`학생 ${Number(program.reportSubmittedCount)}명`:"보고서 연계 가능";
    return `<button class="data-row program-unified-grid program-manage-row" data-program="${escapeHtml(program.id)}" data-search="${escapeHtml([program.date,actualTitle,recordTitle,program.type,program.time,place].join(" ").toLowerCase())}"><time>${escapeHtml(program.date||program.scheduleText||"-")}${program.date===todayKey?'<em class="today-badge">오늘</em>':""}</time><span class="program-kind-badge area-${programAreaKind(program)}">${programAreaLabel(program)}</span><b>${escapeHtml(actualTitle)}</b><span class="record-title">${escapeHtml(recordTitle)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(program.time||"-")}</span><span>${escapeHtml(place)}</span><span>${studentCount||"전체"}${studentCount?"명":""}</span><span class="program-attendance-count"><b>${attended}</b> / ${studentCount||"전체"}${studentCount?"명":""}</span><span class="program-report-stack"><small>${escapeHtml(reportText)}</small><b>${escapeHtml(program.sdgs?`SDGs ${program.sdgs}`:"SDGs 미설정")}</b><em>사진 ${evidence.length}장</em></span><em>열기 ›</em></button>`;
  };
  const autonomyRows=autonomyVisible.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.date||""))).map(unifiedRow).join("");
  const careerRows=careerVisible.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.date||""))).map(unifiedRow).join("");
  const visibleRows=commonProgramArea==="autonomy"?autonomyRows:careerRows;
  const areaLabel=commonProgramArea==="autonomy"?"자율활동":"진로활동";
  return `<section class="common-program-control"><div class="common-area-tabs"><button data-common-program-area="autonomy" class="${commonProgramArea==="autonomy"?"active":""}">공통(자율활동) <b>${autonomy.length}</b></button><button data-common-program-area="career" class="${commonProgramArea==="career"?"active":""}">공통(진로활동) <b>${career.length}</b></button></div>
  <div class="toolbar"><input class="search" type="search" id="programPageSearch" placeholder="활동명·운영일·기록명 검색"><button class="btn secondary" data-program-sheet>시트 연결</button></div><div class="data-card program-table-scroll" id="programPageRows"><div class="data-head program-unified-grid"><span>운영일</span><span>구분</span><span>프로그램명</span><span>영역·기록명</span><span>시간</span><span>장소·운영정보</span><span>대상</span><span>출결</span><span>보고·SDGs</span><span></span></div>${visibleRows||`<div class="empty-state"><b>${escapeHtml(Number(programMonth.slice(5,7)))}월은 공통 ${areaLabel}이 없습니다.</b></div>`}</div></section>`;
}

function programDurationHours(program){
  const direct=Number(program?.hours||program?.durationHours||program?.totalHours);
  if(Number.isFinite(direct)&&direct>0)return direct;
  const sessionCount=Number(program?.sessionCount||program?.sessions||String(program?.sessionNo||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]);
  if(Number.isFinite(sessionCount)&&sessionCount>0)return sessionCount;
  const time=String(program?.time||"");
  const match=time.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*[~〜-]\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  if(match){const start=Number(match[1])*60+Number(match[2]);const end=Number(match[3])*60+Number(match[4]);if(end>start)return Math.round((end-start)/6)/10;}
  return program?.date?1:0;
}
function programPlanningCenter(allPrograms){
  const programs=(allPrograms||[]).filter(Boolean);
  const selected=programs.filter(p=>p.kind==="selected"||p.kind==="general");
  const common=programs.filter(p=>p.kind==="common");
  const after=programs.filter(p=>p.kind==="after");
  const autonomy=selected.filter(p=>programAreaKind(p)==="autonomy");
  const career=selected.filter(p=>programAreaKind(p)==="career");
  const reports=(readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[]).filter(r=>r?.submitted!==false);
  const programById=new Map(programs.map(p=>[String(p.programId||p.id||''),p]));
  const reportProgram=r=>programById.get(String(r.programId||''))||selected.find(p=>String(p.actualTitle||p.title||'').replace(/\s/g,'')===String(r.programName||r.reportGroup||'').replace(/\s/g,''));
  const autonomyReports=reports.filter(r=>programAreaKind(reportProgram(r)||{type:r.kind||r.area||''})==='autonomy');
  const careerReports=reports.filter(r=>programAreaKind(reportProgram(r)||{type:r.kind||r.area||''})==='career');
  const participantKeys=new Set([...selected,...after].flatMap(p=>(p.students||[]).map(st=>attendanceStudentKey(st)||`${st.studentNo||st.number||""}|${st.name||""}`).filter(Boolean)));
  const teacherMap=new Map();
  [...selected,...after].forEach(p=>{
    const raw=String(p.teacher||p.teacherName||p.manager||p.instructor||"").trim(); if(!raw)return;
    raw.split(/[,/·]/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(name=>{
      if(!teacherMap.has(name))teacherMap.set(name,{name,selected:0,after:0,night:0,hours:0,students:new Set(),programs:0});
      const row=teacherMap.get(name),hours=programDurationHours(p); row.programs+=1;row.hours+=hours;
      if(p.kind==="selected"||p.kind==="general")row.selected+=hours;
      else if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(`${p.afterType||""} ${p.type||""} ${p.title||""}`))row.night+=hours;
      else row.after+=hours;
      (p.students||[]).forEach(st=>row.students.add(attendanceStudentKey(st)||`${st.studentNo||st.number||""}|${st.name||""}`));
    });
  });
  const teacherRows=[...teacherMap.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.hours-a.hours||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  const sdgCounts=new Map(SDG_CATALOG.map(([n,name])=>[n,{name,count:0}]));
  autonomyReports.forEach(r=>analyzeReportTags(r).sdgs.forEach((x,i)=>{if(i===0||x.score>1){const row=sdgCounts.get(Number(x.key));if(row)row.count++;}}));
  const careerCounts=new Map(CAREER_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,key])=>[key,{label,count:0}]));
  const majorCounts=new Map();
  careerReports.forEach(r=>{const a=analyzeReportTags(r);a.careers.slice(0,1).forEach(x=>{const row=careerCounts.get(x.key);if(row)row.count++;});a.majors.slice(0,2).forEach(x=>majorCounts.set(x.label,(majorCounts.get(x.label)||0)+1));});
  const usedSdgs=[...sdgCounts.entries()].filter(([,v])=>v.count>0).sort((a,b)=>b[1].count-a[1].count);
  const leastSdgs=[...sdgCounts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[1].count-b[1].count||a[0]-b[0]).slice(0,4);
  const usedCareers=[...careerCounts.entries()].filter(([,v])=>v.count>0).sort((a,b)=>b[1].count-a[1].count);
  const leastCareers=[...careerCounts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[1].count-b[1].count).slice(0,3);
  const recommendations=[];
  if(autonomyReports.length)recommendations.push(`자율활동 보고서에서 가장 적게 나타난 SDGs는 ${leastSdgs.map(([n,v])=>`SDG ${n} ${v.name}(${v.count}건)`).join(', ')}입니다.`);
  else recommendations.push('자율활동 보고서가 연결되면 SDGs 1~17 중 학생 탐구가 적은 분야를 자동 발굴합니다.');
  if(careerReports.length)recommendations.push(`진로활동 보고서에서 상대적으로 적은 진로계열은 ${leastCareers.map(([,v])=>`${v.label}(${v.count}건)`).join(', ')}입니다.`);
  else recommendations.push('진로활동 보고서가 연결되면 문과·이과와 세부 전공군별 부족 분야를 자동 발굴합니다.');
  if(after.length===0)recommendations.push('방과후·야간심화 운영 자료가 연결되면 교사별 운영시간을 근거로 2학기 편성을 지원합니다.');
  const tabs=[['overview','운영현황'],['students','학생분석'],['teachers','교사분석'],['programs','프로그램분석'],['recommend','프로그램추천']].map(([id,label])=>`<button data-program-planning-mode="${id}" class="${programPlanningMode===id?'active':''}">${label}</button>`).join('');
  const summary=`<div class="planning-summary-grid"><span><b>${selected.length}</b>선택활동</span><span><b>${autonomy.length}</b>자율활동</span><span><b>${career.length}</b>진로활동</span><span><b>${after.length}</b>방과후·야간심화</span><span><b>${participantKeys.size}</b>참여 학생</span><span><b>${teacherRows.length}</b>운영 교사</span></div>`;
  let body='';
  if(programPlanningMode==='overview')body=`${summary}<div class="planning-two-column"><section><h4>선택활동 구성</h4><div class="planning-bars"><p><span>자율활동</span><b>${autonomy.length}개</b></p><p><span>진로활동</span><b>${career.length}개</b></p><p><span>공통활동</span><b>${common.length}개 · 단순 현황</b></p></div></section><section><h4>보고서 분석 준비</h4><div class="planning-bars"><p><span>자율 보고서</span><b>${autonomyReports.length}건</b></p><p><span>진로 보고서</span><b>${careerReports.length}건</b></p><p><span>방과후·야간심화</span><b>${after.reduce((a,p)=>a+programDurationHours(p),0).toFixed(1)}시간</b></p></div></section></div>`;
  if(programPlanningMode==='students')body=`${summary}<section class="planning-panel"><h4>학생 결과물 중심 분석</h4><p>학생 개인 참여도는 기존 출석부에서 확인합니다. 이곳에서는 제출 보고서를 통해 자율활동의 SDGs 탐구와 진로활동의 계열·전공군 분포를 다음 프로그램 기획 근거로 사용합니다.</p><div class="planning-bars"><p><span>선택활동 참여</span><b>${new Set(selected.flatMap(p=>(p.students||[]).map(attendanceStudentKey))).size}명</b></p><p><span>자율활동 보고서</span><b>${autonomyReports.length}건</b></p><p><span>진로활동 보고서</span><b>${careerReports.length}건</b></p><p><span>전체 제출 보고서</span><b>${reports.length}건</b></p></div></section>`;
  if(programPlanningMode==='teachers')body=`<section class="planning-panel"><div class="planning-title-row"><div><h4>교사별 운영시간</h4><p>공통활동은 학교교육계획 통계로만 두고, 선택활동·방과후·야간심화의 교사별 실제 운영시간을 확인합니다.</p></div><span>${teacherRows.length}명</span></div>${teacherRows.length?`<div class="teacher-analysis-table teacher-analysis-table-compact"><div class="teacher-analysis-head"><span>교사</span><span>선택</span><span>방과후</span><span>야간심화</span><span>총시간</span><span>담당학생</span></div>${teacherRows.map(r=>`<div><b>${escapeHtml(r.name)}</b><span>${r.selected.toFixed(1)}</span><span>${r.after.toFixed(1)}</span><span>${r.night.toFixed(1)}</span><strong>${r.hours.toFixed(1)}시간</strong><span>${r.students.size}명</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="empty-state"><b>담당교사 또는 운영시간 자료가 연결되지 않았습니다.</b></div>'}</section>`;
  if(programPlanningMode==='programs')body=`<div class="planning-two-column"><section><h4>자율활동 · 보고서 기반 SDGs</h4>${usedSdgs.length?usedSdgs.map(([n,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row"><span>SDG ${n} ${escapeHtml(v.name)}</span><b>${v.count}건</b></p>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-state">자율활동 보고서에서 분석된 SDGs가 없습니다.</div>'}<h4 class="planning-subtitle">가장 적은 SDGs</h4>${leastSdgs.map(([n,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row planning-low"><span>SDG ${n} ${escapeHtml(v.name)}</span><b>${v.count}건</b></p>`).join('')}</section><section><h4>진로활동 · 계열 및 전공군</h4>${usedCareers.length?usedCareers.map(([,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row"><span>${escapeHtml(v.label)}</span><b>${v.count}건</b></p>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-state">진로활동 보고서에서 분석된 계열 자료가 없습니다.</div>'}<h4 class="planning-subtitle">세부 전공군</h4>${majorCounts.size?[...majorCounts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([k,v])=>`<p class="planning-list-row"><span>${escapeHtml(k)}</span><b>${v}건</b></p>`).join(''):'<div class="empty-state">분석된 전공군이 없습니다.</div>'}</section></div>`;
  if(programPlanningMode==='recommend')body=`<section class="planning-panel"><div class="planning-title-row"><div><h4>다음 프로그램 기획 근거</h4><p>프로그램 자체에 태그를 찍지 않고 학생 보고서의 자동 태그 결과를 근거로 부족 영역을 제시합니다.</p></div></div><div class="planning-recommendations">${recommendations.map((r,i)=>`<article><span>${i+1}</span><b>${escapeHtml(r)}</b></article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
  return `<div class="program-planning-center"><div class="module-intro pastel-peach"><span>04</span><div><h3>프로그램 기획센터</h3><p>공통활동은 단순 현황으로만 확인하고, 자율활동은 학생 보고서의 SDGs, 진로활동은 계열·전공군, 방과후·야간심화는 교사별 운영시간을 근거로 다음 프로그램을 설계합니다.</p></div></div><div class="planning-tabs">${tabs}</div>${body}</div>`;
}

function programsView() {
  const monthLabel=`${programMonth.slice(0,4)}년 ${Number(programMonth.slice(5,7))}월`;
  const connectedPrograms = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const localSelected=(state.programs||[]).filter(program=>program.kind!=="after").map(program=>({...program,kind:"selected",actualTitle:program.actualTitle||program.title,recordTitle:program.recordTitle||program.title,reportRequired:program.reportRequired!==false,attendedCount:Array.isArray(program.students)?program.students.length:0,source:"이 PC 로컬"}));
  const localAfter=(state.programs||[]).filter(program=>program.kind==="after").map(program=>({...program,kind:"after",actualTitle:program.actualTitle||program.title,recordTitle:"생활기록부 기록 대상 아님",reportRequired:false,reportToAuthority:false,attendedCount:Array.isArray(program.students)?program.students.length:0,source:"이 PC 로컬",affectsAttendance:true}));
  const commonItems=connectedPrograms.filter(program=>program.kind==="common");
  const selectedConnected=connectedPrograms.filter(program=>program.kind==="selected" || program.kind==="general");
  const selectedKeys=new Set(selectedConnected.map(program=>`${program.programId||program.id}|${program.date}|${program.title}`));
  const selectedItems=[...selectedConnected,...localSelected.filter(program=>!selectedKeys.has(`${program.programId||program.id}|${program.date}|${program.title}`))];
  const connectedAfter=connectedPrograms.filter(program=>program.kind==="after");
  const afterKeys=new Set(connectedAfter.map(program=>`${program.programId||program.id}|${program.date}|${program.title}`));
  // 기본연결시트에 11_방과후학교·12_차시일정 기반 강좌/차시가 있으면 이를 단일 진실원천으로 사용한다.
  // 30_야자출결_정규화에서 파생한 시간대별 행을 프로그램 목록에 다시 합치면
  // 같은 강좌가 야자1·야자2별 별도 프로그램으로 중복 표시되므로, 연결 강좌가 없을 때만 fallback으로 사용한다.
  const derivedAfterFallback=connectedAfter.length?[]:afterSchoolProgramGroups();
  const afterSessions=[...connectedAfter,...derivedAfterFallback.filter(program=>!afterKeys.has(`${program.programId||program.id}|${program.date}|${program.title}`)),...localAfter.filter(program=>!afterKeys.has(`${program.programId||program.id}|${program.date}|${program.title}`))];
  const afterItems=groupedAfterSchoolCourses(afterSessions);
  const afterMasterCount=afterItems.length;
  const afterSessionCount=afterSessions.filter(program=>!program.isCourseMaster).length;
  const afterRosterStudents=new Set(afterSessions.flatMap(program=>(program.students||[]).map(student=>student.studentId||student.studentNo||student.number).filter(Boolean))).size;
  const afterBadStatus=connectedAfter.filter(program=>/일정확인필요/.test(String(program.status||""))).length;
  const afterCoursesWithoutSessions=afterItems.filter(program=>!Number(program.sessionCount||0)).length;
  const afterDbHealthy=afterMasterCount>0&&afterSessionCount>0&&afterRosterStudents>0&&afterBadStatus===0&&afterCoursesWithoutSessions===0;
  const afterDbCheck=`<div class="afterschool-db-check ${afterDbHealthy?"ok":"warn"}"><div><small>기본연결시트 실시간 점검</small><h4>${afterDbHealthy?"방과후학교 연결 정상":"방과후학교 연결 확인 필요"}</h4><p>11_방과후학교 → 12_차시일정 → 13_출석부 순서로 읽습니다. 특색프로그램의 ‘일정확인필요’ 행은 방과후 집계에서 제외됩니다.</p></div><div class="afterschool-db-kpis"><span><b>${afterMasterCount}</b>강좌</span><span><b>${afterSessionCount}</b>차시</span><span><b>${afterRosterStudents}</b>수강학생</span><span><b>${afterBadStatus+afterCoursesWithoutSessions}</b>확인필요</span></div></div>`;
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const monthFilter=(items)=>items.filter(program=>programMonthKey(program)===programMonth);
  const afterMonthFilter=(items)=>items.filter(program=>{
    const sessionDates=Array.isArray(program.dates)?program.dates:[];
    return sessionDates.some(date=>String(date).startsWith(programMonth)) || programMonthKey(program)===programMonth;
  });
  const afterCourseKey=(program)=>String(program.courseId||program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^after-(?:course|master|session)-/,"");
  const afterMonthSessions=(course)=>afterSessions.filter(session=>session&&session.kind==="after"&&!session.isCourseMaster&&afterCourseKey(session)===afterCourseKey(course)&&String(session.date||"").startsWith(programMonth)&&!/일정확인필요|취소|휴강|폐강/.test(String(session.status||session.operationStatus||""))).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||"").localeCompare(String(b.date||""))||String(a.time||"").localeCompare(String(b.time||"")));
  const afterSessionMarkup=(course)=>{const sessions=afterMonthSessions(course);return `<div class="afterschool-month-sessions"><header><b>${escapeHtml(monthLabel)} 차시별 일정</b><span>해당 월 ${sessions.length}차시</span></header>${sessions.length?`<div class="afterschool-session-grid">${sessions.map((session)=>`<button type="button" data-after-program="${escapeHtml(session.id||course.id)}"><em>${escapeHtml(session.sessionNo||"-")}차시</em><time>${escapeHtml(session.date||"-")} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(session.date,false))}</time><b>${escapeHtml(singlePeriodLabel(session))}</b><span>${escapeHtml(session.place||course.place||"장소 확인")}</span><small>${escapeHtml(session.status||"운영")}</small></button>`).join("")}</div>`:`<p>이 달에 실제 운영되는 차시가 없습니다.</p>`}</div>`;};
  const focusStudent=studentFocusStudent();
  const hasFocusStudent=(program)=>!focusStudent||(program.students||[]).some(row=>studentMatches(row,focusStudent))||program.kind==="common";
  const commonMonth=monthFilter(commonItems).filter(hasFocusStudent), selectedMonth=monthFilter(selectedItems).filter(hasFocusStudent), afterMonth=afterMonthFilter(afterItems).filter(hasFocusStudent);
  const summary=(items)=>({count:items.length,today:items.filter(p=>p.date===todayKey).length,students:new Set(items.flatMap(p=>(p.students||[]).map(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)||`${s.number||""}|${s.name||""}`))).size});
  const commonSummary=summary(commonMonth), selectedSummary=summary(selectedMonth), afterSummary=summary(afterMonth);
  const card=(mode,icon,title,subtitle,description,stat,accent)=>`<button class="program-hub-card ${accent} ${programMode===mode?"active":""}" data-program-mode="${mode}"><span class="program-hub-icon">${icon}</span><div><small>${escapeHtml(subtitle)}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(title)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(description)}</p></div><footer><span><b>${stat.count}</b>개 운영</span><span><b>${stat.today}</b>개 오늘</span><span><b>${stat.students}</b>명 대상</span><em>${programMode===mode?"선택됨":"보기"} ›</em></footer></button>`;
  const hub=`${programMonthPicker()}<div class="program-hub-grid program-hub-grid-fixed">${card("common","◉","공통활동","학교 창체운영계획","전체학생을 기본 대상으로 하고 출결 현황을 관리합니다.",commonSummary,"common")}${card("selected","★","선택활동","학년 특색프로그램","모집·참여학생, 자율·진로 영역, SDGs와 보고서를 함께 관리합니다.",selectedSummary,"selected")}${card("after","▦","방과후학교","수업·야자출결 연동","1학기·여름방학·2학기·겨울방학 수업과 수강학생을 관리합니다.",afterSummary,"after")}${card("planning","◎","프로그램 기획센터","다음 학기 설계","운영 이력·학생 수요·교사별 운영시간·SDGs 분포를 바탕으로 기획 근거를 확인합니다.",{count:commonMonth.length+selectedMonth.length+afterMonth.length,today:0,students:new Set([...commonMonth,...selectedMonth,...afterMonth].flatMap(p=>(p.students||[]).map(attendanceStudentKey))).size},"planning")}</div>`;
  if(programMode==="home"){
    const todayAfterSessions=afterSessions.filter(p=>p&&p.kind==="after"&&!p.isCourseMaster&&p.date===todayKey&&!/일정확인필요|취소|휴강|폐강|결강/.test(String(p.status||p.operationStatus||"")));     const todayPrograms=[...commonMonth,...selectedMonth,...todayAfterSessions].filter(p=>p.date===todayKey&&!isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(p));
    return `<div class="module-page program-hub-page">${hub}<div class="program-hub-today"><header><div><small>TODAY</small><h3>오늘의 프로그램</h3></div><button class="btn secondary" data-program-sheet>연결시트 확인</button></header>${todayPrograms.map(p=>`<button data-program="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"><span class="program-kind-badge area-${programAreaKind(p)}">${programAreaLabel(p)}</span><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml([p.time,p.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||"운영정보 확인")}</small><em>${(p.students||[]).length}명 ›</em></button>`).join("")||'<div class="empty-state"><b>오늘 등록된 프로그램이 없습니다.</b><span></span></div>'}</div></div>`;
  }
  if(programMode==="planning")return `<div class="module-page program-detail-page">${hub}<div class="program-content-panel">${programPlanningCenter([...commonItems,...selectedItems,...afterItems])}</div></div>`;
  if(programMode==="common")return `<div class="module-page program-detail-page">${hub}<div class="program-content-panel">${commonProgramAreaPanel(focusStudent?commonItems.filter(hasFocusStudent):commonItems)}</div></div>`;
  const items=programMode==="after"?afterMonth:selectedMonth;
  const title=programMode==="after"?"방과후학교":"선택활동";
  const intro=programMode==="after"?"운영기간·수업시간·수강학생을 관리하고 야자출결과 연동합니다.":"학년 특색프로그램의 참여학생, 자율·진로 영역, SDGs와 보고서를 관리합니다.";
  const uniqueParticipants=new Set();items.forEach(program=>(program.students||[]).forEach(student=>uniqueParticipants.add(attendanceStudentKey(student)||`${student.number||""}|${student.name||""}`)));
  const rows=items.length?items.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.date||""))).map(program=>{const isAfter=program.kind==="after",isCommon=program.kind==="common";const studentCount=Array.isArray(program.students)?program.students.length:0;const attended=Number(program.attendedCount||0);const rowAttr=isAfter?`data-after-program="${escapeHtml(program.id)}"`:`data-program="${escapeHtml(program.id)}"`;const place=program.place||program.scheduleText||"운영정보 확인";const actualTitle=program.actualTitle||program.title||"-";const recordTitle=isAfter?"생활기록부 기록 대상 아님":(program.recordTitle||actualTitle);const evidence=state.programEvidence?.[program.id]||[];const reportText=isAfter?"보고서 없음":program.reportRequired===false?"보고서 미사용":Number.isFinite(Number(program.reportSubmittedCount))?`학생 ${Number(program.reportSubmittedCount)}명`:"보고서 필요";const kindLabel=isCommon?"공통":isAfter?"방과후":"선택";const impact=programHasNightStudyImpact(program)?`<small class="program-attendance-impact">야자출결 영향</small>`:"";const displayDate=isAfter?(program.scheduleText||([program.date,program.endDate].filter(Boolean).join(" ~ "))||program.date||"-"):(program.date||program.scheduleText||"-");const mainRow=`<button class="data-row program-unified-grid program-manage-row" ${rowAttr} data-search="${escapeHtml([displayDate,actualTitle,recordTitle,program.type,program.afterType,program.time,place,program.teacher,kindLabel].join(" ").toLowerCase())}"><time>${escapeHtml(displayDate)}${program.date===todayKey?'<em class="today-badge">오늘</em>':""}</time><span class="program-kind-badge area-${programAreaKind(program)}">${programAreaLabel(program)}</span><b>${escapeHtml(actualTitle)}${impact}</b><span class="record-title ${isAfter?"not-record-target":""}">${escapeHtml(isCommon?(program.type||recordTitle):recordTitle)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(program.time||"-")}</span><span>${escapeHtml(place)}</span><span>${studentCount}명</span><span class="program-attendance-count"><b>${attended}</b> / ${studentCount}명</span><span class="program-report-stack"><small>${escapeHtml(reportText)}</small><b>${escapeHtml(program.sdgs?`SDGs ${program.sdgs}`:program.type||"")}</b><em>사진 ${evidence.length}장</em></span><em>열기 ›</em></button>`;return isAfter?`<article class="afterschool-course-month">${mainRow}${afterSessionMarkup(program)}</article>`:mainRow;}).join(""):`<div class="empty-state month-empty-state"><b>${escapeHtml(monthLabel)}에 등록된 ${programMode==="selected"?"선택프로그램":"방과후학교 프로그램"}이 없습니다.</b></div>`;
  const addKind=programMode==="after"?"after":"general";
  return `<div class="module-page program-detail-page">${hub}<div class="program-content-panel">${programMode==="after"?afterDbCheck:""}<div class="module-intro pastel-peach"><span>03</span><div><h3>${title}</h3><p>${intro}</p></div></div><div class="student-summary"><span><b>${items.length}</b> 운영 단위</span><span><b>${items.filter(p=>p.date===todayKey).length}</b> 오늘 운영</span><span><b>${uniqueParticipants.size}</b> 대상 학생</span><span><b>${items.filter(p=>p.reportRequired).length}</b> 보고서 사용</span></div><div class="toolbar"><input class="search" type="search" id="programPageSearch" placeholder="활동명·운영일·대상 검색"><button class="btn secondary" data-program-sheet>시트 연결</button><button class="btn primary" data-program-add data-program-kind="${addKind}">＋ ${title} 등록</button></div><div class="data-card program-table-scroll" id="programPageRows"><div class="data-head program-unified-grid"><span>운영일</span><span>구분</span><span>프로그램명</span><span>영역·기록명</span><span>시간</span><span>장소·운영정보</span><span>대상</span><span>출결</span><span>보고·SDGs</span><span></span></div>${rows}</div></div></div>`;
}

let workBoardMode="home";

function workBoardData(type){
  if(type==="notice"){
    if(!Array.isArray(state.workNotices))state.workNotices=[];
    return state.workNotices;
  }
  if(type==="deadline"){
    if(!Array.isArray(state.schoolDeadlines))state.schoolDeadlines=[];
    return state.schoolDeadlines;
  }
  return (state.tasks||[]).filter(task=>task?.project!=="UEP"&&(!task.owner||task.owner===currentWorkAudienceContext().name));
}

function alertProgramIsRecordTarget(program){
  if(!program||program.kind==="after"||program.kind==="common"||program.reportRequired===false)return false;
  const label=`${program.kind||""} ${program.activityType||""} ${program.programType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""}`;
  return /선택|자율|진로|챌린지|환경개선/.test(label)||program.kind==="selected"||program.kind==="general";
}
function alertProgramTitle(program){return String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||program?.recordTitle||"프로그램").trim();}
function alertStudentIdentity(raw){
  const student=resolveDashboardStudent(raw)||raw||{};
  const gc=recordGradeClass(student);
  return {studentRef:student,studentId:student.id||student.studentId||raw?.studentId||"",studentNo:student.studentNo||raw?.studentNo||raw?.number||"",name:student.name||student.studentName||raw?.name||"이름 미확인",classNo:String(gc.classNo||student.className||raw?.classNo||raw?.className||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,""),grade:String(gc.grade||student.grade||"")};
}
function studentAlertPrograms(){
  const connected=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride);
  const connectedIds=new Set(connected.map(p=>String(p.id||p.programId||"")));
  const local=(state.programs||[]).filter(p=>p&&!connectedIds.has(String(p.id||p.programId||"")));
  return [...connected,...local];
}
function studentReportAlertRows(){
  const rows=[];
  studentAlertPrograms().filter(alertProgramIsRecordTarget).filter(program=>{const d=program.date?daysFromToday(program.date):null;return d==null||d<=0;}).forEach(program=>{
    (program.students||[]).forEach(raw=>{
      const s=alertStudentIdentity(raw);
      if(raw?.reportSubmitted===true)return;
      rows.push({...s,category:"report",programId:program.id||program.programId||"",programTitle:alertProgramTitle(program),programKind:program.kind||"",date:program.date||"",reportFormUrl:program.reportFormUrl||"",reason:"보고서 미제출"});
    });
  });
  return rows;
}
function studentSelectionAlertRows(){
  const rows=[];
  (readonlyCache?.students||[]).forEach(student=>{
    const errors=typeof selectionErrorsForStudent==="function"?selectionErrorsForStudent(student):[];
    if(!errors?.length)return;
    const s=alertStudentIdentity(student);
    rows.push({...s,category:"selection",reason:`선택과목 오류 ${errors.length}건`,errors});
  });
  return rows;
}
function studentAttendanceAlertRows(){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today), rows=[];
  const late=(readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||"").slice(0,10)===todayKey);
  late.forEach(row=>rows.push({...alertStudentIdentity(row),category:"attendance",reason:"오늘 지각 확인",date:todayKey}));
  (readonlyCache?.programAttendance||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||row.day||"").slice(0,10)===todayKey&&/불참|결석|미참여|오류|확인/.test(String(row.status||row.result||""))).forEach(row=>rows.push({...alertStudentIdentity(row),category:"attendance",reason:String(row.status||row.result||"출결 확인"),programTitle:row.programTitle||row.programName||"프로그램",date:todayKey}));
  return rows;
}
function studentScheduleAlertGroups(){
  const items=[];
  (readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>p&&p.kind!=="after"&&p.kind!=="common"&&p.date).forEach(program=>{
    const d=daysFromToday(program.date);if(d==null||d<0||d>7)return;
    const students=(program.students||[]).map(alertStudentIdentity);
    items.push({id:`schedule:${program.id||program.programId||program.date}`,category:"schedule",title:alertProgramTitle(program),date:program.date,count:students.length,rows:students,meta:d===0?"오늘 운영":`D-${d}`});
  });
  return items.sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date)));
}
function studentAlertRowsByClass(rows){
  if(studentAlertClass==="all")return rows;
  return rows.filter(row=>String(row.classNo||"")===String(studentAlertClass));
}
function studentUnifiedAlertGroups(){
  const groups=[];
  const reportRows=studentAlertRowsByClass(studentReportAlertRows());
  const byProgram=new Map();
  reportRows.forEach(row=>{const key=row.programId||row.programTitle;if(!byProgram.has(key))byProgram.set(key,{id:`report:${key}`,category:"report",title:row.programTitle||"보고서",date:row.date||"",reportFormUrl:row.reportFormUrl||"",programId:row.programId||"",rows:[]});byProgram.get(key).rows.push(row);});
  byProgram.forEach(group=>{group.count=group.rows.length;group.meta=`미제출 ${group.count}명`;groups.push(group);});
  const selection=studentAlertRowsByClass(studentSelectionAlertRows());
  if(selection.length)groups.push({id:"selection:errors",category:"selection",title:"선택과목 수정 제출",rows:selection,count:selection.length,meta:`오류 ${selection.length}명`});
  const attendance=studentAlertRowsByClass(studentAttendanceAlertRows());
  if(attendance.length)groups.push({id:"attendance:today",category:"attendance",title:"오늘 출결 확인",rows:attendance,count:attendance.length,meta:`확인 ${attendance.length}명`});
  studentScheduleAlertGroups().forEach(group=>{
    const scoped=studentAlertClass==="all"?group.rows:group.rows.filter(row=>String(row.classNo||"")===String(studentAlertClass));
    if(group.rows.length&&scoped.length===0)return;
    groups.push({...group,rows:scoped.length?scoped:group.rows,count:scoped.length||group.count});
  });
  return groups;
}
function studentAlertLabel(category){return {report:"보고서",selection:"선택과목",attendance:"출결",schedule:"프로그램 일정"}[category]||"학생안내";}
function buildStudentAlertMessage(group,formUrl=""){
  const rows=group?.rows||[], names=rows.slice(0,60).map(row=>`${row.studentNo||""} ${row.name||""}`.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const link=String(formUrl||group?.reportFormUrl||"").trim();
  if(group.category==="report")return `[운호고 학생 보고서 제출 안내]\n\n${group.title} 활동에 참여한 학생 중 보고서를 아직 제출하지 않은 학생은 보고서를 제출하기 바랍니다. 제출한 내용은 활동의 과정과 성장 내용을 확인하는 생활기록부 작성 근거로 활용됩니다.${link?`\n\n보고서 제출 링크\n${link}`:""}\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
  if(group.category==="selection")return `[운호고 선택과목 수정 제출 안내]\n\n선택과목 신청 내역에서 확인이 필요한 학생입니다. 안내받은 오류 내용을 확인한 뒤 구글폼으로 정확하게 다시 제출해 주기 바랍니다. 이후 제출한 최신 내역을 최종 신청으로 반영합니다.\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
  if(group.category==="attendance")return `[운호고 출결 확인 안내]\n\n오늘 출결 또는 프로그램 참여 상태에서 확인이 필요한 학생입니다. 해당 내용을 확인하고 필요한 경우 담임교사에게 알려 주기 바랍니다.\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
  return `[운호고 프로그램 일정 안내]\n\n${group.title} 프로그램이 ${group.date||"예정일"}에 운영됩니다. 참여 학생은 시간·장소·준비사항을 확인해 주기 바랍니다.${rows.length?`\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`:""}`;
}
function openStudentAlertMessage(groupId){
  const group=studentUnifiedAlertGroups().find(item=>String(item.id)===String(groupId));if(!group)return toast("안내 대상을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const initialUrl=group.category==="report"?String(group.reportFormUrl||""):"";
  const renderMessage=()=>buildStudentAlertMessage(group,$("#studentAlertFormUrl")?.value||initialUrl);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="STUDENT NOTICE";$("#drawerTitle").textContent=`${studentAlertLabel(group.category)} 안내문`;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="student-alert-message"><div class="history-summary"><span><small>구분</small><b>${escapeHtml(studentAlertLabel(group.category))}</b></span><span><small>대상</small><b>${group.count||group.rows?.length||0}명</b></span><span><small>범위</small><b>${studentAlertClass==="all"?"전체":`${studentAlertClass}반`}</b></span></div>${group.category==="report"?`<label class="student-alert-form-link">보고서 구글폼 주소<input id="studentAlertFormUrl" type="url" value="${escapeHtml(initialUrl)}" placeholder="https://docs.google.com/forms/..."><small>프로그램에 저장되어 다음 미제출 안내 때 자동으로 사용됩니다.</small></label>`:""}<textarea id="studentAlertMessageText" rows="16">${escapeHtml(buildStudentAlertMessage(group,initialUrl))}</textarea><div class="drawer-actions">${group.category==="report"?'<button class="btn secondary" data-save-student-alert-url>폼 주소 저장</button>':''}<button class="btn primary" data-copy-student-alert-message>안내문 복사</button></div></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#studentAlertFormUrl")?.addEventListener("input",()=>{$("#studentAlertMessageText").value=renderMessage();});
  $("[data-save-student-alert-url]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{
    const url=$("#studentAlertFormUrl")?.value.trim()||""; const id=String(group.programId||"");
    const local=(state.programs||[]).find(p=>String(p.id||p.programId||"")===id);
    if(local){local.reportFormUrl=url;local.reportRequired=true;}else if(id){state.programOverrides[id]={...(state.programOverrides[id]||{}),reportFormUrl:url,reportRequired:true};}
    group.reportFormUrl=url;await save();$("#studentAlertMessageText").value=buildStudentAlertMessage(group,url);toast("보고서 구글폼 주소를 저장했습니다.");
  });
  $("[data-copy-student-alert-message]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{const value=$("#studentAlertMessageText")?.value||"";try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);toast("학생 안내문을 복사했습니다.");}catch{$("#studentAlertMessageText")?.select();document.execCommand("copy");toast("학생 안내문을 복사했습니다.");}});
}
function studentAlertsView(){
  const groups=studentUnifiedAlertGroups();
  const cats=["all","report","selection","attendance","schedule"];
  const filtered=studentAlertMode==="all"?groups:groups.filter(g=>g.category===studentAlertMode);
  const countBy=cat=>groups.filter(g=>cat==="all"||g.category===cat).reduce((sum,g)=>sum+Number(g.count||0),0);
  const classButtons=["all",...Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1))].map(value=>`<button data-student-alert-class="${value}" class="${studentAlertClass===value?'active':''}">${value==="all"?"전체":`${value}반`}</button>`).join("");
  return `<div class="module-page student-alert-center"><section class="student-alert-hero"><div><small>UEP ACTION CENTER</small><h2>학생 통합알림센터</h2><p>프로그램·보고서·선택과목·출결 상태를 한 곳에서 확인하고 필요한 학생 안내문을 생성합니다. 기존 각 메뉴의 안내 기능은 그대로 유지합니다.</p></div><span>자동 대상 발굴</span></section><div class="student-alert-summary"><span><b>${countBy("report")}</b>보고서 미제출</span><span><b>${countBy("selection")}</b>선택과목 수정</span><span><b>${countBy("attendance")}</b>출결 확인</span><span><b>${countBy("schedule")}</b>일정 대상</span></div><section class="student-alert-controls"><div class="student-alert-tabs">${cats.map(cat=>`<button data-student-alert-mode="${cat}" class="${studentAlertMode===cat?'active':''}">${cat==="all"?"전체":studentAlertLabel(cat)} <b>${countBy(cat)}</b></button>`).join("")}</div><div class="student-alert-classes">${classButtons}</div></section><section class="student-alert-list">${filtered.length?filtered.map(group=>`<article class="student-alert-row kind-${group.category}"><div><small>${escapeHtml(studentAlertLabel(group.category))}${group.date?` · ${escapeHtml(group.date)}`:""}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(group.title)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(group.meta||`${group.count||0}명`)}</p><div class="student-alert-names">${(group.rows||[]).slice(0,8).map(row=>`<span>${escapeHtml(`${row.studentNo||""} ${row.name||""}`.trim())}</span>`).join("")}${(group.rows||[]).length>8?`<em>외 ${(group.rows||[]).length-8}명</em>`:""}</div></div><button class="btn primary" data-student-alert-message="${escapeHtml(group.id)}">안내문 생성</button></article>`).join(""):'<div class="empty-state"><b>현재 안내가 필요한 항목이 없습니다.</b><span>제출·수정 상태가 바뀌면 자동으로 목록도 갱신됩니다.</span></div>'}</section></div>`;
}
function autoAlertOwnerKey(){return String(currentWorkAudienceContext().name||"사용자").trim()||"사용자";}
function autoNoticeReceiptId(source,id){return `AUTO:${String(source||'notice')}:${String(id||'').trim()}`;}
function currentAutoReceipt(source,id){
  const noticeId=autoNoticeReceiptId(source,id), userId=currentNoticeUserId(), teacher=currentLoginTeacherName();
  const local=state?.autoNoticeReceipts?.[`${noticeId}|${userId||teacher}`];
  if(local)return local;
  return (readonlyCache?.noticeReceipts||[]).find(row=>String(row.noticeId||'')===noticeId&&((userId&&String(row.userId||'')===userId)||String(row.teacher||'').trim()===teacher))||null;
}
function autoNoticeIsDismissed(source,id){return Boolean(currentAutoReceipt(source,id)?.dismissed);}
function autoNoticeGovernanceKey(source,id){return `${String(source||'notice')}:${String(id||'').trim()}`;}
function autoNoticeGovernance(source,id){
  return state?.autoNoticeGovernance?.[autoNoticeGovernanceKey(source,id)]||null;
}
function autoNoticeGovernanceStatus(source,id){return String(autoNoticeGovernance(source,id)?.status||'pending');}
function autoNoticeVisibleAfterGovernance(item){
  // 대시보드/일반 배포 화면은 역할과 무관하게 관리자 승인 건만 표시한다.
  return autoNoticeGovernanceStatus(item.source,item.id)==='approved';
}
async function setAutoNoticeGovernance(source,id,status,note=''){
  if(!currentUserCanManageNotices())return false;
  const allowed=new Set(['approved','handled','blocked','pending']);
  if(!allowed.has(status))return false;
  const key=autoNoticeGovernanceKey(source,id);
  state.autoNoticeGovernance=state.autoNoticeGovernance||{};
  state.autoNoticeGovernance[key]={status,note,by:currentLoginTeacherName()||'관리자',at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await save();
  return true;
}
async function bulkAutoNoticeGovernance(status){
  if(!currentUserCanManageNotices())return;
  const rows=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true,includeDismissed:true,includeUnapproved:true})
    .filter(item=>item.days>=-3&&item.days<=7&&autoNoticeGovernanceStatus(item.source,item.id)==='pending');
  if(!rows.length)return toast('검토할 자동공지 후보가 없습니다.');
  const label=status==='approved'?'승인':status==='handled'?'확인':status==='blocked'?'불가(해제)':'처리';
  if(!confirm(`현재 자동공지 후보 ${rows.length}건을 일괄 ${label} 처리할까요?`))return;
  state.autoNoticeGovernance=state.autoNoticeGovernance||{};
  const now=new Date().toISOString(),by=currentLoginTeacherName()||'관리자';
  rows.forEach(item=>{state.autoNoticeGovernance[autoNoticeGovernanceKey(item.source,item.id)]={status,note:`관리자 일괄 ${label}`,by,at:now};});
  await save();render(state.activePage||'dashboard');toast(`자동공지 ${rows.length}건을 ${label} 처리했습니다.`);
}

async function saveAutoNoticeReceipt(source,id,change={}){
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass();
  const classMatch=String(mine||'').match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})(?:반)?$/); const classNo=classMatch?classMatch[1]:'';
  const noticeId=autoNoticeReceiptId(source,id), userId=currentNoticeUserId(), teacher=currentLoginTeacherName()||'사용자';
  const key=`${noticeId}|${userId||teacher}`, previous=currentAutoReceipt(source,id)||{};
  const payload={noticeId,userId,teacher,grade:'1',classNo,sourceType:String(source||'자동공지'),read:true,...change};
  state.autoNoticeReceipts=state.autoNoticeReceipts||{};
  state.autoNoticeReceipts[key]={...previous,...payload,modifiedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  try{await save();}catch{}
  try{
    const result=await window.schoolBoard?.saveNoticeReceipt?.(payload);
    if(!result?.ok){toast(`알림 상태는 이 PC에 반영했습니다. 시트 동기화: ${result?.reason||'저장 실패'}`);return true;}
    try{await refreshReadonlyAfterNotice();}catch{}
    return true;
  }catch(error){toast('알림 상태는 이 PC에 반영했습니다. 시트 연결 시 다시 동기화됩니다.');return true;}
}
function autoAlertAckKey(item){return `${autoAlertOwnerKey()}|${dateKey(today)}|${item.id||item.category||item.title}`;}
function autoAlertIsAcked(item){return Boolean(state.autoAlertAcks?.[autoAlertAckKey(item)]);}
async function acknowledgeAutoAlert(itemId){
  const item=teacherAutoWorkAlerts({includeAcked:true}).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(itemId));if(!item)return;
  state.autoAlertAcks=state.autoAlertAcks||{};state.autoAlertAcks[autoAlertAckKey(item)]={at:new Date().toISOString(),title:item.title};await save();render(state.activePage);toast("오늘 확인한 자동알림으로 처리했습니다.");
}

function currentUserCanManageNotices(){return currentRoleId()==="admin"||currentUserIsGradeHead();}
function currentNoticeUserId(){
  return String(state?.auth?.user?.id||state?.auth?.user?.email||state?.currentUser?.id||state?.currentUser?.email||currentLoginTeacherName()||"").trim();
}
function connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser(item){
  if(!item)return false;
  const grade=String(item.targetGrade||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
  if(grade&&grade!=="1"&&!currentUserCanManageNotices())return false;
  const target=String(item.targetClass||"전체").trim();
  if(!target||target==="전체"||/전체|1~9|1-9/.test(target))return true;
  if(currentUserCanManageNotices())return true;
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass();
  if(!mine)return true;
  const mineNo=mine.split("-")[1];
  return target.split(/[,·\s]+/).some(v=>String(v).replace(/[^0-9]/g,"")===mineNo||String(v)===mine);
}
function directNoticeRows({activeOnly=true}={}){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const source=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[]);
  // __UEP_08160_NOTICE_LATEST_REVISION__
  const revisionTime=item=>String(item?.modifiedAt||item?.updatedAt||item?.['수정일시']||item?.postDate||item?.date||'');
  const byId=new Map();
  source.forEach((item,index)=>{
    const id=String(item?.id||item?.noticeId||item?.['공지ID']||'').trim()||('__row_'+index);
    const prev=byId.get(id);
    if(!prev||revisionTime(item)>=revisionTime(prev))byId.set(id,item);
  });
  const connected=[...byId.values()].filter(connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser);
  return connected.filter(item=>{
    if(item?.status==="삭제"||item?.status==="종료"||item?.done)return false;
    if(!activeOnly)return true;
    const start=String(item.postDate||item.date||"").slice(0,10), end=String(item.endDate||"").slice(0,10);
    if(start&&start>todayKey)return false;
    if(end&&end<todayKey)return false;
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b)=>Number(Boolean(b.important))-Number(Boolean(a.important))||String(b.postDate||b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.postDate||a.date||"")));
}
function currentNoticeReceipt(item){
  const name=currentLoginTeacherName(), userId=currentNoticeUserId();
  return (item?.receipts||[]).find(row=>(userId&&String(row.userId||"")===userId)||String(row.teacher||"").trim()===name)||null;
}
function noticeExpectedCount(item){
  const target=String(item?.targetClass||"전체");
  if(!target||target==="전체"||/전체|1~9|1-9/.test(target))return 9;
  const nums=[...new Set((target.match(/\d+/g)||[]).map(Number).filter(n=>n>=1&&n<=9))];
  return Math.max(1,nums.length||1);
}
function noticeDeadlineTypeLabel(type){
  const value=String(type||'').trim();
  return value==='학생제출'?'학생 제출':value==='담임확인'?'담임 확인':value==='업무처리'?'업무 처리':'마감';
}
function noticeDeadlineStatus(item){
  if(!item?.deadline)return '';
  const raw=String(item.deadline);
  const days=daysFromToday(raw);
  const prefix=noticeDeadlineTypeLabel(item.deadlineType);
  const timeMatch=raw.match(/T(\d{2}):(\d{2})/);
  const timeLabel=timeMatch?(()=>{const h=Number(timeMatch[1]),m=timeMatch[2];return `${h<12?'오전':'오후'} ${h%12||12}:${m}`;})():'';
  if(days==null)return [prefix,timeLabel].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  if(days<0)return `${prefix} · D+${Math.abs(days)}${timeLabel?` · ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
  if(days===0)return `${prefix} · 오늘${timeLabel?` ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
  if(days===1)return `${prefix} · 내일${timeLabel?` ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
  const md=raw.slice(5,10).replace('-','/');
  return `${prefix} · ${md}${timeLabel?` ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
}
function noticeActionState(item){
