  const receipt=currentNoticeReceipt(item);
  return {confirmed:Boolean(receipt?.confirmed),submitted:Boolean(receipt?.submitted),receipt};
}
function addDaysKey(base,days){
  if(!base)return ""; const d=new Date(`${String(base).slice(0,10)}T12:00:00`); if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return ""; d.setDate(d.getDate()+Number(days||0)); return dateKey(d);
}
function automaticDeadlineAlerts(options={}){
  const result=[], seen=new Set(), todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const push=(item)=>{if(!item?.due)return;const d=daysFromToday(item.due);if(d==null)return;if(!options.includePast&&d<0)return;if(!options.includeFar&&d>30)return;const key=`${item.source}:${item.id}:${item.due}`;if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);result.push({...item,days:d});};
  (readonlyCache?.programs||[]).forEach(program=>{
    if(!program||program.isCourseMaster)return;
    const title=program.actualTitle||program.title||"프로그램";
    const operationDate=String(program.date||program.endDate||"").slice(0,10);
    if(operationDate&&program.kind==="selected")push({id:program.id,source:"program",category:"선택활동",title:`${title} 운영`,due:operationDate,meta:[program.time,program.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")});
    if(program.reportRequired&&program.reportDeadline)push({id:`${program.id}:student-report`,source:"report",category:"보고서",title:`${title} 학생 보고서 마감`,due:String(program.reportDeadline).slice(0,10),meta:"학생 보고서"});
    if(program.reportToAuthority&&Number(program.reportAfterDays||0)>0&&operationDate)push({id:`${program.id}:agency`,source:"agency",category:"기관보고",title:`${title} 기관보고 마감`,due:addDaysKey(program.endDate||operationDate,program.reportAfterDays),meta:program.reportAgency||"기관보고"});
  });
  (Array.isArray(state.reportDeadlines)?state.reportDeadlines:[]).forEach(item=>push({id:item.id||item.title,source:"report",category:"보고서",title:item.title||"학생 보고서 마감",due:item.due||item.date,meta:item.className||"보고서"}));
  (Array.isArray(state.schoolDeadlines)?state.schoolDeadlines:[]).filter(item=>!item.done&&item.status!=="완료").forEach(item=>push({id:item.id,source:"school",category:"업무마감",title:item.title||"학교업무 마감",due:item.due||item.date,meta:item.department||"학교업무"}));
  return result.filter(item=>(options.includeDismissed||!autoNoticeIsDismissed(item.source,item.id))&&(options.includeUnapproved||autoNoticeVisibleAfterGovernance(item))).sort((a,b)=>a.days-b.days||String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)));
}
function deadlineBadge(item){
  if(item.days<0)return `D+${Math.abs(item.days)}`; if(item.days===0)return "오늘"; if(item.days===1)return "내일"; return `D-${item.days}`;
}
function automaticDeadlineMarkup(limit=20){
  const rows=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true}).filter(item=>item.days>=-7&&item.days<=30).slice(0,limit);
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="work-empty"><b>현재 자동 마감공지가 없습니다.</b><small>프로그램 운영일·보고서·기관보고·학교업무 마감을 자동 계산해 표시합니다.</small></div>';
  return rows.map(item=>`<button class="work-auto-deadline ${item.days<0?'overdue':item.days<=1?'urgent':''}" data-auto-deadline-source="${escapeHtml(item.source)}" data-auto-deadline-id="${escapeHtml(String(item.id||''))}"><i>${escapeHtml(item.category)}</i><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.due)} · ${escapeHtml(item.meta||'')}</small></span><em>${deadlineBadge(item)}</em></button>`).join('');
}
function automaticDeadlineAdminReviewMarkup(limit=40){
  if(!currentUserCanManageNotices())return automaticDeadlineMarkup(limit);
  const rows=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true,includeDismissed:true,includeUnapproved:true}).filter(item=>item.days>=-7&&item.days<=30).slice(0,limit);
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="work-empty"><b>검토할 자동공지 후보가 없습니다.</b></div>';
  const label=status=>status==='approved'?'승인':status==='handled'?'확인':status==='blocked'?'불가(해제)':'검토대기';
  return rows.map(item=>{const status=autoNoticeGovernanceStatus(item.source,item.id);return `<button class="work-auto-deadline admin-review ${status}" data-auto-deadline-source="${escapeHtml(item.source)}" data-auto-deadline-id="${escapeHtml(String(item.id||''))}"><i>${escapeHtml(item.category)}</i><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.due)} · ${escapeHtml(item.meta||'')} · ${label(status)}</small></span><em>${deadlineBadge(item)}</em></button>`;}).join('');
}
function uniqueActiveAlertStudents(rows=[]){
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const validIds=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.id||s.studentId||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
  const validNos=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.studentNo||s.number||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
  const seen=new Set(),out=[];
  for(const row of rows){
    const id=String(row?.studentId||row?.id||'').trim(),no=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||'').trim();
    const hasKnownIdentity=(id&&validIds.has(id))||(no&&validNos.has(no));
    if((validIds.size||validNos.size)&&!hasKnownIdentity)continue;
    const key=(id&&validIds.has(id)?id:'')||(no&&validNos.has(no)?no:'')||`${row?.classNo||row?.classLabel||''}|${row?.name||''}`;
    if(!key||seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);out.push(row);
  }
  return out;
}
function teacherAutoWorkAlerts(options={}){
  const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
  const report=uniqueActiveAlertStudents(filterRowsByCurrentTeacherScope(studentReportAlertRows()));
  const selection=uniqueActiveAlertStudents(filterRowsByCurrentTeacherScope(studentSelectionAlertRows()));
  const result=[];
  const reportStudents=dashboardReportGroups(report);
  if(reportStudents.length)result.push({id:"auto:report",category:"report",title:"학생 보고서 미제출",count:reportStudents.length,meta:`${scope.label} · ${reportStudents.length}명 확인 필요`});
  if(selection.length)result.push({id:"auto:selection",category:"selection",title:"선택과목 수정 필요",count:selection.length,meta:`${scope.label} · ${selection.length}명 오류 확인`});
  const schedule=studentScheduleAlertGroups().find(group=>{const d=daysFromToday(group.date);return d!=null&&d>=0&&d<=2;});
  if(schedule)result.push({id:`auto:schedule:${schedule.id}`,category:"schedule",title:schedule.title,count:schedule.count,meta:`${schedule.meta} · 프로그램 일정`});
  const teacherName=currentLoginTeacherName();
  const todayNight=(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors||[]).find(row=>String(row?.date||'').slice(0,10)===dateKey(today)&&String(row?.teacher||'').trim()===String(teacherName||'').trim());
  if(todayNight)result.unshift({id:`auto:overtime:${dateKey(today)}:${teacherName}`,category:"overtime",title:"오늘 야자감독 · 초과근무 신청 확인",count:"",meta:`${todayNight.start||'19:20'}~${todayNight.end||'21:30'} · ${todayNight.place||'면학관'} · 16:00 이전 신청 권장`});
  return options.includeAcked?result:result.filter(item=>!autoAlertIsAcked(item));
}

function openAutoWorkAlertDetail(itemId){
  const item=teacherAutoWorkAlerts({includeAcked:true}).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(itemId));
  if(!item)return toast("자동알림을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const detail=item.category==="report"?"학생 보고서 미제출 현황입니다. 대상 학생과 제출 안내가 필요한지 확인하세요.":item.category==="selection"?"선택과목 오류 또는 수정 제출이 필요한 학생 현황입니다.":item.category==="schedule"?"가까운 프로그램 일정입니다. 대상 학생과 운영 준비사항을 확인하세요.":item.category==="overtime"?"오늘 야자감독 일정이 있습니다. 초과근무 신청 여부를 확인하세요. 확인 버튼을 누르면 오늘은 같은 자동알림을 다시 표시하지 않습니다.":"확인이 필요한 자동알림입니다.";
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="AUTO NOTICE";$("#drawerTitle").textContent=item.title;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="auto-work-detail"><div class="history-summary"><span><small>구분</small><b>${escapeHtml(studentAlertLabel(item.category))}</b></span><span><small>대상</small><b>${escapeHtml(String(item.count||0))}명</b></span><span><small>범위</small><b>${escapeHtml(currentTeacherAccessScope().label)}</b></span></div><section><h4>알림 내용</h4><p>${escapeHtml(detail)}</p><small>${escapeHtml(item.meta||"")}</small></section><div class="drawer-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-auto-alert-related="${escapeHtml(item.category)}">상세 화면으로 이동</button><button class="btn primary" data-auto-alert-confirm="${escapeHtml(item.id)}">오늘 확인</button></div><p class="drawer-note">‘오늘 확인’은 해결 처리가 아니라 현재 사용자에게 오늘만 숨김 처리합니다. 조건이 남아 있으면 다음 날 다시 표시됩니다.</p></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("[data-auto-alert-related]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>{const category=item.category;closeDrawer();studentAlertMode=category||"all";studentAlertClass="all";navigate("alerts");});
  $("[data-auto-alert-confirm]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{await acknowledgeAutoAlert(item.id);closeDrawer();if(state.activePage==="dashboard"||state.activePage==="work")render(state.activePage);});
}

function workBoardLabel(type){return type==="notice"?"학교업무 공지":type==="deadline"?"일정·마감":"개인 체크리스트";}
function workBoardEmpty(type){return type==="notice"?"등록된 학교업무 공지가 없습니다.":type==="deadline"?"등록된 일정·마감이 없습니다.":"등록된 개인 체크리스트가 없습니다.";}
function workBoardRows(type){
  const rows=type==="notice"?directNoticeRows({activeOnly:false}):workBoardData(type);
  if(!rows.length)return `<div class="work-board-empty"><b>${workBoardEmpty(type)}</b><small>${type==="notice"?"직접 공지는 기본정보 연결시트와 양방향으로 관리됩니다.":"오른쪽 위 ‘새 글 등록’ 버튼으로 바로 등록할 수 있습니다."}</small></div>`;
  return rows.map(item=>{
    const id=escapeHtml(item.id||""), title=escapeHtml(item.title||"제목 없음");
    if(type==="notice"){
      const stateInfo=noticeActionState(item), expected=noticeExpectedCount(item), confirmCount=Number(item.confirmedCount||0), submitCount=Number(item.submittedCount||0);
      const badges=[item.noticeKind||item.type||"공지",item.homeroomNotice?"종례":"",item.important?"중요":""].filter(Boolean).map(x=>`<i>${escapeHtml(x)}</i>`).join('');
      const progress=currentUserCanManageNotices()?`<small>확인 ${confirmCount}/${expected}${item.submitRequired?` · 제출 ${submitCount}/${expected}`:""}</small>`:`<small>${item.confirmRequired?(stateInfo.confirmed?"확인 완료":"확인 필요"):"확인 선택"}${item.submitRequired?` · ${stateInfo.submitted?"제출 완료":"제출 필요"}`:""}</small>`;
      return `<article class="work-board-row ${item.status==='종료'?'done':''}"><button class="work-board-main" data-work-notice-detail="${id}" data-work-notice-source="connected"><span class="work-board-state">직접</span><div><b>${title}</b><div class="work-notice-badges">${badges}</div><small>${escapeHtml(item.department||item.author||"1학년부")} · ${escapeHtml(item.postDate||item.date||"")}${item.deadline?` · 마감 ${escapeHtml(item.deadline)}`:""}</small>${progress}${item.detail?`<p>${escapeHtml(item.detail)}</p>`:""}</div><em>열기 ›</em></button>${currentUserCanManageNotices()?`<div class="work-board-row-actions"><button data-notice-edit="${id}">수정</button><button class="danger" data-notice-delete="${id}">삭제</button></div>`:""}</article>`;
    }
    const meta=type==="deadline"?[item.department||"담당부서",item.due||item.date||"기한 미정"]:[item.type||"개인업무",item.date||"기한 미정"];
    if(type!=="checklist")meta.push(`대상 ${workAudienceLabel(item)}`);
    const done=Boolean(item.done||item.status==="완료");
    return `<article class="work-board-row ${done?"done":""}"><button class="work-board-main" data-work-edit="${type}" data-work-id="${id}"><span class="work-board-state">${done?"완료":"진행"}</span><div><b>${title}</b><small>${escapeHtml(meta.filter(Boolean).join(" · "))}</small>${item.content||item.memo||item.description?`<p>${escapeHtml(item.content||item.memo||item.description)}</p>`:""}</div><em>열기 ›</em></button><div class="work-board-row-actions"><button data-work-edit="${type}" data-work-id="${id}">수정</button><button class="danger" data-work-delete="${type}" data-work-id="${id}">삭제</button></div></article>`;
  }).join("");
}
function workBoardView(type){
  if(type==="notice"){
    const direct=directNoticeRows({activeOnly:false}), active=directNoticeRows(), auto=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true,includeDismissed:currentUserCanManageNotices(),includeUnapproved:currentUserCanManageNotices()}).filter(x=>x.days>=-7&&x.days<=30), todayDirect=active.filter(item=>String(item.postDate||item.date||"").slice(0,10)===dateKey(today)||item.homeroomNotice);
    return `<div class="module-page work-board-page work-notice-split-v07668"><section class="work-board-head"><button class="btn secondary" data-work-board="home">← 학교업무 홈</button><div><small>SCHOOL WORK NOTICE</small><h2>학교업무 공지</h2><p>직접 공지와 UEP가 원본 날짜에서 계산한 자동 마감공지를 성격별로 분리해 관리합니다.</p></div><button class="btn primary" data-notice-add>＋ 직접공지 등록</button></section><div class="student-summary work-board-summary"><span><b>${active.length}</b> 게시 중 직접공지</span><span><b>${todayDirect.length}</b> 오늘·종례 공지</span><span><b>${auto.filter(x=>x.days>=0).length}</b> 예정 자동마감</span><span><b>${auto.filter(x=>x.days<0).length}</b> 기한 경과</span></div><div class="work-notice-two-track"><section class="work-board-direct"><header><div><small>MANUAL NOTICE</small><h3>직접 공지</h3><p>학년부장이 등록한 공지·확인요청·제출요청·종례전달</p></div><span>${direct.length}건</span></header><div class="work-board-list">${workBoardRows("notice")}</div></section><section class="work-board-deadline-auto"><header><div><small>AUTO DEADLINE</small><h3>자동 마감공지</h3><p>${currentUserCanManageNotices()?'자동공지 후보를 검토하여 승인·확인·불가 처리합니다. 승인된 공지만 담임 대시보드에 표시됩니다.':'승인되어 배포된 자동 마감공지를 확인합니다.'}</p></div><div class="auto-review-tools">${currentUserCanManageNotices()?'<button class="btn secondary" data-auto-bulk-governance="approved">일괄 승인</button><button class="btn secondary" data-auto-bulk-governance="handled">일괄 확인</button><button class="btn secondary" data-auto-bulk-governance="blocked">일괄 불가</button>':''}<span>${auto.length}건</span></div></header><div class="work-auto-deadline-list">${currentUserCanManageNotices()?automaticDeadlineAdminReviewMarkup(40):automaticDeadlineMarkup(30)}</div></section></div><section class="work-board-auto status-auto"><header><div><small>AUTO CHECK</small><h3>상태 기반 자동 확인알림</h3></div><span>${teacherAutoWorkAlerts().length}건</span></header>${workAutoNoticeMarkup(20)}</section></div>`;
  }
  const label=workBoardLabel(type),rows=workBoardData(type),done=rows.filter(x=>x.done||x.status==="완료").length;
  return `<div class="module-page work-board-page"><section class="work-board-head"><button class="btn secondary" data-work-board="home">← 학교업무 홈</button><div><small>WORK BOARD</small><h2>${label}</h2><p>${type==="deadline"?"직접 등록한 업무 일정과 마감을 관리합니다.":"개인 업무를 등록하고 완료 여부와 마감일을 관리합니다."}</p></div><button class="btn primary" data-work-add="${type}">＋ 새 글 등록</button></section><div class="student-summary work-board-summary"><span><b>${rows.length}</b> 전체</span><span><b>${rows.length-done}</b> 진행</span><span><b>${done}</b> 완료</span></div><section class="work-board-list">${workBoardRows(type)}</section></div>`;
}
function workAutoNoticeMarkup(limit=8){
  const autoAlerts=teacherAutoWorkAlerts();
  if(!autoAlerts.length)return '<div class="work-empty"><b>현재 자동 알림이 없습니다.</b><small>보고서 제출·선택과목 수정·프로그램 일정 상태에 따라 자동으로 나타납니다.</small></div>';
  return autoAlerts.slice(0,limit).map(item=>`<div class="work-auto-notice-row"><button class="work-auto-notice kind-${escapeHtml(item.category)}" data-auto-alert-open="${escapeHtml(item.id)}"><span><i>자동</i><b>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.meta)}</small></span><em>${item.count||''} ›</em></button></div>`).join("");
}
function workAcknowledgedAutoMarkup(){
  const rows=teacherAutoWorkAlerts({includeAcked:true}).filter(autoAlertIsAcked);
  if(!rows.length)return "";
  return `<details class="work-auto-acknowledged"><summary>오늘 확인한 자동알림 ${rows.length}건</summary><div>${rows.map(item=>`<button data-open-student-alerts="${escapeHtml(item.category)}"><b>${escapeHtml(item.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.meta)}</small></button>`).join("")}</div></details>`;
}
function workView() {
  if(workEmbeddedMode==="duties") return `<div class="module-page work-embedded-page"><div class="embedded-backbar"><button class="btn secondary" data-work-embedded="home">← 학교업무</button><div><small>SCHOOL WORK</small><h3>업무분장</h3></div></div>${dutiesView()}</div>`;
  if(workEmbeddedMode==="forms") return `<div class="module-page work-embedded-page"><div class="embedded-backbar"><button class="btn secondary" data-work-embedded="home">← 학교업무</button><div><small>SCHOOL WORK</small><h3>각종 양식</h3></div></div>${formsView()}</div>`;
  if(workBoardMode!=="home")return workBoardView(workBoardMode);
  const activeDirect=directNoticeRows();
  const deadlines=workBoardData("deadline").filter(workItemVisibleToCurrentUser), pendingDeadlines=deadlines.filter(item=>!item.done&&item.status!=="완료");
  const checklist=workBoardData("checklist"), pendingChecklist=checklist.filter(item=>!item.done&&item.status!=="완료");
  const autoDeadlines=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true}).filter(item=>item.days>=-7&&item.days<=30);
  return `<div class="module-page work-project-page work-hub-v0761 work-hub-v07629">
    <section class="school-work-hub school-work-hub-compact">
      <header><div><small>SCHOOL WORK HUB</small><h2>학교업무</h2></div><span>업무 통합 관리</span></header>
      <div class="school-work-cards school-work-cards-five school-work-cards-one-line">
        <button data-work-board="notice"><i>공지</i><b>학교업무 공지</b><em>${activeDirect.length + autoDeadlines.length}건</em></button>
        <button data-work-board="deadline"><i>마감</i><b>일정·마감</b><em>${pendingDeadlines.length}건</em></button>
        <button data-work-board="checklist"><i>체크</i><b>개인 체크리스트</b><em>${pendingChecklist.length}건</em></button>
        <button data-work-embedded="duties"><i>분장</i><b>업무분장</b><em>열기 ›</em></button>
        <button data-work-embedded="forms"><i>양식</i><b>각종 양식</b><em>열기 ›</em></button>
      </div>
    </section>
    <section class="work-auto-area"><div class="section-title-row"><div><small>AUTO DEADLINE</small><h3>자동 마감공지</h3></div><button class="text-button" data-work-board="notice">공지에서 보기 ›</button></div><div class="work-auto-deadline-list">${automaticDeadlineMarkup(8)}</div></section>
    <section class="work-auto-area"><div class="section-title-row"><div><small>AUTO CHECK</small><h3>상태 기반 자동 확인알림</h3></div><button class="text-button" data-page="alerts">학생 통합알림센터 ›</button></div><div class="work-auto-list">${workAutoNoticeMarkup()}</div>${workAcknowledgedAutoMarkup()}</section>
  </div>`;
}

function openWorkBoardEditor(type,id=""){
  const rows=workBoardData(type), item=rows.find(row=>String(row.id)===String(id))||{};
  const isEdit=Boolean(item.id), label=workBoardLabel(type);
  const dateValue=type==="deadline"?(item.due||item.date||dateKey(today)):(item.date||item.deadline||dateKey(today));
  const audience=normalizeWorkAudience(item);
  const audienceMarkup=type==="checklist"?"":`<fieldset class="work-audience-field"><legend>배포 대상</legend><div class="work-audience-grid">${workAudienceOptions().map(value=>`<label><input type="checkbox" name="workAudience" value="${value}" ${audience.includes(value)?"checked":""}> ${value}</label>`).join("")}</div><div class="form-row"><label>특정부서<input id="workTargetDepartment" value="${escapeHtml(item.targetDepartment||"")}" placeholder="예: 1학년부, 교육과정부"></label><label>개인선택<input id="workTargetPeople" value="${escapeHtml(item.targetPeople||"")}" placeholder="예: 홍석민, 이태연"></label></div><small>현재 버전은 이 PC에 저장하며, 향후 50_학교업무 시트 연결 시 선택 대상에게 자동 배포됩니다.</small></fieldset>`;
  const alertOptions=type==="checklist"?"":`<fieldset class="work-alert-options"><legend>알림 설정</legend><label><input id="workImportant" type="checkbox" ${item.important?"checked":""}> 중요</label><label><input id="workReadConfirm" type="checkbox" ${item.readConfirm?"checked":""}> 읽음 확인</label><label><input id="workTopAlert" type="checkbox" ${item.topAlert?"checked":""}> 대시보드 상단 알림</label>${type==="deadline"?`<label>마감시간<input id="workDueTime" type="time" value="${escapeHtml(item.dueTime||"17:00")}"></label>`:""}</fieldset>`;
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent=isEdit?"EDIT WORK ITEM":"NEW WORK ITEM";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent=`${label} ${isEdit?"수정":"등록"}`;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<form class="work-editor-form" id="workEditorForm">
    <label>제목<input id="workEditorTitle" required value="${escapeHtml(item.title||"")}" placeholder="업무 제목을 입력하세요"></label>
    <div class="form-row"><label>${type==="checklist"?"분류":"담당부서"}<input id="workEditorGroup" value="${escapeHtml(type==="checklist"?(item.type||""):(item.department||item.author||""))}" placeholder="${type==="checklist"?"예: 학년업무":"예: 1학년부"}"></label><label>${type==="notice"?"게시일":"마감일"}<input id="workEditorDate" type="date" value="${escapeHtml(dateValue)}"></label></div>
    ${audienceMarkup}${alertOptions}
    <label>내용<textarea id="workEditorContent" rows="7" placeholder="상세 내용이나 메모를 입력하세요">${escapeHtml(item.content||item.memo||item.description||"")}</textarea></label>
    <label class="work-editor-done"><input id="workEditorDone" type="checkbox" ${item.done||item.status==="완료"?"checked":""}> 완료 처리</label>
    <div class="work-editor-actions">${isEdit?`<button type="button" class="btn danger" data-work-editor-delete>삭제</button>`:"<span></span>"}<div><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-work-editor-cancel>취소</button><button class="btn primary">저장</button></div></div>
  </form>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("reference-expanded","extension-expanded");$("#drawer").classList.add("reference-maximized");$("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("[data-work-editor-cancel]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>closeDrawer());
  $("#workEditorForm")?.addEventListener("submit",async event=>{
    event.preventDefault();const title=$("#workEditorTitle").value.trim();if(!title)return toast("제목을 입력해 주세요.");
    const group=$("#workEditorGroup").value.trim(),date=$("#workEditorDate").value,content=$("#workEditorContent").value.trim(),done=$("#workEditorDone").checked;
    const target=isEdit?item:{id:`work-${type}-${Date.now()}`};target.title=title;target.content=content;target.done=done;target.status=done?"완료":"진행";
    if(type!=="checklist"){
      target.audience=$$("input[name='workAudience']:checked").map(x=>x.value);if(!target.audience.length)target.audience=[currentUserIsGradeHead()?"전체교사":"1학년"];
      target.targetDepartment=$("#workTargetDepartment")?.value.trim()||"";target.targetPeople=$("#workTargetPeople")?.value.trim()||"";
      target.important=Boolean($("#workImportant")?.checked);target.readConfirm=Boolean($("#workReadConfirm")?.checked);target.topAlert=Boolean($("#workTopAlert")?.checked);target.readBy=Array.isArray(target.readBy)?target.readBy:[];
    }
    if(type==="checklist"){target.type=group||"개인업무";target.date=date;target.project="PERSONAL";target.owner=currentWorkAudienceContext().name;if(!isEdit){state.tasks=state.tasks||[];state.tasks.push(target);}}
    else if(type==="deadline"){target.department=group||"담당부서";target.due=date;target.dueTime=$("#workDueTime")?.value||"17:00";if(!isEdit)state.schoolDeadlines.push(target);}
    else {target.department=group||"담당부서";target.date=date;if(!isEdit)state.workNotices.push(target);}
    await save();closeDrawer();render("work");toast(isEdit?"수정했습니다.":"대상별 배포 설정으로 등록했습니다.");
  });
  $("[data-work-editor-delete]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{if(!confirm("이 항목을 삭제할까요?"))return;await deleteWorkBoardItem(type,id);closeDrawer();});
}
async function deleteWorkBoardItem(type,id){
  if(type==="notice")state.workNotices=workBoardData(type).filter(x=>String(x.id)!==String(id));
  else if(type==="deadline")state.schoolDeadlines=workBoardData(type).filter(x=>String(x.id)!==String(id));
  else state.tasks=(state.tasks||[]).filter(x=>String(x.id)!==String(id));
  await save();render("work");toast("삭제했습니다.");
}

function dutiesView() {
  const departments = [...new Set(DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.map((item) => item.department))];
  const myCount = DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.filter((item) => item.person === "홍석민").length;
  const grouped = departments.map((department, departmentIndex) => {
    const items = DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.filter((item) => item.department === department);
    return `<section class="duty-department" data-duty-group="${escapeHtml(department)}">
      <button type="button" class="duty-department-head" data-duty-group-toggle="${escapeHtml(department)}" aria-expanded="true">
        <span><i>${String(departmentIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</i><b>${escapeHtml(department)}</b><small>${items.length}개 담당 업무</small></span><em>접기 ︿</em>
      </button>
      <div class="duty-department-rows">${items.map((item)=>`<button type="button" class="duty-row" data-duty-index="${DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.indexOf(item)}" data-department="${escapeHtml(item.department)}" data-person="${escapeHtml(item.person)}" data-search="${escapeHtml(`${item.department} ${item.role} ${item.person} ${item.details}`.toLowerCase())}">
        <div class="duty-no">${escapeHtml(item.no)}</div>
        <div class="duty-owner"><small>${escapeHtml(item.department)}</small><b>${escapeHtml(item.role)}</b><span>${escapeHtml(item.person)}</span></div>
        <p>${escapeHtml(item.details)}</p>
      </button>`).join("")}</div>
    </section>`;
  }).join("");
  return `<div class="module-page duties-page">
    <div class="module-intro pastel-mint"><span>01</span><div><h3>2026학년도 업무분장</h3><p>업무부별로 나누어 담당자와 세부 업무를 크게 확인합니다.</p></div></div>
    <div class="student-summary duties-summary"><span><b>${departments.length}</b> 업무부</span><span><b>${DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.length}</b> 담당 항목</span><span><b>${myCount}</b> 내 업무</span><span><b>2026. 2.</b> 기준</span></div>
    <div class="toolbar duties-toolbar">
      <input class="search" id="dutySearch" placeholder="부서·담당자·업무명·세부업무 검색">
      <select id="dutyDepartment"><option value="">전체 업무부</option>${departments.map((name)=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}">${escapeHtml(name)}</option>`).join("")}</select>
      <button class="btn secondary" id="dutyMine">내 업무만</button>
      <button class="btn secondary" id="dutyReset">초기화</button>
    </div>
    <div class="duty-list duty-group-list" id="dutyRows">${grouped}</div>
    <div class="setting-card duties-source"><h3>자료 기준</h3><p>첨부한 「2026. 업무분장(안)」의 업무분장 재구조화 표를 업무부별 조회 구조로 옮겼습니다.</p></div>
  </div>`;
}


function openDutyDetail(index) {
  const item = DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS[Number(index)];
  if (!item) return;
  const related = DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.filter((row) => row.department === item.department && row !== item);
  document.getElementById("dutyDetailLayer")?.remove();
  const layer=document.createElement("div");
  layer.id="dutyDetailLayer";
  layer.className="issue-layer duty-detail-layer";
  layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog duty-detail-dialog-large">
    <header><div><small>SCHOOL WORK · DUTY DETAIL</small><h3>${escapeHtml(item.role)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(item.department)} · 담당자 ${escapeHtml(item.person)} · 업무번호 ${escapeHtml(item.no)}</p></div><button type="button" data-duty-detail-close>×</button></header>
    <section class="duty-detail-large-body">
      <div class="duty-detail-large-label">세부 담당 업무</div>
      <div class="duty-detail-large-text">${escapeHtml(item.details)}</div>
    </section>
    <section class="duty-related duty-related-large"><h4>${escapeHtml(item.department)} 함께 보기</h4>${related.map((row) => `<button data-related-duty="${DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.indexOf(row)}"><span><b>${escapeHtml(row.role)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(row.person)}</small></span><em>상세 ›</em></button>`).join("") || '<p>같은 부서의 다른 업무가 없습니다.</p>'}</section>
    <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-duty-detail-close>닫기</button></div>
  </div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(layer);
  const close=()=>layer.remove();
  layer.querySelectorAll("[data-duty-detail-close]").forEach(button=>button.onclick=close);
  layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)close();};
  layer.querySelectorAll("[data-related-duty]").forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>openDutyDetail(button.dataset.relatedDuty));
}

function formsView() {
  const templates = [["기안문","학교 내부결재 기본 형식","DOCX · 예시"],["품의문","물품구입 및 사업비 집행","DOCX · 예시"],["가정통신문","학부모 안내 및 회신","DOCX · 예시"],["프로그램 운영계획","목적·대상·일정·예산","DOCX · 예시"],["결과보고서","운영결과·참여인원·증빙","DOCX · 예시"],["회의록","안건·협의내용·결정사항","DOCX · 예시"]];
  const programs=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||'').localeCompare(String(a.date||''))).slice(0,300);
  const programOptions=programs.map(p=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(p.id||p.programId||'')}">${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.title||'프로그램')} · ${escapeHtml(p.date||'')}</option>`).join('');
  return `<div class="module-page forms-page"><section class="forms-toolbar"><label class="forms-search">⌕ <input placeholder="양식명·업무명 검색"></label><button class="btn secondary" data-template-upload>＋ 학교 양식 추가</button><button class="btn primary" data-draft-focus>기안문 작성</button><input id="templateFileInput" type="file" accept=".doc,.docx,.hwp,.hwpx,.pdf,.xlsx" hidden></section><section class="template-grid">${templates.map((item,index) => `<article class="template-card"><i>${index < 2 ? "결재" : "양식"}</i><h3>${item[0]}</h3><p>${item[1]}</p><small>${item[2]}</small><div><button data-template-download="${item[0]}">샘플 받기</button><button data-template-preview="${item[0]}">미리보기</button></div></article>`).join("")}</section><section class="draft-assistant" id="draftAssistant"><div class="draft-guide"><small>DRAFT ASSIST · UEP DATA</small><h2>학교 기안문 작성</h2><p>UEP에 이미 등록된 프로그램 자료를 불러와 반복 입력을 줄입니다.</p><ol><li>UEP 프로그램 선택</li><li>자동 채움 내용 확인·보완</li><li>미리보기 후 파일로 저장</li></ol></div><form id="draftForm"><div class="draft-source-row"><label>UEP 프로그램 불러오기<select id="draftProgramSource"><option value="">직접 작성</option>${programOptions}</select></label><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-draft-load-program>선택 프로그램 자동 채움</button></div><div class="form-row"><label>문서 제목<input id="draftTitle" required placeholder="예: 2026학년도 진로체험 프로그램 운영 계획"></label><label>수신·공람<input id="draftRecipient" placeholder="예: 내부결재"></label></div><div class="form-row"><label>관련 업무<select id="draftCategory"><option>학생생활</option><option>교육과정</option><option>진로·진학</option><option>예산·물품</option><option>학년업무</option></select></label><label>시행일<input id="draftDate" type="date" value="${dateKey(today)}"></label></div><label>기안 본문<textarea id="draftBody" rows="8" required placeholder="1. 관련:&#10;2. 목적:&#10;3. 운영 개요:&#10;4. 소요 예산:"></textarea></label><label>붙임<input id="draftAttachment" placeholder="예: 프로그램 운영계획 1부. 끝."></label><div class="draft-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-draft-preview>미리보기</button><button class="btn primary">초안 내려받기</button></div></form></section></div>`;
}

function downloadWordDraft(fileName, title, body) {
  const html = `<!doctype html><html lang="ko"><meta charset="utf-8"><style>body{font-family:'Malgun Gothic',sans-serif;margin:45px;line-height:1.8}h1{text-align:center;font-size:22px}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:25px 0}td{border:1px solid #777;padding:8px}pre{font:14px/1.8 'Malgun Gothic',sans-serif;white-space:pre-wrap}</style><h1>${escapeHtml(title)}</h1>${body}`;
  const blob = new Blob(["\ufeff", html], { type: "application/msword;charset=utf-8" });
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  link.download = `${fileName.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, "_")}.doc`;
  link.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href), 1000);
}

function previewDraft() {
  const title = $("#draftTitle")?.value.trim() || "제목 미입력";
  const recipient = $("#draftRecipient")?.value.trim() || "내부결재";
  const body = $("#draftBody")?.value.trim() || "본문을 입력하세요.";
  const attachment = $("#draftAttachment")?.value.trim() || "붙임 없음. 끝.";
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "DRAFT PREVIEW";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "기안문 미리보기";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="draft-preview"><h2>${escapeHtml(title)}</h2><div><b>수신·공람</b><span>${escapeHtml(recipient)}</span></div><div><b>시행일</b><span>${escapeHtml($("#draftDate")?.value || dateKey(today))}</span></div><pre>${escapeHtml(body)}</pre><p><b>붙임</b> ${escapeHtml(attachment)}</p></div><p class="drawer-note">학교 원본 양식을 연결하면 결재선·로고·문서번호 영역을 해당 형식에 맞출 수 있습니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.add("timetable-expanded");
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
}


function formatNeisYmd(text) {
  const value = String(text || "");
  return /^\d{8}$/.test(value) ? `${Number(value.slice(4, 6))}/${Number(value.slice(6, 8))}` : value;
}

function periodTimeLabel(period) {
  const row = (state.settings.schoolHours || []).filter((item) => String(item?.[0] || "").includes("교시"))[Number(period) - 1];
  return row ? `${row[1]}~${row[2]}` : "";
}

function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
  if(!/^\d{8}$/.test(text))return null;
  const date=new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)),Number(text.slice(4,6))-1,Number(text.slice(6,8)),12,0,0);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?null:date;
}
function expectedSchoolPeriods(value){
  const date=value instanceof Date?value:neisDateToLocalDate(value);
  const weekdayNo=date?.getDay();
  return weekdayNo===3?5:([1,2,4,5].includes(weekdayNo)?7:0);
}
/* __UEP08123_CACHE_COMPAT__ */
function uep08123TeacherKey(value){
  return String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/(선생님|교사)$/,'').trim();
}
function uep08123FirstArray(...values){
  for(const value of values){ if(Array.isArray(value)&&value.length) return value; }
  return [];
}
function uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(data){
  if(!data||typeof data!=='object') return data;
  if(!Array.isArray(data.timetableRows)||!data.timetableRows.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.timetableMaster,data.masterTimetableRows,data.teacherTimetableRows,data.timetable?.rows,data.timetable?.timetableRows,data.timetable?.masterRows);
    if(rows.length) data.timetableRows=rows;
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(data.notices)||!data.notices.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.workNotices,data.directNotices,data.schoolNotices,data.work?.notices,data.operations?.notices);
    if(rows.length) data.notices=rows;
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(data.dormOutings)||!data.dormOutings.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.dormOutingRows,data.outings,data.dorm?.outings,data.dormitory?.outings);
    if(rows.length) data.dormOutings=rows;
  }
  if(!Array.isArray(data.schoolDeadlines)||!data.schoolDeadlines.length){
    const rows=uep08123FirstArray(data.deadlines,data.workDeadlines,data.work?.deadlines);
    if(rows.length) data.schoolDeadlines=rows;
  }
  if(Array.isArray(data.officialAttendance))data.officialAttendance=data.officialAttendance.map((row,index)=>({
    ...row,
    id:String(row.id||row.attendanceId||row['출결ID']||`official-${index}`).trim(),
    studentId:String(row.studentId||row['학생ID']||'').trim(),
    studentNo:String(row.studentNo||row['학번']||'').replace(/\.0$/,''),
    name:String(row.name||row['성명']||row['학생명']||'').trim(),
    className:String(row.className||row.classNo||row['반']||'').replace(/\.0$/,''),
    date:uepComparableDate(row.date||row.day||row.rawDate||row['일자']||row['출결일자']||row['출결일']),
    type:String(row.type||[row['출결구분'],row['세부구분']].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'공결'),
    period:String(row.period||row['인정범위']||[row['시작교시'],row['종료교시']].filter(Boolean).join('~')||'하루 전체'),
    reason:String(row.reason||[row['사유'],row['장소']].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')),
    evidence:String(row.evidence||row['증빙상태']||''),
    sourceTab:String(row.sourceTab||row['원본탭']||'30_공결기록')
  }));
  return data;
}

function masterTimetableRows({grade=1,classNo="",teacher=""}={}){
  // 배포판에서는 연결 전 샘플/고정 시간표를 실제 자료처럼 표시하지 않는다.
  // 시간표마스터가 구글시트에서 정상 수신된 경우에만 렌더링한다.
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.timetableRows)?readonlyCache.timetableRows:[];
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    if(String(row["학년도"]||"")!=="2026"||String(row["학기"]||"")!=="2")return false;
    if(grade&&String(row["학년"]||"")!==String(grade))return false;
    if(classNo&&String(row["반"]||"")!==String(classNo))return false;
    if(teacher&&uep08123TeacherKey(row["교사명"]||row["교사"]||row.teacher||"")!==uep08123TeacherKey(teacher))return false;
    return String(row["적용상태"]||"확정").trim()!=="취소";
  });
}
function teacherTimetableMasterRows(teacher=""){
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.teacherTimetableRows)?readonlyCache.teacherTimetableRows:[];
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    if(String(row["학년도"]||"")!=="2026"||String(row["학기"]||"")!=="2")return false;
    if(teacher&&uep08123TeacherKey(row["교사명"]||"")!==uep08123TeacherKey(teacher))return false;
    return String(row["적용상태"]||"확정").trim()!=="취소";
  });
}
function masterTeacherTimetableData(teacher){
  // 0.81.45: 학급시간표가 아니라 교사별 PDF 원본 탭을 사용하여 선택과목 동시수업 누락을 막습니다.
  const rows=teacherTimetableMasterRows(teacher);
  if(!rows.length)return null;
  const days=["월","화","수","목","금"],week=Array.from({length:7},()=>Array(5).fill(""));
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const p=Number(row["교시"]),d=days.indexOf(String(row["요일"]||""));
    const room=String(row["교실"]||"").trim();
    if(p>=1&&p<=7&&d>=0)week[p-1][d]=`${row["과목"]||""}|${room}`;
  });
  return {week,days,teacher,source:"UEP 교사시간표마스터",reference:true,changed:0};
}
function timetableMasterWeekDates(value){
  const base=new Date(`${String(value||dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if(Number.isNaN(base.getTime()))return [];
  const day=base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate()+(day===0?-6:1-day));
  return Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(base);d.setDate(base.getDate()+i);return d;});
}
function masterClassTimetableMarkup({date="",studentMode=false}={}){
  const grade=String(state.settings.neis.grade||"1"),classNo=String(state.settings.neis.classNo||"1");
  const rows=masterTimetableRows({grade,classNo});
  if(!rows.length)return "";
  const days=["월","화","수","목","금"],weekDates=timetableMasterWeekDates(date||neisData?.date||dateKey(new Date())),cells=[];
  for(let period=1;period<=7;period+=1){
    cells.push(`<div class="tt-period"><b>${period}</b><small>${escapeHtml(periodTimeLabel(period)||"")}</small></div>`);
    days.forEach(day=>{
      const lesson=rows.find(r=>String(r["요일"])===day&&Number(r["교시"])===period);
      const closed=day==="수"&&period>5;
      const tone=timetableSubjectStyle(lesson?.["과목"]||"");
      cells.push(`<div class="tt-cell ${lesson?`has-lesson ${tone}`:"empty"} ${closed?"closed":""}">${lesson?`<div class="tt-lesson-card"><strong>${escapeHtml(lesson["과목"]||"")}</strong><small>${escapeHtml(lesson["교사명"]||"")}</small></div>`:`<div class="tt-empty-card"><strong>-</strong><small>${closed?"수업 없음":"미등록"}</small></div>`}</div>`);
    });
  }
  const weekLabel=timetableWeekLabel(date||neisData?.date||dateKey(new Date()));
  const header=days.map((day,i)=>`<div class="tt-day"><b>${day}</b><small>${weekDates[i]?`${weekDates[i].getMonth()+1}/${weekDates[i].getDate()}`:""}</small></div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="tt-shell"><header class="tt-hero"><div><small>${studentMode?"STUDENT TIMETABLE":"CLASS TIMETABLE"} · UEP MASTER</small><h2>${escapeHtml(grade)}학년 ${escapeHtml(classNo)}반 ${studentMode?"학생 개인":"주간"} 시간표</h2><p>${escapeHtml(weekLabel)} · 2026학년도 2학기 확정시간표 · 2026-08-13 기준</p></div><div class="tt-stats"><span><b>${rows.length}</b>등록 수업</span><span><b>5</b>요일</span><span><b>7</b>최대 교시</span></div></header><div class="tt-status-row"><span class="tt-live">기준자료</span><span>${studentMode?"1학년은 선택과목 이동수업 전까지 학급시간표를 학생 개인시간표로 사용합니다.":"학급시간표: 과목 크게 · 담당교사 하단 표시"}</span></div><div class="tt-grid"><div class="tt-corner">교시</div>${header}${cells.join("")}</div><footer class="tt-legend class-legend"><span>UEP 기본정보 연결시트 · 17_시간표마스터</span></footer></section>`;
}

function classTimetableMarkup() {
  const masterMarkup=masterClassTimetableMarkup({date:neisData?.date||dateKey(new Date())});
  if(masterMarkup)return masterMarkup;
  if (neisLoading && !neisData) return `<div class="widget-loading"><i></i><b>나이스 시간표를 불러오는 중입니다</b></div>`;
  if (!neisData?.ok) return `<div class="widget-empty widget-error-inline"><span>◷</span><b>시간표 연결 대기</b><small>${escapeHtml(neisError || "설정에서 학교·학년·반을 확인하세요.")}</small><button data-neis-retry>다시 시도</button></div>`;
  const days=Array.isArray(neisData.timetable?.days)?neisData.timetable.days:[];
  const diagnostics=Array.isArray(neisData.timetable?.diagnostics)?neisData.timetable.diagnostics:[];
  const headers=days.length?days:["월","화","수","목","금"].map(weekday=>({weekday,date:"",lessons:[]}));
  const maxPeriod=Math.max(7,...headers.flatMap(day=>(day.lessons||[]).map(x=>Number(x.period||0))));
  const totalLessons=headers.reduce((sum,day)=>sum+(day.lessons||[]).length,0);
  const completeDays=diagnostics.filter(x=>x.rowCount>0&&x.complete).length;
  const selectedLabel=`${escapeHtml(neisData.grade)}학년 ${escapeHtml(neisData.classNo)}반`;
  const monday=escapeHtml(formatNeisYmd(headers[0]?.date||""));
  const friday=escapeHtml(formatNeisYmd(headers[4]?.date||""));
  const cells=[];
  for(let period=1;period<=maxPeriod;period+=1){
    cells.push(`<div class="tt-period"><b>${period}</b><small>${state.settings.widgetSettings.timetable?.showTimes!==false?escapeHtml(periodTimeLabel(period)):""}</small></div>`);
    headers.forEach(day=>{
      const lesson=(day.lessons||[]).find(item=>Number(item.period)===period);
      const expected=expectedSchoolPeriods(day.date);
      const isClosed=expected>0&&period>expected;
      const subjectTone=timetableSubjectStyle(lesson?.subject||"");
      const room=lesson?String(lesson.location||`${lesson.grade||neisData.grade}-${lesson.classNo||neisData.classNo}`):"";
      cells.push(`<div class="tt-cell ${lesson?`has-lesson ${subjectTone}`:"empty"} ${isClosed?"closed":""}">${lesson?`<div class="tt-lesson-card"><strong>${escapeHtml(lesson.subject)}</strong><small>${escapeHtml(room||"교실 미등록")}</small></div>`:`<div class="tt-empty-card"><strong>-</strong><small>${isClosed?"수업 없음":"미등록"}</small></div>`}</div>`);
    });
  }
  const rawDetails=`<div class="neis-raw-details hidden" data-neis-raw-details><div class="neis-raw-title"><div><b>시간표 원본 검사</b><small>컴시간 웹을 우선 연결하고 실패할 때만 NEIS를 대체자료로 사용합니다.</small></div><div><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-neis-copy-url>조회 주소 복사</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-neis-show-json>원본 JSON 보기</button></div></div>${headers.map(day=>{const raw=Array.isArray(day.rawLessons)?day.rawLessons:(day.lessons||[]);const periods=raw.map(x=>Number(x.period||0)).filter(Boolean);const classValues=Array.isArray(day.gradeClassValues)?day.gradeClassValues:[];return `<div class="neis-raw-day"><strong>${escapeHtml(day.weekday||"")} ${escapeHtml(formatNeisYmd(day.date))}</strong><span>직접 ${Number(day.rawRowCount||0)}건 · 1~9반 ${Number(day.gradeWideRowCount||0)}건 · 해당반 ${Number(day.gradeMatchedRowCount||0)}건 · PERIO ${periods.length?periods.join(", "):"없음"}</span><small>${classValues.length?`응답 반: ${classValues.map(escapeHtml).join(", ")}`:escapeHtml(day.error||"등록 자료 없음")}</small></div>`;}).join("")}</div>`;
  return `<section class="tt-shell"><header class="tt-hero"><div><small>CLASS TIMETABLE · ${escapeHtml(neisData.timetableSource||neisData.timetable?.source||"NEIS")}</small><h2>${selectedLabel} 주간 시간표</h2><p>${monday} ~ ${friday} · ${neisData.offline?"마지막 저장자료":escapeHtml(neisData.timetableSource||"실시간")}</p></div><div class="tt-stats"><span><b>${totalLessons}</b>등록 수업</span><span><b>${completeDays}</b>검증 요일</span><span><b>${maxPeriod}</b>최대 교시</span></div></header><div class="tt-status-row"><span class="tt-live ${neisData.offline?"offline":""}">${neisData.offline?"저장자료":"실시간 연결"}</span><span>월·화·목·금 7교시 · 수요일 5교시 기준</span><button type="button" data-neis-raw-toggle>시간표 원본 진단 보기</button></div>${rawDetails}<div class="tt-grid"><div class="tt-corner">교시</div>${headers.map(day=>`<div class="tt-day"><b>${escapeHtml(day.weekday||"")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(formatNeisYmd(day.date))}</small></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div><footer class="tt-legend class-legend"><span>학급별 고정색 · 대시보드와 동일 기준</span></footer></section>`;
}
function normalizeTeacherTimetableCell(cell){
  if(!cell)return null;
  if(typeof cell==="string")return {subject:cell,room:"",changed:false};
  if(Array.isArray(cell)){
    const subject=String(cell[1]||cell[0]||"").trim();
    const room=String(cell[3]||cell[2]||"").trim();
    return subject?{subject,room,changed:false}:null;
  }
  const subject=String(cell.subject||cell.subjectName||cell.name||cell.course||cell.className||"").trim();
  const room=String(cell.location||cell.room||cell.classroom||cell.gradeClass||cell.classNo||"").trim();
  return subject?{subject,room,changed:Boolean(cell.isChanged||cell.changed||cell.changeInfo),changeInfo:cell.changeInfo||""}:null;
}
function personalTimetableData() {
  const teacher=String(currentLoginTeacherName()||state.settings.comciganTeacher||state.settings.teacherName||"담당교사").trim();
  // 홍석민 교사는 사용자가 제공한 2026학년도 공식 교사시간표 PDF를
  // 최우선 기준으로 사용해 잘못 매칭된 컴시간 자료가 표시되지 않게 합니다.
  const masterReference=masterTeacherTimetableData(teacher);
  if(masterReference)return masterReference;
  // 연결 전 고정/샘플 교사시간표는 오인 가능성이 있어 배포판에서 사용하지 않는다.
  const timetable=neisData?.timetable||{};
  const teacherMap=timetable.teachers||{};
  const all=timetable.teacherTimetable||{};
  let teacherKey="";
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(teacherMap||{})){
    const name=typeof value==="string"?value:(value?.name||value?.teacherName||value?.label||"");
    if(String(name).trim()===teacher){teacherKey=key;break;}
  }
  let source=all?.[teacherKey]||all?.[teacher]||null;
  if(!source&&Array.isArray(all)) source=all.find(row=>String(row?.teacher||row?.teacherName||row?.name||"").trim()===teacher);
  const week=Array.from({length:7},()=>Array(5).fill(""));
  let found=0,changed=0;
  const setCell=(period,day,cell)=>{
    const p=Number(period),d=Number(day);const normalized=normalizeTeacherTimetableCell(cell);
    if(!normalized||p<1||p>7||d<0||d>4)return;
    week[p-1][d]=`${normalized.subject}|${normalized.room}${normalized.changed?"|changed":""}`;found+=1;if(normalized.changed)changed+=1;
  };
  if(Array.isArray(source)){
    if(source.length>=5&&source.slice(0,5).every(day=>Array.isArray(day))){
      source.slice(0,5).forEach((day,d)=>day.forEach((cell,index)=>setCell(cell?.classTime||cell?.period||cell?.classTimeNum||index+1,d,cell)));
    }else{
      source.forEach((cell,index)=>{
        const d=Number(cell?.dayIndex??cell?.weekdayIndex??cell?.day??cell?.weekDay??-1);
        const day=d>=1&&d<=5?d-1:d;
        setCell(cell?.classTime||cell?.period||cell?.classTimeNum||Math.floor(index/5)+1,day,cell);
      });
    }
  }else if(source&&typeof source==="object"){
    const dayKeys=[["월","monday","mon","1"],["화","tuesday","tue","2"],["수","wednesday","wed","3"],["목","thursday","thu","4"],["금","friday","fri","5"]];
    dayKeys.forEach((keys,d)=>{const day=keys.map(k=>source[k]).find(Boolean);if(Array.isArray(day))day.forEach((cell,index)=>setCell(cell?.classTime||cell?.period||index+1,d,cell));});
  }
  if(found)return {week,days:["월","화","수","목","금"],teacher,source:neisData?.offline?"컴시간 저장자료":"컴시간 실시간",reference:false,changed};
  return {week:[],days:["월","화","수","목","금"],teacher,source:"연결 대기",reference:false,changed:0};
}

function personalTimetableMarkup() {
  const data=personalTimetableData();
  if(!data.week.some(row=>row.some(Boolean)))return `<div class="personal-timetable-trust"><div class="connection-guide"><span>COMCIGAN</span><h3>${escapeHtml(data.teacher)} 교사시간표 연결 대기</h3><p>컴시간 실시간 자료에서 로그인 교사 이름을 찾지 못했습니다. 교사 이름과 컴시간 학교코드를 확인하세요.</p><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-neis-refresh>컴시간 다시 불러오기</button></div></div>`;
  const days=(data.days||['월','화','수','목','금']).slice(0,5);const cells=[];let count=0;
  for(let period=1;period<=7;period+=1){
    cells.push(`<div class="tt-period"><b>${period}</b><small>${escapeHtml(periodTimeLabel(period)||'')}</small></div>`);
    for(let d=0;d<5;d+=1){const raw=data.week[period-1]?.[d]||'';const [subject,room,flag]=String(raw).split('|');if(raw)count+=1;const classTone=timetableClassStyle(room||'');cells.push(`<div class="tt-cell ${raw?`has-lesson ${classTone.cls}`:'empty'}"${raw?classTone.style:''}><div class="${raw?'tt-lesson-card':'tt-empty-card'}"><strong>${escapeHtml(subject||'-')}</strong><small>${escapeHtml(room||'')}</small>${flag==='changed'?'<em class="tt-change-badge">변동</em>':''}</div></div>`);}
  }
  const sourceClass=data.reference?'offline':'';const sourceNote=data.reference?'참고용 고정 시간표 · 결보강/일과변동 미반영':'결보강·일과변동 반영';
  return `<section class="tt-shell personal-tt-shell"><header class="tt-hero"><div><small>PERSONAL TIMETABLE · ${escapeHtml(data.source)}</small><h2>${escapeHtml(data.teacher)} 선생님 주간 시간표</h2><p>${escapeHtml(sourceNote)}</p></div><div class="tt-stats"><span><b>${count}</b>등록 수업</span><span><b>5</b>요일</span><span><b>7</b>최대 교시</span></div></header><div class="tt-status-row"><span class="tt-live ${sourceClass}">${escapeHtml(data.source)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(data.reference?'실시간 연결 전 참고자료입니다.':'컴시간 교사 매칭 정상')}</span><button type="button" data-neis-refresh>새로고침</button></div><div class="tt-grid"><div class="tt-corner">교시</div>${days.map(day=>`<div class="tt-day"><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></div>`).join('')}${cells.join('')}</div><footer class="tt-legend"><span class="subject-korean">국어</span><span class="subject-math">수학</span><span class="subject-english">영어</span><span class="subject-social">사회</span><span class="subject-science">과학</span><span class="subject-creative">창체</span><span class="subject-art">예체능</span><span class="subject-other">기타</span></footer></section>`;
}

function schoolTeacherNames() {
  const masterNames=teacherTimetableMasterRows()
    .map(row=>String(row?.["교사명"]||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if(masterNames.length)return [...new Set(masterNames)].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
  const names = [...DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.map(item=>item.person), ...SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.homeroom.flatMap(row=>[row[2],row[3]])]
    .map(value=>String(value||"").trim())
    .filter(value=>value && !/순회|지도자|담당교사/.test(value));
  return [...new Set(names)].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,"ko"));
}
function teacherMasterTimetableMarkup(teacher){
  const data=masterTeacherTimetableData(teacher);
  if(!data)return `<div class="connection-guide"><span>UEP MASTER</span><h3>${escapeHtml(teacher||"교사")} 시간표가 없습니다</h3><p>17A_교사시간표마스터에 등록된 교사를 선택하세요.</p></div>`;
  const days=data.days||["월","화","수","목","금"],cells=[];let count=0;
  for(let period=1;period<=7;period+=1){
    cells.push(`<div class="tt-period"><b>${period}</b><small>${escapeHtml(periodTimeLabel(period)||"")}</small></div>`);
    for(let d=0;d<5;d+=1){
      const raw=data.week[period-1]?.[d]||"",[subject,room]=String(raw).split("|");if(raw)count+=1;
      const classTone=timetableClassStyle(room||"");
      cells.push(`<div class="tt-cell ${raw?`has-lesson ${classTone.cls}`:"empty"}"${raw?classTone.style:""}><div class="${raw?"tt-lesson-card":"tt-empty-card"}"><strong>${escapeHtml(room||"-")}</strong><small>${escapeHtml(subject||"")}</small></div></div>`);
    }
  }
  return `<section class="tt-shell personal-tt-shell"><header class="tt-hero"><div><small>TEACHER TIMETABLE · UEP MASTER</small><h2>${escapeHtml(teacher)} 선생님 주간 시간표</h2><p>2026학년도 2학기 확정시간표 · 학급 크게 · 과목 하단 표시</p></div><div class="tt-stats"><span><b>${count}</b>등록 수업</span><span><b>5</b>요일</span><span><b>7</b>최대 교시</span></div></header><div class="tt-grid"><div class="tt-corner">교시</div>${days.map(day=>`<div class="tt-day"><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div><footer class="tt-legend class-legend"><span>UEP 기본정보 연결시트 · 17A_교사시간표마스터</span></footer></section>`;
}
function normalizedSchoolHours(){
  const defaults=baseState.settings.schoolHours;
  const current=Array.isArray(state.settings.schoolHours)?state.settings.schoolHours:[];
  const byLabel=new Map(current.filter(row=>Array.isArray(row)&&row[0]&&row[0]!=="쉬는시간").map(row=>[String(row[0]),row]));
  const rows=defaults.map(def=>byLabel.get(String(def[0]))||structuredClone(def));
  state.settings.schoolHours=rows;
  return rows;
}
function schoolHourRange(label){
  const row=normalizedSchoolHours().find(item=>String(item[0])===String(label));
  return row?`${row[1]}~${row[2]}`:"";
}
function schoolHoursPageMarkup({popup=false}={}){
  const hours=normalizedSchoolHours();
  return `${popup?'':`<div class="timetable-toolbar"><div><b>일과운영시간</b><small>단축수업 등 변경 일과를 이 PC에 저장합니다.</small></div></div>`}<div class="card timetable-full school-hours-page ${popup?'school-hours-popup':''}"><form id="inlineSchoolHoursEditor"><div class="hours-editor">${hours.map(([label,start,end],i)=>`<div><b>${escapeHtml(label)}</b><input type="time" data-inline-hour-start="${i}" value="${escapeHtml(start)}"><span>~</span><input type="time" data-inline-hour-end="${i}" value="${escapeHtml(end)}"></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="editor-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" id="inlineResetSchoolHours">기본값</button><button class="btn primary">저장</button></div></form></div>`;
}
function bindSchoolHoursEditor({popup=false}={}){
  const form=$("#inlineSchoolHoursEditor");
  if(form)form.onsubmit=async event=>{
    event.preventDefault();
    state.settings.schoolHours=state.settings.schoolHours.map((row,i)=>[row[0],$(`[data-inline-hour-start="${i}"]`)?.value||row[1],$(`[data-inline-hour-end="${i}"]`)?.value||row[2]]);
    await save();toast("일과운영시간을 저장했습니다.");
    if(popup)openSchoolHoursPopup();else render("timetable");
  };
  const reset=$("#inlineResetSchoolHours");
  if(reset)reset.onclick=async()=>{
    state.settings.schoolHours=structuredClone(baseState.settings.schoolHours);
    await save();toast("기본 일과운영시간으로 되돌렸습니다.");
    if(popup)openSchoolHoursPopup();else render("timetable");
  };
}
function openSchoolHoursPopup(){
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="SCHOOL HOURS";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent="일과운영시간";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="school-hours-drawer-intro"><b>정규·야간 운영시간</b><span>정규수업과 오후자습·야자1·야자2를 수정하면 대시보드 시간표에 반영됩니다.</span></div>${schoolHoursPageMarkup({popup:true})}`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  bindSchoolHoursEditor({popup:true});
}
