function studentTimetableMarkup() {
  const students=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.students)?readonlyCache.students:[])
    .filter(st=>String(recordGradeClass(st).grade||"1")==="1")
    .sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||""),"ko"));
  let selected=students.find(st=>String(st.id||st.studentId||st.studentNo)===String(studentTimetableSelectedId||""))||null;
  const selectedClass=selected?String(Number(recordGradeClass(selected).classNo||0)):"";
  const classOptions=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>i+1).map(cls=>`<option value="${cls}" ${String(cls)===selectedClass?'selected':''}>${cls}반</option>`).join("");
  const classMembers=selectedClass?students.filter(st=>String(Number(recordGradeClass(st).classNo||0))===selectedClass):[];
  const studentOptions=classMembers.map(st=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(st.id||st.studentId||st.studentNo||"")}" ${selected&&String(selected.id||selected.studentId||selected.studentNo)===String(st.id||st.studentId||st.studentNo)?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(st.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(st.name||"")}</option>`).join("");

  let body='<div class="student-timetable-empty"><div class="connection-guide"><span>학생</span><h3>학생을 선택하세요</h3><p>반을 먼저 고른 뒤 학생을 선택합니다. 1학년은 현재 소속 학급 시간표를 그대로 표시합니다.</p></div></div>';
  if(selected){
    const gc=recordGradeClass(selected);
    state.settings.neis.grade=gc.grade||"1";
    state.settings.neis.classNo=String(Number(gc.classNo||1));
    body=`<div class="student-master-note"><b>${escapeHtml(selected.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(selected.name||"")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(gc.grade||"1")}학년 ${escapeHtml(String(Number(gc.classNo||1)))}반 · 현재는 학급시간표 기준</span></div><div class="card timetable-full inline-class-timetable">${masterClassTimetableMarkup({date:neisData?.date||dateKey(new Date()),studentMode:true})}</div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="student-timetable-selector student-timetable-selector-two-step">
    <label>반 선택<select id="studentTimetableClass"><option value="">반을 선택하세요</option>${classOptions}</select></label>
    <label>학생 선택<select id="studentTimetableSelect" ${selectedClass?'':'disabled'}><option value="">학생을 선택하세요</option>${studentOptions}</select></label>
    <small>9개 반을 한 번에 스크롤하지 않고 반 → 학생 순서로 선택합니다.</small>
  </div>${body}`;
}
function schoolPeriodStatus(now) {
  const minute = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  const periods = [[520,570],[580,630],[640,690],[700,750],[820,870],[880,930],[940,990]];
  const index = periods.findIndex(([start, end]) => minute >= start && minute <= end);
  if (index >= 0) return { index, label: `현재 ${index + 1}교시` };
  if (minute < periods[0][0]) return { index: -1, label: "일과 시작 전" };
  if (minute > periods[6][1]) return { index: -1, label: "정규수업 종료" };
  if (minute >= 750 && minute < 820) return { index: -1, label: "점심시간" };
  return { index: -1, label: "쉬는 시간" };
}

function widgetOrderIndex(key) {
  const order = state.settings.dashboardOrder || baseState.settings.dashboardOrder;
  const index = order.indexOf(key);
  return index < 0 ? 99 : index;
}

function widgetAttrs(key) {
  const setting = state.settings.widgetSettings[key] || {};
  const hidden = setting.visible === false ? "display:none;" : "";
  return `data-widget="${key}" data-widget-width="${setting.width || "auto"}" style="${hidden}--widget-font:${(setting.fontScale || 100) / 100};--widget-order:${widgetOrderIndex(key)}"`;
}

function widgetErrorCard(key, title, message) {
  return `<article class="card widget-configurable widget-error-card" ${widgetAttrs(key)}><div class="card-head"><div><span class="card-icon ci-coral">!</span><strong>${escapeHtml(title)}</strong></div><button data-widget-settings="${key}">⚙</button></div><div class="widget-empty"><span>!</span><b>이 위젯만 불러오지 못했습니다</b><small>${escapeHtml(message)}</small><button data-widget-retry="${key}">다시 시도</button></div></article>`;
}

function safeWidget(key, title, renderer) {
  try { return renderer(); }
  catch (error) { console.error(`widget:${key}`, error); return widgetErrorCard(key, title, error?.message || String(error)); }
}

function dashboardTodayLessonsMarkup() {
  const {week,days}=personalTimetableData();
  const todayIndex=Math.max(0,Math.min(4,(today.getDay()+6)%7));
  const lessons=week.map((row,index)=>({period:index+1,cell:row[todayIndex]})).filter(item=>item.cell&&item.cell!=="-");
  const status=schoolPeriodStatus(new Date());
  if(!lessons.length){
    const connected=Array.isArray(days)&&days.length>0;
    return `<div class="uep-empty-state uep-empty-lessons"><span class="uep-empty-icon">◷</span><div><b>오늘은 편성된 수업이 없습니다.</b><small>방학·휴업일이거나 오늘 시간표가 비어 있습니다.</small></div><footer><em>${connected?"개인 시간표 연결됨":"시간표 연결 확인 필요"}</em><button data-page="timetable">시간표 확인 ›</button></footer></div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="uep-lesson-list">${lessons.map(item=>{const [subject,room]=item.cell.split("|"); const current=status.index===item.period-1; const done=status.index>item.period-1 || /종료/.test(status.label); return `<button class="uep-lesson-item ${current?"is-now":done?"is-done":""}" data-page="timetable"><span class="uep-period-badge">${item.period}교시</span><span class="uep-lesson-main"><b>${escapeHtml(room||"교실 미등록")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(subject||"과목 미등록")}</small></span><time>${escapeHtml(periodTimeLabel(item.period)||"")}</time><em>${current?"NOW":done?"DONE":"NEXT"}</em></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`;
}

function dashboardWeekAgendaMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${dashboardWeekDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const monday=new Date(anchor); monday.setDate(anchor.getDate()-((anchor.getDay()+6)%7));
  const friday=new Date(monday); friday.setDate(monday.getDate()+4);
  const rows=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(monday); d.setDate(monday.getDate()+i); const key=dateKey(d); const events=schoolEventsForDate(key); return {d,key,events};});
  const rangeLabel=`${monday.getMonth()+1}/${monday.getDate()}~${friday.getMonth()+1}/${friday.getDate()}`;
  return `<div class="uep-dashboard-calendar-nav"><button data-dashboard-week-shift="-1" aria-label="이전 주">‹</button><b>${rangeLabel}</b><button data-dashboard-week-shift="1" aria-label="다음 주">›</button><button data-dashboard-week-today>이번 주</button><button class="uep-calendar-full-button" data-dashboard-calendar-full="week">전체보기</button></div><div class="uep-week-strip">${rows.map(({d,key,events})=>`<section class="${key===dateKey(today)?"today":""}"><header><button class="uep-week-day-button" data-dashboard-calendar-date="${key}"><b>${["월","화","수","목","금"][d.getDay()-1]||""}</b><span>${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}</span>${events.length?`<i aria-label="${events.length}개 일정">${events.length}</i>`:""}</button></header><div>${events.length?events.map(event=>`<button data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><span>${escapeHtml(calendarShortTitle(calendarDisplayTitle(event),18))}</span></button>`).join(""):`<small>일정 없음</small>`}</div></section>`).join("")}</div>`;
}

function dashboardMiniCalendarMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${dashboardMonthDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const y=anchor.getFullYear(),m=anchor.getMonth(); const first=new Date(y,m,1); const start=new Date(y,m,1-first.getDay());
  let cells=""; for(let i=0;i<42;i++){const d=new Date(start);d.setDate(start.getDate()+i);const key=dateKey(d);const count=schoolEventsForDate(key).length;const tone=calendarDayTone(key);cells+=`<button class="${d.getMonth()!==m?"muted":""} ${key===dateKey(today)?"today":""} ${key===dashboardSelectedDate?"selected":""} ${tone.className}" data-dashboard-calendar-date="${key}"><span>${d.getDate()}</span>${count?`<i aria-label="${count}개 일정">${count}</i>`:""}</button>`;}
  return `<div class="uep-mini-cal-head"><button data-dashboard-month-shift="-1" aria-label="이전 달">‹</button><b>${y}년 ${m+1}월</b><button data-dashboard-month-shift="1" aria-label="다음 달">›</button><button data-dashboard-month-today>오늘</button></div><div class="uep-mini-cal"><div class="sunday">일</div><div>월</div><div>화</div><div>수</div><div>목</div><div>금</div><div class="saturday">토</div>${cells}</div>`;
}

function dashboardSelectedDayMarkup(){
  const key=dashboardSelectedDate||dateKey(today);
  const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const events=schoolEventsForDate(key).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99")));
  return `<div class="uep-selected-day-head"><div><small>SELECTED DAY</small><h3>${date.getMonth()+1}월 ${date.getDate()}일 ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(key,true))}</h3><p>${events.length}개의 학교 일정</p></div><button data-dashboard-calendar-full="month" data-dashboard-calendar-target="${key}">학교일정 전체보기</button></div>${events.length?`<div class="uep-selected-day-list">${events.map(event=>{const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);const meta=[event.place,event.owner,event.target].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||"학교 일정";return `<button class="uep-selected-event kind-${kind}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(calendarDisplayTitle(event))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(meta)}</small></span><em>자세히 ›</em></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`:`<div class="uep-selected-day-empty"><span>○</span><b>이 날은 등록된 일정이 없습니다.</b><small>월간 캘린더에서 다른 날짜를 선택해 보세요.</small></div>`}`;
}

function openDashboardCalendarDate(dateValue){
  const key=dateValue||dateKey(today);
  const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const events=schoolEventsForDate(key).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99")));
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent='DASHBOARD CALENDAR';
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent=`${date.getMonth()+1}월 ${date.getDate()}일 ${weekdayLabelFromKey(key,true)} 일정 · ${events.length}건`;
  $("#drawer").classList.add('dashboard-calendar-drawer');
  $("#drawer").classList.remove('hidden');$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove('hidden');
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="dashboard-calendar-day-detail"><div class="history-summary ${events.length?"positive":""}"><span><small>날짜</small><b>${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}</b></span><span><small>요일</small><b>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(key,true))}</b></span><span><small>일정</small><b>${events.length}건</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>전체 일정</h4>${events.length?`<div class="calendar-selected-list">${events.map(event=>{const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);const meta=[event.target,event.place,event.owner].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||"학교 업무일정";return `<button class="calendar-selected-item kind-${kind} ${calendarEventIsPending(event)?"pending":""}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(calendarDisplayTitle(event))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(meta)}</small></span><em>${escapeHtml(event.status||"")}</em></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`:'<div class="empty-state compact-empty"><b>등록된 일정이 없습니다.</b><span>다른 날짜를 선택하거나 학교일정에서 일정을 추가하세요.</span></div>'}</div><div class="drawer-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-dashboard-calendar-register="${key}">＋ 일정 등록</button><button class="btn secondary" data-dashboard-calendar-full="month" data-dashboard-calendar-target="${key}">월간 전체보기</button><button class="btn primary" data-dashboard-calendar-full="week" data-dashboard-calendar-target="${key}">주간 전체보기</button></div></div>`;
  $$("#drawerBody [data-school-event]").forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>openSchoolCalendarEvent(button.dataset.schoolEvent));
  $$("#drawerBody [data-dashboard-calendar-register]").forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{calendarSelectedDate=button.dataset.dashboardCalendarRegister||key;state.calendarMode="month";closeDrawer();navigate("calendar");toast("선택한 날짜에서 일정을 등록하세요.");});
  $$("#drawerBody [data-dashboard-calendar-full]").forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{calendarSelectedDate=button.dataset.dashboardCalendarTarget||key;state.calendarMode=button.dataset.dashboardCalendarFull||"month";calendarHubMode="schedule";closeDrawer();navigate("calendar");});
}

function currentUserOvertimeTasks(){
  const person=currentLoginTeacherName();
  if(!person)return [];
  const overtime=[];
  if(dashboardDutyStatus('night').state==='on'){
    overtime.push({
      title:'야자감독으로 인해 초과근무 신청',
      type:'초과근무',
      due:'오늘 16:00 이전 신청',
      source:'night-duty'
    });
  }
  const todayAfter=(todayProgramCollections()?.after||[]).filter(program=>{
    const teacher=String(program.teacher||program.instructor||program.manager||'').trim();
    if(!teacher||!teacher.split(/[,·/\s]+/).some(name=>name===person))return false;
    const category=String(program.afterType||program.type||program.category||'');
    return /방과후|야간심화/.test(category)||/방과후|야간심화/.test(String(program.actualTitle||program.title||''));
  });
  todayAfter.forEach(program=>{
    const programName=String(program.actualTitle||program.title||'방과후·야간심화수업').trim();
    overtime.push({
      title:`${programName}로 인해 초과근무 신청`,
      type:/야간심화/.test(String(program.afterType||program.type||programName))?'야간심화 초과근무':'방과후 초과근무',
      due:'수업 시작 전 신청',
      source:`program:${program.id||programName}`
    });
  });
  return overtime.filter((item,index,array)=>array.findIndex(other=>other.source===item.source)===index);
}

function dashboardCompactTasksMarkup(){
  const projectTasks=(state.tasks||[]).filter(task=>task&&task.project==="UEP").sort((a,b)=>Number(a.order||999)-Number(b.order||999));
  const completed=projectTasks.filter(task=>task.done).length;
  const total=projectTasks.length;
  const progress=total?Math.round(completed/total*100):0;
  const next=projectTasks.find(task=>!task.done);
  const visible=projectTasks.filter(task=>!task.done).slice(0,5);
  const statusLabel={planned:"예정",working:"작업중",test:"테스트",done:"완료"};
  const statusIcon={planned:"○",working:"◐",test:"△",done:"✓"};
  return `<div class="uep-dev-task-widget">
    <div class="uep-dev-progress"><div><b>UEP 개발 진행률</b><span>${completed}/${total} 완료</span></div><strong>${progress}%</strong><i><em style="width:${progress}%"></em></i></div>
    <div class="uep-dev-task-list">${visible.length?visible.map(task=>`<label class="uep-dev-task ${task.done?"done":""} status-${escapeHtml(task.status||"planned")}"><input type="checkbox" data-dashboard-task="${escapeHtml(task.id)}" ${task.done?"checked":""}><span class="uep-dev-check">${statusIcon[task.status||"planned"]||"○"}</span><span><b>${escapeHtml(task.title)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(statusLabel[task.status||"planned"]||"예정")} · ${escapeHtml(task.type||"개발")}</small></span></label>`).join(""):`<div class="uep-dev-all-done"><b>오늘의 개발 업무를 모두 완료했습니다.</b><span>완료 이력은 업무 보기에서 확인할 수 있습니다.</span></div>`}</div>
    <footer><span>${next?`다음 작업 · ${escapeHtml(next.title)}`:"모든 작업 완료"}</span><button data-page="work">전체 체크리스트 ›</button></footer>
  </div>`;
}

function dashboardCompactProgramsMarkup(){
  const {selection,after}=todayProgramCollections(); const list=[...selection,...after].slice(0,4);
  const connectedCount=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).length;
  return `<div class="uep-simple-list">${list.length?list.map((p,i)=>`<button ${p.kind==="calendar-first-grade"?`data-school-event="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"`:p.kind==="after"?`data-after-program="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"`:`data-program="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"`}><i>${String(p.time||"종일").slice(0,5)}</i><span><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title||"프로그램")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(p.place||p.department||"장소 확인")}</small></span><em>›</em></button>`).join(""):`<div class="uep-empty-state uep-empty-program"><span class="uep-empty-icon">♧</span><div><b>오늘 운영되는 프로그램이 없습니다.</b><small>방학 중에는 예정 프로그램이 없을 수 있습니다.</small></div><footer><em>연결 프로그램 ${connectedCount}건</em><button data-page="programs">전체 프로그램 ›</button></footer></div>`}</div>`;
}

function dashboardCompactNoticesMarkup(){
  const connected=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[]; const local=[]; const source=connected.filter(item=>String(item?.title||"").trim()!=="방학식 학생 안내 확인"); const rows=source.slice(0,4);
  return `<div class="uep-simple-list">${rows.length?rows.map((n,i)=>`<button ${local.includes(n)?`data-work-edit="notice" data-work-id="${escapeHtml(n.id||"")}"`:`data-connected-notice="${escapeHtml(n.id||"")}"`}><i>${i+1}</i><span><b>${escapeHtml(n.title||"학년 공지")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(n.author||n.department||"1학년부")} · ${escapeHtml(n.deadline||n.date||"확인")}</small></span><em>›</em></button>`).join(""):`<div class="uep-empty-state uep-empty-notice"><span class="uep-empty-icon">♢</span><div><b>새로 확인할 공지가 없습니다.</b><small>학년부 공지가 등록되면 이곳에 바로 표시됩니다.</small></div><footer><em>연결 공지 ${source.length}건</em><button data-page="work">공지 확인 ›</button></footer></div>`}</div>`;
}



function currentLoginTeacherName(){
  const candidates=[
    state?.auth?.user?.name,
    state?.currentUser?.name,
    state?.settings?.loginUserName,
    state?.settings?.teacherName,
    state?.settings?.userName,
    document.querySelector(".profile b")?.textContent
  ];
  return String(candidates.find(value=>String(value||"").trim())||"").trim();
}

function firstGradeDutyHighlightNames(){
  const homeroom = (SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA?.homeroom || [])
    .filter(row => String(row?.[0] || "") === "1")
    .map(row => String(row?.[2] || "").trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  const gradeHead = (DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS || [])
    .filter(row => String(row?.department || "").trim() === "1학년부" && String(row?.role || "").trim() === "부장")
    .map(row => String(row?.person || "").trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  return new Set([...homeroom, ...gradeHead]);
}

function dashboardNextDuty(type, person=currentLoginTeacherName()){
  const source=type==="lunch"?(readonlyCache?.lunchDuties||[]):(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors||[]);
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const row=source.filter(item=>String(item.date||"").slice(0,10)>todayKey && String(item.teacher||"").trim()===person)
    .sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||"").localeCompare(String(b.date||"")))[0];
  if(!row)return null;
  const d=new Date(`${String(row.date).slice(0,10)}T12:00:00`);
  const dateLabel=Number.isNaN(d.getTime())?String(row.date||"").slice(0,10):`${d.getMonth()+1}월 ${d.getDate()}일`;
  const dutyLabel=type==="lunch"?String(row.dutyType||"").trim():String(row.place||"").trim();
  return {row,label:`다음 ${dateLabel}`,detail:[dutyLabel,[row.start,row.end].filter(Boolean).join("~")].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")};
}
function dashboardDutyStatus(type){
  const key=dateKey(today), person=currentLoginTeacherName();
  if(type==="lunch"){
    const manual=state.settings?.lunchDutyOverrides?.[key];
    if(manual){
      if(manual.enabled!==false)return {state:"on",label:"오늘 담당",detail:[manual.time,manual.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),rows:[manual]};
      const next=dashboardNextDuty("lunch",person);return {state:"off",label:"오늘 없음",detail:next?[next.label,next.detail].filter(Boolean).join(" · "):"다음 일정 없음",rows:[]};
    }
    const rows=(readonlyCache?.lunchDuties||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||"").slice(0,10)===key && String(row.teacher||"").trim()===person);
    if(rows.length){const kinds=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>r.dutyType).filter(Boolean))].join(" · "); const time=[rows[0].start,rows[0].end].filter(Boolean).join("~"); return {state:"on",label:"오늘 담당",detail:[time,kinds].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),rows};}
    if(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.lunchDuties)){const next=dashboardNextDuty("lunch",person);return {state:"off",label:"오늘 없음",detail:next?[next.label,next.detail].filter(Boolean).join(" · "):"다음 일정 없음",rows:[]};}
    return {state:"pending",label:"미연결",detail:"급식지도 시트 연결 확인",rows:[]};
  }
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||"").slice(0,10)===key && String(row.teacher||"").trim()===person);
  if(rows.length){const row=rows[0];return {state:"on",label:"오늘 담당",detail:[[row.start,row.end].filter(Boolean).join("~"),row.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),rows};}
  if(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors)){const next=dashboardNextDuty("night",person);return {state:"off",label:"오늘 없음",detail:next?[next.label,next.detail].filter(Boolean).join(" · "):"다음 일정 없음",rows:[]};}
  return {state:"pending",label:"미연결",detail:"야자감독 시트 연결 확인",rows:[]};
}

const dutyCalendarCursor={
  lunch:new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth(),1),
  night:new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth(),1)
};
function dutyMonthRows(type, baseDate=today){
  const y=baseDate.getFullYear(),m=baseDate.getMonth()+1;
  const rows=type==="lunch"?(readonlyCache?.lunchDuties||[]):(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors||[]);
  return rows.filter(row=>{const d=new Date(String(row.date||"")+"T00:00:00");return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getFullYear()===y&&d.getMonth()+1===m;}).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date).localeCompare(String(b.date))||String(a.dutyType||"").localeCompare(String(b.dutyType||"")));
}
function monthlyMealSummaryMarkup(){
  const meals=Array.isArray(neisData?.meals)?neisData.meals:[];
  if(neisData?.ok&&meals.length){
    return `<section class="duty-calendar-meal"><header><div><small>TODAY MEAL</small><h3>오늘 급식정보</h3></div><time>${escapeHtml(dateKey(today))}</time></header><div class="duty-calendar-meal-list">${meals.map(meal=>`<article><b>${escapeHtml(meal.mealType||'급식')}</b><span class="duty-meal-menu-full">${(meal.menu||[]).map(item=>`<i>${escapeHtml(item)}</i>`).join('')}</span><small>${escapeHtml(meal.calories||'')}</small></article>`).join('')}</div></section>`;
  }
  if(neisData?.ok) return `<section class="duty-calendar-meal empty"><header><div><small>TODAY MEAL</small><h3>오늘 급식정보</h3></div><time>${escapeHtml(dateKey(today))}</time></header><p>급식 없음</p><small>방학·휴업일이거나 오늘 실시되는 급식이 없습니다.</small></section>`;
  return `<section class="duty-calendar-meal empty"><header><div><small>TODAY MEAL</small><h3>오늘 급식정보</h3></div><time>${escapeHtml(dateKey(today))}</time></header><p>급식정보 조회 불가</p><small>${escapeHtml(neisError||'NEIS 급식 연결을 확인하세요.')}</small></section>`;
}
function dutyCalendarMarkup(type, baseDate=dutyCalendarCursor[type]){
  const year=baseDate.getFullYear(), month=baseDate.getMonth();
  const rows=dutyMonthRows(type,baseDate), person=currentLoginTeacherName(), title=type==="lunch"?"급식지도":"야자감독";
  const grouped=rows.reduce((m,row)=>{const key=String(row.date||"").slice(0,10);(m[key]??=[]).push(row);return m;},{});
  const firstDay=new Date(year,month,1).getDay(), days=new Date(year,month+1,0).getDate();
  const cells=[];
  for(let i=0;i<firstDay;i++) cells.push('<div class="duty-cal-cell empty" aria-hidden="true"></div>');
  for(let day=1;day<=days;day++){
    const key=`${year}-${String(month+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(day).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    const list=grouped[key]||[];
    const isToday=key===dateKey(today);
    const highlightedNames=firstGradeDutyHighlightNames();
    const items=list.map(row=>{
      const teacher=String(row.teacher||'').trim();
      const mine=teacher===person;
      const firstGrade=highlightedNames.has(teacher);
      if(type==='lunch') return `<button type="button" class="duty-cal-entry lunch ${firstGrade?'first-grade':''} ${mine?'mine':''}" data-lunch-duty-edit="${escapeHtml(row.id||'')}" data-lunch-duty-date="${escapeHtml(key)}" data-lunch-duty-teacher="${escapeHtml(teacher)}" data-lunch-duty-type="${escapeHtml(row.dutyType||'급식실')}"><b>${escapeHtml(teacher||'-')}</b><span>(${escapeHtml(row.dutyType||'급식실')})</span>${mine?'<em>내 근무</em>':''}</button>`;
      return `<button type="button" class="duty-cal-entry night ${mine?'mine':''}" data-night-duty-edit="${escapeHtml(row.id||'')}" data-night-duty-date="${escapeHtml(key)}" data-night-duty-teacher="${escapeHtml(teacher)}"><b>${escapeHtml(teacher||'-')}</b>${mine?'<em>내 근무</em>':''}</button>`;
    }).join('');
    const weekday=new Date(year,month,day).getDay();
    const tone=calendarDayTone(key);
    const dayClass=weekday===0?'sunday':weekday===6?'saturday':'';
    cells.push(`<div class="duty-cal-cell ${dayClass} ${tone.className||''} ${isToday?'today':''} ${list.length?'has-duty':''}"><header><time>${day}</time>${isToday?'<em>오늘</em>':''}</header><div class="duty-cal-list">${items}</div></div>`);
  }
  while(cells.length%7) cells.push('<div class="duty-cal-cell empty" aria-hidden="true"></div>');
  const myCount=rows.filter(r=>String(r.teacher||'').trim()===person).length;
  const commonInfo=type==='night'?`<div class="night-duty-common"><span><b>시간</b> 16:30~21:30</span><span><b>장소</b> 면학관 2~3층</span><span><b>대상</b> 1학년 1~9반</span><small>교사 이름을 클릭하면 감독교사를 변경할 수 있습니다.</small></div>`:'';
  return `<div class="duty-calendar-view duty-calendar-${type}" data-duty-calendar="${type}">
    <div class="duty-calendar-topline">
      <div class="duty-calendar-toolbar"><button type="button" data-duty-month="-1" aria-label="이전 달">‹</button><strong>${year}년 ${month+1}월 ${title}</strong><button type="button" data-duty-month="1" aria-label="다음 달">›</button></div>
      <div class="duty-calendar-summary"><span><b>${Object.keys(grouped).length}</b>운영일</span><span><b>${rows.length}</b>배정</span><span><b>${myCount}</b>내 일정</span></div>
    </div>
    ${commonInfo}
    <div class="duty-calendar-week"><b>일</b><b>월</b><b>화</b><b>수</b><b>목</b><b>금</b><b>토</b></div>
    <div class="duty-calendar-grid">${cells.join('')}</div>
    ${type==='lunch'?monthlyMealSummaryMarkup():''}
    <div class="readonly-rule"><b>연결 원본</b><span>${type==='lunch'?'42_급식지도계획':'43_야자감독계획'}</span></div>
  </div>`;
}
function bindDutyCalendarNavigation(type){
  $("#drawerBody")?.querySelectorAll('[data-duty-month]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const delta=Number(button.dataset.dutyMonth||0), current=dutyCalendarCursor[type];
    dutyCalendarCursor[type]=new Date(current.getFullYear(),current.getMonth()+delta,1);
    renderDutyCalendarDrawer(type);
  }));
}
function bindLunchDutyEditor(){
  $("#drawerBody")?.querySelectorAll('[data-lunch-duty-edit]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>openLunchDutyAssignmentEditor({
    id:button.dataset.lunchDutyEdit||'', date:button.dataset.lunchDutyDate||'', teacher:button.dataset.lunchDutyTeacher||'', dutyType:button.dataset.lunchDutyType||'급식실'
  })));
}
function openLunchDutyAssignmentEditor(row={}){
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent='LUNCH DUTY EDIT';
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent='급식지도 담당 변경';
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<form class="duty-editor-form" id="lunchDutyAssignmentForm"><label>운영일<input value="${escapeHtml(row.date||'')}" readonly></label><label>현재 담당교사<input value="${escapeHtml(row.teacher||'')}" readonly></label><label>변경 담당교사<select id="lunchDutyTeacher">${nightTeacherOptions(row.teacher||'')}</select></label><label>담당 구분<select id="lunchDutyType"><option value="급식실" ${row.dutyType==='급식실'?'selected':''}>급식실</option><option value="방송" ${/방송/.test(row.dutyType||'')?'selected':''}>방송</option></select></label><div class="modal-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-cancel-lunch-edit>취소</button><button class="btn primary">시트에 저장</button></div></form><p class="drawer-note">42_급식지도계획의 해당 행을 직접 수정합니다.</p>`;
  $("[data-cancel-lunch-edit]")?.addEventListener('click',()=>renderDutyCalendarDrawer('lunch'));
  $("#lunchDutyAssignmentForm").onsubmit=async event=>{
    event.preventDefault();
    const teacher=$("#lunchDutyTeacher").value.trim(), dutyType=$("#lunchDutyType").value.trim();
    if(!teacher)return toast('변경할 담당교사를 선택하세요.');
    const submit=event.submitter; if(submit){submit.disabled=true;submit.textContent='저장 중…';}
    try{
      if(!window.schoolBoard?.saveLunchDuty) throw new Error('급식지도 저장 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.');
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveLunchDuty({id:row.id,date:row.date,originalTeacher:row.teacher,teacher,dutyType,role:currentRoleId(),requester:currentLoginTeacherName()});
      if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'저장하지 못했습니다.');
      if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};
      renderDutyCalendarDrawer('lunch'); render('dashboard'); toast('급식지도 담당을 변경했습니다.');
    }catch(error){toast(error?.message||'급식지도 변경 실패'); if(submit){submit.disabled=false;submit.textContent='시트에 저장';}}
  };
}

function bindNightDutyEditor(){
  $("#drawerBody")?.querySelectorAll('[data-night-duty-edit]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>openNightDutyEditor({
    id:button.dataset.nightDutyEdit||'', date:button.dataset.nightDutyDate||'', teacher:button.dataset.nightDutyTeacher||''
  })));
}
function nightTeacherOptions(selected=''){
  const names=[...new Set((SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA?.extension||[]).map(row=>String(row?.[1]||'').trim()).filter(name=>name&&!/급식소|교장실|행정실|특별실|팩/.test(name)))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'ko'));
  if(selected&&!names.includes(selected)) names.unshift(selected);
  return names.map(name=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}" ${name===selected?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(name)}</option>`).join('');
}
function openNightDutyEditor(row={}){
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent='NIGHT DUTY EDIT';
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent='야자감독 교사 변경';
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<form class="duty-editor-form" id="nightDutyEditForm"><label>운영일<input value="${escapeHtml(row.date||'')}" readonly></label><label>현재 감독교사<input value="${escapeHtml(row.teacher||'')}" readonly></label><label>변경 감독교사<select id="nightDutyTeacher">${nightTeacherOptions(row.teacher||'')}</select></label><div class="night-duty-edit-info"><b>16:30~21:30</b><span>면학관 2~3층 · 1학년 1~9반</span></div><div class="modal-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-cancel-night-edit>취소</button><button class="btn primary">시트에 저장</button></div></form><p class="drawer-note">43_야자감독계획의 해당 행을 직접 수정합니다.</p>`;
  $("[data-cancel-night-edit]")?.addEventListener('click',()=>renderDutyCalendarDrawer('night'));
  $("#nightDutyEditForm").onsubmit=async event=>{
    event.preventDefault();
    const teacher=$("#nightDutyTeacher").value.trim();
    if(!teacher)return toast('변경할 감독교사를 선택하세요.');
    const submit=event.submitter; if(submit){submit.disabled=true;submit.textContent='저장 중…';}
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveNightSupervisor({id:row.id,date:row.date,teacher,role:currentRoleId(),requester:currentLoginTeacherName()});
      if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'저장하지 못했습니다.');
      if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};
      renderDutyCalendarDrawer('night'); render('dashboard'); toast('야자감독 교사를 변경했습니다.');
    }catch(error){toast(error?.message||'야자감독 변경 실패'); if(submit){submit.disabled=false;submit.textContent='시트에 저장';}}
  };
}
function renderDutyCalendarDrawer(type){
  const isLunch=type==='lunch';
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent=isLunch?'LUNCH DUTY · 42':'NIGHT DUTY · 43';
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent=isLunch?'월간 급식지도':'월간 야자감독';
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=dutyCalendarMarkup(type);
  $("#drawer").classList.add('duty-calendar-expanded');
  $("#drawer").classList.remove('hidden');$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove('hidden');
  bindDutyCalendarNavigation(type); if(type==='night')bindNightDutyEditor(); else bindLunchDutyEditor();
}
function openTodayLunchSupervisorDrawer(){
  const key=dateKey(today);
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.lunchDuties||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||'').slice(0,10)===key);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent='TODAY LUNCH DUTY';$("#drawerTitle").textContent='오늘의 급식지도';
  const body=rows.length?rows.map(row=>`<div class="today-duty-person ${String(row.teacher||'').trim()===currentLoginTeacherName()?'mine':''}"><strong>${escapeHtml(row.teacher||'-')}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(row.dutyType||'급식실')}</span><small>${escapeHtml([row.start,row.end].filter(Boolean).join('~'))}</small></div>`).join(''):`<div class="night-supervisor-empty"><i>◇</i><b>오늘 급식지도 일정이 없습니다</b><span>42_급식지도계획의 오늘 날짜가 비어 있습니다.</span></div>`;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="today-duty-list"><time>${escapeHtml(key)}</time>${body}</div>${monthlyMealSummaryMarkup()}<div class="drawer-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-reference="lunch-supervisor">빠른열기 · 급식지도 전체 보기</button></div>`;/* __UEP_08166_TODAY_MEAL_IN_DUTY_POPUP__ */
  $("#drawer").classList.remove('hidden');$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove('hidden');
  $("#drawerBody [data-reference='lunch-supervisor']")?.addEventListener("click",()=>openReferenceDrawer("lunch-supervisor"));
}

function openLunchDutyEditor(){
  const key=dateKey(today);
  const saved=state.settings?.lunchDutyOverrides?.[key]||{};
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="TODAY DUTY";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent="점심 급식지도 등록";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<form class="duty-editor-form" id="lunchDutyForm"><label>일자<input type="date" id="lunchDutyDate" value="${escapeHtml(key)}"></label><label>담당 여부<select id="lunchDutyEnabled"><option value="1" ${saved.enabled!==false?"selected":""}>담당 있음</option><option value="0" ${saved.enabled===false?"selected":""}>담당 없음</option></select></label><label>시간<input id="lunchDutyTime" value="${escapeHtml(saved.time||"12:20~13:10")}" placeholder="12:20~13:10"></label><label>장소·구역<input id="lunchDutyPlace" value="${escapeHtml(saved.place||"")}" placeholder="1학년 급식실"></label><label>함께 근무<input id="lunchDutyPartners" value="${escapeHtml(saved.partners||"")}" placeholder="교사 이름"></label><div class="modal-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary" data-close-drawer>취소</button><button class="btn primary">저장</button></div></form><p class="drawer-note">대시보드 시계 아래 급식지도 배지에 오늘 기준으로 표시됩니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("[data-close-drawer]")?.addEventListener("click",closeDrawer);
  $("#lunchDutyForm").onsubmit=async event=>{event.preventDefault();const date=$("#lunchDutyDate").value||key;state.settings.lunchDutyOverrides=state.settings.lunchDutyOverrides||{};state.settings.lunchDutyOverrides[date]={enabled:$("#lunchDutyEnabled").value==="1",time:$("#lunchDutyTime").value.trim(),place:$("#lunchDutyPlace").value.trim(),partners:$("#lunchDutyPartners").value.trim()};await save();closeDrawer();render("dashboard");toast("급식지도 일정을 저장했습니다.");};
}
function openTodayNightSupervisorDrawer(){
  const key=dateKey(today);
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.nightSupervisors||[]).filter(row=>String(row.date||'').slice(0,10)===key);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="TODAY NIGHT STUDY";$("#drawerTitle").textContent="오늘의 야자감독";
  const body=rows.length?rows.map(row=>`<div class="today-duty-person ${String(row.teacher||'').trim()===currentLoginTeacherName()?'mine':''}"><strong>${escapeHtml(row.teacher||'-')}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(row.place||'면학관 2~3층')}</span><small>${escapeHtml([row.start,row.end].filter(Boolean).join('~'))}</small></div>`).join(''):`<div class="night-supervisor-empty"><i>◇</i><b>오늘 야자감독이 없습니다</b><span>43_야자감독계획의 오늘 날짜가 비어 있습니다.</span></div>`;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="today-duty-list"><time>${escapeHtml(key)}</time>${body}</div><div class="drawer-actions"><button class="btn primary" data-reference="night-supervisor">빠른열기 · 야자감독 전체 보기</button></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBody [data-reference='night-supervisor']")?.addEventListener("click",()=>openReferenceDrawer("night-supervisor"));
}

function dashboardDutyBadgesMarkup(){
  const lunch=dashboardDutyStatus("lunch");
  const night=dashboardDutyStatus("night");
  const badge=(kind,title,status,icon,reference)=>`<button class="uep-duty-badge ${status.state}" data-reference="${reference}" data-duty-kind="${kind}" data-quick-kind="lookup" title="${title} 상세 확인"><i>${icon}</i><span>${title}</span><b>${status.label}</b>${status.detail?`<small>${escapeHtml(status.detail)}</small>`:""}</button>`;
  return `<div class="uep-duty-badges">${badge("lunch","급식지도",lunch,"♨","lunch-supervisor-today")}${badge("night","야자감독",night,"◷","night-supervisor-today")}${dashboardStudentStatusCompactMarkup()}</div>`;
}

function timetableClassColorKey(room){
  const match=String(room||"").match(/(?:^|\b)([123])-?(\d{1,2})(?:\b|$)/);
  if(!match)return "";
  return `${match[1]}-${Number(match[2])}`;
}
function timetableClassColorClass(room){
  const key=timetableClassColorKey(room);
  if(!key)return "";
  let hash=0; for(const ch of key)hash=(hash*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;
  return `class-color-${hash%9}`;
}


let dashboardTimetableMode="";
let studentTimetableSelectedId="";
function dashboardTimetableAutoMode(){
  if(dashboardTimetableMode)return dashboardTimetableMode;
  const now=new Date(),minute=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
  const end=(now.getDay()===3)?(14*60+30):(16*60+30);
  return minute>=end?"night":"day";
}
